### [1] [COAL-SQL: Coverage-Guided Augmentation and Failure-Driven Learning for Text-to-SQL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20842)
*Qifeng Cai,Xuanguang Pan,Hao Liang,Chang Xu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COAL-SQL是一个面向Text-to-SQL任务的后训练框架，通过Coverage-Guided Augmentation（CGA）识别种子集中的SQL结构缺口并定向合成补充数据，结合Failure-Driven Learning（FDL）在GRPO训练过程中动态补救模型未解决的样本。FDL包含两个层级：step-level对Solve-None样本进行CoT监督纠正，epoch-level基于失败累积检索结构相关的额外练习样本。仅用12,644条样本，在BIRD开发集上以7B模型达到64.9%执行准确率，数据效率显著优于OmniSQL等大语料方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型在复杂Text-to-SQL生成上仍需任务特定后训练。现有数据集的结构覆盖不完整，而数据增强方法通常盲目扩增；此外，单纯的SFT或RL无法动态应对训练中暴露的能力弱点。需要一种既关注训练数据结构覆盖、又能在训练过程中动态补强的统一后训练框架。

Method: 1) Coverage-Guided Augmentation (CGA)：将SQL抽象为schema无关的骨架(skeleton)，用编码器得到连续向量表示，以K-center贪心策略从外部语料池中选取与种子集互补且彼此多样的骨架，再在目标数据库上实例化为SQL-问题对，经语义一致性验证后加入训练集。2) Failure-Driven Learning (FDL)：以GRPO为主目标，定义"Solve-None"为N个rollout全部执行失败的样本。Step-level将Solve-None样本累积至缓冲队列，达到批量后由强LLM生成CoT推理轨迹并执行验证，再做SFT；Epoch-level在一个epoch结束后对失败去重，按其gold SQL骨架从题库中检索Top-L结构相似的候选项，并结合NL2SQL-BUGs错误诊断进行重排，在全局预算下分配练习样本，生成CoT轨迹后SFT。整体训练：GRPO为主，辅以两类SFT更新。

Result: 以Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct为骨干，在BIRD dev上达64.9% EX，超过GPT-4o(61.9)、GPT-4-Turbo(62.0)，以及OmniSQL(63.9, 2.5M样本)、Reasoning-SQL(64.0, 9.4k)、Share(64.1, 41k)等同类系统；数据效率1.11为对比系统中最高。在Spider test(85.3)、EHRSQL(36.2)、Science Benchmark(53.9)等跨域基准上也表现优异。换用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct同样有效，BIRD dev从42.0提升至61.8。消融实验：CGA与FDL(尤其epoch-level的failure-aware检索)均贡献显著；骨架n-gram覆盖率和恢复率在n=1,2,3上均优于OmniSQL同规模增强。训练动力学显示FDL持续降低solve-none率，结构错误在早期快速下降，schema-grounding错误在FDL辅助下逐步缓解。数据无明显train-dev污染。

Conclusion: COAL-SQL证明，通过骨架级别的覆盖引导数据增强与基于失败驱动的动态监督相结合，可以用约12.6K的小规模、任务相关数据在Text-to-SQL后训练中同时实现高准确率、强跨域泛化和高数据效率。该框架不绑定特定骨干模型，且各组件具有互补性，为面向结构化任务的LLM后训练提供了"先补结构、再动态纠错"的通用范式。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL translates natural-language questions into executable SQL queries, but open-source large language models still require task-specific post-training for complex, real-world SQL generation. Effective post-training requires both training data that cover the capabilities demanded by the target task and a learning strategy that enables the model to acquire them. Existing datasets provide valuable supervision but incompletely cover SQL structures, while augmentation methods typically expand data without identifying structural gaps. Moreover, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or reinforcement learning (RL) alone cannot dynamically address weaknesses exposed during training. We propose COAL-SQL, a unified framework combining Coverage-Guided Augmentation (CGA) and Failure-Driven Learning (FDL). CGA uses greedy selection to identify SQL structures missing from the original dataset and constructs complementary examples, improving structural coverage. FDL retains GRPO as the main optimization objective while supplying targeted supervision for unsolved examples. At the step level, it applies SFT to verified reasoning traces generated by a strong LLM for accumulated failures. At the epoch level, it retrieves structurally related examples based on accumulated failures to create targeted practice, helping the model acquire the corresponding SQL capabilities. With only 12,600 distinct post-training examples, COAL-SQL achieves 64.9% execution accuracy on the BIRD development set and outperforms baselines trained at comparable scale. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [Aligning with Lived Experience: Heterogeneous Benefits of Fine Tuning in Mental Health Support Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21075)
*Mohit Chandra,Nabin Kim,Eli Min,Aamogh Sawant,Tanmay Sutar,Munmun De Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对在线心理健康社区中大量求助帖无回复的问题，构建了基于Reddit社区经验数据的COPES数据集，并通过三轴评估框架衡量大语言模型(LLM)在生成社区一致性同伴支持方面的对齐程度。研究发现SFT/DPO后训练可显著提升通用模型的策略对齐(>50%)，但效果在不同子社区和应对策略类型间存在异质性，并导致模型过度倾向问题聚焦建议而抑制情绪聚焦策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业心理健康服务资源有限，约35%的Reddit心理健康求助帖无回复。尽管LLM在临床基准测试中表现良好，但其在生成基于人类生活经验、社区规范一致的同伴支持方面的能力尚未充分探索。本研究旨在填补LLM在生活经验导向、社区对齐的同伴支持生成方面的研究空白。

Method: 1) 从Pushshift数据集(2011-2021)收集55个心理健康相关subreddit的帖子，经多层过滤获得5,536条帖子；2) 使用GPT-4.1作为LLM评判器进行二分类同伴支持标注和四种应对策略(问题聚焦、情绪聚焦、意义聚焦、社交应对)标注，构建COPES数据集(4,455条，70/15/15划分)；3) 通过汇总Reddit评论生成偏好响应，并基于11种负面支持策略(如情感无效化、误导性乐观)生成拒绝响应以构建DPO偏好对；4) 提出三轴评估框架：策略对齐(Support Strategy Alignment，基于向量嵌入余弦相似度)、策略一致性(Strategy Congruence，响应内策略间平均余弦相似度)、情感与语气对齐(Emotion & Tone Alignment，使用Empath工具计算KL散度)；6) 评估三个模型(Qwen3-4B Instruct、Gemma-4-E4B、MediPhi-Instruct)在zero-shot、SFT、SFT+DPO三种配置下的表现，采用LoRA微调(附录A详述超参数)。

Result: 1) 策略对齐：后训练使通用模型Qwen3-4B和Gemma-4-E4B的策略对齐提升>50%(+56.52%至+60.32%)，而医学专用模型MediPhi-Instruct仅提升4.54%(SFT)；2) 策略一致性和情感语气对齐提升有限或出现下降(如Qwen情感语气对齐降低约2.11%)；3) 异质性表现——Psychosis & Anxiety(C2)和Coping & Therapy(C4)类别改善最大，Compulsive Disorders(C3)中Qwen出现异常高增长(>300%)，Mood Disorders(C5)改善最小(MediPhi出现负增长)；4) 应对策略类型分布发生偏移——后训练使模型从统一采用self-soothing和reframing转向情境特定策略，对Trauma & Abuse问题三者均趋向情绪聚焦策略(但Gemma下降20.8%)，且更倾向问题聚焦建议、抑制情绪聚焦策略；5) 主题对齐(SFT+DPO)普遍下降，Gemma在C1下降32.5%；6) 意义聚焦类应对策略的策略对齐提升最高(Gemma +74.27%, Qwen +69.44%)。

Conclusion: 基于社区驱动数据(Reddit生活经验)的后训练能有效提升LLM生成心理健康同伴支持的策略对齐，特别是对通用模型效果显著。然而，这种提升在不同模型架构、subreddit子社区和特定应对策略类型间存在显著异质性。后训练引发了应对策略分布的偏移，可能导致对某些支持类型(如情绪聚焦)的过度抑制。该工作强调了在将LLM部署到心理健康支持场景时需要考虑社区异质性和策略多样性，并提示COPES应仅作为研究基准而非实际部署依据。

Abstract: As access to professional mental healthcare remains limited, many individuals turn to online platforms such as Reddit to seek peer support situated within human lived experience. However, a significant portion of such queries go unanswered, presenting an opportunity for using Large Language Models (LLMs) to fill this gap. While LLMs have demonstrated strong performance on clinical benchmarks, their ability to generate lived-experience informed and community-aligned peer support is underexplored. Addressing this gap, we introduce the COmmunity-centered Peer Engaged Support (COPES) dataset and a three-axis evaluation framework to assess LLM alignment with community perspectives to mental health support seeking queries. Evaluating zero-shot and post-trained (SFT and DPO) models, we show that post-training on COPES significantly improves Strategy Alignment (>50% for general-purpose models) and alignment in Emotion & Tone. However, we also observe that such improvements are heterogeneous and alignment improvements vary significantly across subreddits and requested coping strategies. Furthermore, post-training induces distributional shifts, heavily favoring problem-focused recommendations while suppressing emotion-focused strategies. Together, this work shows that while curating community-driven data improves the alignment of LLM responses, model performance remains disparate across distinct sub-communities and specific mental health needs.

</details>


### [3] [Geometry of Values: Task Vector Composition for Ethical Preference Alignment in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21094)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含12,000个两选项道德困境的多语言多价值冲突数据集,基于Honesty、Justice、Autonomy三种价值的成对冲突,涵盖英、印地、阿、西、中五种语言。研究发现紧凑LLM(Llama-3.2 1B/3B)经LoRA SFT/DPO微调后能有效学习价值偏好(准确率≥98%),而GPT-5-mini的提示词引导则不稳定且跨语言表现差异大。核心贡献是提出基于任务向量(task vector)的"偏好向量"提取与正交化方法,可将指令跟随向量与价值偏好向量解耦,实现无需重训练即可翻转价值排序,保留≥96%的微调性能。但传递性组合(链式价值推理)失败,表明价值在权重空间中并不具备完全线性组合的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在道德敏感应用中展现出隐藏的价值偏见和跨语言不一致性,而现有的多语言伦理研究多聚焦于文化刻板印象或毒性,缺少对"普世道德原则之间直接冲突"的系统测试。开源小模型(1B-3B)的伦理对齐能力及其价值偏好的"模块化"特性(能否通过权重操作按需切换价值优先级)尚未被探索。

Method: (1)构建数据集:基于20个社会场景手工框架,用GPT-4o为三对价值冲突(A=Honesty, B=Justice, C=Autonomy)各生成4000个两选项困境样本(英文),再机翻为四种其他语言;Gemini-3作为自动验证器修正4055个样本;另由6位标注者构建60条人工gold测试集。(2)基线测试:GPT-5-mini零样本与提示词引导;Llama-3.2 1B/3B零样本。(3)参数高效微调:LoRA(r=4/16)分别训练SFT与DPO(三种随机种子)。(4)任务向量方法:计算偏好模型与基模型的权重差Δ,利用一对相反偏好模型差值的均值估计"指令跟随向量",将Δ正交化以分离出"纯偏好向量",再通过网格搜索混合系数γ₁、γ₂将其反向加回基模型以翻转价值排序。

Result: (1)基线:GPT-5-mini在所有语言中一致偏好Honesty>Autonomy(B>C约70%);提示词引导总体有效但印地语与对抗默认偏好的场景失败;Llama小模型存在强首选项位置偏见。(2)微调:LoRA SFT/DPO均将准确率提升至≥98%,在人工gold集上保持≥90%,证明泛化至未见叙述风格。(3)任务向量:偏好反转在3B模型上保留≥96%的微调性能(多数情况下),1B模型保留≥80%;传递性组合(chain of value preference)失败——各偏好向量在权重空间中近似正交(夹角>80°),无法通过线性算术合成新排序。

Conclusion: 紧凑LLM经轻量PEFT能学习稳定且可泛化的价值偏好;提示词策略对跨语言与对抗默认偏好的指令不可靠。任务向量算术可成功解耦"指令跟随"与"价值偏好"成分,实现无需重训练的按需价值切换,具备实用价值多元化的潜力。但价值几何并非完全线性——局部二元偏好可反转,但传递性组合不成立,这限定了方法的可组合性边界。该工作为价值多元化的工程化对齐提供了经验证据与几何视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in applications that must weigh clashing moral values, yet even strong models exhibit hidden biases and brittle instruction-following across languages. We introduce a 12,000-instance dataset of two-option dilemmas covering pairwise three value conflicts: Honesty vs. Justice, Justice vs. Autonomy, and Autonomy vs. Honesty, along with their translations into Hindi, Arabic, Spanish, and Chinese, to probe cross-lingual behavior. Benchmarking on GPT-5-mini reveals that it consistently favors Honesty over Autonomy across all five languages when no policy is given. The Llama-3.2-1/3B models exhibit strong first-option bias; however, both plain fine-tuning and Direct Preference Optimization fine-tuning effectively remove this bias, increasing accuracy to greater than 98%. In order to decouple the effect of learning correlations in the dataset from abstract values, we propose a task vector transfer based experiment where after computing the task vectors for a direction of value preference we orthogonalize it with respect to the general instruction following vector. Our experiment shows that this method is effective in isolating the direction of the specific value preference that can successfully be used to conduct task arithmetic to obtain a model with the opposite stance.

</details>


### [4] [From Task Success to Productive Success: Evaluating Human-AI Collaboration by Quality and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21117)
*Saki Imai,Mert İnan,Malihe Alikhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以"产出质量相对于交互成本"为核心的人机协作生产力评估框架，用以区分"高效成功"与"高成本成功"，并在四个任务上揭示了主观评分无法捕捉成本差异、对话模式与生产力之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生产力评估（如任务完成时间、经济价值、产出质量）将人机协作视为黑箱，仅衡量最终产出而忽略了产生该产出所需的交互成本。两个质量相同的协作会话可能在用户付出的交互努力上存在巨大差异，因此需要一种能同时考量质量与成本的评估框架。

Method: 基于经济学中的生产力概念和Clark的"最小协作努力"原则，定义协作生产力 P_z = z(Q) - z(C)，即任务内标准化的产出质量与加权token交互成本之差。在两个数据集（CoGym三个任务+自收集可视化任务，共233个会话）上实施该指标，并使用基于接地行为（grounding acts）和正向摩擦（positive friction）的对话标注来分析生产性对话模式。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 相同质量评分的会话在交互成本上可相差高达70倍；(2) 质量-成本关系因任务而异（相关工作写作ρ=+0.55为正，可视化ρ=-0.48为负）；(3) 主观评分不能可靠替代生产力度量；(4) 高生产力会话中智能体更早进行探测性提问，用户减少修复和澄清工作，呈现"智能体探测→用户确认"的模式。

Conclusion: 生产性人机协作并非简单地缩短交互，而在于交互工作如何在双方之间分配。有效的摩擦（agent主动澄清）是生产性的，无效的摩擦（用户被迫修复）则是昂贵的。生产力评估必须基于任务进行解读，主观评分应明确其所测量的具体构念，AI系统应被设计为前置澄清、主动探测以减轻用户的接地负担。

Abstract: AI productivity is often measured by task completion time, economic value, or improvements in outcome quality. However, these measures usually treat collaboration as a black box where they capture what output was produced, but not the interaction cost required to produce it. Motivated by economics literature, we introduce a productivity-oriented framework for evaluating human-AI collaboration as outcome quality relative to interaction cost. Across two datasets spanning four tasks, we show that: (1) sessions with identical quality ratings can differ by up to 70 times in interaction cost; (2) quality-cost relationships vary by task, with some tasks rewarding extended interaction and others favoring fast convergence; (3) subjective user ratings are not reliable substitutes for productivity; and (4) productive sessions are characterized by agents probing earlier and users spending less effort repairing the interaction. By distinguishing productive success from costly success, our framework makes interactional cost visible and shows how dialogue analysis can inform the evaluation and design of AI systems.

</details>


### [5] [Hallucination-R1: Robustness-Oriented Paraphrase Generation for Factual Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21227)
*Wenhan Yu,Wenxin Wu,Hao Wang,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Hallucination-R1 框架，通过两阶段 GRPO 优化生成"语义等价但能暴露事实一致性缺陷"的释义，用于评估和提升 LLM 在事实问答中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在语义等价的释义改写下可能出现事实幻觉（原本答对，改写后答错），表明存在"潜在事实不稳定性"。通用释义要么近乎复制（信号弱）、要么过度改写（破坏语义等价），无法有效用于鲁棒性监督。

Method: 两阶段 GRPO 训练基于 Qwen2.5 的释义生成器：(1) 语义稳定阶段——以语义一致性为硬约束、组内相对多样性(Self-BLEU + 句向量余弦)为软奖励；(2) 鲁棒性挑战阶段——额外加入下游 QA 模型在释义上的事实不一致暴露奖励（DeepSeek-V3 作为 LLM 评判）。训练信号包含一致性、多样性、事实不一致暴露三部分；QA 模型采用确定性解码以隔离输入扰动影响。

Result: 在 SimpleQuestions、PopQA、TruthfulQA 上，仅 3B 参数的 Hallucination-R1 即可跨多个 QA 模型家族（Qwen2.5/3、LLaMA-3.1、DeepSeek-V3/R1、GPT-5）暴露显著的事实一致性退化（WER@5 最高达 87%+）。消融显示两阶段缺一不可，且小模型经训练后优于更大未经训练的释义模型。诊断分析表明失败并非源于表层长度/句法变化，而是 QA 拼接表征层面的语义漂移和后期层对正确/错误答案 token 的偏好翻转。轻量 LoRA SFT 实验显示，使用 Hallucination-R1 生成数据微调 Qwen3-8B 可提升 Robust Acc. 且跨数据集/跨释义模型泛化。

Conclusion: 将释义生成重新定义为面向鲁棒性的学习问题，可同时作为事实稳定性的诊断探针和鲁棒性训练的监督信号；事实不一致性在语义保持的表层变化下并非平凡伪影，而反映非平凡的内部事实偏好漂移。

Abstract: Factual hallucination is commonly defined by incorrect factual outputs. We study a paraphrase-induced hallucination setting, where a model answers a factual question correctly in its original form but generates an incorrect answer under a semantically equivalent paraphrase. Such inconsistencies expose latent factual instability under semantic invariance. However, general-purpose paraphrases are often insufficient as robustness-oriented supervision: near-copy paraphrases provide weak signals, while overly diverse paraphrases may break semantic equivalence. In this paper, we propose HALLUCINATION-R1, a robustness-oriented paraphrase generation framework that learns to produce semantically faithful yet robustness-challenging paraphrases for factual consistency. Through two-stage optimization, it first stabilizes meaning-preserving and diverse paraphrasing, then rewards paraphrases that reveal factual consistency degradation in downstream QA models. Experiments on SimpleQuestions, PopQA, and TruthfulQA show that HALLUCINATION-R1 achieves a strong consistency--diversity trade-off and exposes robustness failures across multiple model families and datasets. Further analyses indicate that these failures are not reducible to surface-level artifacts or semantic drift, but reveal non-trivial factual instability under meaning-preserving variation. A lightweight fine-tuning study also shows that HALLUCINATION-R1-generated data improves robust accuracy under paraphrase variations, suggesting its utility for robustness-oriented training. Our code and models are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [6] [From Memory to Behavior: A Behavior-Aware Role-Playing Framework for Social Media Influencers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21349)
*Ji-Lun Peng,Yi-Zhen Zhang,Chun-Nan Chou,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SIBPersona 框架，通过引入"情境–内部状态–行为"（SIB）三元组来建模社交媒体影响者的情境依赖行为策略，同时设计了一种参考增强的 LLM 评估协议用于评估对知名度较低的真实个体的角色扮演效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 ICL 的角色扮演方法主要依赖历史对话中提取的事实性信息（如记忆和知识），但未能捕捉个体在不同情境下的行为策略；同时，针对虚构角色的 LLM 评估协议对知名度较低的真实个体效果不佳，因为评估模型对这些个体的先验知识有限。本文旨在解决这两个挑战。

Method: 1）提出 SIBPersona 方法：将个体行为建模为反应过程（Reaction Process），即"情境（Situation）→内部状态（Internal State）→行为策略（Behavior）"的三元组（SIB Triplet）。数据变体人格被划分为记忆、说话风格和反应过程三个维度语料库。两阶段管线构建：先进行维度相关性过滤，再进行维度文档提取。在推理时通过 RAG 从三个语料库各检索 top-3 相关文档（共 9 篇）作为 LLM 的上下文演示。2）设计参考增强的评估协议：针对知名度较低的真实个体，从测试样本池中通过 K-medoids 聚类选取代表性维度文档作为 LLM 评估器的参考，并采用基于缺陷惩罚的评估方式（severity 1–5）。3）构建了一个来自 CMoney Forum 的六位金融影响者数据集（涵盖评论-回复对），并通过分层抽样方法确保覆盖不同交互类型。

Result: 1）在六位影响者上，SIBPersona 在说话风格（T）和反应过程（RP）维度上持续优于 Naive、CoT、IMPersona 和 Vanilla RAG 基线，具有统计显著性；消融实验表明移除 RP 维度会导致显著性能下降。2）参考增强评估协议与人类判断呈中等相关（Pearson r=0.561, Spearman ρ=0.530），显著优于 BLEU/ROUGE/BERTScore 等传统指标。3）在 CharacterEval 和 RoleAgentBench 虚构角色基准上也取得了具有统计显著性的改进，性能可与 CoSER 等专门微调的模型相媲美。4）人类评估显示可解释的 SIB 三元组显著提升回复质量，SIBPersona 在比较评估中与真人回复的相近度得分（0.71）与真实回复相同。

Conclusion: 通过将行为信息（情境依赖的反应过程）融入角色扮演框架，可显著提升对社交媒体上真实个体的角色扮演保真度，且该方法可泛化至虚构角色基准。SIBPersona 提供了结构化、可解释、可追溯的人格表示，支持错误诊断和修正。所提出的参考增强评估协议为评估对知名度较低的真实个体的角色扮演提供了合理的自动化方案。局限：难以处理实时信息查询和讽刺评论；目前仅限单轮回复；维度文档通过 ICL 而非模型训练注入。

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong potential as role-playing agents for real individuals, yet faithful impersonating remains challenging. Existing in-context learning-based methods fail to capture how individuals react under different situations. In addition, LLM-based evaluation is difficult for obscure individuals. To address these challenges, we propose Situation--Internal state--Behavior Persona method to incorporate situation-dependent behavioral strategies. We further design an evaluation protocol that provides LLM evaluators with references about the impersonated individual. We evaluate our approach on a newly constructed dataset for the task of generating replies on social media. Experimental results show that our proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art ICL-based baselines, while our evaluation protocol achieves moderate correlation with human judgment. Besides, experiments on fictional-character benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed method is applicable beyond the social media setting. These findings suggest that incorporating behavioral information broadly improves the fidelity of role-playing for real individuals on social media or fictional characters.

</details>


### [7] [Consistent Relexicalization of Clinical Documents using Graph-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21387)
*Dipankar Das,Atri Mandal,Sandeep Singh,Tushar Shandhilya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: G-RELIC提出了一种基于图结构的临床文档再词汇化框架，通过LLM与知识图谱结合，解决了纵向病历中实体身份碎片化、时间不一致和医学不合理性等一致性难题，在关系完整性（提升30.4个百分点）和时间一致性（提升45.9个百分点）方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有再词汇化方法对实体进行独立替换，导致纵向病历中出现身份碎片化（同一实体被映射为不同代理）、身份合并（不同实体映射为同一代理）以及时间不一致（出院日期先于入院日期等医学不合理现象），严重降低了合成数据集对下游临床研究的分析价值。

Method: 提出G-RELIC框架，采用三阶段流水线：(1) PHI/PII实体识别与角色分类（NER+LLM）；(2) 构建临床知识图谱（CKG），通过分层实体对齐机制（O(1)确定性主标识符匹配+概率性次级属性加权相似度评分，使用Jaro-Winkler距离等度量）实现一对一映射；(3) 时间再词汇化，采用混合验证引擎（基于ICD-10的规则层+LLM验证层）确保时序一致性，满足时序一致性、区间一致性、日历一致性和医学合理性四项约束。

Result: 在400份临床文档（50位患者、15位临床医生、5家医疗机构）的异构语料上评估：实体一致性达92.5%（基线62.1%，提升30.4个百分点），时间一致性达91.9%（基线46.0%，提升45.9个百分点），同时满足PHI/PII去标识化的隐私基准（AON Recall）。效率方面，输入token减少83%，输出token减少84%，P90延迟降低65%。生产环境中，7天内处理1,176,642次请求，成功率98.7%。

Conclusion: G-RELIC通过图结构与LLM结合，提供了数学上有界、确定性的一致性保证，显著提升了再词汇化数据集的分析效用。未来工作包括引入图神经网络（GNN）学习疾病特异性时序属性、集成差分隐私保证、多语言扩展以及大规模生产环境评估。

Abstract: Relexicalization is a pivotal technique in clinical NLP, as it facilitates robust masking of sensitive information while synthesizing datasets that retain high-fidelity, real-world characteristics. However, preserving structural integrity, relational coherence, and temporal consistency during transformation remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches frequently rely on independent entity replacement, which results in clinical inconsistencies across longitudinal records. This reduces the value of such relexicalized datasets for downstream scientific analysis. To address these limitations, we introduce G-RELIC (Graph Based Contextual Relexicalization with Improved Consistency) which combines the power of LLMs with graphs. G-RELIC implements a graph-based mapping mechanism which optimizes for one-to-one correspondence between original and surrogate entities. It also introduces a deterministic temporal repositioning algorithm to preserve temporal consistency. Empirical evaluations on diverse, real-world clinical datasets validate that G-RELIC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. G-RELIC yields a 30.4 percentage point improvement in relational integrity (62.1% to 92.5%) and 45.9 percentage point improvement in temporal coherence (46% to 91.9%) without compromising on the recognized privacy benchmarks for clinical datasets. This maximizes the analytical utility of relexicalized datasets while minimizing re-identification risk.

</details>


### [8] [Talking Past the Machine: Morality, Politeness, and Alignment in Human-AI Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21401)
*Marina Mitiaeva,Lu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对约27,000个对话（15,881个Human-ChatGPT，10,784个Human-Human）的大规模计算分析，系统比较了人类-AI对话与人类-人类对话在道德表达、礼貌和语言对齐三个合作性交流维度上的差异。研究发现AI能够"模拟"合作性交流的表面特征，但缺乏支撑这些特征的社会架构：AI的道德表达是预先配置的而非协商生成的，情感温暖缺乏面子敏感性，对话中的语言融合持续下降。更为惊人的是，部分支撑人类相互适应的合作机制在AI语境下发生了方向反转。唯一在两种交互类型中均稳定预测对齐的因素是"主动性"（agency），即给予用户塑造交流的空间。研究呼吁将评估视角从单轮转向交互全程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话AI虽能生成流畅且社会恰当的回应，但其所展现的是合作交流的真实特征还是仅模拟其表面形式仍不明确。这一问题关系到AI系统的评估、信任和设计方式。现有研究多单独考察道德、礼貌或对齐维度，鲜有研究将三者置于同一分析框架下，考察其在多轮对话中共同演化的过程，以及与人类-人类对话的差异。这种缺口可能将"社会信号流畅性"误判为"能力"，进而导致过度依赖。

Method: 研究采用计算语料库分析对比两类公开对话数据集：Human-AI交互来自WildChat（含GPT-3.5、GPT-4 Preview/Turbo、GPT-4o、GPT-4.1 Mini），Human-Human交互来自Topical-Chat。三个核心构念的测量方法分别为：道德（Moral Foundations Theory，通过ME2-BERT对五大道德基础care、fairness、loyalty、authority、sanctity进行0-1评分）；礼貌（采用Politeness R包提取32个礼貌标记，按modulation、agency、affect、structure四个功能维度归一化）；对齐（在词汇、句法、语用、情感四个通道上测量相邻对话轮的融合度）。分析采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验检测对话者内部不对称性，通过对话级别斜率追踪时序演化，并拟合控制前一轮对齐的混合效应回归预测下一轮对齐，同时进行多重中介分析。设计上以同一语料库内部对话者差异作为对比对象，避免跨语料库绝对值比较。

Result: RQ1显示Human-AI对话中存在显著的说话者不对称：AI承担了大部分道德内容（loyalty和authority维度差异最大）和情感温暖，但缺乏相应的人际敏感性，结构化组织工作则由人类单独承担；Human-Human对话则接近对称。RQ2显示AI的道德强度在对话中保持平稳（预配置式），而人类的道德表达则动态协商；且Human-AI对齐持续下降，Human-Human对齐则趋于均衡；模型版本迭代中道德表达趋于缓和但合作性并未相应改善（GPT-3.5对齐最强，GPT-4 Turbo/4o下降最陡）。RQ3显示部分合作机制方向反转：hedging/softening在人类中预测更高的对齐但在AI中预测更低的；sanctity框架在人类中引发分歧但在AI中引发用户向其靠拢；唯一方向一致的正向预测因子是agency。Prior alignment对AI下一轮对齐的预测力约为人类的9倍。

Conclusion: 研究结论为：AI能够再现合作性交流的表面信号却未参与其下的社会架构；支撑人类相互适应的机制在AI语境下甚至会反向运行。从设计角度看，agency是值得关注的可测试目标，但单纯降低表达性不足以改善合作；从评估角度看，逐轮评估可能误导——单轮得分高与交互级合作性差可能共存。研究呼吁将评估视角从单轮转向对话全程，更关注交互轨迹而非单次输出，并指出未来需通过匹配启动的实验设计、跨模型家族复现和纵向研究来验证这些发现的普适性。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems produce fluent, socially appropriate responses, yet whether they participate in cooperative communication or merely simulate its surface forms remains unclear - a question central to how these systems are evaluated, trusted, and designed. This study investigates how morality, politeness, and alignment - three dimensions central to cooperative dialogue - function in human-AI interaction compared to human-human conversation. We analyze 15,881 human-ChatGPT and 10,784 human-human multi-turn dialogues, using mixed-effects models to identify which features predict turn-to-turn alignment. We observe a consistent dissociation: AI produces the surface features of cooperative communication without the underlying social architecture. Moral output appears preconfigured rather than negotiated; warmth is generated without face sensitivity; linguistic convergence declines persistently. Most strikingly, the cooperative mechanisms themselves reverse direction: hedging and softening associated with greater accommodation between humans are associated with reduced alignment when produced by AI, and purity framing associated with human divergence coincides with users converging toward the AI. Agency - giving users room to shape the exchange - is the most consistent predictor of alignment across both interaction types, while lower moral assertiveness in more recent models is not accompanied by better cooperation. Together these patterns suggest that AI reproduces the surface of cooperation without the mutual adaptation that grounds it between humans - and, more surprisingly, that mechanisms sustaining human accommodation can run in reverse with AI, suggesting a turn-level view may be insufficient for interaction-level success.

</details>


### [9] [Steering LLMs Responses Towards Moral Foundations on the Norwegian MFQ-30](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21636)
*Hans Andersen,David Dichas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用挪威语版 MFQ-30 道德基础问卷对 6 个开源大语言模型进行测试,并与 N=1282 的挪威受访者样本对比,系统比较了"提示词层人格引导"与"激活层 ActAdd"两种引导方法的效果,发现前者能显著缩小模型与人类道德画像的差距,后者在固定中层注入时反而使画像扁平化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类心理测量问卷(如 MFQ)直接套用于 LLM 是否真能测量出稳定构念,以及能否将模型的"道德画像"引导至目标人群,是当前 LLM 价值对齐研究中的开放问题。已有研究多在英语世界进行,且少有工作将提示词层与激活层引导方法做头对头比较。本文以挪威语场景为切入点,试图同时回答这两个问题。

Method: 1) 数据:使用 Enstad & Finseraas (2024) 验证的挪威语 MFQ-30 及 N=1282 挪威 Kantar 网络样本作为人类参照;2) 模型:6 个开源 LLM(Qwen2.5/3、Gemma 4-E4B-it、norallm 系列 Normistral);3) 解码策略:首 token 概率解码(对 1–6 数字 token 取 softmax 后计算加权平均),并在提示词后追加 "Svar:" 后缀以避免模型退化为单一数字;4) 评估指标:基于人类协方差矩阵的 Mahalanobis d²,以及两道注意力检测题的联合通过概率;5) 鲁棒性扰动:在 2×2 网格上同时变换正向/反向 Likert 标度与逐项/批量呈现方式;6) 引导干预:提示词层注入 Nordic 应答者人格(nordic_b),以及 ActAdd 在固定层 15、α=5 处注入由对比对构造的引导向量;7) 报告方式:对正反向标度取中点作为无偏估计。

Result: 基线阶段:3 个模型(Gemma 4-E4B-it、Qwen3-14B、Qwen3-8B)通过注意力检测,另 3 个(Normistral-7B/11B-T、Qwen2.5-1.5B)输出扁平或中心倾向,均值看似接近人类实则未真正作答。Gemma 4 与 Qwen3-14B 在正反向标度下均稳定通过。提示词层引导:nordic_b 人格(不包含任何分布信息)使三个通过模型的 Mahalanobis d² 分别下降 44%(Gemma 4: 3.65→2.05)、62%(Qwen3-14B: 6.26→2.37)与 77%(Qwen3-8B: 11.59→2.69)。尤其 Qwen3-8B 在反向标度下的注意力通过率从约 30% 跃升至约 98%。ActAdd 引导:在固定中层 15、α=5 处注入时,一对对比向量并未选择性调节单一基础,而是使整体画像扁平化;Qwen3-8B/14B 在 α=±5 下五个基础得分差异 <0.13;Gemma 4 在忠诚/背叛向量下虽然忠诚上升 5.7,但权威与纯洁同步上升 8.4 与 5.9,呈现明显的"集群漂移"而非单基础调节。

Conclusion: 1) 人类心理问卷在 LLM 上未必测量稳定构念,半数模型默认输出看似人类却未真正作答,注意力检测不可或缺;2) 简洁的中性人口学人格提示足以将"参与作答"的模型大幅推向挪威人群画像,且该效果不依赖任何答案分布泄露;3) 同一段人格甚至能让原本不参与的模型(如 Qwen3-8B 反向标度下)开始参与问卷,这正是 Peereboom et al. (2025) 所警示的"认知幽灵"现象——问卷直到模型被要求扮演应答者时才"测量"到东西;4) 轻量单对 ActAdd 在固定中层注入无法实现选择性单基础引导,而是把整个画像压平,提示激活层引导需在层位、系数与对比对的构造上做更系统的搜索(可能需要平均多对的 CAA 变体)。

Abstract: Recent work applies human psychometric questionnaires to large language models to elicit moral and value profiles, but it is not clear whether these instruments measure anything stable in models or whether the resulting profiles can be moved toward a target human population. We administer the Norwegian Moral Foundations Questionnaire (MFQ-30) to six open-weight LLMs and compare their foundation profiles to a sample of N = 1,282 Norwegian respondents. We test two steering interventions, prompt-level persona steering and activation-level ActAdd. Half the models engage with the questionnaire under our attention check. The other half default to flat or central-tendency outputs that look near-human on average without tracking item content. A neutral Nordic-respondent persona, written without any distributional information from the human sample, brings the engaging models 44-77% closer to the Norwegian mean in Mahalanobis $d^2$. One-pair ActAdd at a fixed mid-layer flattens the foundation profile rather than steering individual foundations. For at least one model the same persona that shifts the profile also induces engagement that was absent at baseline, a concrete instance of the cognitive phantoms that Peereboom et al. (2025) warn about.

</details>


### [10] [Chinese Competitive Debating Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21637)
*Zongrui Yang,Haoyuan Li,Zhongsheng Wang,Zhirui Zeng,Pengqian Han,Yi Zhou,Yuting Wang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于评估大语言模型理解中文竞技辩论能力的多粒度数据集与基准。该数据集包含148场比赛（最终保留自182场）、2698个阶段和20542个交流单元，由120名专业裁判依据统一评分量表进行现场裁定，涵盖了比赛、阶段和发言人级别的三种评估任务。零样本评估显示，当前大语言模型在获胜倾向预测、阶段评分预测和最佳辩手预测任务上与专业裁判的判断仍存在较大差距，最佳模型的获胜预测准确率仅为66.2%，最佳辩手预测准确率为56.8%，阶段评分的最高皮尔逊相关系数仅为0.250。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的辩论数据集很少同时具备细粒度的辩论文本和真实竞赛中由专业裁判依据统一量表所做出的评判，导致难以系统评估大语言模型对辩论过程中交互式论证的理解能力及其与人类专业裁判的一致性。本文旨在填补该空缺。

Method: （1）设计统一的三维度阶段评分量表（真诚度判断、推进程度、任务完成度）；（2）组织182场真实4v4与2v2中文辩论比赛，由120名经过资质筛选的专业裁判独立评分；（3）进行转录校对、阶段切分与人工核验，构建比赛、阶段、交流单元三级粒度数据；（5）设置获胜倾向预测、阶段评分预测、最佳辩手投票分布预测三项任务；（6）在零样本条件下对多个主流大语言模型进行评测，并分析模型预测与人类裁判判断的一致性。

Result: 获胜倾向预测任务中，模型准确率范围为56.1%至66.2%（正方基线为50.7%）；阶段评分预测任务中，最高皮尔逊相关系数仅0.250，最高非平局一致率为63.8%；最佳辩手预测任务中，最高准确率为56.8%（gpt-5.6-sol）。各模型的绝对校准能力与相对排序能力并不一致，表明不同指标反映不同的判断能力维度；结构先验（如"获胜方三辩手"+ "阶段角色"）能解释部分表现，但不能完全解释模型预测，表明模型确实利用了部分赛场内表现信息。

Conclusion: 比赛结果、阶段表现和个体贡献体现了三种不同的判断能力维度；不同评测指标反映的能力并不一致，单一指标无法充分表征模型对辩论的评判能力。当前大语言模型尚不能稳定地在整体结果、局部过程和个体贡献三个层面重现专业裁判的判断。该数据集与基准为研究大语言模型对交互式论证的理解及与人类专业判断的对齐提供了细粒度测试平台。

Abstract: Debate adjudication requires tracking how arguments develop through interaction, yet existing datasets rarely combine fine-grained debate transcripts with professional judgments collected during real competitions under a shared rubric. We introduce a dataset and benchmark for evaluating large language models' understanding of competitive Chinese-language debate at the match, stage, and speaker levels. We organized 182 matches and recruited 120 professional judges, with each match independently adjudicated by three judges using a predefined rubric. After excluding matches with incomplete records, the dataset contains 148 matches, 2,698 stages, and 20,542 exchange units, with manually verified transcripts and segmentation. It preserves original stage scores, match votes, best-debater ballots, and adjudication rationales. We define three tasks: winner-tendency prediction, stage-score prediction, and best-debater prediction. Zero-shot evaluation of multiple large language models yields a highest winner-prediction accuracy of 66.2%, a highest Pearson correlation of 0.250 between model stage scores and mean human ratings, and a highest best-debater prediction accuracy of 56.8%. The dataset and benchmark provide a testbed for studying large language models' understanding of interactive argumentation and their agreement with professional judges.

</details>


### [11] [When Steering Fails in Latent Reasoning: A Latent-to-Language Transition Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21662)
*Gaoxiang Huang,Lei Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现激活引导（activation steering）在潜空间推理（latent CoT）中失效：在连续思维（continuous thoughts）上施加干预虽能显著移动隐藏表示，却几乎不影响后续语言生成。作者提出"潜空间到语言的过渡间隙"（latent-to-language transition gap）假设，并通过两个补充实验证实：在过渡边界处输出分布发生突变，任务相关方向在双向控制中效果大幅减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导在显式思维链（CoT）中被广泛用于因果探针式地理解模型表征与生成的联系，但其在潜空间 CoT（连续思维）中的有效性尚不明确。如果潜空间表征是未来推理的关键接口，必须验证干预效应能否跨越"潜空间到语言"的过渡传递到输出。

Method: 方法包括：1) 使用 CAA（Contrastive Activation Addition）在第 16 层残差流上施加方向干预，强度 λ∈{±1,±5,±10}；2) 在 Llama-3.1-8B 和 Llama-2-7B 的显式 CoT 与 COCONUT 风格 latent-CoT（5 个连续思维）变体上进行对照；3) 三个任务（情感、政治立场、真理性）上的评估；4) 三项指标：生成文本的引导成功率（SR）、评判器无关的 stance-margin 变化 Δstance、以及标准化表征位移 dz；5) 进一步通过 AUROC 验证任务信息在连续思维中仍可解码；6) 通过相邻位置 JS 散度比 Rtransition 衡量过渡边界处输出分布的突变程度；7) 用双向引导增益 Godd 衡量任务方向对输出的双向控制强度。

Result: 主要结果：1) 失败模式——在 λ=10 时，latent-CoT 的表征位移略大于显式 CoT，却仅保留 1–5% 的 stance-token 概率变化，引导成功率显著降低（如情感任务上 46% → 3%）；2) 任务信息在连续思维中仍高度可解码（AUROC 0.842–1.000，跨两种模型、三个任务）；3) 过渡边界处输出分布发生突变——Llama-3.1-8B 上 latent-CoT 的 Rtransition 为 14.77–24.22，是匹配 CoT（1.13–1.25）的 13–19 倍；4) 双向引导增益显著减弱——latent-CoT 比显式 CoT 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上弱 26.6–70.2 倍，Llama-2-7B 上弱 9.4–13.7 倍。

Conclusion: 结论区分了激活引导中常被混同的三个性质：任务信息是否可表征、干预是否移动表征、移动是否控制输出。研究表明表征层面的可解码性与位移并不能保证对生成输出的因果控制——在 latent-CoT 中，干预效应在潜空间到语言的过渡处传递失败。潜空间引导方法必须将"过渡界面"作为评估与设计的关键目标，未来工作应探索过渡感知的干预机制与底层因果机制。

Abstract: Activation steering has become a widely used approach for controlling language models during explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, motivating its extension to latent CoT. However, we find that steering continuous thoughts produces substantially weaker effects on subsequent language generation than steering explicit CoT, even when the hidden representations are moved by comparable amounts. We first show that task information remains identifiable in continuous thoughts. Hence, we hypothesize a \textbf{latent-to-language transition gap}, in which an intervention effect in latent space fails to transfer to language generation. Two further results support this hypothesis: the output distribution changes abruptly at the transition boundary, and task-related directions exert much weaker bidirectional control in latent CoT than in explicit CoT. These findings identify the transition interface as a central target for evaluating and designing future latent-steering methods.

</details>


### [12] [CIBuzzBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Understanding of Chinese Internet Buzzwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21722)
*Yifan Wang,Junyu Lu,Qifan Wang,Shun Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Jiahao Liu,Zhijun Cao,Lingbin Bu,Fanliang Bu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CIBuzzBench，首个针对中文网络流行语的中英跨语言理解基准，包含3,001条标注条目和三个评测任务（释义、等价词选择、危害性检测），实验表明主流大模型在文化隐喻、谐音及语用精确匹配方面仍存在显著困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文网络流行语具有高度非字面性、文化根植性和快速演化特性，其含义往往通过谐音、隐喻、引语、缩写等机制产生。现有研究多聚焦于中文单语理解，而模型能否将这些文化负载知识跨语言迁移至英语，并在跨文化传播与内容安全场景中准确传达原意，仍是一个未充分探索的问题。

Method: 1) 从中文网络流行语百科网站和已有数据集人工收集3,001条网络用语及其中文释义；2) 按六类形成机制（经验、引语、修辞、谐音、俚语、缩写）标注类别；3) 使用GPT-5.5辅助生成英文释义、英文等价词及三个受控干扰项（字面、文化错配、语用近邻），并由NLP研究生团队进行多轮审核与验证；4) 设计三项零样本评测任务：Meaning Explanation、Equivalent Selection、Harmfulness Detection；5) 评测六个代表性大模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.1 Pro、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Qwen3.7-Max、GLM-5.2）在中英两种提示语下的表现，并辅以少样本提示、LoRA微调及模型规模对比实验；6) 通过人工评分与LLM评分（0-5分制）以及宏F1进行评估，并分析错误类型与类别分布。

Result: 1) 释义任务：Gemini 3.1 Pro表现最佳（人工分3.90-4.10，LLM分4.04-4.12），但所有模型平均仅约3.34-3.69（满分5），难以精准表达非字面含义；2) 等价词选择：Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.5最稳定（宏F1约87-88%），平均83-85%；错误集中在字面和语用近邻干扰项；3) 危害性检测：平均宏F1 75-76%，有害类F1仅约57-59%，校准性较差；4) 类别差异显著：引语类最难做等价匹配，谐音和修辞类最难做危害性检测；5) 少样本提示和模型规模增大均带来提升，但LoRA微调在释义和危害性检测上仍弱于API模型；6) 中文提示总体略优于英文提示，但模式因模型而异。

Conclusion: CIBuzzBench揭示了当前大模型在中英跨语言文化负载理解上的持续短板：模型可识别宽泛主题或情感倾向，却难以精准捕捉非字面语义、语用立场及隐性危害，且释义正确并不保证等价词选择和危害性判断的一致性。研究呼吁未来在时序更新、丰富语境、多目标语言及解耦字面形式/语用意图/文化指涉/安全判断等方面进一步探索，以提升模型对网络语言的跨文化解读与安全校准能力。

Abstract: Chinese social media has generated a vast and continually evolving lexicon of internet buzzwords whose meanings are often non-literal and deeply rooted in local cultural and pragmatic contexts. Existing research has primarily focused on interpreting these buzzwords within Chinese, leaving largely unexplored whether LLMs can transfer such culturally grounded knowledge across languages and accurately convey the intended meanings in English. This cross-lingual capability is also critical for safety, as harmful expressions may obscure their offensive content through culture-specific homophony, euphemism, irony, or coded language. In this paper, we investigate the ability of advanced LLMs to understand Chinese internet buzzwords across languages. To this end, we introduce CIBuzzBench, the first benchmark for cross-lingual Chinese-to-English understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords. CIBuzzBench comprises 3,001 Chinese internet buzzwords annotated with English meaning explanations, English equivalents, category labels, and harmfulness labels. Based on these annotations, we design three evaluation tasks: Meaning Explanation, Cross-lingual Equivalent Matching, and Culturally Grounded Harmfulness Detection. We evaluate representative state-of-the-art proprietary and Chinese LLMs under both English- and Chinese-prompting settings. Our results show that LLMs continue to struggle with the cross-lingual understanding of Chinese internet buzzwords, particularly in fine-grained non-literal interpretation, robust equivalent matching under option perturbations, and calibrated harmfulness detection. These findings highlight the persistent challenges posed by culturally grounded language phenomena for multilingual LLMs and safety-oriented evaluation. The dataset and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [13] [Do Personality-Tuned LLMs Make Better Social Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21857)
*Tim Krabbe,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs are increasingly used in social simulations for socially interactive agents and robots, offering more flexibility than rule-based systems. However, even though they mimic human behaviour very well, there is a persistent alienness to them. This work investigates whether personality-aware fine-tuning can reduce this gap by improving the consistency and controllability of personality-conditioned dialogue generation compared with instruction prompting alone. We fine-tune two small open-weight LLMs, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Ministral-8B-Instruct, using a corpus that combines personality-labelled social media posts and dialogues to create a personality-based dialogue engine for social simulation. The resulting models are evaluated across multiple social interaction scenarios using three independent LLM judges, which assess personality fidelity and provide evidence-based behavioral interpretations. We additionally quantify inter-rater agreement and lexical characteristics of the generated dialogue. Results indicate that fine-tuned models are not better at role-playing different personalities than their respective baseline models. However, low inter-rater agreement limits the confidence with which these results can be interpreted. Concerning the quality of generated texts, fine-tuned models are mostly comparable to the baselines, with fine-tuning improving the linguistic diversity of the Qwen models. While the results appear generally usable and the baseline models offer the best overall performance, future studies should place greater emphasis on the quality and domain alignment of training data for accurate personality role-playing.

However, even though they mimic human behaviour very well, there is a persistent alienness to them. This work investigates whether personality-aware fine-tuning can reduce this gap by improving the consistency and controllability of personality-conditioned dialogue generation compared with instruction prompting alone. We fine-tune two small open-weight LLMs, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Ministral-8B-Instruct, using a corpus that combines personality-labelled social media posts and dialogues to create a personality-based dialogue engine for social simulation. The resulting models are evaluated across multiple social interaction scenarios using three independent LLM judges, which assess personality fidelity and provide evidence-based behavioral interpretations. We additionally quantify inter-rater agreement and lexical characteristics of the generated dialogue. Results indicate that fine-tuned models are not better at role-playing different personalities than their respective baseline models. However, low inter-rater agreement limits the confidence with which these results can be interpreted. Concerning the quality of generated texts, fine-tuned models are mostly comparable to the baselines, with fine-tuning improving the linguistic diversity of the Qwen models. While the results appear generally usable and the baseline models offer the best overall performance, future studies should place greater emphasis on the quality and domain alignment of training data for accurate personality role-playing.

### [14] [NemotronLabs VoiceChat: An Open Full-duplex Speech-to-Speech Model with Tool Calling Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21967)
*Jagadeesh Balam,Travis Bartley,Edresson Casanova,Sanjay Chauhan,Chen Chen,Zhehuai Chen,Zijia Chen,Francesco Ciannella,Slyne Deng,Mikyas Desta,Harishchandra Dubey,Slim Essid,Nourchene Ferchichi,Boris Ginsburg,Mariana Graterol Fuenmayor,Negar Habibi,Kevin Hu,Anand Joseph,Viraj Karandikar,Myungjong Kim,Viacheslav Klimkov,Seelan Lakshmi Narasimhan,Lily Lee,Jason Li,Eileen Long,Ameya Mahabaleshwarkar,Aditya Malte,Adi Margolin,Sasha Meister,Valentin Mendelev,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Ankita Pasad,Yifan Peng,Elena Rastorgueva,Jayda Ritchie,Jason Roche,Nikhil Srihari,Yuanhang Su,Yoshi Suhara,Viet Anh Trinh,Jinhan Wang,Piotr Zelasko,Hui Wang,Puhui Meng,Chaosen Zhang,Yunsheng Liu,Shawn Wang,Wenjing Li,Zhonglei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NemotronLabs VoiceChat 是 NVIDIA 发布的一个开源全双工语音到语音模型，将流式语音编码器、仅解码器语言模型（Nemotron-Nano-9B-v2）、专用并行输出流（智能体文本与结构化函数调用）、RNN-T 增量用户转写分支以及流式 TTS 解码器（VoiceChat-TTS）集成于统一架构中，可在同一时间轴上实现"边听边说"、转写、推理、调用工具并保持自然对话时序行为，是首个具备原生通用工具调用能力的完全开源全双工语音模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音助手多为半双工级联架构，缺乏人类对话的全双工动态特性（打断、抢话、犹豫、回应反馈等），且开源全双工模型普遍不支持通用工具调用。论文旨在证明：全双工交互、语音识别/生成、通用语言能力与外部工具调用可在单一开源模型中统一实现，且不牺牲实时对话自然性。

Method: 1) 架构：FastConformer 流式编码器（600M 参数，80ms 帧，70 帧左上下文，零右上下文）→ 模态适配器 → Nemotron-Nano-9B-v2 LLM（双头：智能体文本头 + 函数调用头，融合权重 1:1:2）→ 并行输出流；辅助 RNN-T 分支共享编码器预测用户转写；VoiceChat-TTS（778M Gemma3 + 199M 因果音频编解码器，22kHz/12.5Hz/31 层 RVQ + 高斯混合头 + 字符感知子词编码器）持续流式合成语音。2) 工具调用：专用自回归函数通道，状态机协议 <SOTC>/<EOTC>/<EOTR> + <TOOLCALL> JSON payload，支持多步与并行调用；推理时填充预定义确认语（filler message）掩盖工具执行延迟。3) 训练：两阶段——持续预训练（CPT，伪对话+语音合成）→ 监督微调（SFT，加权轮询采样）；SFT 损失 L = 0.5(L_text + L_FC) + 0.5·L_T2T，文本 token 加权（轮首 12.5、轮尾 7.5、内容 5.0、填充 1.0），函数通道大幅加权内容（64.0）抑制虚假调用；增强策略含早期打断（p=0.1）、反馈注入（p=0.05）、文本通道 160ms 延迟、DNS5/DEMAND 噪声、房间/麦克风冲激响应、编解码增强。4) 推理：CUDA Graph 感知编码 + 自定义 vLLM fork + Triton 推理服务器 + FastAPI WebSocket，端到端 p95 延迟 118ms/160ms 块（1.36× 实时）。

Result: 1) FDB 1.0 暂停接管率（合成 15.3%、CANDOR 25.5%）为开源系统最低；用户打断后 100% 接管，回复质量 4.33/5（开源最高）。2) FDB 1.5 用户反馈条件下 93% 恢复率（开源最高，超过 Freeze-Omni 80%），仅 1% 错误响应。3) VoiceBench 归一化均值 55.1，与 Freeze-Omni（55.2）持平，大幅优于 Moshi（29.5）与 PersonaPlex（30.6），知识类任务（OBQA 61.3、MMSU 46.1）显著优于 Freeze-Omni，AdvBench 安全 100% 拒答。4) FDB 3.0 工具选择 F1 达 82.5%，超越 Gemini Live 2.5（78.6%）与 3.1（81.7%）；但参数准确率 42.2% 与 Pass@1 33.0% 落后于 Gemini Live。6) VoiceChat-TTS 在 LibriTTS 上 WER 2.0%（首轮未见说话人）、SQuIM-MOS 4.380；ASR 在 OpenASR 排行榜 80ms/160ms 块平均 WER 9.02%/8.28%。

Conclusion: 论文证明：通过并行专用流（而非共享自回归通道）设计，开源全双工语音到语音模型可同时实现自然对话交互、知识/推理能力与结构化工具调用，在 FDB 1.0/1.5 与工具选择任务上达到甚至超越闭源商用系统水平。主要局限：上下文窗口约 2 分钟；工具调用参数准确率与端到端执行仍弱；执行中不支持打断；强噪声/混响鲁棒性不足；建议每会话工具数 ≤5。未来方向为扩展对话记忆、改进参数提取与多步工具组合、实现打断感知工具执行。

Abstract: We introduce NemotronLabs VoiceChat, an open full-duplex speech-to-speech model with native tool-calling capabilities. NemotronLabs VoiceChat combines a streaming speech encoder and decoder-only language model with parallel specialized output streams for agent text and structured function calls, an auxiliary RNN-T branch for incremental user transcription, and a streaming TTS decoder. This design enables the model to listen, transcribe, reason, invoke tools, and speak within a unified streaming architecture while preserving the temporal behavior required for natural conversation. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0, NemotronLabs VoiceChat achieves the lowest pause-handling takeover rates among evaluated open-weight systems, 100\% takeover following user interruptions, and a 4.33/5 post-interruption response-quality score. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5, it resumes its response after user backchannels in 93\% of cases. NemotronLabs VoiceChat obtains a 55.1 normalized average on VoiceBench and, on Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0 (FDB 3.0), achieves 82.5\% tool-selection F1, while argument accuracy and end-to-end tool execution remain areas for improvement. These results demonstrate that full-duplex interaction, speech recognition and generation, general language capabilities, and external tool use can be integrated in a single open speech-to-speech model without sacrificing real-time conversational behavior.

</details>


### [15] [DiaVLo: Diagnosing Behaviours of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22008)
*Lorenzo Corti,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiaVLo是一个用于诊断视觉语言模型（VLM）行为的框架，通过构建期望行为（SHOULD-KNOW，SK）和观察行为（REALLY-KNOW，RK）的规范，结合因果建模量化视觉概念对模型输出的影响，从而揭示模型的对齐、扩展和偏离行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM评估方法（基准测试、可解释性方法）依赖代理指标，难以映射到真实场景中人类使用VLM的情况，导致VLM行为不可预测、欠明确，阻碍了对其内部机制的理解和可靠部署。

Method: DiaVLo包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过场景图生成（SGG，使用IETrans）和人工标注构建SK规范；(2) 利用VLM的自我解释能力提取RK规范，并采用双机器学习（DML）估计器和概念级图像遮挡生成反事实样本，量化视觉概念的因果效应；(3) 通过语义相似度匹配（余弦相似度）对SK和RK进行对齐、扩展或偏离的分类。在四个开源VLM（InternVL2、LLaVa-1.6、Qwen2.5-VL、ShareGPT4V）和四个VQA数据集上进行分类和生成条件下的评估。

Result: 实验显示，DiaVLo分类的行为标签与模型性能存在相关性（互信息MI较高），能为标准评估指标提供上下文。识别的行为模式包括：(1) VLMs倾向于描述性而非组合性的表达；(2) 概念组织方式与人类不同（上下位词差异）；(3) 在概念优先级排序上存在差异。其中对齐行为仅占6.4%，扩展行为占52.5%，偏离行为占41.1%。因果效应分析表明，多选题数据集中大量零效应概念可能表明VLMs激活了与问题无关的概念。

Conclusion: DiaVLo提供了一套系统化的工具，用于诊断VLM行为并揭示其与人类期望之间的偏差，超越了传统基准测试的范畴。该框架通过形式化期望与观察行为的对比，有助于识别未明确说明或有害的行为模式，为VLM的系统级行为研究和缓解技术提供了基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on storing and transferring appropriate information across their sub-components. Verifying that the VLMs exhibit desired behaviours, while avoiding harmful ones, is central to their reliable deployment. Yet, methods that identify VLM behaviours remain scarce. We present DiaVLo, a diagnostic framework that leverages human curation and VLMs' generation capabilities to construct specifications of desired and observed VLM behaviours, surfacing potential misalignments. Beyond this, DiaVLo also provides causal estimates to identify the most influential concepts steering VLM behaviours. We evaluate DiaVLo on several open-source VLMs under both classification and generation conditions. Our experiments show that DiaVLo produces behaviour labels that correlate with model performance and provide context for measured performance. DiaVLo surfaced behaviours that are clearly aligned and misaligned, alongside patterns in how VLMs perceive, organise, and prioritise concepts.

</details>


### [16] [Towards Secure Cloud-Native Computing: Unveiling Kubernetes Misconfigurations with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20834)
*Mostafa Anouar Ghorab,Mohamed Aymen Saied*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的Kubernetes配置错误检测框架,包含统一分类法、四个数据集构建策略,以及多模型对比实验。结果显示LLaMA3在分段平衡数据集上表现近乎完美,显著优于六种主流基于规则的检测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes已成为云原生编排的事实标准,但其配置复杂性导致大量配置错误,引发安全漏洞和性能问题。现有检测工具(如Snyk、KubeScore、Datree等)依赖静态规则集,存在规则覆盖不全、难以检测新型错误、上下文感知能力弱等问题,且各工具分类标准不统一,缺乏统一的分类参考框架。GenKubeSec等LLM方法也受限于token长度,只能分析82%的配置文件。

Method: 1. **分类法构建(RQ1)**:从Stack Overflow收集2015-2024年4785篇Kubernetes相关帖子,经关键词过滤(17个专家验证关键词)得到681篇,由两位领域专家迭代分类,最终形成7类配置错误分类法(容器安全、资源管理、探针配置、权限与访问、镜像配置、API使用、网络与加密配置)。
2. **数据集构建(RQ2)**:从GitHub收集yaml/yml配置文件,用`kubectl apply --dry-run`验证语法,使用Snyk/KubeScore/Datree三工具联合标注(任一工具标记即为错误配置),构建四个粒度不同的数据集(D1:文件级13723条;D2:行级26984条;D3:排除可疑后继行32956条;D4:对象级276520条,采用删除/插入/修改三种变异操作缓解类别不平衡)。
3. **模型选择与微调**:对比BERT、CodeBERT、RoBERTa和LLaMA3,统一超参数(lr=1e-4, batch=8, weight_decay=0.01, 5个epoch),采用4-bit量化(nf4)+LoRA(r=160, α=8, dropout=0.05)提升效率。
4. **基准对比(RQ3)**:使用Helm官方仓库2097个配置文件作为独立测试集,与Snyk、KubeScore、Datree、Checkov、KubeLinter、Terrascan六种规则工具对比。

Result: 1. **分类法(RQ1)**:形成7类Kubernetes配置错误统一分类法,具备可扩展性。
2. **频率与风险(RQ2)**:Deployment错误配置最多(779例),其次是Pod(245)、NetworkPolicy(197);ClusterRole、Service、RBAC被识别为"非常高"风险对象。
3. **模型性能(RQ2)**:在D4数据集上所有模型均接近完美,LLaMA3表现最佳(P=99.84%, R=99.91%, F1=99.88%);D2/D3上F1约90%;D1上BERT表现较差(F1=20.53%)。错误分析显示LLaMA3对探针配置和资源管理类识别准确率高,但对RBAC、NetworkPolicy等语义复杂配置假阴性较高。
4. **工具对比(RQ3)**:最佳LLM模型检测出3209个错误配置,远超KubeScore(1082)、Snyk(1058)、Datree(611)、Checkov(409)、Terrascan(316)、KubeLinter(298);在Pod(713 vs Snyk 249)、Deployment(1436 vs KubeScore 543)等对象上优势显著。

Conclusion: LLM(尤其是LLaMA3)在结构化、平衡的数据集上能显著超越传统基于规则的检测工具,具备检测新颖、上下文相关配置错误的能力。但在RBAC、NetworkPolicy等语义微妙的对象上仍有局限。研究贡献包括:(1)首个可扩展的统一Kubernetes配置错误分类法;(2)四个互补的数据集构建策略(尤其是对象级分割与变异增强);(3)证明LLM通过学习多工具标注结果可获得更广的覆盖范围和更强的检测能力。未来工作将探索LLM驱动的自动修复、跨版本错误配置演化分析,以及标准化基准测试平台的开发。

Abstract: In the rapidly evolving landscape of cloud-native computing, Organizations are increasingly adopting infrastructure models that emphasize scalability, flexibility, and efficiency. Kubernetes has become the de facto standard for orchestrating containerized applications in these environments. However, the inherent complexity of cloud-native ecosystems introduces significant challenges, particularly in the form of misconfigurations that can compromise both security and performance. This study explores the potential of Large Language Models (LLMs) in identifying Kubernetes misconfigurations. We introduce a comprehensive taxonomy of common misconfiguration types, offering a structured framework to better understand and categorize these issues. Additionally, we conduct an empirical evaluation of state-of-the-art detection tools to benchmark their effectiveness. Furthermore, we analyze the Kubernetes objects most prone to misconfiguration and evaluate the severity of the identified issues. By leveraging advanced machine learning techniques, including LLMs, we provide novel insights into enhancing misconfiguration detection methodologies.

</details>


### [17] [RecreationWorld: Scalable and Verifiable Environments for Hybrid Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22000)
*Shuai Bai,Jiayong Deng,Yikun Fu,Chang Gao,Xuhao Hu,Mianqiu Huang,Yizhen Jiang,Yuheng Jing,Dehui Kong,Keliang Li,Ning Li,Wanli Li,Dayiheng Liu,Dunjie Lu,Changwei Luo,Que Shen,Zheyuan Wang,Zijian Wang,Jie Wu,Gao Wu,Zhihui Xie,Rui Xie,Haiyang Xu,An Yang,Jiakang Yuan,Yanming Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Xi Zhang,Zhenru Zhang,Zhuo Zhen,Mingkang Zhu,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecreationWorld提出"应用复现"任务范式与五平台评测基准RecreationBench，用于研究混合型计算机使用代理（hybrid CUAs），即能在GUI探索、代码实现与执行验证之间自主循环的长程智能体；其使用开源应用生成3.5万条训练轨迹，证明复现式监督可迁移至五个分布外基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI代理与代码代理各自发展但彼此割裂，无法支持真实数字工作中两者交替融合的需求；需要一个既能评测又能大规模生成训练数据的框架来推动混合CUAs研究。

Method: RecreationWorld：五平台（Ubuntu、macOS、Windows、Android、Web）可复现环境+统一代理支架（GUI控制+编码工具）+参考应用作为行为验证Oracle；用开源应用扩展为35,000条SFT轨迹；RecreationBench含250个任务，使用参考验证的程序化与视觉断言进行冻结式自动评分。

Result: RecreationBench上GPT-6 Astra以58.1%总体领先，但完整程序化测试通过率仅2.8%；代理在静态界面结构上的得分远高于交互与计算输出，重建产物规模与文件数均显著小于参考；两个模型初始化在5个OOD基准（编码+混合CUA）上均有最高17.9百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 复现式训练生成的混合代理经验可迁移到下游编码与计算机使用基准，但当前前沿代理仍远未达到参考级行为保真度；静态结构与动作条件行为之间存在一致差距，生成产物更小更集中。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have advanced along two separate lines: graphical interaction and software development through code and the command line. Real digital work requires both, interleaved rather than stacked end to end. We study hybrid CUAs that autonomously decide when to explore an interface, implement software, and run and visually verify their artifacts. We introduce RecreationWorld, a five-platform framework built around recreation: given a running reference, an agent must discover its behavior and build a faithful implementation with no prescribed workflow. RecreationWorld provides reproducible environments on Ubuntu, macOS, Windows, Android, and Web, plus a unified harness with native GUI control and coding tools. The running reference serves as an oracle for hidden behavioral tests, providing execution-grounded rewards. We scale trajectory generation with high-quality open-source applications. Models trained on these trajectories improve across five out-of-distribution coding and hybrid computer-use benchmarks and more frequently verify their rendered outputs, providing evidence of transfer beyond recreation. For held-out evaluation, we introduce RecreationBench, comprising 250 diverse tasks across domains and platforms. Reference-grounded programmatic and visual assertions cover action-conditioned outcomes at multiple interaction depths; each is validated on the reference and by human reviewers before the suite is frozen for automatic scoring. GPT-6 Astra leads at 58.1% overall, but passes all programmatic tests on just 2.8% of tasks. Agents reproduce static interface structure more reliably than interactions and computed outputs, while generated applications remain smaller and more monolithic than their references. We release the benchmark, environments, and test suites.

</details>


### [18] [HERMES: Contrast-Aware Knowledge Graph Reasoning from Clinical Notes for Patient Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20825)
*Gia-Bach Nguyen,Hoang-Ha Nguyen,Tuan-Cuong Vuong,Trang Mai Xuan,Duy Quoc Ngo,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Huan Vu,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERMES是一个基于图结构的临床预测框架，仅利用非结构化临床文本构建个性化知识图谱，结合对比逻辑建模和图注意力网络，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV的院内死亡率和30天再入院预测任务上优于纯文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测方法主要依赖结构化EHR数据，将临床笔记视为扁平序列，丢失了临床叙事中的显式关系和时序结构；基于token的语言模型无法捕捉症状-诊断、治疗-反应、临床矛盾等临床关键依赖。

Method: 1) 实体提取：结合spaCy医学NER与LLM引导提取（CoT推理+置信度），通过相似度合并生成实体集，并用SapBERT编码；2) 关系提取：LLM引导的关系提取，引入对比逻辑建模区分两类关系——动态关系（时序变化、治疗矛盾、治疗失败）和静态临床逻辑关系，并通过置信度过滤（τ_r=0.7）；3) 表示学习：两层GAT（8头注意力）+残差连接+LayerNorm，最大池化+两层MLP分类器，BCE损失训练。

Result: 在MIMIC-III院内死亡率预测上AUROC达85.92，AUPRC 55.49；MIMIC-IV院内死亡率AUROC 83.24，AUPRC 58.83；30天再入院预测在两数据集均达到最优（AUROC 77.75/77.31），相比最强非图基线LR+TF-IDF提升显著（+2.38 AUROC, +4.82 AUPRC）。消融实验证实GAT+最大池化优于GCN/RGCN及均值池化组合。零样本GPT-5.4 mini表现较差（72.36 AUROC），验证结构化关系建模不可由通用推理替代。

Conclusion: HERMES验证了显式关系建模与对比逻辑建模对临床结局预测的价值，纯文本框架结合个性化KG构建可有效提升预测性能；未来工作将扩展至多模态数据融合、提取流程优化及外部医院数据集验证，并探索KG内在质量评估。

Abstract: Clinical predictive models often rely on structured Electronic Health Record data, such as time-series and procedure codes. While recent approaches have begun leveraging unstructured clinical notes, they typically encode them as flat sequences, which may lose explicit relational and temporal structure present in clinical narratives. In response, we propose HERMES, a graph-based framework that operates exclusively on clinical text while preserving clinical relationships. This approach builds on two key ideas. First, personalized Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are constructed through Large-Language-Model-guided extraction from clinical notes with Contrastive Logic Modeling that explicitly captures temporal dynamics and treatment failures and changes in outcomes. Second, a Graph Attention Network synthesizes patient representations through graph-based learning over the KGs. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV for in-hospital mortality and 30-day readmission prediction show that HERMES consistently outperforms strong text-only baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicit relational modeling with Contrastive Logic Modeling significantly advances predictive performance.

</details>


### [19] [TALON: A Temporally Aware Longitudinal Framework for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20826)
*Nien-Tsyr Sun,Min-Chen Chen,Hui Nien Hung,Vincent S. Tseng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TALON 框架，一种用于放射学报告生成的时序感知纵向学习方法，通过双通道时序融合模块（DCTFM）自适应整合可变长度的患者历史，在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上取得了临床指标的 SOTA 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成模型多基于单次检查或最近一次历史检查，难以进行有意义的纵向比较和发现细微的间隔变化。虽然已有方法开始引入多次历史检查，但通常在融合前未明确建模每个历史检查的角色依赖相关性。放射学随访实践中，需要区分"稳定性证据"（持续存在）和"变化证据"（新出现），且同一历史检查对这两种证据的相关性可能截然不同。

Method: 方法核心是双通道时序融合模块（DCTFM）：1) 当前-Conditioned Spatial Alignment：通过 cross-attention 将每个历史检查与当前检查对齐；2) 相似性通道：评估历史检查支持持续结构的相关性；3) 变化通道：独立评估间隔变化证据；4) 通道门控：学习每个历史在稳定性与变化之间的平衡；5) 因子化时序流：通过共激活流和残差流分别整合稳定性与变化信息；6) 残差整合：使用零初始化增益，训练从当前图像路径开始。整体采用两阶段对比学习到生成范式，使用 RAD-DINO 视觉编码器、CXR-BERT 文本编码器和 DistilGPT2 解码器。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 数据集上，TALON 在 10 项指标中的 6 项取得第一，其余 4 项排名第二；在 CheXbert F1（0.525）和 RadGraph-F1（0.305）上取得最优，相对 PriorRG 在 F1 上提升 1.4%；当历史检查越多时优势越大（≥5 个历史时提升 2.8%）；消融实验证实相似性通道与变化通道具有互补性，移除任何组件都会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: TALON 通过对稳定性和间隔变化进行先验（prior-wise）、角色依赖的选择，证明了纵向放射学报告生成中"选择性融合优于全量融合"的设计理念有效。未来工作将扩展到多视图输入和更长的病史轨迹。

Abstract: Current radiology report generation (RRG) models usually produce descriptive reports based on a single examination or only the most recent prior examination, limiting their ability to perform accurate and meaningful longitudinal comparisons and detect subtle interval changes. Although recent approaches have begun to incorporate multiple prior examinations, they usually aggregate a fixed-length history without explicitly modeling the role-dependent relevance of each prior examination before fusion. To address this, we propose TALON, a Temporally Aware LONgitudinal RRG framework that adaptively integrates variable-length patient histories. The underlying Dual-Channel Temporal Fusion Module (DCTFM) compares the current examination with each prior examination through complementary similarity and change channels to capture persistent findings and interval changes, respectively. The specially designed channel-specific attention estimates the relevance of each prior examination, while a learned prior-specific gate adaptively integrates informative longitudinal evidence and suppresses redundancy. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR show that TALON outperforms the current state-of-the-art method on various clinical efficacy and graph-based metrics. When more prior examinations become available, TALON's performance on these metrics improves even further, emphasizing the strength of TALON's DCTFM in modeling longitudinal RRG across longer and more complex patient histories than existing approaches.

</details>


### [20] [Beyond WER: Entity and Disfluency Recall in Accented Conversational ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20828)
*Fiza Husain,Ankit Pandey,Yash Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对口音英语对话场景，提出三阶段流水线（启发式SQL数据筛选 → 区域LoRA适配 → 错误诊断分类法），基于Qwen2.5-Omni-3B实现单前向同时输出逐字与修正转写，在印度、印尼、拉美三种口音上将实体召回率从53-55%提升至80-85%、填充词召回率从<5%提升至76-86%、WER降至6-10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用ASR以聚合WER为优化目标，无法保留口音对话中的命名实体和填充停顿（如"uh""um"），而这两者对语言学习反馈至关重要。WER将时态错误和实体拼写错误同等惩罚，掩盖了下游教育应用中的关键失败。

Method: 1) 启发式SQL过滤器：利用连续大写词、缩写、称谓、句中大写四种词汇规则从BigQuery日志中筛选实体丰富语料，实体密度达随机采样的约2.8倍；2) 区域LoRA适配：在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B上以rank=32、α=32对注意力投影矩阵微调，每区域10k精选样本训练2 epoch，使用Gemini 2.5 Pro生成参考转写（750条人工核验），单次前向同时输出原始与修正转写JSON；3) 六类错误分类法（C1音素替换/C2实体错误/C3声学OOV/C4幻觉/C5遗漏/C6不可用音频），由Claude Sonnet 4.5作为LLM裁判（与人工标注83.8%一致率）。采用配对Bootstrap检验验证数据筛选贡献。

Result: 在印度、印尼、拉美三区域各~2k测试集上：WER降至5.95–10.00%，CER降至3.18–5.83%；实体召回率79.52–84.60%（基线53–55%）；填充词召回率76.47–87.94%（基线<5%）。EH vs RS消融实验显示数据筛选单独贡献2.76–4.19 pp实体召回提升（p<0.0001）。以3B参数量在实体召回上超越Whisper与商用ASR Universal-3-Pro，并匹配零样本30B Qwen3-Omni模型。vLLM推理P95延迟约800ms，单A100-40GB训练约6小时。

Conclusion: 在口音对话ASR中，"训练什么"与"怎么训练"同等重要——轻量级数据筛选是面向实体的ASR的实用首选方案，无需推理时引入LLM后处理。但存在三点局限：测试参考为银标而非全人工核验；填充词召回仍落后于30B模型；仅评估英语，尚未覆盖区域常见的代码转换场景。

Abstract: ASR systems optimised for Word Error Rate (WER) often miss named entities and filled pauses in accented conversational English, both critical for language-learning feedback. We present a three-stage pipeline for speakers from India, Indonesia, and Latin America: (1) heuristic SQL filters curating entity-rich training data at 2.8x the entity density of random sampling, (2) regional LoRA adapters fine-tuned on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B producing both verbatim and corrected transcripts in a single forward pass, and (3) a six-category error taxonomy validated by an LLM-based judge (83.8% agreement, 210 human-labelled samples). The pipeline achieves 80-85% entity recall (up from 53-55%), 76-86% filler recall (up from <5%), and 6-10% WER across 6k test utterances, outperforming Whisper and a commercial ASR on entity recall while matching a zero-shot 30B model with 10x fewer parameters. Paired bootstrap tests confirm that curation alone accounts for 2.8-4.2 pp of entity recall gain (p<0.0001).

</details>


### [21] [Recursive Language Models Generalize Out of Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20831)
*Chenxiao Yang,Zhiyuan Li,David McAllester,Nathan Srebro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在推理时限制可见信息（上下文隔离）是否有利于学习。作者比较了标准思维链（CoT）与递归语言模型（RLM）：在分布内，两者性能相近；但在分布外，CoT因可依赖子任务外的上下文走捷径而泛化失败，而递归通过强制隔离排除了此类失败模式。核心结论是：要超越分布准确性实现真正推理，仅覆盖正确规则是不够的，还需排除错误的"简单"规则，这与经典学习理论形成对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CoT允许语言模型在推理时访问完整上下文痕迹，这可能导致模型在训练时依赖子任务外的上下文信息形成"捷径"，在分布外测试时因这些令牌变化而失败。研究动机是探究：限制模型可见信息（递归隔离上下文）是否能避免此类捷径失败，从而实现真正泛化。

Method: 方法上采用理论对比分析：定义两类学习者——通用型CoT（可读完整推理痕迹）和递归语言模型RLM（在隔离上下文中解决每个子任务）。在PAC/统计学习理论框架下分析：(1) IID设定下两者的样本复杂度差异仅为常数因子；(2) OOD设定下利用简单性偏置（simplicity bias）分析CoT为何倾向拟合捷径而非真实规则，而递归隔离如何排除子任务外依赖。

Result: 主要结果：(1) IID泛化：CoT可高效模拟递归规则，两者的泛化保证仅差常数因子，递归几乎无优势。(2) OOD泛化：CoT的假设类仍包含递归规则，但简单性偏置会使其倾向于选择依赖外部上下文的"捷径"而非真实规则，导致分布外失败；递归通过上下文隔离从结构上排除此类捷径。(3) 理论贡献：即使假设类覆盖了真实规则，覆盖正确性也不足以保证分布外泛化。

Conclusion: 结论：要使语言模型真正推理（而非仅在分布内准确），仅在假设类中覆盖正确的计算规则是不够的——还必须避免模型选择更简单但错误的捷径规则。这与经典学习理论（PAC可学习性仅取决于假设类对真实规则的覆盖）形成鲜明反差，凸显了分布外鲁棒推理需要额外的归纳偏置（如递归上下文隔离）来抑制简单性偏置带来的捷径学习。

Abstract: We study when limiting what a language model can see improves learning. We compare standard CoT, the more general learner that reads the full trace, with recursive language models, which restricts itself by solving each subtask in an isolated context. In-distribution, this generality comes for free: CoT can efficiently simulate the recursive rule, so the IID generalization guarantee changes only by a constant factor, and recursion does not offer much. But out of domain, CoT can fit training by relying on context outside the current subtask, i.e. a shortcut that breaks once those tokens change; recursive context isolation rules out this failure mode. Even though CoT's class still covers the recursive rule, simplicity bias picks the shortcut over the truth. Thus, to go beyond distributional accuracy and truly reason, covering the right rule is not enough; this contrasts with classical learning theory.

</details>


### [22] [PhysioBench: A Unified Benchmark for Physiological Signal Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20836)
*Mengxuan Li,Junfa Chen,Jinze Xia,Yundan Chen,Lixin Fan,Ke Liu,Keyue Shi,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysioBench是一个统一的生理信号问答基准，将22个公开数据集的异构注释统一为6140万个跨30个任务的问题-答案对，覆盖ECG、PPG、EEG和PSG四种模态，评估了21个代表性模型（包括LLM、VLM、TSLM和PSFM）在三种评估设置下的表现，结果显示没有模型能在所有模态和任务上取得稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生理信号基础模型（PSFM）通常需要针对每个下游任务进行特定适配，缺乏统一的语言交互接口；尽管自然语言为多任务预测提供了共同接口，但当前模型在生理信号模态上遵循此类指令的能力尚未得到充分评估。

Method: 1) 从22个公开数据集统一注释为61,418,821个问题-答案对，覆盖ECG（16个任务）、PPG（5个任务）、EEG（2个任务）、PSG（7个任务）；2) 设计三种互补评估设置：零样本泛化（评估LLM/VLM/TSLM）、冻结编码器迁移（评估PSFM）、语言条件化（使用共享信号-文本适配器）；3) 评估21个代表性模型，分析其跨模态和跨任务的迁移性与鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 零样本设置下，没有模型在所有模态和任务上均优于随机基线，Lingshu-7B整体表现相对较好但跨模态不一致；2) 冻结编码器迁移中，所有PSFM均优于随机基线（如OSF在PSG的macro-F1达0.631，Sigma-PPG在PPG达0.754），但不同模型在不同任务上的优势各异；3) 语言条件化表明问题文本提供有用任务信息，但性能对问题表述敏感，EEG模态对语言条件依赖较弱；4) 多模板训练可改善ECG和PSG的未见模板性能，但PPG和EEG效果不一致。

Conclusion: PhysioBench揭示了生理信号问答领域面临的三大挑战：异构信号表示、数据与任务分布不平衡、鲁棒的信号-文本对齐。现有模型无法在所有模态和任务上实现稳健性能，语言条件化支持跨任务共享预测，但高度依赖问题表述。PhysioBench作为可扩展平台，将通过增加专家撰写问题、更多模态和任务来推动通才型生理信号模型的发展。

Abstract: Physiological signals support diverse clinical and monitoring tasks, yet existing physiological signal foundation models typically require task-specific adaptation for each task. Natural language provides a common interface for specifying different prediction objectives, but the ability of current models to follow such instructions across physiological signal modalities remains insufficiently evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce PhysioBench, a unified benchmark for physiological signal question answering. PhysioBench harmonizes annotations from 22 public datasets into 61.4 million questions across 30 tasks. Each question-answer pair is grounded in a signal segment and traceable to its source annotation. We evaluate 21 representative models, including large language models, vision-language models, time-series language models, and physiological signal foundation models under three complementary settings. The results show that none of the evaluated models achieves consistently strong performance across physiological signal modalities and tasks. The incorporation of natural language supports unified prediction across tasks, although performance remains sensitive to question formulation. Beyond these findings, PhysioBench offers an extensible platform for fine-grained analysis and future research on physiological signal understanding. Our codes are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [VISPATH: Visual-Intent-Guided Path Reasoning for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20843)
*Jinke Wu,Zhengpin Li,Mengzhe Jia,Yang Li,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 VISPATH 框架解决多模态知识图谱问答（MM-KGQA）中现有方法仅在初始实体定位时使用视觉信息、中间跳退化为纯文本图搜索的问题。通过在每跳重计算多模态意图、推理链剪枝和证据充分性检查，使视觉证据参与整个多跳推理过程。同时构建 VISPATH-Bench 基准（2000 个 2-4 跳问题），以 GPT-4o 为骨干的 VISPATH 在多个基准上超越强基线，在 VISPATH-Bench 上平均准确率相对提升 10.6%，2 跳推理提升 13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MM-KGQA 方法存在核心局限：多模态信息仅用于起始实体定位或证据检索，随后的多跳推理退化为纯文本图搜索，无法利用在中间跳变得重要的视觉线索。这导致初始定位的误差会逐跳累积，且问题文本未必明确指定每跳所需关系。

Method: VISPATH 包含四个协调阶段：(1) 视觉与图结构感知的起始实体识别——用多模态识别对象，结合图结构出关系兼容性验证修正视觉链接；(2) 意图引导的推理路径发现——在每跳由 VLM 根据图像、问题和当前部分路径重新计算跳特定多模态意图 q_Q^(i)，选取 top-M₁ 边扩展；(3) 推理链剪枝——用 LLM 提取推理草图与预测深度 D̂，对候选路径计算与问题、草图、跳意图的链级一致性得分 σ_{p,i}，再用 LLM 检验整体链；(4) 视觉接地的问答——VLM 总结所选路径、检查证据充分性 η_i，按停跳规则 i_stop=min{i|η_i=1 ∧ i≥D̂} 决定是否提前停止，否则进行终极答案选择。默认超参 M₁=30、M₂=30、M₃=3、D_max=3、δ=0.05。

Result: 在 VISPATH-Bench（2000 题，500/1200/300 分布 2/3/4 跳，52 个关系）以及 ReasonVQA、MMhops、E-VQA 四个基准上，以 GPT-4o 为文本 LLM、Qwen2.5-VL-72B 为 VLM、all-MiniLM-L6-v2 为句编码器、SBERT@0.3 为指标。结果：VISPATH 在 VISPATH-Bench 上平均 64.5%（2 跳 74.0、3 跳 62.4、4 跳 57.0），超过最强基线 GPT-5.4（58.3）相对 10.6%；2 跳相对提升 13.1%、3 跳 9.9%、4 跳 8.2%。在 ReasonVQA、MMhops 上平均 68.0%、84.3%，亦达最优。起始实体定位在 ReasonVQA 上从 24.1% 提升到 57.2%（GLM-4.6V 骨干）。消融显示图感知验证最关键（移除后 -8.3 点）、视觉意图与推理链剪枝各贡献 5.5/6.0 点。成本：GPT-4o-mini 下 5.8 次 LLM 与 5.2 次 VLM 调用、3.6k token、27.3 秒/题，对多模态推理而言可控。

Conclusion: VISPATH 通过将视觉证据持续嵌入到逐跳路径推理中，使 MM-KGQA 不再退化为纯文本图搜索，并在多跳深度上保持稳定优势。VISPATH-Bench 填补了现有基准对二跳以上多模态多跳推理评估的空白。未来可向时序 MMKGQA 扩展，加入时间感知的多模态意图。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) enables models to answer natural-language questions through structured graph reasoning and has achieved substantial progress across many benchmarks and applications. Recently, multimodal KGQA (MM-KGQA) has attracted increasing attention because many questions require jointly using multimodal inputs and KG evidence. However, existing MM-KGQA methods typically use multimodal information only for starting entity grounding or evidence retrieval, after which multi-hop reasoning degenerates into text-only graph search. As a result, they cannot exploit multimodal cues that become important at intermediate hops. To address this limitation, we propose VISPATH, a visual-intent-guided path reasoning framework for MM-KGQA. VISPATH first identifies a reliable starting entity by combining multimodal grounding with graph-structural cues. It then performs intent-guided path discovery by recomputing hop-specific multimodal intent from the input, question, and current partial paths, so that each expansion is guided by the current reasoning state. The discovered paths are further refined through reasoning-chain pruning, which evaluates candidate paths as complete evidence chains based on their consistency with the question, reasoning sketch, and hop-specific intent. Finally, VISPATH checks whether the selected evidence is sufficient for answer generation. We further construct VISPATH-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating multimodal multi-hop reasoning over KGs, covering questions that require two to four hops over KG paths. Extensive experiments on VISPATH-Bench and three additional multimodal QA benchmarks show that VISPATH consistently outperforms strong baselines. Notably, with GPT-4o as the backbone, VISPATH surpasses GPT-5.4 on VISPATH-Bench, achieving a 10.6% relative improvement in average accuracy and a 13.1% improvement at 2-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [24] [Reading Less While Writing: A Closed-Form Bandwidth Dial for Streaming Multimodal Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20845)
*Yasir Mehmood,Kashif Javed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ZENDAYA，一种基于单一连续参数γ的跨注意力调度机制，将离线解码器与流式解码器统一为一个模型族。γ控制可见源前缀作为生成进度的闭式函数，按输入预测长度缩放；平均源消耗服从Ē(γ)≈1/(1+γ)闭式关系。论文证明结构依赖定理：对于任何预先固定的非递减调度，发出的token不能依赖尚未到达的输入。在Charades-STA、ActivityNet Captions和LibriHeavy三个数据集上，仅29M参数的从零训练解码器匹配或超过固定wait-k策略，在最低延迟时增益最显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列到序列解码器在生成第一个词前需看完整个输入，这在线性流式场景中不可行。现有的wait-k等流式方法使用固定偏移量，对所有输入和所有生成步骤施加相同的等待token数，与输入长度和节奏脱节。同时，过多源输入会稀释注意力——尤其在生成最早期、模型自身输出锚点最少的时刻。因此需要一种能够根据输入长度与生成进度动态调整读取量的机制。

Method: ZENDAYA的核心机制是将标准因果解码器中跨注意力的可见窗口替换为Ω_i = ⌈F·(i/N̂)^γ⌉，其中γ为单一连续参数、i为生成步骤、F为源token总数、N̂为预测的有效目标长度。具体设计包括：(1) 闭式调度：γ→0对应全曝光离线解码器；γ=1对应线性实时；γ>1对应饥饿模式。平均源曝光服从Ē(γ)≈1/(1+γ)的Riemann和结果。(2) 结构依赖定理：对于任何预先固定的非递减调度i↦Ω_i，步骤i的logit仅依赖文本前缀y_{0:i}和源前缀X_{<Ω_i}，证明基于层间归纳，利用单调性闭包自注意力归纳步骤、跨注意力掩码使softmax权重在j≥Ω_i处为零。(3) 辅助长度头：以λ_len=0.1权重训练离散化长度预测，仅推理时使用。(4) ZENDAYA-inf：滑窗扩展至无界流，通过前缀强制（损失掩码屏蔽历史句）和基于缓冲B的每窗口因果长度预测实现；Corollary 1扩展依赖保证至任意非递减到达过程，定义有效视界Ω^eff_{k,j}=min(Ω_{k,j}, A_k(t_j))并证明其非递减性。

Result: 在三种公开语料上实验：Charades-STA（视频，CLIP B/32编码，源密集型ρ=2.75）、ActivityNet Captions（视频，C3D编码，源密集型ρ=3.38）、LibriHeavy（音频，Whisper-base编码，文本密集型ρ=0.61）。模型为29M参数从零训练解码器，4层，d=768，8头，T4 GPU训练。离线结果：Charades-STA BLEU-4 0.0402（γ=0.30）vs基线0.0336，相对+19.6%；ActivityNet BLEU-4 0.0210（γ=0.10）vs基线0.0143，相对+46.9%；LibriHeavy BLEU-4 0.2143（γ=0.30）vs基线0.2125，相对+0.8%。流式结果：Charades-STA在γ=0.2时比最强wait-k在BLEU-4和METEOR上均更优，且早5.7个源token到达；ActivityNet在γ=1.5比wait-5在负延迟下BLEU-4 +28%、METEOR +12.6%；LibriHeavy在γ=1.0延迟1.7个源token时保留64%的离线BLEU-4最优值，比最近固定偏移高45%。配对bootstrap 95%区间在三个数据集的流式METEOR增益上均显著。三种子复现（63次训练运行）确认了几何预测但修正了ActivityNet的最佳γ值。

Conclusion: 单一连续参数γ可同时充当延迟设置、可解释的暴露预算和结构依赖保证。一个机制、一个定理、一个训练配方在ρ=0.61和ρ=3.38两侧的语料上均有效。最反直觉的发现是：看得少反而写得更准——因为在生成最早期，源信息洪流会稀释模型注意力，而该阶段模型自身输出锚点最少，因此限制曝光反而提升质量。未来工作可学习每窗口γ（保持定理保证），限制包括流式结果仅为单次运行、未与学习的自适应策略直接对比、未测量完全异步到达场景。

Abstract: A decoder that turns video or audio into text conventionally consumes the entire input before emitting a word. Offline this is merely more than the task requires; live it is impossible, since a caption cannot wait for a match to end. Streaming systems bolt on a fixed rule such as wait-$k$, which waits for the same number of input tokens before every word, regardless of the input's length or pace. We replace the fixed offset with ZENDAYA, a schedule governed by a single continuous parameter $\gamma$. It makes the visible source prefix a closed-form function of generation progress, scaled by the input's own predicted length, so an ordinary offline decoder and a real-time streaming decoder become two endpoints of one family rather than separate models. The same scalar fixes, in closed form, the mean fraction of source consumed per emitted word, $\bar{E}(\gamma) \approx 1/(1+\gamma)$, making it at once a latency dial and an interpretable budget. We prove a structural dependency theorem: under any schedule fixed in advance and non-decreasing, no emitted token can depend on input that has not yet arrived. The guarantee holds for trained and untrained weights alike, and extends to unbounded streams under arbitrary asynchronous arrival. The empirical result is counterintuitive: seeing less can produce better text, because a flood of source dilutes attention exactly when the model has the least of its own output to anchor on. Trained from scratch across two modalities and three public corpora (Charades-STA, ActivityNet Captions, LibriHeavy), a compact 29M-parameter decoder matches or beats the fixed schedule while reading less of the source, with the sharpest gains at the lowest latencies, where a fixed offset collapses. Streaming METEOR gains are statistically significant on all three corpora.

</details>


### [25] [Rewarding Efficient Reasoning Improves Abstention on Underspecified Tasks in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20846)
*Polina Tsvilodub,Max Höth,Michael Franke,Björn Deiseroth,Carina Kauf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大推理模型（LRMs）在面对信息不足的任务时无法有效"识别应放弃回答"的问题，结合人类认知实验，提出了一种基于GRPO的新型奖励函数SURE（Sufficiency-aware Reasoning Efficiency），通过惩罚在识别到关键信息缺失后继续产生的冗余推理链，使模型在保持回答准确性的同时显著提升放弃回答能力和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大推理模型（LRMs）在数学推理等任务上表现出色，但在识别"何时不应回答"（abstention）这一关键能力上存在明显缺陷。研究者发现LRMs在不可回答的任务上浪费更长的CoT，这与人类学会方式形成鲜明差距，凸显了将"资源理性"人类认知视角引入模型优化的必要性。

Method: 方法包含三个核心部分：（1）开展人类实验（N=126），通过贝叶斯混合效应回归模型对比人类在可回答与不可回答任务上的反应时与准确率；（2）评估六个模型家族的推理模型与指令微调模型在QuestBench和AbstentionBench上的放弃回答表现与CoT长度；（3）提出SURE奖励函数，结合结果奖励（准确率）与过程奖励（CoT中识别信息缺失前的内容占比），采用GRPO对4B参数LRMs（Qwen3-4B-Thinking、Phi-4-mini-reasoning、Nemotron-3-nano）进行LoRA微调。

Result: 实验结果显示：（1）人类在不可回答任务上的反应时被可回答任务所界定上限，而LRMs则相反，不可回答任务产生更长CoT；（2）SURE微调使放弃回答性能平均提升12.8%，CoT缩短44%，且保持回答能力不变（-0.012），优于仅使用准确率+长度惩罚基线（-0.136）；（3）SURE保留了CoT长度的自然分布与灵活性，在"高重要性"提示下能产生更长CoT。

Conclusion: 本文证明推理后训练是导致LRMs放弃回答能力退化和CoT冗余的根本原因，而通过奖励机制鼓励在识别信息缺失后立即停止推理，可同时改善模型的放弃回答准确性、推理效率和CoT灵活性。SURE方法相比朴素的长度惩罚基线具有显著优势，为构建既帮助性强又能识别应放弃回答的LRMs提供了有效路径。

Abstract: While modern large reasoning models (LRMs) excel at providing correct answers in many tasks, we provide additional evidence for the observation that they often struggle with a critical capability: knowing when to abstain from answering. We analyze this gap by comparing LRM behavior to results from a human study, revealing that human reasoning effort on unanswerable tasks is upper-bounded by answerable tasks, whereas LRMs waste computational resources by generating longer Chains of Thought (CoTs) on unanswerable than on answerable prompts. To overcome this inefficiency, we take inspiration from a resource-rational perspective on human cognition and introduce a novel GRPO reward that encourages efficient reasoning about whether the task contains all the information needed to solve it. Fine-tuning several 4B LRMs with this reward leads to human-like abstention performance gains (+12.8% on average) while retaining answering capabilities and boosting the models' efficiency (44% shorter CoTs on average).

</details>


### [26] [How Many Humans Is a Judge Panel Worth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21277)
*Chao Li,Yingying Yu,Yunfeng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文审计了由语言模型组成的评判小组相对于人类标注分布的"等效人数"，发现谱多样性与分布恢复是不同目标，不能互换使用。通过对三个ChaosNLI任务的32个模型进行分析，论文揭示了不同度量指标可能产生显著差异的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越来越多地被用于评估和数据标注，理解一个模型评判小组实际代表多少人类判断变得至关重要。论文指出仅凭评判者数量无法说明其判断贡献，并强调有效计数需要明确的目标匹配。

Method: 论文使用32个模型的固定池（来自10个提供商家族），在三个ChaosNLI任务上进行审计。方法包括：(1) 通过参与率（PR）的归一化残差Gram矩阵匹配人类参考独立抽样，得到ν_H；(2) 匹配分布平方误差得到ν_MSE；(3) 推导谱恒等式分解误差与成员能量和平均方向的关系；(4) 计算共识方向方差占比γ_co。

Result: 32个评判者在三个任务上得到ν_H=4.24–6.50，而ν_MSE=2.30–3.75，两者的比值约为1.72–1.89。在MNLI-m上谱与分布恢复排名高度一致（0.960–0.977），但在αNLI上一致性很低（0.223–0.419）。一个可实现的反例显示PR增加14.3%时误差反而增加25%。MNLI-m上的共识方向方差占比γ_co=43.8%，SNLI上为33.7%。

Conclusion: 评判小组的有效规模是目标特定的度量。谱多样性和分布恢复不应被视为可互换的面板质量度量或通用的人类替代率。论文提供了对齐投票和分析协议以审计这些区别。

Abstract: How many human judgments does a panel of language models represent? The answer depends on what is matched. We audit categorical judge panels against empirical human label distributions, retaining disagreement that binary errors relative to one gold label collapse. We measure spectral residual diversity by matching the participation ratio of a normalized residual Gram matrix to conditionally independent human-reference draws, giving nu_H. We separately match distributional squared error, giving nu_MSE. Across three ChaosNLI tasks, the same 32-judge panels have nu_H=4.24--6.50 but nu_MSE=2.30--3.75. A spectral identity separates the eigenvalues, member energies, and averaging-direction weights that determine error. Realizable hard-label panels show that greater spectral diversity can accompany worse distribution recovery even with equal member energies and nonnegative correlations. In the observed panels, within-size ranking agreement varies sharply by task; some member additions produce conflicting changes that persist across two item halves. The consensus-direction share of centered residual variance is gamma_co=43.8% on MNLI-m and 33.7% on SNLI, quantifying shared variation retained by averaging. We provide aligned votes and analysis protocols for auditing these distinctions. Effective size is therefore a target-specific measurement: spectral diversity and distribution recovery should not be treated as interchangeable measures of panel quality or as general human-replacement rates.

</details>


### [27] [Prediction Dynamics in Depth-Recurrent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21383)
*Xinyue Luo,Fei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文从几何与组成两个角度，分析深度循环语言模型（如 Huginn-3.5B 和 Ouro-1.4B）中预测分数更新与答案稳定性之间的关系，回答"为什么中间层的答案可以与最终层一致，但其分数仍在变化"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度循环语言模型通过重复潜在更新来精炼预测，但人们尚不清楚中间答案与最终答案一致时分数变化的机理。该论文旨在用精确的数学分解解释有限深度下的答案保留现象。

Method: 论文推导出关于终点边缘（margin）的精确分解定理，将基于幅度的边缘界的保守性分解为三个非负部分：(1) 共同平移 A_tr，(2) 相对于胜出者的方向 A_dir，(3) 每个竞争者的更新与其分数差的配对 A_pair，并建立四层嵌套的最早合格深度准则 D_raw ≥ D_q ≥ D_dir ≥ D_res。对于共享预测分布，将共同与对比（contrast）成分正交分解，并进一步用候选集质量 M_t 和集合内集中度 κ_t 表达共同成分。

Result: 在 Huginn-3.5B 和 Ouro-1.4B 上，考虑方向和竞争者配对后，在完整答案文本评分下，将最早合格深度的平均值在平移去除基础上再降低 22.5–34.4% 的总深度。在标签评分下亦有显著贡献。共同与对比能量衰减速率不同，允许偏好变化的相对份额随绝对更新缩小而增长。在 Huginn 上，实际更新对胜出者的保持率低于精确的坐标置换参考。配对 LSE 中心化的残差依赖于分数尺度。

Conclusion: 答案在有限深度下的保留可以通过观察到的分数变化的几何结构（方向与竞争者配对）和组成（共同平移与对比运动的正交分解，含集合质量与集中度）来解释。回顾性几何增益并不能直接等同于预测性收益：在前缀预测设置中，商排序优于原始排序但落后于均值中心化。因此，几何优势需要单独的预测评估才能用于设计停止策略。

Abstract: Depth-recurrent language models refine predictions through repeated latent updates. Why can intermediate answers agree with the endpoint while their scores continue to change? We derive a sharp margin characterization that decomposes the conservatism of a magnitude bound into common translation, direction relative to the winner, and the pairing of each competitor's update with its score gap. Across Huginn-3.5B and Ouro-1.4B, accounting for update direction and competitor pairing reduces the mean earliest qualifying depth by a further 22.5-34.4% of the total depth beyond translation removal under full answer-text scoring. This retrospective comparison uses completed trajectories. Substantial contributions also occur under label scoring. For shared predictive distributions, we separate common and contrast motion orthogonally and express the common component through candidate-set mass and within-set concentration. Common and contrast energies can attenuate at different rates, allowing a growing preference-change share to coexist with shrinking absolute updates. These findings explain finite-depth answer preservation through the geometry and composition of observed score changes.

</details>


### [28] [Analysing the Linearity of Linguistic Relations in Language Model Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21655)
*Vasudevan Nedumpozhimana,Fathima Thekkekara,John Kelleher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分析语言关系在语言模型嵌入空间中线性编码程度的框架，通过约束线性近似在扩展BATS数据集上对GloVe、RoBERTa和ModernBERT三种模型进行了实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的探针方法通常仅测试特定信息是否存在于嵌入中，而无法揭示信息是如何编码的（线性还是非线性）。受线性表征假说的启发，作者希望开发一个能够分析语言关系在嵌入空间中线性编码程度的框架，以理解哪些关系结构最容易被模型线性访问，从而解释模型行为并支持安全干预。

Method: 方法核心是将线性编码形式化定义为存在两个线性算子将相关词对的嵌入映射到零向量，而非相关对映射到非零向量。由于精确的线性编码条件难以满足，提出了一种线性近似方法：通过最小化相关对的投影范数，同时约束非相关对的投影范数≥1，将优化问题通过SVD分解转化为线性规划问题求解。扩展了BATS数据集（每种关系增加至100对相关词），在GloVe、RoBERTa和ModernBERT三种模型上对四类语言关系（屈折、派生、词典、百科）进行实验。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 屈折和派生关系在所有三种模型中均呈现近乎完美的线性编码（近似误差为0）；(2) 词典和百科关系的线性编码误差显著更高，尤其是一对多和多对多映射；(3) RoBERTa和ModernBERT通常比GloVe编码关系更具线性；(4) 一对一关系比一对多关系更容易线性编码；(5) 百科关系中，确定性、专有性强的关联（如特定国家-语言）比非确定性关联（如香蕉的颜色）更易线性编码。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效揭示语言模型嵌入空间中不同关系结构的线性可访问性，可作为比较不同架构关系几何特征的紧凑工具。形态学关系（屈折和派生）几乎完美线性编码，而语义关系（尤其是一对多和多对多）线性编码程度较低，表明更复杂的关系结构需要非线性的表示方式。现代神经语言模型比传统词向量模型在线性编码关系方面表现更优。

Abstract: We propose a framework to analyse how strongly different linguistic relations are linearly encoded in language model embedding spaces. We formalise linear encoding via a constrained linear approximation over related and unrelated word pairs and apply this to an extended BATS dataset covering inflectional, derivational, lexicographic, and encyclopedic relations in GloVe, RoBERTa, and ModernBERT. Our experiments show near-perfect linear encodings for inflectional and derivational relations, but substantially higher errors for lexicographic and encyclopedic relations, especially for one-to-many and many-to-many associations. We also find that RoBERTa and ModernBERT generally encode relations more linearly than GloVe. These results indicate that our framework can reveal which relational structures are most linearly accessible in embeddings, offering a compact tool for probing and comparing relational geometry across models.

</details>


### [29] [RheoSampling: Resolving the One-Hot Dilemma in Stochastic Dynamic-Tree Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21827)
*Qiao Hu,Yepeng Weng,Bo Zhang,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RheoSampling 提出了一种双身份解耦机制，将动态树投机解码中的拓扑构建与随机采样分离：通过为采样 token 分配用于树扩展/剪枝的代理概率和用于验证的真实采样概率，首次实现了兼具上下文感知动态树结构与随机采样的无损投机解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态树投机解码（如 EAGLE-3）在贪心解码下表现优异，但在随机解码（T>0）时，确定性的 top-K 扩展和全局剪枝机制会使提案分布退化为 one-hot，导致接受率严重下降。这形成了一个两难困境：动态树方法为保留上下文相关拓扑而牺牲随机采样，静态树方法则为保留随机采样而使用上下文无关结构。

Method: 1. 双身份解耦（Dual-Identity Decoupling）：在 top-K 候选池中，将前 m 个 token 作为确定性 lead token，再从剩余分布中采样一个 token Y 注入池中。Y 在树构建阶段使用代理概率 q_proxy = min{q(x_m), z}（z 为残差质量），在验证阶段使用真实采样概率 q_tilde。2. 等价类分析（Equivalence-Class Analysis）：通过将所有剪枝到相同 R 节点树的未剪枝树归为等价类，证明无损性。3. RheoVerification（基于最优传输的验证策略）：先独立验证采样 token，拒绝后在残差分布上对剩余候选进行序贯拒绝。4. 稀疏草稿分布：将 logits 截断到 top-128 以降低计算开销。5. 流变参数 m 控制代理概率大小，平衡采样 token 的生存概率与树结构质量。
