Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Vicuna-13B-v1.3 和 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 上，于 Alpaca、GSM8K、HumanEval、MT-bench、Natural Questions 和 CNN/DailyMail 六个基准测试中，RheoSampling 在平均接受长度（τ）上获得 +0.14 至 +0.22 的提升（相对增益约 2.4%–4.4%），同时带来 3.2%–1.8% 的实际加速（2.84×→2.93× 等）。在 m=1 时取得最佳或近最佳性能。验证策略消融实验中，OT-based RheoVerification 在受控设置下达到 85.4% 接受率，优于 RRSw（83.5%）和 vanilla Top-K（80.0%）。稀疏支持大小为 128 时覆盖 91% 分布质量，开销可忽略。

Conclusion: RheoSampling 成功解决了动态树投机解码中确定性扩展与随机生成之间的根本冲突，是首个同时实现上下文感知动态拓扑与随机采样且保持严格无损性的方法。其双身份概率框架、等价类无损性证明、OT 验证策略和稀疏草稿机制的组合，在理论上和实验上均验证有效，为将先进验证算法适配到复杂树结构提供了基础模板。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens in parallel, with tree-based methods further improving efficiency through hierarchical structures. Dynamic-tree methods such as EAGLE-3 perform well under greedy decoding via deterministic top-K expansion and global pruning. However, in stochastic decoding (T>0), this mechanism collapses the draft distribution into one-hot probabilities, causing a severe drop in acceptance rate. This creates a dilemma: dynamic-tree methods sacrifice stochastic sampling to preserve context-aware topology, while static-tree methods preserve stochastic sampling with context-agnostic structures. The issue arises because the same probability distribution is used for two conflicting tasks: constructing the tree and verifying tokens. This coupling makes direct injection of randomness challenging due to the resulting stochastic process. We resolve this by decoupling these roles: RheoSampling assigns a token sampled from the draft distribution a proxy probability for tree expansion and pruning alongside its true sampling probability for verification. Specifically, we inject a sampled token among the deterministic top-K slots and treat it with different probabilities during construction and verification, making RheoSampling the first dynamic-tree method with both context-aware top-K construction and stochastic sampling while maintaining losslessness. We establish the lossless guarantee through an equivalence-class analysis that compresses the stochastic tree space into tractable classes. An OT-based verification strategy and a sparse draft mechanism ensure that theoretical gains translate into practical efficiency. Experiments across LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate improvements in acceptance rate and speedup over state-of-the-art dynamic tree methods. This framework may provide a template for analyzing stochastic tree structures.

</details>


### [30] [Do small language models know what they don't know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20824)
*Prashant Mudgal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于熵的置信度信号能否帮助参数量小于3B的小语言模型(SLMs)识别自身知识边界。通过在7个模型对、5个NLU基准上评估7种方法，发现token级熵在SLMs中几乎失效（91%的配置下熵近零），而语义熵（通过多采样聚类答案计算分布不确定性）可恢复有效的置信信号，据此进行选择性路由至更大专家模型，精度提升最高达+50个百分点。跨家族路由（如SmolLM 360M→Phi-3.5-mini）平均+22.0%，显著优于同家族路由的+6.8%，表明专家模型质量比架构兼容性更重要。核心启示是：熵基方法在SLM中的价值不在于节省算力，而在于"智能算力分配"——在最需要的地方花费更多token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token级Shannon熵的置信度估计方法主要在大型推理模型(LLM)上验证有效，但小语言模型(SLMs，参数量<3B)正成为消费级硬件部署的主流选择，这些方法能否迁移到小模型上尚不清楚。小模型倾向于生成极短、高置信度的输出，可能导致token级熵失去判别能力，因此需要系统性探索适用于SLMs的不确定性信号。

Method: 评估7种置信度驱动方法：基线单次生成、token熵早停、MaxProb早停、ProbGap早停、token熵路由、语义熵早停、语义熵路由。语义熵通过温度采样生成N=5个独立样本，按语义聚类（MCQ用精确匹配，自由文本用词袋嵌入+凝聚层次聚类），计算聚类分布的Shannon熵。阈值τ通过15个校准样例上正确回答的均值熵标定。覆盖7个模型对（Qwen2.5、Llama-3.2、SmolLM2、Gemma3，2个跨家族配置），5个数据集（BoolQ、HellaSwag、ARC-C、ARC-E、WinoGrande），每组合20-50样本。全部在Apple Silicon消费级硬件上无量化运行。

Result: 1) Token级熵在SLMs中失效：35个数据集-模型组合中32个(91%)平均token熵<0.01bits，正确与错误答案的熵值无差异，阈值标定收敛至零。2) 语义熵恢复有效信号：语义路由在13/35(37%)组合中取得最佳精度，最高+50pp（如SmolLM 360M→Phi-3.5在ARC-C/E上）。3) 专家质量主导：跨家族路由平均+22.0% vs 同家族+6.8%；SmolLM 360M路由至同家族1.7B仅+3.0%，但路由至Phi-3.5-mini达+26.0%。4) Gemma3-1B为失败案例：语义层也过度自信，低语义熵导致路由信号无法触发。5) 计算开销增加：所有35组中语义路由均消耗更多token（~640 vs 基线2-120），价值不在于省算力而在于精准分配。

Conclusion: Token级熵作为SLM置信度信号不可用；语义熵通过在答案级别而非token级别测量不确定性，可有效识别SLM的"无知"，驱动智能级联路由。部署建议：使用小型快速模型(<1B)处理多数查询，维护单一高质量专家(如Phi-3.5-mini)作为共享后备，语义熵阈值τ控制路由率以平衡精度与成本。核心范式转变：熵基方法的价值主张从"节省token"转向"在正确处花费token"，将模型的局限性转化为可操作的计算分配信号。

Abstract: We explore whether entropy-based confidence signals can be leveraged to improve the accuracy of Small Language Models (SLMs) with fewer than 3 billion parameters, running entirely on consumer hardware. We evaluate seven distinct approaches, including token-level entropy early stopping, semantic entropy estimation, and uncertainty-aware routing to larger expert models, across 7 model pairs and 5 standard NLU benchmarks. Our key finding is that token-level entropy is effectively blind in SLMs: in 91% of dataset-model combinations, mean token entropy is near zero regardless of answer correctness, rendering token-based confidence signals unusable at this scale. We demonstrate that semantic entropy, computed by generating multiple samples, clustering answers by meaning, and measuring distributional uncertainty, recovers a viable confidence signal. Using semantic entropy to selectively route uncertain queries to a larger expert model yields accuracy improvements of up to +50 percentage points. Notably, cross-family routing (e.g., SmolLM 360M to Phi-3.5-mini) averages +22.0% improvement compared to +6.8% for same-family routing, revealing that expert model quality matters more than architectural compatibility. Our results suggest that the value proposition for entropy-based methods in SLMs is not computational savings but intelligent compute allocation: spending more tokens where they matter most.

</details>


### [31] [From Discharge Notes to Patient Understanding: Persona-Grounded, Open-Ended Simulation of LLMs as Discharge Educators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20827)
*Won Seok Jang,Zonghai Yao,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DischargeBench，一个面向出院教育场景的、以患者角色画像（persona）为驱动的开放式多轮模拟评测框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）作为出院教育者的能力，强调以"患者理解"为核心而非文本质量或答题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出院教育是影响患者再入院率、术后恢复和满意度的关键环节，但现有 LLM 评测多聚焦静态任务（材料生成、摘要、QA），无法衡量在开放对话中"患者是否真正理解"。同时既缺少面向异质患者的标准化评测，也缺少对任意 LLM 进行公平对比的框架。

Method: 1) 基于 MIMIC-IV 与 MIMIC-IV-Note 构建 MIMIC-IV-Ext-DischargeBench 数据集（477 例，跨 24 个 ICD 章），并为每例生成 5 维 persona 属性（医学档案、教育水平、健康素养、人格、病史回忆能力）。2) 采用多智能体仿真：候选 LLM 担任 Educator，与由 Llama-3.3-70B 驱动的 Virtual Patient 进行多轮对话；后台由 Qwen3.5-9B 担任 Education Monitor Agent (EMA)，仅对患者侧进行质量监管与终止控制，保留 Educator 评估信号的完整性。3) 通过 LLM-as-a-Judge（Gemma-4-31B）从四个临床轴评估：Conversation Quality（自然度、响应性、清晰度、临床相关性、可读性）、Topic Checklist Score（TCS）、Comprehension Score（患者后测理解）、Factual Consistency（ROUGE-L + 余弦相似度），并以两名急诊医生的人工标注做对齐校验。4) 进行 ICD 章节、人格、失败率等多维分层分析。

Result: GPT-5 系列（尤其 GPT-5.5）在对话质量、TCS、理解度和事实一致性四项上均领先；开源模型中 MedGemma-27b-text-it 与 Qwen3-32B 表现最佳，但整体仍落后 GPT-5 家族 0.06–0.1。聚合分数掩盖了显著的临床相关异质性：在某些 ICD 章节（如 NEO、CPC）TCS 跨度可达 0.17–0.23；在 Minimiser、Anxious、Distrustful 三类困难 persona 上，所有模型（包括 GPT-5）的自然度、可读性、TCS、理解度和事实一致性均出现系统性下降；最小模型（Qwen3-4B、MedGemma-4b-it）在这些 persona 上失败率显著上升（MedGemma-4b-it 对 Distrustful 患者失败率达 26%）。LLM Judge 与医生的一致性在 Conversation Quality 上接近医生间水平（wκ≈0.24），但在 TCS（0.25）和 Comprehension（0.17）上显著低于医生间一致性（0.57、0.56）。

Conclusion: 出院教育的 LLM 评估必须以"患者是否真正理解"为核心，而不能仅依赖文本质量或答题准确率。聚合指标会掩盖跨疾病章节与患者画像的临床重要差异；困难 persona 会暴露主题覆盖不足、理解缺口和事实偏离等问题。同时，LLM-as-a-Judge 在结构化轴上的一致性低于医生间水平，应作为可扩展的近似工具而非个体病例评判的替代。

Abstract: Hospital discharge education is an interactive teaching task: a clinician adapts a discharge plan to a patient's literacy, recall, and personality. Existing LLM evaluations target static or artifact-generation tasks and do not measure patient understanding under open-ended dialogue. We introduce DischargeBench, a persona-grounded simulation in which a candidate LLM educator conducts a multi-turn session with a Virtual Patient, while an Education Monitor Agent regulates patient realism without modifying the educator, protecting the evaluation signal. We curate MIMIC-IV-Ext-DischargeBench, 477 cases over 24 ICD chapters with persona axes (personality, education level, health literacy, past-medical-history recall) for stratified analysis. Each simulation is scored on four axes -- Conversation Quality, Topic Checklist, Comprehension, and Factual Consistency -- by an LLM-as-a-Judge aligned against physician annotations. Across closed- and open-source LLMs, aggregate scores conceal clinically relevant variation across ICD chapters and patient personas; difficult personas expose coverage failures, comprehension gaps, and reduced source-answer agreement. LLM evaluation for discharge education should center patient understanding, not text quality or answer accuracy alone.

</details>


### [32] [SAGE: Schema-Guided LLMs for Grant Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20829)
*Erik Varapaev,Andrei Chetvergov,Stepan Ukolov,Timofei Sivoraksha,Alexander Evseev,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAGE（Schema-Guided Aspect-Based Grant Evaluation）是一个将资助评审标准（rubric）转化为可执行模式（schema）的LLM辅助评审系统。它通过将评审标准分解为8个一级指标、37个子指标和218个原子评估点（aspects），对申请书进行结构化、证据关联的预评审，生成可供专家审核与修正的草稿。在35份非营利资助申请的评估中，SAGE在独立历史比较中获得中等序数一致性（κ=0.29），而在专家参考其草稿进行的辅助重评中达到κ=0.58，显著优于单一提示基线（κ=0.33），并在排序相关性和误差上均更优。对生成声明的审计显示，51.2%被专家确认、18.6%被反驳、30.2%未被回应，揭示了系统特定的待验证模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资助评审需要依据复杂多维度的评分标准审查申请材料包，但现有LLM"自由生成评审"的方式存在两个核心问题：（1）模型可能忽视评分标准的具体要求；（2）流畅的评语可能掩盖证据缺失。专家批评难以审计其差异导致结论可信度受限。该研究旨在通过将资助方法论编码为可执行的、评估有效的模式（schema），生成结构化、证据关联、可审计的评审草稿，使专家能够高效地核查、质疑与修正。

Method: SAGE框架包含四个核心组件：  
- **领域特定性Schema构建**：将资助方的评审标准（8个一级指标）分解为子指标（37个）和原子评估点（228个），每个原子点关联预期证据来源、检查动作和结构化输出字段（分数、置信度、证据、优势、劣势、建议、推理依据）。  
- **确定性上下文组装**：为每个子指标预定义文档层映射，从表单字段、附件文本、链接摘要中提取相关上下文，记录缺失证据而非用推断填充。  
- **模式引导生成**：为每个子指标构建包含角色、指标定义、评估清单、上下文、评分规则、JSON schema的提示，调用LLM生成并解析；仅修复语法错误，保留空字段以暴露缺失。  
- **聚合与输出**：按加权平均聚合为一级指标分数，组装结构化评论，生成同时包含分数和证据记录的"评审器-面向制品（reviewer-facing artifact）"。  
评估采用两轮设计：第1轮事后与原始比赛评审（105份加权分数、615个指标对比）比较；第2轮基金会评审员参考参考草稿进行指标级重评（202个评估），同时与"单一提示-每-指标"基线对比，并通过LLM辅助的声明-级别审计分析1,584条提取声明的确认/反驳/未回应状态。

Result: **第1轮（独立历史比较，n=105）**：SAGE达到Spearman ρ=0.41、二次加权Cohen's κ=0.29、MAE=0.76分（5分制），偏差+0.57（偏宽松）。指标级别分析显示4个通用指标κ介于0.29-0.42，可选指标因样本少或尺度不同而一致性较低。  
**第2轮（辅助重评，n=202）**：SAGE达到ρ=0.61、κ=0.58、MAE=0.49、偏差+0.24；最强一致性出现在结果导向（κ=0.67）和证据质量（κ=0.70）。  
**基线对比（n=201公共子集）**：基线仅达到ρ=0.27、κ=0.33、MAE=0.72。SAGE在排序相关性上提升0.34 [0.20, 0.49]、κ提升0.25 [0.15, 0.38]、MAE降低0.23 [0.15, 0.31]，所有自举置信区间均不包含零。  
**声明审计（n=1,584）**：51.2%被确认、18.6%被反驳、30.2%未被评论。五类需重点核查的失败模式：跨指标证据混用、缺失信息推断、数值解读、部分支持过度赋分、上下文覆盖不全。

Conclusion: SAGE通过将资助评审方法论编码为可执行模式，生成结构化、证据关联、可审计的预评审草稿，在历史独立评审和辅助工作流两种场景下均验证了其有效性。在辅助场景中达到中等偏高的一致性（κ=0.58），显著优于低成本的单一提示基线，且超过一半的生成声明得到专家确认。该系统将专家的精力从起草转向核查与修正，并提供了五种具体的可操作验证目标。未来可通过确定性数值检查、缺失/负面证据分离、声明级置信度排序进一步改进，并通过受控多项目研究分离评估其对评审质量、一致性、效率的独立影响。

Abstract: Grant reviewers must apply detailed criteria to application forms, budgets, and supporting documents while producing assessments that colleagues can inspect. We present SAGE, Schema-Guided Aspect-Based Grant Evaluation, a system that translates a grant rubric into structured checks and links its judgements to evidence from the application package. We evaluate SAGE in two stages on 35 nonprofit grant applications. A post-factum comparison with 105 reviews from the original competition shows fair ordinal agreement (kappa = 0.29). The foundation then conducted a criterion-level re-review after inspecting SAGE, producing 202 assessments. In this assisted round, SAGE reached kappa = 0.58 and outperformed a one-prompt-per-criterion baseline (kappa = 0.33 on the common subset), with higher rank correlation and lower error. A claim-level audit further identifies confirmed, disputed, and unaddressed parts of the structured draft. SAGE operationalizes the review methodology by producing a detailed, evidence-linked, and auditable draft for expert correction.

</details>


### [33] [Reviser: Revision-Capable Text Generation via Autoregressive Cursor Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20830)
*Sean Diab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Reviser，一种基于解码器Transformer的文本生成模型，通过在可变画布上以自回归方式预测单一光标动作（INSERT/MOVE/STOP），实现真正的非单调生成，同时保持接近标准自回归的计算开销，并在续写任务上显著优于扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归Transformer只能从左到右生成，修改早期内容需要在其后追加补丁；现有非自回归和编辑方法虽支持修订但通常依赖多次全序列前向，计算开销巨大。本文旨在提出一种既能实现真正的非单调（可修订早期内容）又能保持接近标准AR计算量的生成方法。

Method: Reviser是仅解码器Transformer，将文本生成为光标在可变画布上的相对动作序列。每个时间步预测恰好一个动作token：INSERT(token)、MOVE(Δ) 或 STOP，作用于一个画布和光标状态。Transformer仅在编辑历史动作序列H_t上做自回归（不直接关注画布token），画布通过执行器和合法性掩码被隐式访问。训练采用"混淆-恢复"（obfuscation-restoration）监督方案：通过随机删除/移动动作构造混淆轨迹，再反转并求逆得到恢复轨迹作为监督目标。

Result: 在C4续写基准上，Reviser 100M胜AR Baseline 61.3% vs 38.7%，300M胜54.4% vs 45.6%；对比扩散基线优势更显著：SEDD Small 169M胜率85.93% vs 14.07%，MDLM 170M胜率78.33% vs 21.67%，SEDD Absorb 353M胜率68.47% vs 31.53%。MAUVE指标上Reviser 300M达0.9349，为所有对比模型最高。轨迹统计显示模型确实进行了大量非单调生成：100%示例含≥1次向后修订，约64-66%的移动为向后移动，96%以上为非末尾插入。计算开销仅为AR基线的1.25-1.50倍，远低于扩散模型（140-1400倍）。

Conclusion: Reviser证明了对编辑动作进行自回归这一简单设计可以实现真正的非单调文本生成，同时保持轻量级的"下一步动作"接口。其在续写任务上显著优于扩散基线，并与同规模AR基线具有竞争力，为模型在生成过程中主动修订自身响应提供了一条实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Revision-capable generation is appealing because it can insert or revise earlier content, but many non-autoregressive and edit-based approaches obtain this flexibility through repeated sequence-level computation. We propose Reviser, a decoder-only Transformer that generates a response as a sequence of cursor-relative actions on a mutable canvas. At each step, Reviser predicts exactly one action token: INSERT(token), MOVE($\Delta$), or STOP, and is autoregressive over edit-history actions rather than final text order. This design enables genuinely non-monotonic generation while preserving a simple next-action interface. On a continuation benchmark, Reviser is strongly preferred to SEDD and MDLM in our arena evaluations, and trajectory statistics confirm that the model performs frequent backward moves and mid-canvas insertions rather than merely emulating end-append decoding. Against size-matched autoregressive baselines, Reviser is competitive at both the 100M and 300M scales. Under our shared FLOPs convention, Reviser also requires substantially less inference compute than representative multi-pass refinement and diffusion-style baselines.

</details>


### [34] [TatBLiMP: A Benchmark of Linguistic Minimal Pairs for Tatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20832)
*Ilshat Saetov,Dmitry Gaynullin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TatBLiMP是首个针对鞑靼语（Tatar，ISO 639-3 tat，属钦察突厥语族，使用西里尔字母书写）的语言学最小对立对基准测试，涵盖16种形态句法现象、共1248个句子对，每个对仅通过单个语素区分语法和非法项；该基准在多种模型上表现出对针对性鞑靼语训练的高敏感性，与参数规模无明显相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对鞑靼语的语言模型语法性评估资源。即使是涵盖101种语言的MultiBLiMP也未包含鞑靼语。因此需要构建首个针对鞑靼语的形态句法评估工具，以填补低资源突厥语族语言评估的空白。

Method: 1) 现象清单与单语素扰动操作借鉴TurBLiMP，并适配至鞑靼语，新增"bare-noun number after numerals and quantifiers"这一鞑靼语特有现象，共16种形态句法现象。
2) 每个最小对中的语法句取自鞑靼语文学散文真实例句；非法句由apertium-tat转录器进行确定性单语素扰动生成。
3) 构建遵循"plausibility principle"，使非法项模拟真实语言使用中可能出现的错误，而非任意破坏。
4) 每个句子对均经过母语者审核确认。
5) 利用模型已分配的概率进行评分，无需文本生成或句法分析器，可应用于基础模型及中训练检查点。

Result: 1) TatBLiMP成功区分不同模型的鞑靼语能力：478M从零训练模型和125M单语模型得分约0.97（领先）；7B跨语言适配模型表现稍逊；30-120B参数的前沿多语言大模型得分仅0.80-0.92；轻微调优的多语言模型表现最弱。
2) 基准得分与针对性鞑靼语训练强度相关，而非参数规模——大规模多语言LLM反而不如中小规模专用模型。
3) 局限性：继承自TurBLiMP的现象分类体系未涵盖对母语者最为突出的形态音韵现象（元音和谐与辅音同化），需构建本土第二层评估加以补充。

Conclusion: TatBLiMP是首个针对鞑靼语的语法性评估基准，通过单语素最小对立对设计，实现了对模型鞑靼语形态句法能力的精准测量。结果表明，针对性训练比参数规模更有效。然而，该基准目前缺乏形态音韵层面的覆盖，未来工作应开发包含元音和谐与辅音同化等现象的补充评估层。

Abstract: We introduce TatBLiMP, the first benchmark of linguistic minimal pairs for Tatar (tt, ISO 639-3 tat), a Qypchaq Turkic language written in Cyrillic. To our knowledge it is the first grammaticality evaluation for Tatar language models of any kind, since even the 101-language MultiBLiMP does not include Tatar. TatBLiMP covers 16 morphosyntactic phenomena in 1248 sentence pairs. Each pair differs by a single morpheme, one grammatical and one ungrammatical. A model passes a pair when it assigns higher probability to the grammatical member. Scoring compares probabilities the model already assigns, so the benchmark needs no text generation and no parser, and it runs on base models and on mid-training checkpoints. TatBLiMP adapts the phenomenon inventory and single-morpheme breaking operations of TurBLiMP to Tatar and adds one phenomenon specific to Tatar, bare-noun number after numerals and quantifiers. The grammatical member of every pair is an attested sentence from Tatar literary prose. The ungrammatical member is produced by a deterministic single-morpheme perturbation with the apertium-tat transducer. Every pair is ratified by a native speaker. A plausibility principle governs construction, so the ungrammatical member is a plausible real-world error rather than an arbitrary corruption. Across from-scratch Tatar models, cross-lingual adaptations, and frontier multilingual LLMs, the benchmark tracks focused Tatar training rather than parameter scale. A 478M from-scratch model and a 125M monolingual model lead near 0.97, a 7B adaptation trails, frontier LLMs of 30-120B parameters fall to 0.80-0.92, and a lightly tuned multilingual model is weakest. We close with the benchmark's main limitation. Its inherited taxonomy omits the morphophonology, vowel harmony and consonant assimilation, that is most salient to native speakers, and we sketch a native second layer that would add it.

</details>


### [35] [Transsion's Speaker-Attributed Multilingual ASR System for the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20833)
*Zhecheng Ren,Xuanji He,Xiaoxiao Li,Zhichen Han,Gaoyang Dong,Gaosheng Zhang,Minchuan Chen,Fengjie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了Transsion语音团队提交至MLC-SLM 2026挑战赛Task 1的系统,聚焦多语言对话语音的说话人归属转录任务,采用级联框架实现,并在官方评估集上取得tcpMER 15.41%、排名第二的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言对话场景下的说话人归属转录(将转录文本与对应说话人关联)仍是实用化难点,需兼顾说话人日志、多语言ASR及融合对齐。MLC-SLM 2026挑战赛Task 1针对该问题,旨在推动相关技术发展。

Method: 提出三模块级联框架:1) 基于DiariZen的说话人日志模块,通过局部说话人活动估计与全局说话人聚类生成说话人同质片段;2) 基于Qwen3-Omni的多语言长语音ASR模块,辅以外部CTC对齐模型以获得词级与字符级时间戳;3) 融合模块结合日志输出与带时间戳转录,生成说话人归属STM结果。

Result: 在官方评估集上的实验表明所提框架有效,提交系统取得tcpMER 15.41%,在所有参赛队伍中排名第二。

Conclusion: 所提级联框架(DiariZen日志 + Qwen3-Omni多语言ASR + 外部CTC对齐 + 融合)在多语言对话说话人归属转录任务上具有竞争力,为该方向提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the Transsion Speech Team submission to Task 1 of the MLC-SLM 2026 Challenge, which focuses on speaker-attributed transcription for multilingual conversational speech. We propose a cascaded framework consisting of three components: a speaker diarization module, a long-form multilingual ASR module, and a speaker-transcription fusion module. The diarization module is built upon DiariZen and produces speaker-homogeneous segments through local speaker activity estimation and global speaker clustering. The ASR module is based on Qwen3-Omni and generates multilingual transcriptions, while an external CTC-based alignment model provides precise word- and character-level timestamps. Finally, the fusion module combines diarization outputs with timestamped transcriptions to generate speaker-attributed STM outputs. Experimental results on the official evaluation set demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework. The submitted system achieves a tcpMER of 15.41% and ranks second among all participating teams.

</details>


### [36] [A Generative Grammar Underlying the Voynich Manuscript, the Pastiche Hypothesis: Evidence from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20835)
*Nicolas Turenne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对15世纪的《伏尼契手稿》进行跨学科综合分析，结合概率建模、语音分解、稀有事件检测和多模态图像分析，揭示该手稿虽然内容尚未破译，但其结构呈现出高度系统化的"仿自然语言"生成特征，与中世纪药草传统密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《伏尼契手稿》作为历史上最著名的未破译文本之一，其神秘的未知文字系统和植物插图长期引发学界关注。以往研究多集中于单一方面（纯语言学或图像学），缺乏将概率语言模型、语音模式比较与AI辅助图像对齐相整合的系统性研究。本文旨在通过多学科方法填补这一空白，验证"该手稿是否为一种结构化的仿自然语言生成系统"这一假设。

Method: 1. 基于重新转写的新语料库，构建位置依赖的概率语法模型，分析词级与字母级分布；2. 跨语言语音模式比较，将伏尼契符号与印欧语系、闪米特语系及亚洲语言进行对照；3. 利用大语言模型驱动的图像-文本对齐方法，识别插图中可能的植物对应关系；4. 稀有事件检测，分析重复首字母序列的极低概率现象。

Result: 1. 伏尼契符号在功能上表现为字母而非音节单位，而其词长分布却与音节结构相似；2. 语音模式与希伯来语、阿拉伯语等辅音密集型语言更为接近，与印欧语系吻合度较低；3. 概率模型成功再现了Zipf-like分布，并揭示重复首字母序列概率极低，证实其系统化仿制特征；4. 图像分析显示，植物插图与地中海传统的《伪阿普莱乌斯药草志》存在显著对应，符合作中世纪医药书籍的模仿行为。

Conclusion: 研究支持如下假设：《伏尼契手稿》遵循一种结合语言规律与药草知识的结构化生成系统；同时展示了将概率建模与AI辅助方法相整合在历史手稿分析中的重要价值，为未来未破译文本的研究提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Background: The Voynich Manuscript is a fifteenth-century codex written in an unknown script whose content remains undeciphered. Previous studies suggest that its statistical properties resemble those of natural languages, while its illustrations - primarily plants - recall medieval herbals. Methods: We present a multidisciplinary analysis combining probabilistic modeling, phonetic decomposition, rare-event detection, and multimodal image analysis, based on a newly transliterated corpus. Word- and letter-level distributions are modeled using position-dependent probabilistic grammars, while phonetic patterns are compared across Indo-European, Semitic, and Asian languages. Image-text alignment methods based on large language models are applied to identify potential botanical correspondences. Results: The results indicate that Voynich symbols behave as letters rather than syllabic units, while word-length distributions resemble syllabic structures. Phonetic analyses show closer alignment with consonant-heavy languages such as Hebrew or Arabic than with Indo-European languages. Probabilistic modeling reproduces Zipf-like distributions and reveals extremely low probabilities for repeated initial-letter sequences, indicating a structured imitation of natural language. Image analysis suggests strong correspondences between Voynich plant illustrations and those found in Pseudo-Apuleius herbals from the Mediterranean tradition, consistent with an imitation of medieval medicinal books. Perspectives: These findings support the hypothesis that the Voynich Manuscript follows a structured generative system combining linguistic regularities and herbal knowledge, and demonstrate the value of integrating probabilistic and AI-assisted approaches in the analysis of historical manuscripts.

</details>


### [37] [From Generation to Detection: Exploration of Discourse Driven Scenario based LLM Generated Fake News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20838)
*Zeynep Özdemir,Murat Osmanoğlu,Sevgi Yiğit-Sert,Ömer Özgür Tanrıöver,Yılmaz Ar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验，系统评估了7个主流大语言模型（LLM）在4种操控场景下生成与检测假新闻的能力，并探究了优化提示词对检测效果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成文本能力日益强大，虚假信息（misinformation）的自动化生成与检测成为迫切的现实问题。然而，目前尚缺乏在受控条件下，系统比较不同LLM生成假新闻的质量、可检测性以及模型间相互检测能力的综合性研究。

Method: 1. 操控场景设计：基于新闻业话语框架，定义四种假新闻生成场景——开放生成、改写、操控提示词、属性提示词。2. 语料库构建：使用7个广泛应用的LLM，在四种场景下共生成14000篇文章，构建合成假新闻语料库。3. 语言学分析：分析生成文本在结构与语义层面与真实新闻的相似度。4. 检测实验：每个模型作为"判断者"，使用基础提示词和通过迭代优化（从真伪文本对中提取误导模式）的提示词进行检测，评估检测性能。

Result: 1. 不同LLM在生成与检测假新闻方面表现出显著差异。2. 生成策略对检测难度有强烈影响——某些策略产出的假新闻更易识别，某些则更隐蔽。3. 通过迭代优化得到的"精炼提示词"并未提升检测性能，反而经常造成性能下降。

Conclusion: 本研究对LLM在典型生成场景下检测LLM生成假新闻的能力提供了系统性评估。结果表明：(1) 生成策略显著影响可检测性；(2) 当前LLM在检测同类生成内容方面能力有限且不稳定；(3) 简单的提示词优化并非提升检测能力的有效路径，凸显了开发更专业检测方法的必要性。

Abstract: In this study, we examine how modern LLMs generate and detect fake news under controlled settings across four manipulation scenarios. These are open-ended generation, rewriting, manipulation prompts and attribute based prompts grounded in the journalistic discourse framework. Firstly, using seven widely adapted models, we created a synthetic fake news corpus with 14000 generated articles across these four scenarios. Then we analyzed its linguistic properties to assess how closely model-generated news resembles real news structurally and semantically. Finally, to evaluate detection performance, we conducted experiments where each model judges generated fake news, starting with a basic detection prompt and improved prompts developed through an iterative refinement process that extracts misleading patterns from real-fake pairs. Our results revealed substantial variation across models in both generating and detecting misinformation, demonstrated that the generation strategy strongly influences detectability, and show that the refined prompt does not improve and often harms detection performance. Therefore, the study provides a systematic assessment of LLMs detection capability of LLMs generated fake news across typical generation scenarios.

</details>


### [38] [Curriculum-Based Noise Adaptation for Phoneme-to-Text Reconstruction in Visual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20839)
*Matthew Kit Khinn Teng,Haibo Zhang,Takeshi Saitoh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为渐进式错误课程训练（PECT）的课程学习框架，用于音素中心视觉语音识别（VSR）中的音素到文本重建。该框架通过逐步将NLLB重建模型从合成音素扰动适应到多域伪标签，再到目标域伪标签，有效缩小了训练与推理之间的分布差距，在LRS2和LRS3基准上显著降低了词错误率（WER），并能跨多种VSR前端模型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音素中心VSR系统中，音素到文本重建模型通常在干净的音素序列或合成扰动上训练，与推理时VSR前端产生的真实、上下文相关的音素预测错误之间存在显著分布差距，导致重建性能受限。本文旨在通过课程学习策略桥接这一训练-推理不匹配问题。

Method: 提出渐进式错误课程训练（PECT）框架，包含三个顺序阶段：(1) 使用20%噪声比的合成错误注入器（随机插入、删除、替换）训练基础鲁棒性；(2) 使用五折交叉验证在LRS2+LRS3合并数据集上生成的多域伪标签进行适应；(3) 使用目标域（LRS2或LRS3）的K折伪标签进行精细化适应。学习率在阶段间逐步降低（5e-5→5e-6→5e-7），每阶段训练10个epoch。K折策略通过留出折预测防止数据泄露。

Result: 在LRS2和LRS3基准上，PECT一致地改善了多种VSR前端（V-ASR、PV-ASR、HP-VSR系列）的重建性能。其中HP-VSR-FiLMFuse (L4) 在LRS2上WER从23.3%降至22.2%，HP-VSR-ResFiLM在LRS3上WER从30.3%降至29.7%。HP-VSR-Base获得最大提升（LRS2: 33.3%→29.1%，LRS3: 40.8%→36.1%）。消融实验证明课程顺序至关重要（S₂₃→P₂₃→Pₜ为最优），反向课程或混合采样策略性能显著下降。定性分析显示PECT能有效恢复音素歧义、缺失功能词、上下文相关词汇并减少幻觉词插入。

Conclusion: PECT为音素中心VSR中的音素到文本重建提供了一种有效且可泛化的课程学习策略，通过渐进式适应真实音素预测错误显著提高了重建鲁棒性。最佳课程路径为合成错误→多域伪标签→目标域伪标签，证明性能提升源于监督信号的渐进式排序而非单纯的数据多样性。未来工作将探索VSR与重建模型的联合优化、多语言扩展以及音位相似性感知的错误生成方法。

Abstract: Phoneme-centric visual speech recognition reconstructs sentences from intermediate phoneme predictions, making overall recognition performance highly dependent on the robustness of the phoneme-to-text reconstruction model. Existing reconstruction approaches are commonly trained on clean phoneme sequences or synthetically corrupted inputs, leading to a mismatch between training conditions and the realistic phoneme prediction errors encountered during inference. To address this limitation, this paper proposes progressive error curriculum training (PECT). This curriculum learning framework progressively adapts a No Language Left Behind (NLLB)-based phoneme-to-text reconstruction model using synthetic phoneme perturbations, multi-domain pseudo-labels, and target-domain pseudo-labels generated by a visual speech recognizer. By gradually exposing the reconstruction model to increasingly realistic phoneme prediction errors, the proposed framework improves robustness while preserving sentence-reconstruction accuracy. Experiments on the LRS2 and LRS3 benchmarks demonstrate that PECT consistently improves reconstruction performance across multiple phoneme-based visual speech recognition frontends, including visual automatic speech recognition (V-ASR), point visual automatic speech recognition (PV-ASR), and head-pose-aware visual speech recognition (HP-VSR) variants. In particular, PECT reduces the word error rate (WER) of HP-VSR-FiLMFuse (L4) from 23.3% to 22.2% on LRS2 and reduces the WER of HP-VSR-ResFiLM from 30.3% to 29.7% on LRS3. Comprehensive ablation studies and qualitative analyses further demonstrate the effectiveness of progressively adapting the reconstruction model to realistic phoneme prediction errors. These results show that PECT provides an effective and generalizable curriculum learning strategy for phoneme-to-text reconstruction in phoneme-centric visual speech recognition.

</details>


### [39] [Boosting Deepresearch and LongContext Ability with Self-Generated Deepresearch Rollouts Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20844)
*Zihan Wang,Hao Wang,Boyuan Jiang,Yiqun Zhang,Shi Feng,Xiaocui Yang,Yiwen Ye,Jianghang Lin,Xiaozhong Ji,Jinghao Lin,Kai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对深度研究（DeepResearch, DR）智能体在多轮搜索与访问过程中上下文迅速增长、导致长上下文理解不足的问题，提出了一种无需额外标注的自增强训练框架 DLD-RL。该框架首先通过 DR-RL 收集 rollout 轨迹，再利用 DR-to-Long 方法将轨迹中压缩的网页摘要/搜索片段替换为完整网页内容，构建多文档长上下文 QA 数据，随后执行 LongQA-RL 以强化长上下文能力，最后恢复完整 DR-RL 进行微调。在三类深度研究基准（BrowseComp-300、SEAL-0、XBench-2510）上平均相对提升约 7.3%，在三类长上下文基准（LongBench-v2、Frames、LongReason）上平均相对提升约 13.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 DR-RL 训练之后，61.6% 的剩余预测错误仍可归因于长上下文理解不足（如长上下文幻觉与跨文档证据整合失败）。标准 DR-RL 中正反馈主要来自短轨迹，长轨迹贡献不足，因此难以稳定提升模型在长上下文下的决策能力。这一瓶颈需要通过显式强化长上下文能力来突破。

Method: 1. **DR-to-Long**：将 DR-RL 收集的 rollout 轨迹（包含搜索历史、访问页面、证据片段与最终答案）中的压缩观察替换为对应网页的完整内容，生成 64K–128K token（可扩展至 1M+ token）的多文档长上下文 QA 实例。失败轨迹通过注入成功轨迹中的高质量证据片段进行增强以保证答案可解性。
2. **DLD-RL（DR → LongQA → DR）**：四阶段训练框架——(a) 短期 DR-RL 收集数据；(b) DR-to-Long 转换为 LongQA 数据；(c) 加入 KL 正则的 LongQA-RL 以保留智能体能力并强化长上下文推理；(d) 恢复完整 DR-RL 进行退火对齐。
3. 优化器采用 GRPO，长上下文阶段保留冻结参考策略并加入 KL 约束，奖励为基于 LLM 评判的二元奖励。

Result: 在 4B 与 9B 两个基座上：
- **DR 基准**：BrowseComp-300 +6.4%、XBench-2510 +14.3%、Seal-0 +8.2%（平均约 +7.3%）。
- **长上下文基准**：LongBench-v2 +40.1%、Frames +13.7%、LongReason +2.4%（4B 规模，DR-Venus-4B-SFT 家族）；9B 规模对应为 +10.2%、+5.1%、+9.4%，平均约 +13.5%。
- **消融实验**：移除 KL 正则会损害智能体行为；排除失败轨迹数据使训练难度降低；将 LongQA-RL 置于完整 DR-RL 之后则削弱最终 DR 性能。
- **行为分析**：LongQA-RL 后模型更频繁使用 Visit 工具访问完整页面，并减少冗余相似搜索查询。
- **可扩展性**：通过扩展更多 URL，构造数据可达约 1.5M token，可作为超长上下文预训练语料。

Conclusion: 长上下文理解能力是 DR 智能体的关键瓶颈，二者存在相互促进的关系。DR-to-Long 提供了一种零标注成本的长上下文数据构造方法，DLD-RL 通过 DR → LongQA → DR 的循环训练同时提升两类能力。研究成果不仅适用于 DR 任务，也可为超长上下文预训练提供数据来源，揭示了搜索/浏览与长上下文推理之间协同进化的潜力。

Abstract: Deepresearch (DR) agents interact with real-world web environments through multi-turn search and visit, causing their contexts to grow rapidly over time. We observe that, even after DR Agentic Reinforcement Learning (DR-RL), 61.6% of the model's remaining prediction errors can still be attributed to insufficient long-context understanding, including longcontext hallucination and failures in cross-document evidence integration. It motivates us to further break the bottleneck of DR-RL by strengthening the model's long-context ability. However, effective LongContext training requires more than simply increasing context length. To bridge the data gap, we propose `DR Rollouts to LongContext-QA (DR-to-Long)'. The method repurposes DR-RL trajectories, which naturally contain search histories, visited webpages, evidence snippets, and final-answer supervision. It then replaces the compact snippets and webpage summaries in each trajectory with the full contents of their corresponding URLs, producing substantially longer multi-document contexts while preserving the original evidence relationships. Building on DR-to-Long, we introduce DLD (DR -> LongQA -> DR)-RL. DLD-RL first performs a short DR-RL stage to collect rollout trajectories, which are then converted into LongQA instances at zero annotation cost. The model is subsequently optimized with LongQA-RL to strengthen LongContext ability, followed by full DR-RL to continue improving its DR capability. Experiments show that DLD-RL outperforms standard DR-RL by 7.3% on three Deepresearch benchmarks and improves performance by 13.5% on three long-context benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [Reading Anxiety or Reading the Label? Comparing Fine-Tuned and Frontier Models for Anxiety Detection on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20847)
*Cris Huynh,Arlene Pham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对社交媒体(Reddit)焦虑检测任务，在统一协议下对比了六种模型条件(经典TF-IDF、通用BERT、心理健康领域MentalBERT、QLoRA微调的3B小模型、以及两个零样本前沿大模型)，并提出"词汇依赖性"(lexical dependence)作为新的衡量方式。研究揭示了焦虑标注语料的评估存在关键词泄露的严重混杂因素，导致已发表指标均为上界，且微调模型受此影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线焦虑相关内容早于临床就诊即出现，是被动筛查的重要信号。实践中存在三种部署路径选择：调用前沿商用API、自行微调小型模型、或部署传统分类器。已有文献(如Kermani等)在固定8B规模下发现微调vs零样本差距显著(0.86 vs 0.74)，但未曾在焦虑任务上直接对比"小型微调模型"与"前沿零样本模型"。更重要的是，已有评估协议存在一个未被识别的混杂：子版块派生的标签使69.3%的焦虑帖包含"anxiety"等关键词，分类器可能仅靠关键词匹配而非建模真实语言模式即可获得高分。

Method: 使用SWMH语料(Reddit心理健康数据集)的4分类形式(抑郁、焦虑、双相、自杀意念)，在固定train/val/test划分下对比6个条件：(1) SVM+TF-IDF n-grams；(2) BERT-base微调；(3) MentalBERT微调；(4) Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct QLoRA微调；(5) GPT-5.6-luna零样本；(6) Claude Sonnet 5零样本。每个模型在两种文本变体下评估——原始全文(full)与删除诊断词族的keyword-stripped版本——差值定义为词汇依赖性Δ。所有数据集、划分、prompt、解码设置在条件间保持恒定；测试集最终一次性使用以避免泄露。主要指标为Macro-F1和Anxiety类F1，并追踪前沿模型的refusal率与不可解析输出率。

Result: 主要结果：(1) RQ1的回答取决于所选前沿模型——微调Qwen2.5-3B(焦虑F1=0.812)略胜GPT-5.6 Luna(0.806)但输给Claude Sonnet 5(0.846)，两个前沿模型之间相差4.0点，H1无法在"前沿整体"层面被确认或拒绝。(2) RQ2显示方法代际收益递减——TF-IDF→BERT-base提升4.3点，加入心理健康领域预训练仅再增0.7点，且在Macro-F1上MentalBERT反而低于BERT-base 0.8点。(3) RQ3显示词汇依赖性跨度为8.6至25.4点，与模型能力不相关——QLoRA微调的Qwen最依赖关键词(Δ=0.254，高于TF-IDF的0.149)，而前沿零样本模型依赖最低(Δ=0.086)；Qwen的剥离文本下焦虑召回率跌至0.418并默认到多数类(抑郁)。GPT-5.6 Luna有15.1%的不可解析输出率，拉低其Macro-F1至0.667(最低)。Claude Sonnet 5未评估剥离变体(API预算限制)。

Conclusion: 论文得出三点核心结论：(1) "前沿零样本对比小型微调"问题无可比答案，因为前沿模型之间的差异(4.0点)超过其与微调模型的差异，单一厂商对比不应被泛化为能力层级结论。(2) 110M参数的MentalBERT距最强前沿模型仅差1.5点且无API依赖，数据驻留与成本使小型模型在心理健康监测场景中具有结构性优势；心理健康领域预训练在焦虑类上仅贡献0.7点而在Macro-F1上反而劣于通用BERT。(3) 最重要贡献是评估协议本身：诊断词删除造成8.6–25.4点的F1下降，子版块衍生语料上的已发表焦虑检测分数应被视为上界，且通胀幅度恰好在文献最常报告的微调模型上最大——因为它们在69.3%含关键词的训练数据上学会了更强的关键词捷径。作者提出"词汇依赖性"作为一项简单、可复现的补充度量，建议与F1一同报告，并指出未来工作应通过对比同一基础模型在全文与去关键词语料上微调后的依赖性来隔离其因果机制。

Abstract: Anxiety is among the most common mental health conditions, and people often write about it online well before seeking clinical help. Practitioners building detection tools face a concrete choice: call a frontier commercial model, fine-tune a smaller model in-house, or deploy a conventional classifier. We compare six conditions spanning all three on a held-out Reddit test set under a single controlled protocol. We also identify a confound in how this task is evaluated. In the corpus used here, 69.3% of anxiety-labelled posts contain the word "anxiety" or a variant, roughly twice the rate of comparable conditions, so a classifier can score well by keyword matching rather than by modelling the language of the condition. We therefore evaluate every model twice, on original text and with those terms deleted, and report the difference as lexical dependence. A frontier model leads on anxiety F1 (0.846), but a 110M-parameter domain-adapted encoder reaches 0.831 with no external API dependency, and mental-health domain pretraining accounts for only 0.7 of those points. Lexical dependence spans 8.6 to 25.4 points and does not track model capability: the LoRA fine-tuned 3B model is the most keyword-dependent condition tested, above even a TF-IDF classifier, while the frontier zero-shot model is the least. Published figures on this corpus are therefore upper bounds, and the inflation is largest for the fine-tuned models such figures typically report.

</details>


### [41] [Enhancing Audio Reasoning via Semantic Summary Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20849)
*Francesco Bonzi,Pooneh Mousavi,Cem Subakan,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARE（Semantic Prediction for Audio REasoning）方法，通过在大型音频语言模型（LALM）的CoT推理序列开头注入一个寄存器（register）标记，并利用Sentence-BERT对最终结论生成语义嵌入，施加余弦相似度对齐损失。该方法在不增加推理开销的前提下，改善了SALMONN 13B在MMAU和MMAR基准上的零样本推理性能，并经注意力图分析证实增强了早期层对声学特征的关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在复杂问答任务中表现出色，但显式Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理有时反而降低准确率，出现"推理鸿沟"。作者假设长推理序列使注意力从音频输入漂移至已生成的文本，导致模型忽视音频信号并产生流畅但缺乏依据的答案。现有解决方案依赖大规模监督数据或强化学习，缺少从架构或训练正则化角度出发的轻量化方法。

Method: 以SALMONN 13B为基础模型，在AF-Think标注的YouTube8M子集上进行微调。方法核心为：在输入序列[Question, Audio, Choices, [REG], CoT, Conclusion]中的CoT前插入[REG]寄存器标记，并在训练时通过余弦相似度损失将[REG]的最终隐藏状态与Sentence-BERT提取的最终结论语义嵌入进行对齐。总损失为交叉熵损失加λ=2.0的余弦相似度损失。由于[REG]不参与后续token的因果注意力掩码，推理时可完全丢弃，零额外计算开销。通过对6个随机种子的自注意力图分析，比较首层和末层中答案tokens及[REG]对音频帧的注意力分数。

Result: 在MMAU上达到58.03%（比SFT提升+3.38%，比Audio MuToR提升+5.01%），在MMAR上达到40.32%。Zero-shot CoT表现反而低于Zero-shot（18.03% vs 36.02% on MMAU），证实"推理鸿沟"现象。消融实验显示λ=2.0最优且方法对λ变化较为鲁棒（λ=1.0或3.0仍优于所有基线），但λ=3.0时方差增大（±2.83）。在每个章节前都插入寄存器（多结论摘要）的变体性能下降至55.43%，训练稳定性变差。注意力分析显示SPARE首层[REG]对音频帧的注意力显著高于SFT基线，且答案tokens对音频的注意力也有所增强。

Conclusion: SPARE作为一种潜在表征正则化策略，能有效弥合LALM中的推理鸿沟，无需架构改动或额外推理成本。通过对齐寄存器标记与最终结论语义嵌入，该方法促使模型在解码前形成有依据的目标导向音频表征，提升了零样本CoT推理性能，并通过注意力分析提供了声学聚焦增强的机制性证据。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) perform well on complex question answering but often show a reasoning gap, where explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reduces accuracy compared to direct answers. We hypothesize that long reasoning sequences shift attention away from the audio input. To address this, we propose SPARE (Semantic Prediction for Audio REasoning), which introduces a register token aligned with the final conclusion using a cosine similarity loss with a Sentence-BERT embedding. This conditions the model's latent space with the target semantic goal before reasoning begins. Experiments on MMAU and MMAR with SALMONN show improved zero-shot reasoning and stronger early attention to audio without additional inference cost.

</details>


### [42] [MME-Safety: A Fine-grained Benchmark for Safety Evaluation of MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20850)
*Yueming Lyu,Yilian Shi,Haoxiang Tan,Linzhuang Zou,Qihao Wang,Guihua Yu,Jie Qin,Xin Gao,Chenyang Si,Jing Dong,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MME-Safety基准，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)安全性的细粒度基准。它引入了四维标注框架（风险场景、危害严重程度、图像/文本隐匿等级）和分层评估体系，并在17个SOTA MLLM上进行了零样本评估，揭示了CoT推理会反向加剧安全风险、跨模态隐匿攻击构成防御盲区等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM安全基准缺乏细粒度的意图相关标注，且依赖一维（如二元攻击成功率）评估指标，无法系统分析跨模态隐匿策略如何破坏模型安全对齐，更无法量化危害严重性与防御结构的完整性。

Method: 1）四维标注体系：风险场景（9类）、危害严重程度（0-10分四档）、图像隐匿等级与文本隐匿等级（各分低/中/高三级）；2）数据集构建：对MM-SafetyBench与SIUO进行严格人工筛选（保留559个高质量样本），通过GPT-4o生成不同隐匿等级的文本变体，并采用引导生成、规则设计、人工核验三种策略增扩至3,801个样本；3）分层评估框架：核心指标（RelR/HR/AHS/RefR/OS）评估风险暴露与安全意识，辅助指标（IdR/RsnR/AltR/DIS及TUR/IUR）评估防御结构完整性与模态利用情况；4）在17个MLLM上进行零样本评估，并对比CoT推理轨迹与最终输出。

Result: 1）闭源模型显著优于开源模型，Claude4-Sonnet-T以86.24 OS居首，Qwen3VL-235B以85.11位居开源第一；2）CoT推理反成"推理税"：激活CoT会提升HR与AHS，且最终输出的危害严重程度超过内部推理轨迹；3）模型规模存在"能力诱发脆弱性"：InternVL-2.5-MPO在78B+规模HR与AHS同时上升；4）跨模态协同效应：双高隐匿（H-H）构成语义盲区，中等隐匿组合（如Image-M/Text-H）成为攻击者最优策略；5）RefR与HR强负相关（ρ=-0.97），去除Llama与o4-mini等仅拒绝型异常点后，DIS与RefR相关性从0.53升至0.95。

Conclusion: MME-Safety为MLLM安全评估提供了超越二元拒绝指标的细粒度诊断工具，揭示当前多模态系统面临CoT推理诱发风险、规模扩展引入新漏洞、跨模态语义伪装构成防御盲区三大核心挑战，呼吁构建具备推理感知能力的安全对齐机制，以推动真正有益且无害的多模态系统发展。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show remarkable advancements, their cross-modal capabilities introduce complex vulnerabilities that easily bypass unimodal filters. Existing benchmarks lack fine-grained intent-related annotations and rely on unidimensional metrics, hindering comprehensive robustness evaluation. To address this, we propose MME-Safety, a rigorously verified benchmark featuring a unique four-dimensional annotation schema that categorizes risk scenarios, harm severity, and modality-specific stealth levels. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical evaluation framework to assess fundamental response reliability, actual risk exposure, and the structural integrity of defensive behaviors. Extensive zero-shot evaluations across 17 state-of-the-art MLLMs provide a comprehensive safety profile of current multimodal systems. Our analysis systematically investigates cross-modal input configurations and uncovers safety implications associated with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. These multifaceted findings underscore the urgent need for robust, reasoning-aware safety alignment in the multimodal landscape.

</details>


### [43] [Generative Artificial Intelligence Chatbots for Motivational Interviewing: A Scoping Review From System Design to Intervention Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20902)
*Runze Hu,Jingqi Kong,Yang Yang,Yihang Yang,Jingyao Liu,Haizhou Tang,Shanghang Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇范围综述（scoping review），系统梳理了基于生成式AI（GenAI）的动机性访谈（MI）聊天机器人在系统设计、安全性、MI质量、用户感知及干预效果方面的证据，共纳入48项研究，发现其能提供MI一致且用户感知良好的交互，但对持续行为或功能改变的证据有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI为通过对话系统传递动机性访谈（MI）提供了新途径，但相关证据在系统设计、安全性、MI质量评估及临床转化方面较为分散，缺乏系统性的梳理，亟需对该领域的研究现状进行全面总结以指导未来研究。

Method: 采用PRISMA-ScR范围综述方法，检索9个数据库中2015年1月1日至2026年6月2日期间发表或公开的研究，使用预定义框架提取数据并进行描述性综合分析。最终纳入47份报告（48项研究），分别从系统设计、安全性、MI质量、用户感知和干预结果五个维度进行表征。

Result: 结果显示：20项研究（41.7%）仅关注系统设计而无参与者直接使用，28项（58.3%）涉及直接交互；多数系统为基于文本的非具身聊天机器人，23项（47.9%）包含动态适应能力；安全性报告不均衡；在直接使用研究中，75.0%报告了知情同意或用户教育；62.5%评估了MI质量，结果总体倾向于MI一致；用户对共情、易用性、有用性和使用意愿评价较高；37.5%报告了干预结果，积极发现更多体现在短期动机而非持续行为或功能改变。

Conclusion: 基于GenAI的MI聊天机器人能够提供MI一致且用户感知良好的交互，但关于持续行为或功能改变的证据仍有限。未来研究应加强运行时安全监测、统一MI质量评估标准，并采用具有行为和功能结果的长期对比性设计。

Abstract: Motivational interviewing (MI) is a collaborative approach to elicit autonomous motivation for health behavior change. Generative AI (GenAI) offers new ways to deliver MI via conversational systems, but evidence on their design, assessment, and translation into interventions remains fragmented. This scoping review characterized evidence on GenAI-MI chatbots across system design, safety, MI quality, user perceptions, and intervention outcomes. We conducted a PRISMA-ScR scoping review. Nine datasets were searched for studies published or publicly available from January 1, 2015 to June 2, 2026 that used GenAI to generate MI chatbot responses or counselor utterances. Data were extracted using a predefined framework and synthesized descriptively. Forty-seven reports (48 studies) were included. Twenty (41.7%) focused on system design without direct participant use; 28 (58.3%) involved direct interaction. Most systems were text based and disembodied; 23 (47.9%) incorporated dynamic adaptation. Safety measures were unevenly reported. Among studies with direct use, 21/28 (75.0%) reported informed consent or user education. Thirty (62.5%) assessed MI quality, generally suggesting MI-consistent interactions. User perceptions were favorable, especially empathy, usability, helpfulness, and intention to use, though measures were heterogeneous. Eighteen (37.5%) reported intervention outcomes, mostly after a single session. Positive findings were more consistent for short-term motivation than sustained behavioral or functional change. GenAI-MI chatbots can deliver MI-consistent interactions perceived favorably, but evidence for sustained behavioral or functional change is limited. Future research should strengthen runtime safety monitoring, standardize MI quality assessment, and use longer-term comparative designs with behavioral and functional outcomes.

</details>


### [44] [$μ^2$-Bench: A Multilingual Machine Unlearning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20945)
*Kyomin Hwang,Hyeonjin Kim,Hyunho Lee,Yearim Kim,Yeji Song,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 μ²-Bench，一个针对多语言大语言模型机器遗忘（Multilingual Machine Unlearning, MMU）的综合基准。它通过构建包含12种语言的多语言问答数据集，模拟了记忆、遗忘和评估的完整流程，并在训练语言和保留语言上评估知识遗忘效果，验证了多语言特化方法的有效性，并发现了代码混合输出这一副作用现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大语言模型（LLMs）通过直接训练和跨语言传播（cross-linguistic spread）两条途径获得知识，因此不良信息（如有害内容或私人数据）也会嵌入到训练语言和保留语言中。现有的机器遗忘（Machine Unlearning, MU）评估存在以下不足：1）仅在训练语言上评估；2）仅按语言逐个评估，而未在知识层面整体评估；3）未充分考虑通过跨语言传播获得的知识。这导致无法判断目标知识是否在所有语言中被真正遗忘。

Method: 1. **数据集构建**：使用 Faker 库生成 250 个合成人物档案（每人 20 个属性），通过模板和 GPT-5.4-mini 生成 5,000 个英文 QA 对，然后使用 GPT API 将其翻译成 11 种目标语言（含英语共 12 种），并通过回译（back-translation）验证语义一致性。2. **语言配置**：设计了两种设置（Setting 1 为拉丁语系训练，Setting 2 为非拉丁语系训练），每种设置包含 2 个高资源和 2 个低资源训练语言，以及 2 个拉丁和 2 个非拉丁保留语言。3. **遗忘模拟框架**：200 个档案用于记忆，50 个用于隐私泄露评估；分别在 1%、3%、5% 的遗忘比例下进行测试。4. **评估协议**：使用参考模型（仅在非目标知识上训练）作为理想遗忘的金标准，从三个维度评估——聚合遗忘质量（FQ：概率分数 PS、语义等价分数 SE、隐私泄露）、可分离性质量（SQ：知识可分离性分数 KSS）和通用知识质量（UQ：TyDi QA）。

Result: 实验在 Gemma3-12B 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上比较了四种遗忘方法：Gradient Ascent（GA）、Gradient Difference（GAGDR）、Negative Preference Optimization（NPO）和专为 MMU 设计的 LingTea。结果表明：1. **LingTea 在保留语言上的优势更显著**：在训练语言上 58/84（约 69%）的指标中排名第一，在保留语言上 72/84（约 86%）排名第一，平均优势从 +14.96 扩大到 +27.94。2. **训练语言分析**：Qwen2.5 显示高资源语言遗忘更有效；Gemma3 在不同设置下趋势不一致。3. **保留语言分析**：未发现资源级别或脚本对遗忘效果的一致趋势。4. **代码混合输出现象**：发现 MMU 后模型可能用其他语言回答（如用西班牙语回答英语问题），但语义上仍正确，这表明基于表面形式的指标（如 ROUGE-L）会低估遗忘效果，强调了语义评估的重要性。5. **通用能力保持**：遗忘后 TyDi QA 分数与记忆模型相当，说明模型实用性得以保留。

Conclusion: μ²-Bench 为多语言机器遗忘提供了严格的评估测试平台，证明了多语言特化方法（如 LingTea）的必要性，特别是在跨语言传播导致的保留语言知识遗忘方面。该基准的三个核心贡献——多样化语言集、训练/保留语言分离设置、知识层面评估——共同提供了更全面的 MMU 测量。此外，代码混合输出的发现揭示了基于表面形式指标的评估局限性，并强调了语义评估的重要性。总体而言，本文为多语言场景下的遗忘研究提供了测试平台和深度分析，但仍存在翻译验证缺乏母语者参与的局限性。

Abstract: Undesired information such as harmful content and private data propagates through Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) via direct training and indirect cross-linguistic spread. Multilingual Machine Unlearning (MMU) aims to remove such information, yet its evaluation remains underexplored, leaving unclear whether unlearning truly eliminates target knowledge across all languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce $\mu^2$-Bench, an MMU benchmark that simulates the full pipeline of memorization, unlearning, and evaluation across diverse languages. It 1) spans a broad set of languages, 2) evaluates on both training and hold-out languages, and 3) assesses knowledge as dispersed across multiple languages. We show that successful MMU requires methods that reflect multilingual characteristics, and conduct analysis to provide deeper insights into MMU.

</details>


### [45] [Scaling Forced Alignment to End-User Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21145)
*Lawry Sorenson,Michael Crandall,Eric K. Ringger,Stephen D. Richardson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对语音-文本强制对齐（Forced Alignment）中Viterbi算法的时间和空间复杂度问题，提出两种优化：Hirschberg-Viterbi算法将内存复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，以及基于约束随机游走模型的搜索空间剪枝方法。三小时音频的内存使用从140GB降至5MB，CPU上运行速度为torchaudio的3倍，在超过20分钟的输入上剪枝可再获2倍加速，且在98%以上的测试用例中保持对齐准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的语音处理面临数据稀缺问题，常用方法是对公开音频（如宗教经典、议会录音）进行强制对齐（FA）以挖掘训练数据。然而现有FA实现具有O(n²)的时间和空间复杂度，难以处理长输入序列，无法在终端用户设备上运行。该研究旨在提高FA的可扩展性，使其能处理长序列且适用于普通硬件。

Method: 提出两种优化方法：(1) Hirschberg-Viterbi算法：应用分治法和meet-in-the-middle策略，将Viterbi对齐的内存复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，通过原地计算前向和后向得分找到中点最优对齐后递归处理小子问题；(2) 搜索空间剪枝：将语音-文本对齐建模为受约束的随机游走，基于中心极限定理推导对齐位置的法向近似先验，结合转录精度和召回率的下界估计，使用联合界校正获得置信区间，从而动态确定窗口大小，将搜索空间限制在动态窗口内。

Result: 在Buckeye、CJCLDS-GC和EuroSpeech三个数据集上的实验表明：Hirschberg优化将三小时输入的内存使用从140GB降至5MB，CPU上运行时间为torchaudio的1/3；剪枝在超过20分钟的输入上提供额外2倍加速，在98%以上的测试用例中保持对齐准确性。未剪枝的HV方法在所有数据集上达到100%精确匹配对齐，显著优于Kaldi（67.5%-93.8%）和ctc-seg（0%）。配对的HV+VAD方法在CJCLDS-GC和EuroSpeech上达到98.2%-98.3%精确匹配。该实现可作为开源Python包供研究使用。

Conclusion: 该工作为计算强制对齐中的窗口大小提供了理论基础，以可解释的转录精度为指导，结合现有的Hirschberg算法显著提高了FA的可扩展性。研究希望促进低资源语言社区的研究工作，使社区能够在不需要大量硬件条件的情况下处理自己的数据。局限性包括剪枝方法对数据不可见（与Kaldi基于数据的剪枝相反）、假设语速恒定、以及仅识别单一对齐路径。未来工作可结合两种剪枝策略、建模可变语速，以及修改算法以考虑关键点之间的所有可能路径（类似beam search）。

Abstract: The Viterbi algorithm has been previously used to perform forced alignment of audio to text to mine training data from online resources. However, many existing implementations have quadratic time and space complexity, scaling poorly to long input sequences. We propose two optimizations to address this issue. First, we apply the Hirschberg algorithm to perform the alignment in place using linear memory. Second, we model the alignment between speech and text as a constrained random walk, allowing us to prune the search space with arbitrary confidence while accounting for transcription errors. The Hirschberg optimization reduces memory usage from 140 GB to 5 MB for three-hour inputs while producing identical alignments in one-third the time of torchaudio when both run on a CPU. We achieve an additional 2x speedup with pruning on inputs longer than 20 minutes while preserving alignment accuracy in more than 98% of tested cases.

</details>


### [46] [CoLearn: An Agentic Tutor that Learns its Learner in a Human--AI Co-Learning Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21154)
*Kailai He,Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Runcong Zhao,Yulan He,Jiazheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoLearn 是一个交互式、agentic 的智能辅导系统，通过"人类-AI 共同学习循环"实现个性化辅导。它使用基于软证据的贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT）持续更新学习者掌握程度的估计，并挖掘常见误区，动态生成针对学习者最薄弱主题和反复出现的错误概念的下一道题目。系统还提供可视化界面和盲测 A/B 对比，使个性化过程透明可验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能辅导系统存在三大不足：(i) 经典知识追踪将每个回答简化为对/错的二值信号，丢失了自由文本回答中的诊断信息；(ii) 现有 LLM 辅导系统大多"无记忆"，无法跨会话积累学习者状态；(iii) 适应性调整过程不透明，学习者无法检视系统对其的认知模型。该研究旨在通过连接"答题级诊断—持久化学习者状态记忆—适应性出题"这一闭环，弥补这些缺陷。

Method: CoLearn 包含三个核心组件：(1) 持久化学习者状态记忆——采用软证据贝叶斯知识追踪（soft-evidence BKT），其中 LLM 充当连续观察函数，输出 mastery_evidence ∈ [0,1] 和 evidence_strength ∈ [0,1]，不进行二值化，按公式 p_next = p_cond + o(1-p_cond)·p_transit 进行能力门控的学习转换；(2) 适应性出题——针对学习者最弱主题及最多两个已存误区生成下一题，MCQ 选项直接绑定具体误区作为干扰项；(3) 证据视图——提供实时掌握度可视化、概念雷达图以及内置盲测 A/B 对比界面。系统采用 Docker Compose 单节点容器栈部署，支持 Bedrock Claude Sonnet 4.5 等可切换后端。

Result: 盲测 A/B 评估中，18 名有学科背景的评分者完成 12 组对比，个性化题目在 Biology 学科偏好率 68%、Chemistry 69%（p=0.008，符号检验）；混合能力学习者偏好率更高（74%/77%），强能力学习者较低（73%/66%）。Persona 模拟（隐藏真实掌握度）显示 Adaptive 条件下弱技能命中率 0.72（vs Random 0.55 / Frozen 0.57），掌握度 MAE 最低（0.12 vs 0.16/0.20）。LLM 观察函数与真实掌握度的相关性 r=0.68（按能力层级区分明显，但弱能力层级内仅 r≈0.15）。挖掘的误区匹配 F1≈0.56。可用性问卷（16 人）总体均值 4.0/5，75% 愿意复用。延迟方面，短答题约 32 秒/轮，MCQ 约 7.6 秒/轮。

Conclusion: CoLearn 展示了 LLM 辅导系统如何维护持久化学习者状态记忆、利用该记忆生成个性化题目、并通过实时可视化和内置 A/B 评估暴露适应性过程。其软证据知识追踪记忆可随会话推进收敛，生成器针对最弱主题和误区，证据视图使任何人都可检验个性化效果。作为一个可部署的容器化研究原型，CoLearn 为未来研究"可见、收敛的个性化是否能带来持久、可迁移的学习"这一更困难的问题提供了平台。局限方面：b 端掌握度估计是信念而非直接人类知识测量；BKT 参数为共享先验而非按技能拟合；LLM 观察函数对低表现学习者最不可靠；未验证生成题目是否真正引出目标误区；策略自动自我演化及远迁移学习尚未实现。

Abstract: Good tutoring adapts to the individual: it tracks what a learner knows, notices why they go wrong, and asks the next question that will help most. Most deployed tutoring tools instead serve fixed item banks and treat a wrong answer as a single bit of signal. We present CoLearn, an interactive, agentic tutor that supports an iterative tutoring loop: the learner practises, and the system builds an evidence-grounded memory of the learner's mastery and misconceptions. This memory is updated as evidence accumulates and is used to generate the next personalised question. CoLearn has three components: (i) a persistent learner-state memory that updates per-topic mastery with a soft-evidence variant of Bayesian Knowledge Tracing, where a large language model acts as a continuous observation function; (ii) adaptive question generation that targets the learner's weakest topic and recurring misconceptions; and (iii) an evidence view that makes personalisation visible and testable through live progress visualisation and blind A/B comparison. In blind A/B evaluation, questions conditioned on this memory are preferred over non-personalised ones 68-69% of the time, and in persona simulations with hidden ground-truth mastery the agent's belief converges toward the learner's true mastery.

</details>


### [47] [Not All Irregularity Is Equal: Causally Isolating a Rare Failure Mode in Japanese Morphological Inflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21179)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对日语过去时动词屈折任务，对字符级Transformer模型进行了诊断性与因果性分析。研究发现，尽管两种架构在整体上达到97%以上的准确率，但一个仅占数据不足1%的特定低频不规则子类（Type 4-2：以/e/结尾的词干需在词尾添加促音/gemination/的不规则动词）占据了30–43%的剩余错误，其"差异比"高达34–48倍。受控消融实验进一步表明，仅移除该子类所带来的准确率提升大于移除所有不规则动词的总和，从而在因果层面证明：错误的集中并非由"不规则性"本身驱动，而是由极低频形态模式与特定正字法过程之间的交互作用所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态学生成系统的整体高准确率常掩盖稀有子类中的系统性错误，体现形态学习中的"长尾"问题。已有研究多依赖聚合指标，缺乏对特定低频结构子类的因果性分析。论文以日语过去时屈折为测试平台，将整体准确率视为可能掩盖系统性失败的不充分诊断指标，旨在揭示聚合指标所隐藏的、稀有结构子类的失败模式，并通过因果消融实验验证该子类对泛化能力的真实影响。

Method: 方法包含"诊断"与"因果隔离"两个互补环节。诊断部分：构建平假名形态数据集（3,958个动词），划分为Type 1（Godan）、Type 2（Ichidan）、Type 4-1（/i/+gemination）、Type 4-2（/e/+gemination，37个）、Type 4-3（局部异常）几类；采用两种字符级Transformer架构——PGT（带指针生成拷贝机制的Transformer）和LST（词元分离训练评估的Transformer），在5个随机种子上进行评估；定义六类正字法/音系敏感的错误分类法，并引入"差异比"（DR = 错误占比/数据占比）量化错误集中度。因果隔离部分：对每个架构设置8种受控数据条件（含全部数据、仅规则动词、分别减去4-1/4-2/4-3、以及两两组合的减法），观察准确率变化。显著性通过单侧精确二项检验验证。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）整体表现：两种架构在完整数据集上平均准确率约97%（PGT: 97.17%±0.65；LST: 96.97%±0.82）。（2）错误分布：促音相关错误（漏插与误插）占所有错误的75.5%（PGT）和80.3%（LST），且漏插远多于误插。（3）子类不对称性：Type 4-2占据30.2%（PGT）和43.1%（LST）的全部错误，对应差异比为33.6和47.9（即为其数据占比的34–48倍）；其逐类错误率达43%（PGT）和60%（LST），是Type 1的70倍以上。二项检验以p<10⁻¹⁹级别拒绝"错误与类型无关"的零假设。（4）跨模型一致性：两种架构的错误模式高度一致，包括相同的UNK错误实例。（5）因果消融：对LST，仅移除Type 4-2使准确率从97.73%升至99.75%（+2.02，约相当于错误率下降89%），该增益显著大于移除所有不规则动词（+1.22），也大于所有其他单类或成对移除方案；对PGT，移除Type 4-2仍为最佳单一条件（+1.00）。两系统中，移除所有Type 4均未获得最大增益，表明问题并非"不规则性"本身，而是Type 4-2所特有的结构构型（词干末/e/后的促音）。

Conclusion: 论文得出三点核心结论：第一，神经形态学习中的错误集中并非由不规则性普遍驱动，而是由极低频形态模式与特定正字法过程（此处为词干末/e/后的促音插入）的交互所导致；第二，聚合准确率可掩盖结构性局部失败，需进行细粒度子类分析与差异比度量；第三，形态学评估实践应纳入子类层面诊断，而非仅依赖整体准确率，这对数据高效且具发展心理学合理性的语言模型预训练具有直接意义。未来工作将考察大规模语言模型在零样本条件下是否仍表现出Type 4-2敏感性，并将差异比度量推广至其他形态丰富语言。局限包括：消融实验未做多次重复、仅研究了两种架构与单一形态范式、未能独立分离频率与结构复杂度的贡献。

Abstract: Neural morphological generation systems often achieve high aggregate accuracy on benchmark datasets, yet such performance can conceal systematic errors clustered in rare morphological subclasses. We present an orthography-aware diagnosis of Japanese past-tense verb inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium but as a representational system that encodes morphophonological structure. Using two character-level Transformer architectures evaluated across five random seeds, we show that although both systems exceed 97% aggregate accuracy, a single structurally specific irregular subtype, verbs whose stems end in /e/ and require gemination before the past-tense suffix and make up fewer than 1% of the data, accounts for a disproportionate 30-43% share of residual errors and contributes roughly 34-48x its prevalence to total errors. We then move from diagnosis to causal isolation: controlled ablation experiments show that removing this subtype alone produces larger accuracy gains than removing all irregular verbs combined. These findings indicate that error concentration in neural morphological learning is not driven by irregularity per se, but by the interaction between extreme low-frequency morphological patterns and specific orthographic processes. We argue that morphological evaluation should incorporate fine-grained subclass analysis, and discuss implications for data-efficient, developmentally plausible language model pretraining.

</details>


### [48] [When Better Turns Do Not Make Better Agents: Diagnosing the Gap Between Next-Turn Metrics and Workflow Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21187)
*Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar,Xue-Yong Fu,Gundeep Singh,Karol Chang,Kevin Sanders,Shi Zong,Tania Habib,Julien Bouvier Tremblay,Shayna Gardiner,Harsh Saini,Matthias Lee,Elena Khasanova,Quinten McNamara,Shashi Bhushan TN*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了智能体评估中的一个关键问题：基于"金标准历史"的下一回合评估指标（next-turn metrics）与端到端工作流执行成功之间存在显著差距。研究发现，尽管监督微调（SFT）一致提升了所有模型在文本回合层面的表现，但这些提升几乎不能迁移到自主工作流执行中——所有模型在整体工作流评估下的成功率最高仅10.4%，且没有任何一个模型在综合评判中完成任何需要工具调用的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型智能体的评估普遍采用"下一回合"协议：在每个决策点提供金标准历史，让模型预测下一步动作并与参考比对。然而，这种评估存在两大局限：（1）每次预测都基于正确的历史，早期错误无法影响后续决策；（2）基于参考的指标可能奖励形似但错误的回答。这引发了一个核心研究问题：金标准历史下的下一回合评分提升是否能预测端到端工作流成功的提升？

Method: 研究在多轮客户支持工作流场景下，对比了Qwen3（4B/14B）和Gemma 3（4B/12B）四个模型在SFT前后的表现。训练数据来源于Dialpad专有的130个客户支持工作流，经GPT-5双实例（用户+智能体）生成、Claude-4.5-Opus和Gemini-2.5-Pro双重审核后保留1,027个对话（5,834个训练样本）。评估采用五个互补协议：（1）文本相似度（ROUGE-1）；（2）金标准历史回合成功（LLM评判）；（3）严格工具正确性（精确匹配+参数F1）；（4）闭环重放（使用模型自身的历史+确定性用户模拟器）；（5）整体工作流评判（LLM评判端到端目标完成）。

Result: 结果显示严重的评估-能力鸿沟：（1）文本层面提升显著——SFT使ROUGE-1平均提升24.4点（24.8→49.2），文本回合成功率提升25.2点（33.2%→58.4%）；（2）工具层面提升有限——工具回合成功率仅提升5.9点（17.8%→23.7%），精确调用准确率从3.5%升至8.5%，Gemma 3模型甚至出现负向变化（如Gemma 3-4B的精确调用准确率从3.0%降至0.0%）；（3）端到端工作流表现极差——SFT后最佳结果仅为Qwen3-14B完成8/77（10.4%），而整体LLM评判下没有任何模型完成任何需要工具的工作流。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：金标准历史的下一回合评估不是工作流成功的可靠代理指标。SFT虽然在所有模型上提升了参考匹配的文本和下一动作预测，但工具调用的正确性因指标、规模和模型族而异，端到端完成率仍然很低，整体评估未发现任何成功的工具型工作流。论文呼吁智能体评估应分别报告文本质量、局部动作正确性、工具执行和端到端任务完成，而非将聚合的下一回合表现视为端到端能力的证据。未来工作需开发明确针对错误恢复、状态维护和端到端任务完成的训练方法。

Abstract: Agent models are frequently evaluated one decision at a time, where the model predicts the next action based on the gold interaction history, which is scored against a reference. We investigate whether improvement under this protocol is predictive of improved autonomous workflow execution. We study pre-SFT and supervised fine-tuned (SFT) Qwen3 models at 4B and 14B parameters and Gemma 3 models at 4B and 12B parameters on multi-turn customer-support workflows. We find that SFT consistently improves text-turn success, and that overall next-turn success increases for every model under gold-history evaluation. However, these improvements do not transfer to autonomous workflow execution. Tool-specific gains also vary across metrics and models. None of the four SFT models succeeds under holistic workflow evaluation, with strict trajectory completion reaching at most 10.4% workflow success. Our results show that next-turn evaluation is not a reliable proxy for workflow success, motivating separate reporting of text quality, local action correctness, tool execution, and end-to-end task completion.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Reference-Based Evaluation: Reward Models for Meta-Evaluation of Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21231)
*Ruotian Wu,Bill E. Johnson,Gene Saunders,Osama Hamzeh,Ankit Vadehra,Pascal Poupart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RM-EVAL，一种基于奖励模型的免参考GEC元评估器，在SEEDA基准上达到与GPT-5相当的人类判断相关性；并展示了同一奖励模型通过奖励引导文本生成（RGTG）可改善GEC输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEC评估指标（如M²、ERRANT）依赖参考编辑集，假设参考穷举了所有有效校正，从而会惩罚语法正确且意义保留但措辞不同的校正，与人类判断对齐较差。需要一种免参考、基于人类偏好的评估方法。

Method: RM-EVAL：在SEEDA人类偏好数据上微调Llama3.2-1B和Qwen2.5-1.5B作为奖励模型，使用标准pairwise偏好损失和时序差分损失进行训练，支持全序列及部分序列评分。RGTG：冻结基础GEC生成器，在解码阶段将奖励模型的指数形式与生成器对数似然结合（log P + β·exp(R)），通过FaRMA等在线RLHF式解码方法引导生成。

Result: 在SEEDA五折交叉验证中，基于Qwen的RM-EVAL平均准确率0.84、Kendall相关系数0.62，与GPT-5（0.83/0.63）相当，优于M²（0.17）、ERRANT（0.13）等传统指标。RGTG中FaRMA变体获得最高平均奖励（3.53），且通过Wilcoxon检验显著优于SFT（p=0.030）和ARGS（p=0.038）；GPT-5外部评估显示FaRMA对ARGS胜率68%、对PARGS胜率63%。

Conclusion: RM-EVAL提供了一个统一、轻量、免参考且开源的GEC评估框架，性能接近GPT-5水平；同一奖励模型还可作为生成改进的学习信号，展示了奖励模型在评估与生成增强双重用途上的潜力。局限性是可能继承SEEDA标注偏差，对不同学习者群体或领域的泛化性需要额外偏好标注来重新校准。

Abstract: Reference-based metrics for Grammatical Error Correction (GEC) such as M$^2$ and ERRANT assume that the reference set enumerates all valid edits, and therefore often penalize corrections that are grammatical and meaning-preserving but phrased differently. We introduce RM-EVAL, a reward model trained on human preference data from SEEDA, as a reference-free meta-evaluator that predicts human-like quality judgments at both full-sequence and partial-sequence levels. Beyond evaluation, we show that the same reward model can be used as a learning signal to improve GEC generation via Reward-Guided Text Generation (RGTG), which keeps a base GEC model frozen and performs online, reward-driven decoding. Across SEEDA, RM-EVAL achieves strong agreement with human rankings, and RGTG yields consistent gains in reward and external validation, demonstrating a unified framework for both assessing and enhancing GEC systems without relying on gold references.

</details>


### [50] [When Does Reasoning Help in Machine Translation? A Hierarchical Analysis of LRM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21247)
*Yuxiang Liu,Jiaming Luo,Eleftheria Briakou,Colin Cherry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了大推理模型(LRM)在机器翻译中产生的推理痕迹,探究推理语言、长度和结构对翻译质量的影响,并提出Hierarchical Meta-Summarization (HMS)框架从数据中自动归纳多层级推理模式。研究发现:最佳推理语言具有模型特异性、推理长度与质量呈非单调关系、通用LRM共享"理解/规划—翻译/起草—修正/验证"三阶段高层结构但领域内分配各异。结论是MT中的推理应采取模型感知、长度感知和模式感知的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型(LRM)通过生成中间推理痕迹提升机器翻译质量,但推理痕迹本身(用何种语言推理、推理多长、包含哪些步骤)对翻译质量的影响尚未被系统研究。现有研究多集中于数学或逻辑任务的离散答案分析,缺乏针对MT这一跨语言、效率敏感任务的专用分析方法,且现有结构分析依赖预定义分类法,难以扩展并可能掩盖领域特异性。

Method: 方法包含三个层面:(1) 推理语言分析:定义Reasoning-Language Instruction-Adherence (RLIA)指标,评估模型对推理语言指令的遵循度;(2) 推理长度分析:对每个源文本采样16条推理痕迹按长度排序分箱,采用domain-stratified document-cluster bootstrap分析长度与质量关系;(3) 推理结构分析:提出Hierarchical Meta-Summarization (HMS)框架——先用Gemini 2.5 Flash将原始痕迹分割为原子步骤并抽象,再通过subsampling-based归纳+K-means聚类+LLM分类产生Level-0高层模式和Level-1细粒度子模式。在6个LRM(Qwen-14B/32B、Llama-8B、gpt-oss-20B、Gemma-4-E4B、DRT-14B)上,跨WMT24++、CultureMT、DRT-Literature三个数据集、9个语言对进行评估,使用COMET-22、MetricX、MetricX-QE三个指标。

Result: 三大主要发现:(1) 最佳推理语言是模型特异性的——Qwen系列和Llama-8B偏好中文,获得更高RLIA和翻译质量;gpt-oss-20B和Gemma-4-E4B偏好英文。提出"Cognitive Consistency"概念:模型在最能可靠遵循的语言中推理时翻译质量最佳。(2) 推理长度与翻译质量呈非单调关系——中等长度有益或具竞争力,过长则常导致质量退化(过度思考);显式增加推理effort使长度近翻倍但质量提升有限。(3) HMS揭示通用LRM共享三阶段宏观组织:Understanding/Planning (U/P)、Translating/Drafting (T/D)、Refining/Verifying (R/V)。Level-0推理比率跨领域稳定,Level-1分配和模式-质量关联呈领域特异性。U/P是最稳定地正相关的模式,T/D对口语类领域帮助明显,R/V效果最依赖领域与示例难度。MT专用模型DRT-14B则将诊断与修正融合,采用以评估/批评为核心的工作流。人工验证显示HMS摘要忠实率92%,Level-0标签正确率88%,Level-1标签正确率79%,R/V步骤91%有效。

Conclusion: LRM-based MT中的推理不应被统一鼓励,而应进行模型感知(model-aware)、长度感知(length-aware)和模式感知(pattern-aware)的控制。最佳推理语言因模型而异,需通过RLIA等廉价诊断识别;推理长度应自适应而非单调增加;HMS提供的数据驱动可扩展框架可揭示高层共享结构与领域特异性变体,为阶段特异性推理分析与控制奠定基础。未来工作可扩展至更多模型架构和低资源语言。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models increasingly use intermediate traces for machine translation, but it remains unclear when such reasoning helps or hurts. We analyze reasoning traces across models, languages, domains, and datasets, focusing on reasoning language, length, and structure. We find that the best reasoning language is model-specific, reasoning length has a non-monotonic relationship with quality, and traces exhibit recurring functional patterns. To uncover these patterns, we introduce Hierarchical Meta-Summarization (HMS), a scalable framework that induces coarse- and fine-grained reasoning structures without predefined taxonomies. HMS reveals a shared organization--understanding/planning, translating/drafting, and refining/verifying--alongside domain-specific variation. Our results suggest that MT reasoning should be controlled in a model-aware, length-aware, and pattern-aware manner rather than uniformly encouraged.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Atomic Tokens: Factorizing Syllables for Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21362)
*Nghia Hieu Nguyen,Thai Bao Huynh,Binh-An Dinh-Le,Phu Gia Hoang,Dat Tien Nguyen,Kiet Van Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于音位学的中文和越南语分词器（Phonemic Tokenizer），将每个音节分解为声母、韵母和声调三个音位组件，共享同一上下文位置，无需语料库驱动的词汇学习即可获得极小词汇表（中文112、越南语256）；并基于此构建PhonemicBERT，在两种语言上均取得与或优于现有分词方案的竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词方法（BPE、WordPiece等）将token视为原子符号，忽略了音节内部的音位结构；中文和越南语作为声调语言，其音节可自然分解为声母、韵母、声调，但统计方法可能合并或碎片化音节，无法显式建模音位共享关系。

Method: 1) 音节→IPA转换：越南语通过确定性正字法-音位规则；中文通过短语级Pinyin查找（最长匹配）后转为IPA；2) 音节分解为(声母, 韵母, 声调)三元组，三者共享一个上下文位置；3) 不支持单元通过字符级fallback处理；4) PhonemicBERT使用共享嵌入表+线性投影融合三个组件，并设计Holistic MLM（同时掩码并重建完整三元组，使用三个独立预测头）。

Result: 内在评估：词汇表压缩约202×（中文）和117-977×（越南语），Rényi效率在两种语言上均显著领先，越南语标准字典Fertility恰好为1；下游任务中PhonemicBERT-Zh在受控中文设置下与字符/子词/SubChar方案竞争或更优，PhonemicBERT-Vi在14个越南语任务中的9个上达到最优；同音字替换实验显示对发音保持型扰动具有强鲁棒性（37.5%替换下性能几乎不变）。

Conclusion: 音位分解为声调语言提供了一种紧凑、高效、可解释的原子和统计分词的替代方案，在显著缩小词汇表和参数量的同时保持竞争性下游性能，并天然具备对同音字替换的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Conventional tokenizers represent text as characters or statistically derived subwords, overlooking the internal phonological structure of syllables and often requiring large vocabularies. We introduce \textbf{Phonemic Tokenizer}, a linguistically motivated tokenizer for Vietnamese and Chinese that converts each syllable into IPA and factorizes it into three phonological components: onset, rime, and tone. The three components jointly occupy one contextual position, preserving syllable-level sequence length while enabling representation sharing across phonologically related syllables. Non-phonological and unsupported units are handled through character-level fallback. This deterministic design requires no corpus-dependent vocabulary learning and yields vocabularies of only 112 entries for Chinese and 256 for Vietnamese. Intrinsic evaluation shows that the tokenizer achieves substantially higher Rényi efficiency in both languages, represents every entry in a standard Vietnamese syllable dictionary with a Fertility of exactly one, and generally produces shorter Vietnamese sequences than existing pretrained tokenizers. We further instantiate the tokenizer in \textbf{PhonemicBERT}, which combines factorized component embeddings and reconstructs complete masked syllables using three prediction heads. Under a controlled Chinese pretraining setup, PhonemicBERT-Zh is competitive with or outperforms character, subword, and SubChar alternatives across diverse language-understanding tasks. PhonemicBERT-Vi also achieves competitive or superior results to established Vietnamese and multilingual pretrained models. These results establish phonemic factorization as a compact, efficient, and interpretable alternative to atomic and statistically segmented text representations.

</details>


### [52] [ArenaFlow: From Trajectory Ranking to Hierarchical Credit Propagation for Open-Ended Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21378)
*Qiang Zhang,Ruixue Ding,Fanrui Zhang,Xi Chen,Boli Chen,Shihang Wang,Yinfeng Huang,Yi Zheng,Pengjun Xie,Kaipeng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ArenaFlow提出了一种面向开放域智能体强化学习的分层信用传播框架，通过锦标赛式相对排序生成轨迹级奖励信号，并将其传播至关键推理步骤和可复用技能，实现更精准的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放域智能体RL方法（如成对评估）将丰富的比较反馈压缩为单一的轨迹级奖励，导致两个问题：(1) 关键中间推理步骤的贡献被稀释；(2) 成功行为无法被提炼为可复用技能以指导未来探索。解决"信用稀释"和"经验难以复用"的难题。

Method: 核心方法包含三个层面：(1) 锦标赛式相对排序——通过种子单淘汰赛生成轨迹级偏好信号；(2) 结构化反思评估——评判模型除给出偏好外，还输出三类监督信号（关键成功步骤、可复用策略技能、检索技能的使用归因）；(3) 分层信用传播——步骤级：利用锦标赛深度作为置信度指标，将轨迹级优势加权传播至高置信关键步骤；技能级：从群体使用归因估计技能效用，通过效用感知更新、剪枝和检索维护全局技能记忆。

Result: 在Open-Travel、Open-DeepResearch和DeepResearch Bench三个开放域智能体基准上验证：Open-Travel平均分从ArenaRL的35.9提升至55.4，Open-DeepResearch从54.7提升至69.1；DeepResearch Bench迁移评估达到43.2，超越ArenaRL 5.6分；消融实验验证了步骤信用、技能效用估计、技能记忆和深度加权的各自贡献；人类标注的F1一致性达77.8%。

Conclusion: ArenaFlow通过将比较反馈从轨迹级分解到步骤级和技能级，实现了更有效的开放域智能体优化。关键步骤的精确信用分配提升了样本效率和最终性能，而可复用技能的积累为未来探索提供了策略先验，二者协同发挥作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has substantially improved large language model (LLM) agents in verifiable domains, but remains difficult to apply to open-ended agent tasks, where solutions are diverse and reliable scalar rewards are hard to obtain. Recent pairwise evaluation methods alleviate reward discrimination collapse by replacing pointwise scoring with relative preferences. However, they still compress rich comparative feedback into a single trajectory-level reward, obscuring decisive intermediate steps and preventing successful behaviors from being consolidated into reusable skills. We propose ArenaFlow, a hierarchical credit propagation framework for open-ended agent reinforcement learning. ArenaFlow leverages tournament-based relative ranking to derive trajectory-level reward signals. Each comparison is further equipped with structured reflective evaluation, which reveals three types of supervision: pivotal success steps, reusable strategy skills, and usage attribution of retrieved skills. At the step level, ArenaFlow propagates trajectory-level advantages to high-confidence pivotal steps according to tournament survival depth, enabling more targeted optimization of local reasoning behaviors. At the skill level, ArenaFlow estimates skill utility from group-level usage attribution and maintains a global skill memory through utility-aware updating, pruning, and retrieval. The resulting high-utility skills further serve as policy priors for future exploration. Extensive experiments validate ArenaFlow's effectiveness on open-ended agent tasks.

</details>


### [53] [Omni Demand Understanding: A Benchmark for Contextual User-Intent Inference in Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21392)
*Qi Chen,Yunfei Chu,Haolin He,Yifan Yang,Zihan Liu,Yuxuan Wang,Ziyang Ma,Ruiyang Xu,Meng Gao,Yinsong Yan,Ling Wang,Hui Wang,Wen Huang,Yiheng Chen,Guanrou Yang,Qiuqiang Kong,Jin Xu,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"全模态需求理解"(Omni Demand Understanding, ODU)任务及其评测基准 ODU-Bench，将多模态对话中"是否需要响应"以及"用户真实意图的上下文推断"作为明确的评测目标，区别于以往仅关注回答质量的评测。包含 2,078 个场景，覆盖中文/英文、单轮/多轮、生成/真人录制数据。评测 14 个原生多模态大模型，最强模型(Gemini 3.1 Pro)平均仅 72.6%(音视频)/77.3%(纯音频)，且 11/14 模型在非需求场景的误触发率超 50%，凸显出当前 MLLM 严重缺乏从视觉、声学与对话历史中推断隐式用户意图的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向交互的基准主要评估响应质量，隐含假设用户需求已被正确识别。但现实中用户表达常存在欠指定、含糊、修正、口语不流利、噪声干扰等问题，且类请求语句未必构成实际需求。因此需要将"需求理解"显式化为独立评测维度，衡量模型在多模态与对话上下文中的用户意图推断能力。

Method: 1) 问题形式化：将需求理解拆为五个维度——是否存在需求(检测)、结构化意图与所需上下文(M2 关键点)、时间定位、转写文本、用户画像。2) 评测协议：基于关键点命中率(LLM-as-judge)评估语义，使用 macro-F1 评估检测，IoU 评估定位，编辑距离评估转写，字段准确率评估画像，并单独报告非需求场景的误触发率(FTR)。3) 数据构建：以六维挑战驱动分类法(模态依赖、上下文消歧、意图表达形式、声学环境、需求类型、场景领域)为目标，通过代理式粗到细脚本生成+挑战有效性筛选+多片段时序连续渲染，结合 30 位演员的真人录制，形成 1,347 音视频/731 纯音频场景；所有标注由媒体反推并经人工审核。4) 评估对象：14 个原生音频/音视频 MLLM，含闭源与开源，以 GPT-5.4+ASR 作为文本基线对比。5) 下游验证：通过盲测 A/B 比较，证明引入正确需求标注可显著提升回答质量(85.8% 偏好率)。

Result: 1) 整体能力不足：最强原生模型 Gemini 3.1 Pro 在音视频仅获 72.6%、纯音频 77.3% 平均分；开源最佳 Qwen3-Omni-Think 仅 59.9%/67.4%。2) 强感知≠强理解：Gemini 3.1 Pro 在定位/转写/画像上均超 81%，但 M2(关键点覆盖)仅 63.4%，且 FTR 高达 40.6%。3) 证据通道鸿沟：模型对口头请求的关键点恢复率(76–84%)远高于对视/听/对话历史上下文(<60%)；最强模型仅恢复 44.7% 的上下文关键点。4) 误触发系统性：11/14 模型在非需求场景上 FTR > 50%，含"问题/祈使/抱怨"等强语言信号及"错误来源/错误对象"最难；Seed 2.0 Lite 在纯音频上 M2 高达 78% 但 FTR 达 80.7%。5) 间接/描述型需求恢复最差，跨系统平均低于自身均值 7–8 分。6) 真人录制下领先模型基本稳健，但部分开源模型转写降幅显著(Ming-Flash-Omni 2.0 M4 降 27.3 分)。7) A/B 实验显示注入正确需求信息使回答质量获得 85.8% 偏好率，无需求场景中误答率从 60% 降至 15%。

Conclusion: ODU 首次将"需求理解"确立为多模态交互评测的独立目标，证实当前 MLLM 存在系统性能力缺口：在跨模态/对话上下文推断隐式意图方面严重不足，且过度依赖类请求表层语言而忽略源/对象错误。证据通道分析表明高感知性能并不能保证正确的情境化意图推断，而显式提供正确需求信息能显著提升下游响应质量，表明解决需求理解是构建真正有效多模态助手的关键前提。

Abstract: Natural audio-visual interaction is emerging as an important interface for AI assistants, allowing users to communicate through speech and vision rather than carefully composed text prompts. However, existing benchmarks of interactive capabilities still focus primarily on response quality, leaving a more fundamental question underexplored: can a model correctly infer the user's underlying demand from complex multimodal interaction? Real-world user demands are often underspecified in speech and must be inferred from multimodal cues and dialogue history. This inference is further complicated by ambiguous or disfluent expression and noisy acoustic environments. Conversely, request-like speech may not constitute a demand to the assistant, leading to false triggers. We establish Omni Demand Understanding (ODU) as a distinct multimodal contextual inference problem: given an interaction stream, a model must detect whether a user demand is present and infer intent from multimodal and conversational context. ODU evaluates this capability along five dimensions, covering both single-turn and multi-turn interactions. We construct ODU-Bench using a challenge-driven taxonomy, taxonomy-guided agentic video generation, and human-recorded interactions, followed by media-grounded annotation and human verification. We evaluate 14 native MLLMs. Even the strongest, Gemini 3.1 Pro, recovers only 44.7% of key information that must be inferred from visual, acoustic, or conversational context. Moreover, 11 of the 14 models exhibit false-trigger rates above 50% on non-demand scenarios. These results reveal a systematic capability gap in current MLLMs' ability to infer contextual user demands. We hope ODU can establish the evaluation of a previously underexplored yet essential capability in multimodal interaction: correctly understanding user demands before generating an appropriate response.

</details>


### [54] [Benchmarking Gender Bias in Machine Translation Evaluation Metrics across Occupations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21490)
*Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Giorgos Filandrianos,Wafaa Mohammed,Giuseppe Attanasio,Chrysoula Zerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文基于 GAMBIT+ 的职业平衡子集，评估了 WMT 2026 机器翻译质量评估共享任务中 22 个评分预测系统和多个错误标注系统的性别偏见，发现整体倾向于给阳性（masculine）翻译更高分数，但偏好强度因评估器而异，需从方向、幅度和频率多维度综合衡量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译及其自动评估中存在系统性性别偏好问题。当源文本未指定性别时，阳性与阴性翻译均合理，但 MT 系统和评估指标可能仍表现出偏见。此前 GAMBIT+ 研究已揭示此类问题，但 WMT 2026 需更小但仍具职业覆盖的基准集，且单维度聚合指标不足以全面刻画评估器偏见。

Method: 构建 GAMBIT+ 的职业平衡子集（每个 ISCO-08 职业 3 个样本，共 436 个职业×3=1,308 对），新增德语作为目标语言，共覆盖 7 个英语-目标语言对（AR、CS、DE、EL、IS、RU、UK）。从三个互补维度分析评估器行为：(1) 有符号差异 S_kℓ 衡量方向偏好；(2) 平均绝对差异 A_kℓ 衡量敏感度；(3) 偏好频率衡量一致性。采用 436 职业块自助法（5,000 次重抽样）获取 95% 区间，并使用 Benjamini-Hochberg 校正的双侧 t 检验评估显著性。对错误标注任务，比较阳性和阴性版本被判为无误的比率差异。

Result: 在 148 个系统-语言组合中，131 个呈现统计显著的方向性差异，其中 126 个倾向于给阳性翻译更高评分（S% 最高者 xCOMET XXL 达 5.75%）。平均绝对差异 A% 在德语最小（3.14），冰岛语最大（4.64）。俄语有符号差异最大（S%=2.72）。COMETKiwi22 平均幅度小，但 78.1% 的样本偏好阳性；gemba-poly 61.8% 的样本无差异。错误标注任务中，多数系统对阳性版本判为无误的频率更高（Gemma 4 俄语达 27.8 个百分点，Lexicala 阿拉伯语约 30 个百分点）。职业层面，极端差异与刻板印象一致：助产士、育儿工偏阴性，水管工、猎人偏阳性。fluency2-gemini35-esa 平均绝对差异大（12.69%）但有符号均值接近零，说明正负偏好相互抵消。

Conclusion: 性别偏见在现代 MT 评估系统中依然存在，但无法用单一聚合指标充分描述。效应因评估器、语言和职业而异，最强的职业级差异与既有刻板印象吻合。建议通过方向、幅度和一致性等多维互补指标综合评估评估器偏见。本文引入的职业平衡 GAMBIT+ 子集保留了完整的 ISCO-08 职业覆盖，同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Gender bias remains a persistent concern in machine translation (MT), affecting both generated translations and their automatic evaluation. When a source text leaves a person's gender unspecified, translations may realize that person using masculine or feminine forms, and both MT systems and evaluation metrics may exhibit systematic preferences between these alternatives despite the source providing no basis for such a distinction. We study this behavior in the WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Systems Shared Task using an occupation-balanced subset of GAMBIT+. We consider seven English-source language pairs, six from the original dataset, targeting Arabic, Czech, Greek, Icelandic, Russian, and Ukrainian, and extend the original resource with German. The subset contains 1,308 masculine/feminine translation pairs per target language, with three examples for each of the 436 ISCO-08 occupational groups. We evaluate shared-task submissions and baselines for score prediction and error annotation, examining the direction, magnitude, and frequency of gender-related differences. We find an overall tendency for masculine translations to receive higher scores, as well as differences per occupation following stereotypical gender representations, although the strength and consistency of this preference vary considerably across evaluators and languages. Our results show that gender bias remains present in MT evaluation, but that capturing its extent requires looking beyond a single aggregate measure to complementary dimensions of evaluator behavior.

</details>


### [55] [MIRAGE: Multi-Perspective Creative Language Model Reasoning with Reinforcement Learning Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21554)
*Arash Lagzian,Srinivas Anumasa,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE框架，通过Selector选择最优推理视角（如代数、概率等），Reasoner在选定视角下求解，无需微调基础LLM，在多个推理基准上以更低推理成本实现显著优于CoT和DIPPER的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂数学、科学和逻辑推理任务上表现脆弱，现有方法（CoT、Self-Consistency、DIPPER等）存在提示脆弱、推理路径单一或计算成本过高的问题。受人类认知灵活性（多视角动态切换）的启发，希望让LLM也能灵活切换表征框架以解决难题。

Method: MIRAGE包含两个核心组件：(1) Selector——基于REINFORCE训练的轻量级模块（Qwen2.5-7B），在20个预定义概念推理视角（代数、概率、几何、图论、信息论、博弈论等）中对查询进行排序；(2) Reasoner——按排序依次在选定视角下求解，若置信度达到阈值则提前返回，否则聚合多个视角的答案。Selector仅在已解题例上训练一次，推理时无需修改基础LLM权重。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH500、MMLU-Pro和Game-of-24四个基准上，使用DeepSeek-v3、ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3.7-Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash和Qwen2.5-7B五种模型进行测试。MIRAGE相较CoT最高提升+24.7个百分点准确率，平均仅需不到2次推理调用（四个基准中三个），成本仅为DIPPER(n=5)的1/5。例如Gemini 2.0 Flash在Game-of-24达到99.20%准确率（平均1.13次调用），在MMLU-Pro达到83.75%（平均2.14次调用）。消融实验证实动态选择和聚合机制均为关键贡献。

Conclusion: MIRAGE验证了人类认知灵活性原则可有效迁移至LLM推理，通过在概念视角间动态路由实现了成本-准确率的优越权衡，为可部署、资源感知的推理系统提供了实用路径。未来工作包括自动发现新视角、强化学习在线训练、更丰富的置信度估计及与符号工具的集成。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized artificial intelligence and how human interact with AIs. Despite impressive advancements, LLMs struggle with complex mathematical, scientific, and logical tasks. Inspired by human cognitive flexibility - our ability to dynamically switch mental perspectives - we propose MIRAGE (Multi-perspective Inference-time Reasoning via Agent-Guided Exploration), a novel inference-time creative thinking framework. MIRAGE includes a Selector that prioritizes effective conceptual perspectives (e.g., algebraic, probabilistic) and a Reasoner that sequentially solves tasks until a confident solution emerges, otherwise aggregating multiple perspectives. Tested on GSM8K, MATH500, MMLU-Pro, and Game-of-24 benchmarks, MIRAGE consistently outperforms methods like Chain-of-Thought and diverse prompting ensembles, significantly boosting accuracy with minimal inference overhead, providing a scalable solution for practical applications.

</details>


### [56] [Evaluating In-Context Learning and Retrieval Strategies for Devanagari Post-OCR Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21595)
*Abhishek Bhandari,Gaurav Harit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统评估了大语言模型（3B-32B）在天城文（印地语和马拉地语）OCR后文本纠错中的上下文学习能力，并提出了一种基于字符n-gram BM25相似度的检索策略CharBM25。实验表明，检索策略是决定纠错质量的关键因素，CharBM25在无需GPU的情况下优于领域随机选择和稠密语义检索，且结合12B以上参数的通用LLM可实现可靠的免训练纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OCR后纠错方法对天城文（Devanagari）文字（如印地语和马拉地语）的研究存在空白。虽然大语言模型（LLM）的上下文学习提供了一条免训练的纠错路径，但其在天城文上的效果尚未被探索。此外，传统的稠密语义检索需要GPU资源，且对于OCR错误模式的匹配可能不够精准，因此需要一种轻量级、基于字符错误模式的检索方法。

Method: 1. 评估对象：3B-32B参数规模的开源LLM（如Gemma-3-27B），针对印地语和马拉地语进行OCR后纠错。
2. 检索策略对比：
   - 领域随机选择（domain-random selection）
   - 稠密语义检索（dense semantic retrieval）
   - CharBM25（本文提出）：基于OCR输入的字符n-gram BM25相似度检索示例，以匹配目标测试句的共享错误模式。使用字符trigram效果最佳。
3. 数据集：包含20,000个句子，涵盖五个新闻领域。
4. 评估指标：词错误率（WER）降低的绝对百分点。

Result: 1. CharBM25在印地语上比领域随机选择绝对WER降低2.8-4.0个百分点，在马拉地语上降低2.9-3.8个百分点。
2. 字符trigram一致优于bigram和unigram。
3. 模型规模主导性能：Gemma-3-27B在CharBM25-5下，印地语WER降低55.0%，马拉地语降低33.3%。
4. 少样本增益受容量门控：8B以下模型不可靠地优于OCR基线；马拉地语上3B模型恶化句子多于改进。
5. 马拉地语在所有规模下都比印地语更难纠正，反映了其更高的形态复杂度。
6. CharBM25以极低的计算成本匹配或超过稠密检索。

Conclusion: CharBM25被确立为一种有效、无需GPU的检索策略，能以可忽略的计算成本匹配或超越稠密检索。将CharBM25与12B以上参数的通用LLM结合，可在无需任务特定微调的情况下，为天城文提供可靠、免训练的OCR后纠错。马拉地语的形态复杂性使其比印地语更具挑战性。

Abstract: In-context learning using Large Language Models (LLMs) offers a compelling path to training-free post-OCR correction, yet its effectiveness for Devanagari script remains entirely unexplored. We present the first systematic evaluation of LLMs (3B-32B) for post-OCR correction in Hindi and Marathi, comparing three in-context example retrieval strategies: domain-random selection, dense semantic retrieval, and our proposed CharBM25, which retrieves examples by character n-gram BM25 similarity over OCR inputs to target shared error patterns with the test sentence. Across a 20,000-sentence benchmark spanning five news domains, retrieval strategy is the decisive factor in correction quality: CharBM25 outperforms domain-random selection by 2.8-4.0pp absolute WER on Hindi and 2.9-3.8pp on Marathi, using character trigrams, which consistently outperform bigrams and unigrams. Scale dominates performance: Gemma-3-27B achieves WER reductions of 55.0% for Hindi and 33.3% for Marathi under CharBM25-5. Few-shot gains are capacity-gated: models below 8B do not reliably improve over the OCR baseline, and on Marathi the smallest models (3B) degrade more sentences than they improve. Marathi is persistently harder to correct than Hindi across all scales, reflecting its greater morphological complexity. These findings establish CharBM25 as an effective, GPU-free retrieval strategy that matches or exceeds dense retrieval at negligible computational cost, and show that combining it with a general-purpose LLM of 12B+ parameters delivers reliable, training-free Devanagari post-OCR correction without task-specific fine-tuning. Dataset: this https URL

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking Human-Aligned Evaluation: An Analysis of Semantic Metrics Beyond WER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21663)
*Hritika Sharma,Thibault Bañeras-Roux,Alessandra Pinto,Petr Motlicek,Hyunggu Jung,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Somang Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 HATS-en，一个基于 LibriSpeech test-clean 构建的、包含 1000 个三人比较英语 ASR 评估数据集，系统比较了 WER、CER、BERTScore 和 SemDist 在不同语言模型、层和池化策略下与人类判断的一致性，发现 WER 与人类偏好一致性最差，SemDist 最佳配置优于 CER，但统计学上不显著，呼吁默认报告 CER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WER 是 ASR 领域最常用的评估指标，但它将所有词级别偏差视为同等代价，无法反映人类对转录质量的真实判断。需要大规模人类偏好标注数据来评估和比较自动评估指标。

Method: 构建 HATS-en 数据集（1000 个来自 LibriSpeech test-clean 的三人比较实例，由 4 个不同 ASR 系统转录，119 名标注者参与）。系统性地在多种语言模型（Encoder-based、Qwen、BLOOM 等）、不同层和不同池化策略（Last、Mean、Mean*、Wgt.、Wgt.*）下测试 BERTScore 和 SemDist，使用 Fleiss' Kappa 和 McNemar 检验评估一致性。

Result: WER 与人类判断一致性最低（75.47%），CER 显著更高（86.28%）。最佳 SemDist 配置（Bloom-3b）达成最高一致性，统计上显著优于 WER（p=5.87×10⁻¹⁵），但与 CER 差异未达显著性。BERTSote 优于 WER 但不及 CER。无单一模型在所有设置下最优。

Conclusion: 建议默认报告 CER 作为 ASR 评估的基准指标（兼具可解释性和低成本），并将经验证的语义指标（如 SemDist）作为补充。未来工作应扩展到自发语音领域，并探索 LLM 作为评估器的潜力。

Abstract: Word Error Rate (WER), the most commonly used metric for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR), treats every lexical deviation from the reference as equally costly, regardless of whether it changes meaning.
