Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [85] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bio-MF是一种基于Transformer的潜空间无关、单步MeanFlow框架，用于EEG到fNIRS的跨模态生成，专为混合运动想象脑机接口设计。其核心创新包括：空间-时间交互式4D编码（Interactive 4D）、跨模态无分类器引导（CFG）以及噪声级别门控的FFT正则化。模型可在RTX PRO 6000 GPU上以7.0 ms的速度生成单个fNIRS试次，相比1000步SCDM基线实现857倍加速，同时在下游运动想象分类任务中保持或超越SCDM的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合EEG-fNIRS MI-BCI系统性能优于EEG-only系统，但同时采集两种信号不便。现有的EEG-to-fNIRS跨模态生成方法（如SCDM、TADM）存在两个主要问题：1）依赖多步扩散采样导致推理延迟高，难以满足实时BCI需求；2）迭代精炼过程的缺失会降低生成保真度并引入非生理性伪迹。本文旨在解决速度与保真度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 1. 架构：基于Transformer的潜空间无关框架，采用4块共享主干+独立4块u-head和v-head的设计，隐藏维度384，8个Transformer块。
2. 信号空间x预测：直接在原始fNIRS信号空间预测干净信号（而非噪声残差），避免VAE压缩重建。
3. MeanFlow速度监督：将信号空间输出转换为平均速度场u=(z_t - x_u)/t，使用MeanFlow恒等式构建监督复合速度V。
4. 1-NFE推理：单次网络评估即可从噪声端点t=1生成t=0的干净fNIRS。
5. 空间-时间交互式4D编码：使用连续(x,y,z,t)坐标表示EEG电极和fNIRS通道，引入空间-时间傅里叶特征的乘法交互项r_i=(W_s s_i)⊘(W_t q_i)。
6. 多速率分词：EEG(160Hz)和fNIRS(10Hz)按匹配的物理时间窗口分块对齐。
7. 跨模态CFG：v-head在r=t时评估有条件和零条件EEG速度，构建导向速度目标v_g=v_t+(1-1/ω_int)(v_c - v_u)。
8. 两阶段训练+噪声门控FFT正则化：Stage I(150 epochs)仅优化L_MF；Stage II(90 epochs)在t<0.4低噪声状态下施加FFT幅度谱对齐损失，抑制高频伪迹。
9. 实现细节：RTX PRO 6000 GPU，总训练8小时，240 epochs。

Result: 1. 数据集：Dataset 1为29名受试者的配对EEG-fNIRS数据（30通道EEG+36通道fNIRS）；Dataset 2为100名受试者的64通道EEG-only数据，用于零样本跨设备评估。
2. 下游分类性能（ACC）：Dataset 1上EEG+合成fNIRS相比EEG-only提升HbR 3.37pp、HbO 4.15pp；Dataset 2零样本下仍提升HbR 2.98pp、HbO 2.50pp。Bio-MF在几乎所有Dataset 1生成模态ACC比较中超越SCDM（HbR融合平均仅低0.09pp）。
3. 零样本性能：Dataset 2上Bio-MF在所有ACC/SPE/PRE/SEN指标上均优于SCDM，HbR/HbO融合ACC分别领先1.58/1.97pp，且标准差更小。
4. 生成延迟：单试次生成仅需7.0 ms（RTX PRO 6000），相比SCDM的6.0 s（1000步）实现857倍加速。
5. 消融实验：移除Interactive 4D、CFG或FFT正则化均导致性能下降；全局FFT（非门控）甚至比无FFT更差，验证了两阶段噪声门控策略的必要性。
6. 生理可视化：空间对应性、HRF曲线和头皮地形图均显示合成fNIRS保留了真实fNIRS的主要激活模式和低频趋势。

Conclusion: Bio-MF通过潜空间无关的单步MeanFlow生成实现了低延迟、高保真的EEG-to-fNIRS合成，成功解决了速度-保真度权衡问题。Interactive 4D编码、跨模态CFG和噪声门控FFT正则化三者协同工作，在保持毫秒级推理速度的同时保留了任务相关的血液动力学结构。7.0 ms的生成延迟使其适用于实时混合MI-BCI反馈场景。局限在于：零样本评估仅基于EEG-only Dataset 2的解码性能而非通道级合成-真实一致性；训练数据来自单一配对数据集，可能限制神经血管对应模式的多样性；合成信号与真实信号仍存在局部幅度差异。未来工作应探索配对的跨设备EEG-fNIRS数据集、更广泛的受试者和范式、不确定性感知生成以及用户中心的闭环验证。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [86] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究测试时多智能体通信机制，发现通过共享工作空间进行信息交换的智能体团队在需要新颖问题解决、算法优化和机器学习工程等开放性任务中，能显著优于同等计算资源下的独立并行尝试。关键发现是"经验证的进展共享"机制——当智能体能够客观评估中间进展时，团队可以将最小求和转化为求和最小值，实现指数级分离。在ARC-AGI-3上，5个通信智能体团队的解题率相当于33个独立智能体；在多边形拼装任务上刷新了0.945的最优记录；在MNIST分类器压缩上以1957字节击败了2461字节的人类最佳方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统主要采用独立并行采样后聚合的策略（如best@k），忽略了科学发现本质上依赖协作迭代的本质。先前多智能体辩论等工作结果参差不齐，缺乏对通信何时有效的清晰诊断。本文旨在通过控制性实验，揭示测试时通信在何种条件下能带来增益，以及其底层机制是什么。

Method: 设计最小化的测试时通信协议：相同智能体在共享容器中通过追加式通信日志、共享评分板和原子槽位所有权机制进行异步通信，无预定义角色或中心编排器。核心对比为team@k（k个通信智能体的最佳结果）与best@k（k个独立智能体的最佳结果），在相同模型、推理配置和每智能体资源下进行。在ARC-AGI-3（Claude Sonnet 4.6）、Frontier-CS多边形拼装（Sonnet 4.6和Opus 4.6）和MNIST分类器压缩（GPT-5.6 Sol）三个任务上评估，并辅以Terminal-Bench 2.0作为反例。提出"经验证的进展共享"理论模型，证明在多阶段可验证任务中通信能带来指数级优势。

Result: ARC-AGI-3上：team@3匹配13个独立智能体的解题率（4.3倍），team@5匹配33个（6.6倍），乘数随k增长。LP85游戏从64次单智能体试验零成功率提升至team@5的65%。多边形拼装：3小时Sonnet团队达到0.945，超越此前最优0.894；72小时长时程下Opus团队达0.922。MNIST压缩：4智能体团队96小时产出1957字节分类器，超越2461字节的人类最佳方案，比best@4的3160字节小38%。Terminal-Bench 2.0上team@2优于单智能体但未超越pass@2，验证了可验证反馈的重要性。通信存在协调税（初期落后），但足够计算下能反超。

Conclusion: 测试时通信能产生超越简单分治并行化的累积进展，前提是存在智能体可访问的验证器来客观评估中间进展。当反馈密集且具有区分度时，通信将"最小求和"转化为"求和最小值"，使团队能在每个阶段使用不同智能体的突破。多智能体系统设计应重视通信协议设计，同时通信增益并非无条件——计算不足或缺乏可验证进度信号时，独立智能体可能更优。未来工作应探索角色分配、模型异质性和通信拓扑对效果的影响。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [87] [EnSol: an environment-aware graph neural network for molecular solubility prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21151)
*Thao Nguyen,Saman Shafaei,Zhengyi Zhang,Huimin Zhao,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EnSol，一种环境感知的概率图神经网络框架，用于跨溶剂和温度的分子溶解度预测。该模型将溶质和溶剂建模为分子图，通过交叉注意力捕获二者相互作用，并利用FiLM机制将温度作为连续环境变量融入溶剂表征，最终通过混合密度网络输出完整的溶解度分布。在SolProp和Leeds基准以及实验验证中均优于现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子溶解度是药物开发、配方设计和工艺选择中的关键性质，但实验测量成本高、样本稀疏。现有计算模型存在三大局限：①固定溶剂假设或静态特征表示，无法捕捉溶质-溶剂间的特定交互；②将温度视为简单输入变量而非连续环境调制因子；③输出确定性点预测，无法反映实验测量的不确定性和多模态行为。

Method: EnSol框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 基于AttentiveFP的GNN编码器分别对溶质（2层）和溶剂（1层）进行非对称图编码，原子特征12维、键特征6维；(2) 4头交叉注意力机制耦合溶质与溶剂的节点级表征，建构上下文相关的交互特征；(3) 温度通过RBF扩展（10个中心位于训练温度分位数）+单调外推特征编码为12维向量，经两层MLP生成FiLM的scale和shift参数，对溶剂图嵌入进行仿射调制（scale约束在(0,2)）；(4) 混合密度网络（K=3高斯分量）输出完整溶解度分布，提供预测均值和分解的偶然不确定性+混合不确定性。采用Adam优化器（lr=1e-4），batch size 32，基于BigSolDB（100,570条测量，1,375个溶质，70种溶剂，243-426K）按溶质级别90:10划分，三个随机种子训练。

Result: 在SolProp基准上，EnSol的Spearman相关系数达0.876±0.005（RMSE=0.824±0.032），显著优于FASTSOLV（0.509）和Vermeire模型（0.569）；在Leeds基准上达到0.602±0.013（RMSE=0.944±0.017），优于Vermeire（0.245）并匹配FASTSOLV。溶剂排序任务中top-1召回率0.61（vs FASTSOLV 0.35），top-3召回率0.85（vs 0.56）。实验验证（10种溶质×10种溶剂，100对）获得Spearman=0.715±0.035（RMSE=0.699±0.062），优于FASTSOLV（0.31）和Vermeire（0.595）。消融实验表明AttentiveFP编码器和交叉注意力贡献最大（各使Spearman降低0.088），其次为FiLM温度调制和MDN头。迁移学习显示在数据有限时预训练带来更明显收益，但在ESOL和AqSolDB上的差异均未达统计显著。模型在结构异常的膦烷和氯噻吨酮等罕见化学类型上表现较弱。

Conclusion: EnSol通过联合建模溶质结构、溶剂结构和温度依赖的环境上下文，在两个独立外部基准和实验验证中均展现出优于现有最优模型的溶解度预测和溶剂排序能力，预测分布能够反映实验不确定性。预训练表征对数据有限的水溶解度预测有一定迁移价值。未来工作可结合自动化实验、主动学习和闭环溶剂优化，构建持续改进的溶解度模型。当前局限包括：机理可解释性有限、对训练数据覆盖范围的依赖、以及在稀疏采样区域（罕见溶质、极端温度、混合溶剂）的预测置信度较低。

Abstract: Molecular solubility directly affects key aspects of molecular development such as reaction feasibility, formulation performance, separation efficiency, and solvent selection. However, experimental measurement across solutes, solvents, and temperatures remains costly and sparsely sampled. Existing computational models often rely on fixed-solvent assumptions, deterministic formulations, or simplified representations of solute-solvent interactions, limiting their ability to capture complex molecular interactions, continuous temperature effects, and experimental uncertainty. Here, we introduce EnSol, an environment-aware probabilistic framework for molecular solubility prediction. EnSol represents the solute and solvent as molecular graphs and learns separate representations for each before bringing them together through cross-attention to capture solute-solvent interactions. Temperature is incorporated directly into the solvent environment through feature-wise modulation, and a mixture density network predicts full solubility distributions to capture both temperature-dependent behavior and experimental uncertainty. On the independent SolProp and Leeds benchmark datasets, EnSol achieved Spearman correlations of 0.876 and 0.601, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art solubility prediction models across both benchmarks. Beyond computational benchmarking, experimental validation across chemically diverse solute-solvent pairs showed that EnSol maintained strong predictive performance and supported reliable solvent ranking, achieving a Spearman correlation of 0.715. These results show that EnSol can support reliable solubility prediction and solvent selection across diverse chemical systems while accounting for predictive uncertainty.

</details>


### [88] [SWE-Proof: Can Language Models Resolve Real-World Issues with Machine-Checked Proofs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21190)
*George Ma,Benjamin Mikek,Haoyu Li,Ferhat Erata,Yuhao Zhang,Zeren Shui,Behrooz Omidvar Tehrani,Jun Huan,Murali Krishna Ramanathan,Somayeh Sojoudi,Hao Zhou,Anoop Deoras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SWE-Proof 基准与 Benchproofer 构建流水线，将 SWE-bench Verified 中 500 个真实 GitHub issue 转化为带有形式化规约、可验证实现和机械证明的实例，并用三种验证后端（Nagini、Velvet、Lean）保障"验证通过则问题被解决"的可追溯性，从而以形式化验证取代隐式测试作为代码正确性的判定标准。实验发现：测试套件漏检严重（1/4 至 1/2 通过测试的补丁仍可被反例驳倒），结构化自然语言规约无法弥补；提供正确规约可将解析率从 85% 提升到 95%，但让模型自行生成规约并无收益（仅 62% 通过审计），主要失败模式是"忠实性"——规约只约束了部分行为而将其余留为空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 代码生成评估主要依赖隐藏测试套件作为正确性判定标准，但测试天然不完备且易受记忆化影响。形式化验证虽能解决这两点，但现有工作仅覆盖小规模或独立任务，对涉及大型代码库、且以模糊自然语言表述意图的真实 issue 无能为力。亟需一套在真实仓库规模上、向 LLM 施加形式化验证判官、并能系统化衡量规约合成质量的基准与方法。

Method: Benchproofer 流水线核心包括：(1) 规约合成，将自然语言 issue 转为带前置/后置条件的形式规约；(2) 环境公理化，对未修改的调用函数以最弱必要性质做公理化摘要，验证仅针对修改部分；(3) 验收门控，包括机械化验证、判别（前修复实现必须失败）、等价性（实现与补丁语义一致）、公理健全性（模糊测试与对抗审计）以及揭示性（视图不泄漏修复）等十三项检查；(4) 端到端可追溯性，建立"实现验证 ⇒ 补丁解析"的链式声明，并通过对应映射将每个形式构件与仓库代码绑定。三种验证后端分别为带契约注解的 Python（Nagini/SMT）、Lean 内的命令式 DSL（Velvet/SMT）、纯 Lean 核检查证明。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 全 500 实例上以 Opus 4.8 与 GPT-5.5 评测：(1) 解析通过测试的补丁中 26.8% 可被反例审计驳倒，结构化自然语言规约 EARS 并不能缩小此差距；(2) 提供正确形式规约使解析率从 85.0% 升至 96.2%（Opus，Nagini），从 81.2% 升至 95.3%（GPT-5.5），平均提升 11–14 个百分点；(3) 让模型自行合成规约则无明显收益，规格合成设置中仅 49.2%–69.2% 通过五属性审计；(4) 主要失败为"忠实性"（覆盖率不足）：在 42.5% 的实例上失败，且在解析成功与失败组间差距最大（差 38–47 个百分点）；(5) SWE-bench Pro 上的方法可迁移（92% 实例在所有后端下合格），但较大任务对模型的相对优势缩小，对较弱模型来说，提供规约就不再优于纯定位提示。

Conclusion: 本文证明在真实代码库规模上，向 LLM 接入形式化验证判官在原则上是可行的，而当前主要瓶颈在于自动合成对真实 issue "忠实"的形式规约——这被确立为一项具体且可度量的开放问题。所释放的 Benchproofer 流水线、SWE-Proof 语料以及验证/审计流程为后续工作提供了可复现的基线和可重用的对抗攻击套件，但同时也警示：记诵式训练、跨任务难度假设、以及把"验证通过"等同于"语义正确"的解读，都应避免。

Abstract: Ensuring the correctness of LLM-generated code is a core challenge for modern software engineering. Benchmarks for agentic code generation check correctness with held-out test suites, which are inherently incomplete and increasingly susceptible to memorization. Formal verification avoids both problems, but existing work covers only standalone tasks whose specifications are given as input, not real issues, which touch large repositories and state intent in vague natural language. We present Benchproofer, a pipeline that turns a coding task with a known correct patch into a formally verified one: it writes a specification for the new code, summarizes the existing functions that code calls with axioms, and admits an instance only after mechanical and adversarial gates agree. Applying it to SWE-bench Verified yields SWE-Proof, 500 real issues whose correctness is formally verified rather than tested, and it extends to SWE-bench Pro. Across two frontier models, verification catches what tests miss: a quarter to a half of test-passing patches admit counterexamples, which a structured natural-language specification does not fix, while a correct formal one lifts resolution from 85% to 95% for Opus 4.8. Writing that specification is the hard part: models that must write their own gain nothing over an unaided baseline, and only 62% of their specifications pass our audit. The usual failure is faithfulness, a specification that constrains part of the required behavior and leaves the rest free. Specification quality still tracks the outcome, failing on 89% of unresolved instances against 47% of resolved ones, making faithful specification synthesis a concrete open problem.

</details>


### [89] [Deep Reinforcement Learning with Buffered Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21327)
*Mohammad Alipour-vaezi,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep-BQRL，一种基于下缓冲分位数（lower-buffered quantile）目标的无模型分布式深度强化学习框架，用于风险敏感决策。它通过学习条件回报分位数、构建缓冲动作分数并利用集成不一致性引导探索，将分位数敏感控制扩展到神经函数逼近，适用于小规模表格问题之外的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 风险敏感RL中，点分位数目标对回报分布的小扰动极为敏感，且精确的分位数规划需要计算昂贵的分布优化。2. 现有的基于下缓冲分位数的方法（如UCB-BQRL）依赖模型和显式回报分布规划，仅适用于小规模表格问题，无法扩展到大规模状态-动作空间。3. 因此需要一种无模型的神经函数逼近方法，在保持分位数敏感决策可解释性的同时，避免显式转移模型估计和回报律规划。

Method: Deep-BQRL采用三项近似：(1) 将轨迹信息压缩为增广输入 X_h=(h, S_h, C_h)（阶段、状态、累积奖励），处理分位数决策的历史依赖性；(2) 维护 M≥2 个独立的分布式评论家，每个估计 K 个固定分位数水平 η_j=(j-1/2)/K 下的条件剩余回报分位数，使用分位数回归Huber损失训练；(3) 通过对 [τ-β, τ] 区间内学习分位数的加权聚合构建缓冲动作分数 B̂_m(x,a)，并取集成均值作为策略选择依据。探索时通过各评论家缓冲分数的标准差 σ_B 引导不确定性高的区域。目标网络采用软更新。该方法不需要显式转移模型或回报分布规划。

Result: 在资产出售（最优停止）问题上，Deep-BQRL在τ=0.1和τ=0.9下的累积点分位数策略间隙均小于PPO和TRPO（如τ=0.9时分别为92.78 vs 173.51 vs 221.36），但UCB-BQRL仍保持最小间隙（0.79）。学到的停止策略随目标分位数变化而呈现出可解释的风险敏感行为（小分位数接受更低报价，大分位数等待更高报价）。在Slippery FrozenLake上，Deep-BQRL在5000回合后奖励接近基于模型的方法（0.627/0.687/0.673），显著优于PPO（约0.51-0.53）和TRPO（接近零）；τ=0.5时累积点分位数间隙排名第二（400.0 vs UCB-BQRL的9.67），优于PPO（3313.33）和TRPO（5000.0）。

Conclusion: Deep-BQRL作为一种实用的分位数敏感RL方法是可行的，但并非基于模型规划的全面替代方案。它避免了显式转移模型估计和回报律规划，并展示了与目标分位数直接相关的可解释风险敏感行为。点分位数性能与期望回报性能需分开评估，因为前者评估贪婪策略而后者包含探索奖励。在二值回报环境下（如FrozenLake），不同目标分位数不一定产生不同最优策略，且一旦成功率超过0.5，中分位数度量将无法继续区分改进。缓冲宽度β和分位数数量K的选择对性能的影响仍需进一步敏感性分析。未来工作应在Atari等高维离散控制基准上测试可扩展性，并系统研究超参数敏感性。

Abstract: Quantile-based reinforcement learning provides an interpretable approach to risk-sensitive decision-making by optimizing a prescribed quantile of the cumulative-return distribution. Despite this appeal, learning under a point quantile objective is challenging: quantiles can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, and exact quantile-sensitive planning requires computationally demanding distributional optimization. Lower-buffered quantiles alleviate the former difficulty by averaging neighboring quantiles immediately below the target level, providing a smoother surrogate while preserving the underlying point-quantile objective. Existing methods based on this principle, however, remain model-based and rely on explicit return-law planning, limiting their applicability beyond small tabular problems. We develop Deep-BQRL, a model-free distributional reinforcement-learning framework that extends buffered-quantile learning to neural function approximation. The method learns conditional return quantiles directly from sampled transitions, constructs buffered action scores from the relevant region of the learned quantile function, and uses ensemble disagreement to guide exploration. An augmented input representation allows the learned policy to respond to trajectory information without explicitly reproducing the quantile-state recursion required by exact planning. Experiments on an asset-selling optimal-stopping problem and slippery FrozenLake compare Deep-BQRL with model-based UCB-BQRL and tabular PPO and TRPO implementations. In asset selling, Deep-BQRL attains smaller mean cumulative point-quantile policy gaps than PPO and TRPO at the reported target levels, while UCB-BQRL retains the smallest gaps. The learned stopping decisions also vary with the target quantile, providing an interpretable illustration of the method's risk-sensitive behavior.

</details>


### [90] [Knowledge-Graph-Augmented Chronos-2 for HEC-RAS Surrogate Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21381)
*Edward Holmberg,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KG-Chronos-2模型,将冻结的Chronos-2时间序列基础模型与水文工程知识图谱耦合,用于HEC-RAS水位高程(WSE)的代理预测。在4675个断面、71个河段的固定基准上,KG-Chronos-2的RMSE为0.246970,较冻结Chronos-2降低14.13%,较DCRNN-style模型降低29.38%,较GeoFNO降低39.54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEC-RAS数值时间步进计算成本高昂,促使研究者开发数据驱动代理模型用于近短期预测。然而,传统监督模型(GeoFNO、DCRNN、LSTM)需要大量本地训练且难以利用外部先验。本文探究将预训练时间序列基础模型(Chronos-2)与原生水文工程知识(断面归属、河段邻接、水力属性、输入调度)耦合,能否提升代理预测性能。

Method: KG-Chronos-2采用三阶段架构:(1) 精确状态残差解码——对WSE增量做PCA降维(K=256),冻结Chronos-2预测潜在增量,加上精确当前状态yt重建;(2) 图条件历史检索——构建14维河段描述符和7个输入通道,在2,353个源训练起点中用欧氏距离检索8个最近邻,反距离加权融合类比轨迹;(3) 输入对齐残差校正——通过互相关选择每个河段-通道的最佳响应滞后(0-168h),用35维特征(过去/未来输入、状态、静态属性、基线预测)的岭回归做导通校正(门控仅应用于27.35%的河段-超前对)。增量乘数α_r和门控g_rh均基于源验证集校准,所有项目特定参数在2008年模拟上拟合。

Result: 在64个24小时窗口(2011和2002模拟)、4675个断面、71河段上,六个系统的EB RMSE(原生WSE单位):持续性0.492611、残差LSTM 0.483055±0.005266、循环GeoFNO 0.408510、水力DCRNN-style 0.349729±0.012500、冻结Chronos-2 0.287613、KG-Chronos-2 0.246970。KG-Chronos-2较冻结Chronos-2的配对RMSE差异为-0.040643,95%分层自举区间[-0.075177,-0.016317];在活跃窗口RMSE(0.351510)和最终超前RMSE(0.434783)上均最低。冻结Chronos-2本身已优于所有本地训练神经网络对比方法。

Conclusion: 将冻结时间序列基础模型与水文工程知识图谱耦合,能显著提升HEC-RAS代理预测精度。方法将时间预测与项目特定适应分离,重用预训练权重,将项目特定学习集中在状态表示和知识耦合层。结果支持在共享几何基准上采用预训练时间预测器进行暖启动预报的设计。局限包括:仅两个评估事件、固定基准可迁移性证据有限、上游拟合量的源校准共享可能使门控标定偏乐观;未来需评估缺失状态、不确定输入、新几何和物理单位验证。

Abstract: We investigate whether coupling a time-series foundation model to hydraulic project knowledge improves surrogate forecasting of HEC-RAS water-surface elevation (WSE). We present KG-Chronos-2, which combines a frozen Chronos-2 predictor with exact-state residual decoding, graph-conditioned historical retrieval, and input-aligned correction. We compare the method with persistence, a residual LSTM, project-conditioned recurrent GeoFNO, a hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and frozen Chronos-2. Task-specific fitting uses the 2008 simulation. Evaluation covers 64 fixed 24-hour windows from the 2011 and 2002 simulations at 4,675 cross sections in 71 reaches on a shared geometry. KG-Chronos-2 achieves event-balanced root-mean-square error 0.246970 in native WSE units. It reduces RMSE by 14.13% relative to frozen Chronos-2, 29.38% relative to the hydraulic DCRNN-style model, and 39.54% relative to recurrent GeoFNO. The 95% hierarchical-bootstrap interval for its event-balanced RMSE difference from frozen Chronos-2 is [-0.075177, -0.016317]. KG-Chronos-2 also achieves the lowest active-window and final-lead RMSE among the six completed systems. These results support coupling a frozen temporal predictor to project knowledge for warm-start HEC-RAS forecasting on the fixed benchmark.

</details>


### [91] [OneBid: A Unified Auto-Bidding Foundation Model for Diverse oCPX Advertising Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21550)
*Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Yitian Li,Pengfei Lv,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OneBid是首个面向oCPX广告的统一自动出价基础模型，通过预训练+后训练两阶段范式替代传统的"一场景一模型"流水线。在快手全面部署后，整体ADVV提升+2.2%，ROAS场景峰值+13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业oCPX广告包含注册、激活、购买等异构转化场景，传统做法为每个场景单独训练模型，导致流程碎片化、工程冗余，且未能挖掘跨场景建模潜力。受LLM等基础模型跨任务泛化能力启发，探索能否为自动出价构建统一的"基础模型"。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1)预训练阶段——基于Decision Transformer，将单一RTG扩展为RTG（转化价值）+CTG（成本比率）双原子控制信号；采用序列级MoE（S-MoE）架构，共享专家编码跨场景共性知识，路由专家按oCPX场景身份捕获场景特异性模式，保持每请求激活计算量固定；训练目标为下一动作预测+辅助Q/V价值预测（IQL风格期望回归）。(2)后训练阶段——设计可控奖励系统与可扩展critic学习，提出CROP（Critic-guided Relative Offline Policy optimization）：采样候选出价动作组，用critic进行组内相对评分优化策略，配合KL约束限制策略偏移，避免在线探索风险。

Result: 离线实验：模型规模从4M扩展到0.5B、数据从1M扩展到70M时，NAP损失呈幂律下降（$\mathcal{L}(N,D)\approx 8.5\times10^{-4}N^{-0.24}D^{-0.84}$，R²=0.82）；S-MoE在匹配激活参数量下优于Dense；CROP的Q值提升（1.90%）显著优于AWR（0.12%）和GAS（0.06%）。延迟上，S-MoE比token级MoE快13×（1.71ms vs 22.18ms），峰值内存降低约40%。在线A/B（33%流量、≥5天）：Form Submit场景中预训练OneBid比DT基线+1.0% ADVV/+1.7pp VR，后训练后+1.9%/+2.8pp；跨场景累计Purchase +2.5%、ROAS +13.1%、Activation +1.8%、Leads Submit +2.3%；60M模型推理延迟52ms与10M DT基线40ms可比。

Conclusion: OneBid验证了基础模型范式在工业自动出价中的可行性：通过RTG/CTG双信号解耦多目标控制、序列级MoE实现延迟友好的容量扩展、CROP实现安全的离线策略改进。自2026年5月在快手全面部署，为oCPX广告带来+2.2%整体ADVV增益。未来可扩展至 pacing、预算分配、拍卖机制设计及与检索/排序的联合优化。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is central to computational advertising, where strategies must maximize advertisers' conversion value under economic constraints. It has evolved from rule-based controllers to reinforcement learning and generative methods such as Decision Transformer (DT). Yet these methods increasingly mismatch the prevailing optimized cost-per-X (oCPX) paradigm, which spans heterogeneous scenarios (e.g., registration, purchase), each served by a separate model, leading to fragmented pipelines and underexploring cross-scenario modeling. Inspired by foundation models like LLMs, unifying these oCPX scenarios into one model raises three challenges: multi-objective control, scalable capacity under strict latency, and safe offline policy improvement. We present OneBid, a unified auto-bidding foundation model that learns a reusable backbone from heterogeneous oCPX logs and adapts it to scenario-specific deployments via offline post-training. Building on DT, OneBid extends single Return-to-Go conditioning to two atomic signals, Return-to-Go for conversion value and Cost-to-Go for cost ratio, plus value-aware regularization on next-action prediction. To absorb distributional heterogeneity, we design a sequence-level Mixture-of-Experts architecture, where shared experts encode cross-scenario knowledge and sparsely-routed experts capture scenario-specific patterns at low latency, yielding consistent scaling with model size and data. During post-training, we align the backbone with scenario preferences via Critic-guided Relative Offline Policy optimization (CROP): a learned critic scores candidate actions group-relatively, avoiding the unsafe online exploration of GRPO-style fine-tuning while constraining policy shift to reduce OOD risk. Validated via online A/B tests and fully deployed at Kuaishou, OneBid delivers an overall +2.2% ADVV gain on oCPX Ads, peaking at +13.1% in the ROAS scenario.

</details>


### [92] [On Repulsive and Attractive Teachers: Separating Correctness from Behavior in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21561)
*Anton Baumann,Akmal Ashirmatov,Leo Schmidt-Traub,Frederike Lübeck,Jonas Hübotter,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自蒸馏（self-distillation）中"吸引型"和"排斥型"教师信号对模型行为与正确性的差异化影响。作者将吸引型自蒸馏（向给定正确答案的特权教师靠拢）与排斥型自蒸馏（远离特权教师）分离开来，发现两者都会引发强烈的、方向相反的行为偏移。论文进而提出对比型自蒸馏（contrastive self-distillation），结合"向正确解教师靠拢"与"远离错误解教师推离"，以抵消共享的行为偏移，保留与正确性直接相关的token级信号。该方法无需GRPO辅助即可稳定提升推理性能，适用于非思考模式、仅指令模式及已启用思考模式的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线自蒸馏（on-policy self-distillation）通过让模型基于特权信息（如正确答案）生成教师分布，并将其蒸馏回学生模型，提供密集的token级监督信号。然而，特权信息不仅改变教师"知道什么"，还会改变其"行为方式"（如抑制探索、变得更简洁自信），这导致正确性相关的学习信号与意外的行为偏移纠缠在一起。现有方法（如AntiSD、Rebellious Student）通过反转蒸馏符号来鼓励探索，但仍依赖GRPO提供正确性信号，无法区分能力提升与行为偏移。论文动机在于：隔离分析吸引与排斥信号本身，弄清它们各自引发何种行为变化，以及如何组合使用以分离"正确性信号"与"行为偏移"。

Method: 1. **在线自蒸馏基础**：学生生成回答 y∼πθ(·|x)，相同模型以特权上下文 c 为条件生成教师分布 p_c^t，对学生尝试进行逐token重评分，使用反向KL散度作为损失。  
2. **吸引型 vs 排斥型自蒸馏**：吸引型最小化KL（推向教师），排斥型最大化KL（远离教师），对应token级优势函数符号翻转 A_rep = -A_attr。  
3. **对比型自蒸馏**：构造两个条件教师——正教师 p+（以正确解 c+ 为条件）和负教师 p-（以错误解 c- 为条件），损失为加权组合 ℒ = λ·KL(p_S∥p+) - (1-λ)·KL(p_S∥p-)；当 λ=1/2 时，学生项精确抵消，token级信号变为 (1/2)·log[p+(y_t)/p-(y_t)]，反映两个教师间的相对概率。  
4. **实验设置**：在Qwen3-4B（非思考/思考模式）、Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507（仅指令模式）上，于DeepMath（数学推理）和Reasoning Gym的Group Anagrams任务上进行实验，采用广义JSD散度（α=0.5）、EMA冻结教师、无GRPO辅助。

Result: 1. **排斥型主要引发行为偏移**：在非思考模式初始化下，排斥型自蒸馏会激活模型潜在的思考模式（开始发出<think>标签），准确率短暂上升但不超过思考模式基线；之后响应长度失控增长直至达到token上限，发生截断崩溃。在仅指令模型上，排斥型无法激活思考模式，响应长度同样失控导致崩溃，准确率无提升。  
2. **吸引型偏向过度自信**：训练准确性上升但评估准确性基本不变，响应变短，抑制探索性推理。  
3. **对比型自蒸馏稳定有效**：在三种设置（非思考、仅指令、已思考）下，训练与评估准确性均稳步提升，响应长度逐步增长且远离上限，无需GRPO即可稳定训练。  
4. **token级信用分配至关重要**：将对比优势在响应窗口内池化（pooling）会逐步削弱学习效果；在窗口大小为响应全长时完全退化为序列级策略梯度，无法带来提升；随机排列优势对齐也大幅降低准确率（62.2% vs 85.5%）。  
5. **特权上下文控制推理长度**：保留模型自身思考痕迹使推理长度增长；移除思考痕迹或使用专家解会缩短思考段。

Conclusion: 该论文揭示了在线自蒸馏中"吸引"与"排斥"信号各自的机制：吸引型推向简洁自信、抑制探索；排斥型推向延长表达、激活潜在思考模式或导致长度失控崩溃。两者引发的行为偏移在对比框架中相互抵消，使学习信号主要反映正确性而非行为风格。作为独立目标（无需GRPO），对比型自蒸馏在三种模型配置下均能稳定提升推理性能并保持响应长度可控，表明其增益并非仅来自"唤醒沉睡的思考模式"。该工作为将自蒸馏扩展到推理任务提供了一种实用方案，未来方向包括不同的教师平衡方式、正负上下文设计及更大规模模型的扩展。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation provides dense, token-level supervision by conditioning a model on privileged information and distilling the resulting teacher distribution back into the model. However, privileged information can change not only what the teacher knows, but also how it behaves, entangling correctness-relevant learning signals with unintended behavioral shifts. We study this effect in reasoning tasks by contrasting attractive self-distillation, which moves the model toward a privileged teacher, with repulsive self-distillation, which moves it away from a privileged teacher. We find that both objectives can induce strong and opposing behavioral shifts: attraction suppresses exploratory reasoning and promotes shorter, more confident responses, whereas repulsion increases response length, can trigger unintended switches into a model's latent thinking mode, and ultimately becomes unstable. Motivated by these observations, we study contrastive self-distillation, which combines attraction toward a correct-solution-conditioned teacher with repulsion from an incorrect-solution-conditioned teacher. In contrast to prior work that combines such distillation signals with a GRPO objective, we isolate the self-distillation objective and study its behavior on its own. We find that the shared behavioral shifts of the two teachers largely cancel, leaving a token-level signal that more directly reflects correctness. Across non-thinking, instruct-only, and already-thinking models, this contrastive objective improves reasoning performance while maintaining stable response lengths.

</details>


### [93] [Matrix AdaGrad: Row-wise and Column-wise Adaptive Subgradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21815)
*Wenpeng Zhang,Runsheng Yu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对矩阵值参数的自适应优化框架,基于在线镜像下降(OMD)推导出行式矩阵AdaGrad (Row-AdaGrad)和列式矩阵AdaGrad (Column-AdaGrad),通过利用矩阵的行/列结构而非逐元素缩放,实现了更紧的遗憾界和更好的优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应优化器(如AdaGrad、Adam)主要针对向量值参数设计,将矩阵参数展平为向量后独立处理每个标量元素,未能利用矩阵的内在几何结构。虽然Shampoo、Muon等矩阵感知优化器在实践中展现了结构化优化的优势,但缺乏类似AdaGrad那样基于在线遗憾最小化的原则性理论框架来推导矩阵感知自适应方法。

Method: 1. 在矩阵空间直接定义行式和列式矩阵近端函数(Mahalanobis范数),度量矩阵通过左乘/右乘施加几何结构;2. 构建带矩阵决策变量的OMD框架,使用Bregman矩阵散度作为近端正则化器;3. 推导带自适应近端函数的通用遗憾界,揭示自适应几何的收益(降低对偶范数累积代价)与代价(Bregman散度差异)之间的权衡;4. 采用对角参数化将遗憾界分解为行/列可分离形式,通过AM-GM不等式最小化得到最优缩放比例;5. Row-AdaGrad的行缩放向量正比于各行累积梯度ℓ₂范数的平方根;6. Column-AdaGrad通过矩阵转置等价性从Row-AdaGrad直接推导。

Result: 理论结果:建立了Row-AdaGrad和Column-AdaGrad的遗憾界R(T)≤√2·D·Σᵢ√(Σₜ‖Gₜ,ᵢ,:‖₂²),在行稀疏/列稀疏梯度设置下严格优于逐元素AdaGrad(改进因子分别为√n和√m),且自适应状态从O(mn)降至O(m)或O(n)。实验结果:在MovieLens 100K矩阵分解任务中,Row-AdaGrad显著优于逐元素AdaGrad;在无归一化/残差的堆叠MLP训练中,行式缩放在25层网络和大学习率下仍可稳定训练,而逐元素缩放在15层即出现振荡、22层后完全失败。

Conclusion: 本文建立了矩阵值参数自适应优化的原则性理论框架,所提出的Row-AdaGrad和Column-AdaGrad是该框架的具体实例,当梯度具有轴对齐结构时显著优于逐元素AdaGrad;框架揭示了自适应优化中动态调整几何结构的基本权衡机制,为未来设计原则性更强的矩阵感知优化器提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Adam are widely used in modern neural-network training, but their adaptive scaling is primarily designed for vector-valued parameters and does not explicitly exploit matrix structure. Recent matrix-aware optimizers demonstrate the benefits of structured optimization, yet a general theoretical framework for deriving matrix-aware adaptivity comparable to that of AdaGrad remains lacking. In this work, we develop a general Online Mirror Descent framework with adaptive proximal functions for matrix-valued parameters, providing a principled approach to deriving matrix-aware adaptive optimization through online regret minimization. By introducing row-wise and column-wise matrix proximal functions and analyzing the resulting regret trade-off, we derive Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Row-AdaGrad) and Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Column-AdaGrad), with adaptive scaling determined by the accumulated row-wise or column-wise gradient norms. We establish regret guarantees and show that these matrix-aware bounds can be strictly tighter than those of entry-wise AdaGrad under structured gradients. Experiments on matrix factorization and deep neural-network training further demonstrate the benefits of aligning adaptive scaling with matrix structure, including improved optimization stability and trainability at larger learning rates and greater network depths.

</details>


### [94] [Federated Deep Clustering Networks for High-Dimensional and Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21829)
*Morris Stallmann,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Marcin Pietrasik,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedDCN: 将深度聚类网络(DCN)推广到联邦学习场景,通过合成数据增强和几何正则化解决高维数据在非IID分布下的聚类问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式深度聚类在高维数据上表现优异,但联邦学习场景下数据隐私和分布异构性挑战大。现有联邦深度聚类方法(F-DEC、FDEC等)在非IID数据下性能显著下降(高达50%),亟需对数据异构性鲁棒的联邦深度聚类方法。

Method: 三阶段联邦深度聚类框架:(1)本地DCN训练并收集聚类中心和标准差,服务器在潜空间聚类中心周围采样并通过解码器生成合成数据;(2)在合成数据上预训练全局模型并构建UMAP几何嵌入;(3)联邦训练阶段,同时优化重构损失、k-means聚类损失和基于UMAP的几何正则化损失,通过合成数据批增强实现潜空间对齐。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和USPS数据集上,FedDCN在IID和非IID场景下均达到与现有方法相当的性能,尤其在非IID场景下表现出显著鲁棒性(MNIST非IID准确率0.76 vs F-DEC 0.57/FDEC 0.63),消融实验证实几何正则化项是关键组件。

Conclusion: FedDCN是首个将DCN推广到联邦设置的方法,无需中心化数据预训练,通过合成数据增强和几何感知损失在数据异构性下表现鲁棒;但对超参数敏感,未来工作将探索其他潜空间采样策略、扩展数据集和漂移/异常检测应用。

Abstract: Clustering high-dimensional data is a fundamental task in unsupervised machine learning with applications to a variety of domains. In the centralized data scenario, this task is commonly solved using deep clustering methods that utilize deep neural network architectures to learn clustering-friendly latent space representations. In Federated Learning, where data is distributed between clients and is private, deep clustering methods are less explored. In particular, recently introduced federated deep clustering methods, despite showing very promising performance, still fall short in reliably providing good performance if data across clients are non-identically-independently distributed. In this work, we introduce a generalization of Deep Clustering Networks to the federated scenario, named FedDCN, that simultaneously optimizes a reconstruction loss and a clustering loss. To ensure robustness and latent space alignment in non-identically-independently distributed data scenarios, FedDCN generates synthetic data augmentations, and its learning objective includes a geometric regularization for latent space alignment. Through experimental evaluation, the effectiveness of the approach under IID and non-IID assumptions is demonstrated, and future research directions are identified.

</details>


### [95] [Neural Cellular Automata Learn General Features in their Hidden Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21870)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经细胞自动机（NCA）隐藏通道的内部动力学，提出一种通过注入预训练教师模型的隐藏状态来实现迁移学习的新机制，在少样本和尺度变化的MNIST基准上以极少的参数（约9,800个）实现优于循环和前馈架构的泛化能力，并揭示隐藏通道捕获了通用的、尺度不变的几何拓扑基元而非类别特定模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习依赖过度参数化，在少样本场景下易过拟合和记忆化；而现有NCA研究主要关注输出，忽视了其内部隐藏通道的作用与机制。

Method: 1）在MNIST少样本和尺度变化基准上对比NCA、全局循环模型与前馈模型的泛化性能；2）提出隐藏状态注入机制：将预训练教师NCA的非求解隐藏通道（在T=0时刻）注入学生NCA；3）通过空间方差、跨通道余弦相似度和UMAP潜轨迹分析，揭示隐藏通道的内部动力学与功能分工。

Result: NCA在少样本（K-shot）和尺度缩放（25%–100%）MNIST测试中普遍优于参数量相当的前馈和循环模型；在注入隐藏通道后性能进一步显著提升（80%阈值在3–4样本时跨越）；教师仅用数字0-5训练的隐藏特征可直接迁移至学生识别数字6-9；隐藏通道空间方差高而读出通道近零，通道间平均余弦相似度趋于0。

Conclusion: NCA隐藏通道并非简单的中间消息传递通道，而是去中心化的、动态的特征计算基底，能够解耦特征提取与分类共识，捕获尺度不变的拓扑基元；通过隐藏状态注入可实现高效的参数高效迁移学习，为去中心化架构的可解释性研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Modern deep learning models achieve impressive generalization through over-parameterization, but this paradigm often struggles with overfitting and memorization in few-shot regimes. Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) offer a highly parameter-efficient alternative, yet research has focused primarily on their output, leaving the role of their internal hidden channels largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the internal dynamics of NCA hidden channels and introduce a novel transfer-learning mechanism that injects a pretrained teacher's hidden states into a student model to guide early optimization. Evaluated on few-shot and scale-variant MNIST benchmarks, NCAs outperform comparable recurrent and feed-forward architectures, demonstrating superior generalization with a minimal parameter budget (~9,800 parameters). Mechanistic analysis reveals that the hidden channels decouple feature extraction from uniform classification consensus by absorbing morphological complexity and converging to mutually orthogonal states. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these hidden channels capture general, scale-invariant topological primitives rather than class-specific templates. This allows a student model to achieve strong few-shot performance on unseen classes using features transferred from a teacher trained only on a subset of digits (0-5). Our results highlight the potential of utilizing hidden-state dynamics as a robust, decentralized computational substrate for parameter-efficient transfer learning

</details>


### [96] [Detecting Pretraining Data in Large Language Models from a Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21888)
*Chenye Ke,Zirui Liu,Qi Liu,Yan Zhuang,Jintao Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型预训练数据检测中"高似然可能源于泛化而非训练暴露"的核心挑战，提出了一种基于预测损失与预测熵联合空间的熵校正检测分数，并通过Helmholtz自由能诠释提出了Energy Transfer Detection (ETD)方法。ETD将每个token的预测损失减去λ倍预测熵作为"残余自由能贡献"，并仅在首次出现位置聚合，从而在期望成员-非成员间隔不变的前提下显著降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灰盒预训练数据检测主要依赖似然性打分，但自然语言中存在大量重复和相似表达模式，模型可能对未见文本也预测良好（泛化能力强），导致仅用水平阈值（只看损失）的检测器会把"可预测的非成员"误判为成员。本文观察到在prediction loss × predictive entropy联合空间中，成员与非成员沿对角方向分离，因此提出需要"斜边界"而非"水平边界"。

Method: 核心方法为Energy Transfer Detection (ETD)：1)计算每个token的预测损失ℓ_j和预测熵h_j；2)在首次出现位置(FOS)上聚合残差自由能贡献f_j = ℓ_j − λ·h_j；3)序列级得分S_ETD为所有首次出现位置f_j的平均；4)用阈值τ判定成员/非成员。理论层面证明：(a)当成员与非成员均值熵相同时，熵校正保持期望成员-非成员间隔的下界(Theorem 1)；(b)当损失与熵的间隙协方差为正时，λ在(0, 2c/v_H)范围内可严格降低方差(Theorem 2)；(c)得分形式与Helmholtz自由能F=U−TS代数同构。广义化分析(Appendix C)处理μ_H≠0情形。

Result: 在WikiMIA、StackMIAsub、MIMIR三个基准上的实验表明：ETD在AUROC平均最高提升3.5%，TPR@5%FPR最高提升5.1%；在GPT-Neo-2.7B、Mamba-2.8B、OPT-6.7B、Pythia-12B、GPT-NeoX-20B及Pythia系列5个尺度上均取得最优平均性能；在长序列(256词)和paraphrased设置下优势更显著；消融实验显示熵校正与FOS聚合各自贡献明显(74.3% vs 仅损失68.5%)；受控继续预训练实验显示ETD在中等训练暴露水平下对likelihood-only方法有显著提升(TPR@5%FPR提升17.86%)；经验分析证实损失-熵间隙正协方差在16种数据集-设置-长度组合下均显著大于零。

Conclusion: 高似然既可能源于训练也可能源于泛化，因此需要用预测熵来校准预测损失。该工作的斜边界评分在期望间隔不变时可降低方差，提升了标准化的成员-非成员区分度。得分形式与Helmholtz自由能同构，引出ETD——从宏观残余自由能转移视角看预训练数据检测：成员样本更易被学习到的模型分布"容纳"，推理时残余自由能转移更少。三个基准的广泛实验和受控继续预训练验证了ETD的有效性与鲁棒性。未来计划将该自由能视角推广到更广泛的模型-数据交互场景。

Abstract: Detecting pretraining data in large language models is challenging because high likelihood can reflect either training exposure or strong generalization. In the joint space of prediction loss and predictive entropy, a likelihood-only detector uses a horizontal boundary and can mistake predictable non-members for members. Motivated by this, we introduce an inclined boundary that evaluates prediction loss relative to predictive entropy. Our analysis shows that entropy correction can preserve the expected membership signal while reducing its variance, thereby improving standardized member--non-member separation. We further extend the mean--variance analysis to the more general setting with a nonzero mean entropy gap. Interestingly, this entropy-adjusted score admits a Helmholtz free-energy interpretation, leading to Energy Transfer Detection (ETD), which views pretraining data detection from a macroscopic residual free-energy transfer perspective. Extensive experiments show that ETD achieves the best average detection performance, improving average AUROC by up to 3.5\% and TPR@5\%FPR by up to 5.1\%, while remaining robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [97] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedeRage，一种基于CVaR的风险规避联邦学习算法，在客户端参与概率未知且高度偏斜的情况下实现可证明的收敛。核心贡献是将CVaR嵌入局部目标函数中，通过隐式加权高损失和低参与频率的客户端来解决数据异构性和参与异构性的耦合问题，同时仅增加每个客户端一个标量状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中存在两个主要异构性来源：数据异构性（非IID数据）和参与异构性（客户端可用性随机且未知）。现有方法通常预设服务器已知客户端参与概率，这在实际部署中不成立。基于最优传输的聚合对齐方案需要参与分布作为输入，无法在不可知场景下使用。FedAvg在偏斜参与下会偏向频繁参与的客户端，降低公平性和性能。

Method: 1. 扩展随机访问模型（RAM）到变长子集选择，定义参与诱导的边缘分布权重p_i。
2. 证明不可知FedAvg在未知参与分布下收敛到由p_i加权的随机目标，速率O(1/√T)。
3. 提出FedeRage算法：在局部目标中嵌入均值-CVaR函数，通过单个标量β估计损失分位数。
4. 风险权重w = (1-γ) + (γ/α)·1{f≥β}，隐式加权高损失客户端。
5. 建立O(κ/√T)收敛速率，其中κ(α,γ) = (1-γ) + γ/α为风险规避的"代价"。
6. 在MNIST、FashionMNIST、CIFAR-10上验证N=30、M=3的设置。

Result: 在均匀和偏斜参与两种设置下，FedeRage在三个基准数据集上均优于FedAvg和FedProx，与SCAFFOLD相当或更好。在偏斜可用性场景下优势更明显：对最少参与客户端的准确率显著提高，公平性更好，收敛更快。通信开销仅增加每个客户端一个标量β，远少于SCAFFOLD的d'维控制变量。

Conclusion: FedeRage为不可知联邦学习提供了一个原则性强且实用的解决方案，通过风险规避目标函数隐式处理参与偏斜，无需估计参与分布。理论保证和实验结果均支持其在数据异构性和参与异构性耦合场景下的有效性。未来方向包括自适应风险参数、多CVaR约束、混合对齐-对冲策略以及非凸场景的扩展。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(\kappa/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $\kappa$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [98] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"（Sparse Priors）框架和"稀疏维度"（Sparse Dimension）概念，用于在贝叶斯风险下分析分布学习问题，证明了在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，且在TV距离下能达到匹配的上界O(√(k log n / n))，有效缓解了传统极小极大理论中对样本数n的维度依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布学习的极小极大最优样本复杂度为O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，遭受维度灾难诅咒，且远不及实际生成式AI（如扩散模型、GAN）所表现出的优异性能。例如，ImageNet的估计内在维度至少为19，按已有理论需要超过10^11个样本才能获得10倍的TV距离改善。作者认为现有基于光滑性（如Hölder或Sobolev）的假设过于悲观，未能捕捉实际分布的稀疏聚类结构。

Method: 方法包括：（1）形式化定义"稀疏先验"和"稀疏维度"k，刻画先验在分布空间中形成良好分离的稀疏聚类；（3）利用相关分析工具及贝叶斯非参数后验收缩理论（Ghosal et al., 2000），建立分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性；（2）通过Fano不等式构造参数型硬实例族（k个元素的离散分布族）证明TV和W1距离下的Ω(√(k/n))下界；（4）通过后验中心估计器在有界似然比假设下证明O(√(k log n / n))上界；（5）进一步放松似然比假设至高概率o(n)增长，并给出高斯位置族满足条件的具体例子。

Result: 主要结果：（1）稀疏维度定义（Definition 3）：k-稀疏先验意味着集合A中存在子集B使P(B)≥εP(A)且diam(B)≤cε^{1/k}diam(A)；（2）下界（Theorems 4.2, 4.3）：k-稀疏先验下分布学习贝叶斯风险为Ω(√(k/n))，与维度d无关；（3）上界（Theorem 5.2）：有界似然比假设下存在估计器使贝叶斯风险为O(√(k log n / n))，仅差对数因子；（4）分布学习与学习采样在贝叶斯设定下统计等价（Theorems 4.1, 5.1）；（5）具体例子：均匀分布参数的稀疏维度为1.2，Cantor分布的稀疏维度为-log_a 2，高斯位置族满足Corollary 5.2.1的放松条件。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一类不遭受维度灾难的现实先验类，通过稀疏维度k刻画先验结构，将分布学习的样本复杂度从最坏情况下的n^{-1/Θ(d)}改善为与维度d无关的√(k/n)，仅依赖于先验的内在稀疏结构。这一框架在贝叶斯风险框架下弥合了理论与实践之间的差距，为解释生成式AI实际性能提供了理论基础，并表明选择合适的稀疏先验可以有效克服维度灾难。未来工作包括研究计算效率、从数据中学习合适先验的元学习方法以及将先验融入基于梯度下降的学习算法中。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/\Theta(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $\Omega(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [99] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)，一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构，通过查询条件化的动态阈值预测和乘法抑制机制，在长上下文解码中实现硬件加速的同时保持密集注意力质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受限于KV缓存的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法（启发式驱逐、固定k路由、训练时硬剪枝）在速度与质量之间存在严重权衡：刚性启发式丢弃必要上下文导致质量下降，而训练时硬删除（logits置为-∞）使学到的注意力分布对GPU块级过度包含敏感。注意力沉陷（attention sinks）现象也迫使后验方法必须固定初始token，限制了灵活性。

Method: ETA包含三个核心组件：(1) 阈值预测——每层附加一个轻量级线性投影（<0.1%参数），从post-RoPE查询向量预测每头标量阈值，采用start-dense初始化（权重零初始化，偏置-8.0）；(2) 乘法抑制——通过退火sigmoid门控σ(β(S-τ))对子阈值分数进行乘法缩放而非置-∞，β从1.0线性退火至5.0，训练时建立均匀注意力基底（tail熵从0.790升至0.916），实现两面向收缩使正/负子阈值logit都向0靠拢；(3) 块稀疏推理内核——使用Triton实现GQA感知的块稀疏解码内核，通过双概率-几何索引（质心μ_B、坐标方差σ²_B、最大范数M_B）以O(1)时间筛选块，避免HBM传输。配套离线校准算法通过1/t加权分数直方图二分搜索求解静态阈值c^ℓh用于固定域部署。

Result: 1.45B参数ETA模型在FineWeb上预训练42B tokens后：语言建模达到与密集注意力相当的困惑度（FineWeb 14.83 vs 14.70；C4 18.99 vs 18.96），推理基准（ARC-Easy 44.5% vs 44.0%；HellaSwag 40.0% vs 36.0%）超越密集注意力；训练时约85%稀疏度，解码时约38%活跃密度；注意力沉陷质量从>50%降至<2%；长上下文检索（Needle-in-a-Haystack）在L=8192时密集注意力检索崩塌为0%，ETA保持22.2%；在NVIDIA H100上vs FlashAttention-2达最高2.5×加速（稀疏16%并密度下B≥64，L≤512K）。126M参数上vs NSA实现2.72×更低延迟。

Conclusion: ETA通过端到端可微的查询条件化稀疏注意力和训练时乘法抑制策略，统一解决了长上下文解码中的速度-质量权衡问题。均匀注意力基底机制是实现训练-推理表征一致性的核心，使模型天然抵抗GPU块级过度包含并消除局部注意力沉陷。未来工作将集中于扩展到7B-70B+规模、多模态序列以及集成Hopper TMA、FP8/INT4量化等下一代GPU特性。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [100] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文围绕天体物理时序分析中神经SDE模型，提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（Continuous-DMSGD）和探索性反向Stratonovich SDE（EB-SSDE）两个新方法，通过将离散迭代过程的过去状态纳入优化路径来提升探索能力和收敛精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体光变曲线建模需要捕捉非马尔可夫依赖（如X射线-光学波段的光谱重处理时间延迟τ∝λ^{4/3}），而传统神经SDE假设马尔可夫性，无法显式建模此类延迟。同时，现有连续时间RL方法依赖求解HJB PDE，缺乏高效的梯度计算途径。论文旨在通过引入延迟记忆机制统一优化算法和连续时间RL框架。

Method: 方法分三部分：（1）将单延迟SDDE推广为连续延迟记忆梯度流（使用积分核Λ(s)建模分布式延迟），离散化为DMSGD迭代公式；（2）在二维凸二次、Rastrigin非凸和Styblinski-Tang损失景观上进行仿真实验；（3）基于Wang-Zhou的探索性随机控制公式，推导EB-SSDE（Exploratory Backward Stratonovich SDE），并通过Itô公式和Khasminskii定理证明所提SDDE的唯一全局解存在性条件（局部Lipschitz连续性+Lyapunov约束）。

Result: 实验观察显示：（1）在凸损失景观中，DMSGD表现出比Vanilla SGD更精确的收敛行为；（2）在Rastrigin非凸景观中，Continuous-DMSGD通过延迟反馈力的"恢复力"成功逃离局部极小并到达全局最优点；（3）在Styblinski-Tang景观中两者收敛到不同局部极小；（4）理论证明EB-SSDE的最优性条件恢复Wang等人的Gibbs策略，避免求解HJB PDE；（5）大Λ增强探索但可能导致发散，小Λ提高收敛稳定性。

Conclusion: 论文成功将延迟记忆机制从神经SDDE推广到优化算法层面，提出的Continuous-DMSGD在高维非凸景观中展现出优于Vanilla SGD的逃逸局部极小能力；EB-SSDE为探索性连续时间RL提供了一条绕过HJB PDE的梯度途径。未来工作包括：Lévy驱动跳跃扩散扩展、延迟耦合参数Λ的决策边界理论、RL基准测试以及贝叶斯不确定性量化。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [101] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在里德堡原子阵列量子测量数据上训练的自回归Transformer模型（RydbergGPT）的神经缩放规律，发现近临界点处数据遵循类LLM的幂律缩放，并通过互信息分析揭示多尺度统计结构是稳定缩放行为的根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经缩放定律（如Kaplan和Hoffmann缩放）几乎完全基于自然语言数据，其普适性存疑：稳定幂律缩放究竟是自然语言特有的，还是结构化概率分布与Transformer架构的更一般特性？本文旨在通过量子物理系统检验这一假设。

Method: 1. 在6×6里德堡原子阵列的量子蒙特卡洛测量数据上训练固定架构的RydbergGPT自回归Transformer，沿激光失谐δ扫描数据集大小（10%–100%），用幂律加损耗地板（loss floor）形式拟合损耗曲线。2. 定义熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息"两点函数"ρ̂_MI(s)，比较里德堡数据与AG News/Yahoo Answers自然语言语料在不同空间/序列分离下的统计依赖结构。

Result: 近临界点（δ/Ω≈1.05–1.17）处，RydbergGPT验证损耗对数据集大小呈高质量幂律拟合（R²≥0.98），种子间稳定性高；远离临界点处R²显著下降、参数波动增大。互信息剖面显示：近临界里德堡数据与自然语言具有最相似的功能形态（强短程依赖+衰减正尾部），而远临界配置的互信息快速塌缩至零水平。互信息径向幂律指数η从δ/Ω=0.93处的2.24降至δ/Ω=1.17处的1.08，表明近临界点统计依赖延伸至更大尺度。

Conclusion: 稳定幂律神经缩放并非模型架构的固有属性，而是模型-数据对的联合属性。多尺度统计依赖结构（类比自然语言中跨越短/中/长程的语法与语义层级）是产生类LLM缩放行为的关键条件。在里德堡原子阵列等量子模拟器中，需将系统调至量子临界点附近才能观察到稳健的神经缩放定律。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [102] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM递归训练于AI同行评审中的风险及缓解方法**——一种开放源代码LLM评估平台,通过训练时的语料策展和测试时的成对激活引导,缓解科学判断坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机:随着大语言模型(LLM)被广泛应用于同行评审(包括自动评审和辅助人类评审),模型生成的评审内容可能进入公开数据和未来训练语料,形成递归反馈环路:后一代评审者部分地从前一代LLM的判断中学习。这与递归训练中的"模型坍塌"现象相关,引发一个具体问题:如果评审者被训练于生成的评审,其科学判断的表达范围是否会变窄?

Method: 方法:在受控设置下研究递归AI评审训练的一步反馈环路。从Llama 3.1 8B基础模型出发,首先在ICLR 2018-2023官方评审上微调生成评审者M1,再用M1为ICLR 2024论文生成合成评审,然后训练四个后继模型M2(0%,33%,66%,100%合成评审比例),保持初始化、过滤和优化设置一致。引入TrustReviewer系统,包含两个互补的干预阶段:(1)训练时:在策划过的语料上单阶段微调核心审查者,降低低质量和语义退化的监督;(2)测试时:成对激活引导,使用同一论文的官方与生成评审之间的对比,缓解坍塌倾向而不需额外训练或专家标注。

Result: 结果:(1)证实了"科学判断坍塌"现象:引入合成评审压缩了评分分布(标准差从1.73降至1.44,熵从2.38降至2.14),并单调降低了同论文(约11%降幅)和语料级别(约5%降幅)的语义多样性。(2)TrustReviewer在多项指标上优于基线:Exact Match达75.40%(优于OpenReviewer 73.33%, Qwen 61.85%, Llama 33.93%),MAD为1.079,评分熵达2.18,接近官方评审参考值2.38。(3)测试时激活引导在无额外训练情况下将Exact Match提升1.55个百分点(从73.85%到75.40%),同时扩大语料级语义扩展。

Conclusion: 结论:本文揭示了递归AI评审训练中的一种具体风险——科学判断坍塌,并提供了实用的干预机制(TrustReviewer)来构建更具多样性和更好对齐的AI辅助科学评估系统。局限性:研究仅涵盖一个递归步骤、一个基础模型家族和一个科学评审领域;判断多样性不等同于评审质量;嵌入距离仅是语义多样性的代理指标,未来需要人类评估来补充。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$. To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯自适应强化学习（BA-LCRL）的方法，用于在未知环境下满足线性时序逻辑（LTL）规约的策略合成。该方法通过将LTL的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）表示与贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，并提出一种新的Product-BAMCP（P-BAMCP）规划算法，实现了高效的探索-利用权衡，并在有限和无限时域任务上展示了优于传统无模型方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LTL约束强化学习（LCRL）方法多为无模型方法，在LTL稀疏奖励环境下表现不佳。贝叶斯强化学习通过BAMDP可以自然地平衡探索与利用，但如何将贝叶斯规划与LTL规约结合是一个开放问题。

Method: 1. 将LTL公式转换为LDBA；2. 将BAMDP与LDBA同步构建Product BAMDP；3. 提出P-BAMCP算法（基于UCT/PUCT的MCTS），跟踪自动机状态以处理非接受汇合状态；4. 设计基于势函数的奖励整形以缓解稀疏奖励；5. 使用Dirichlet-Multinomial模型（离散）或神经网络集成（连续）维护后验信念。

Result: 实验在Slippery Grid和Cartpole环境中验证了六种LTL任务（含有限时域和无限时域任务）。结果表明BA-LCRL在PSP和样本效率上均优于无模型LCRL基线；P-BAMCP显著优于经典BAMCP；奖励整形对顺序任务至关重要；加入一步前瞻机制的Cautious BA-LCRL可将训练期间的违规次数减少约13%。

Conclusion: BA-LCRL提供了一种基于贝叶斯模型的端到端RL框架，能够高效合成满足LTL规约的贝叶斯最优策略，具有良好的样本效率和规约满足能力，并为安全强化学习提供了灵活的扩展途径。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [104] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单统一的框架，将非平稳在线学习中的动态遗憾最小化问题归约为切换遗憾最小化问题。核心思想是为任意比较器序列构造一个无偏随机辅助序列，通过代理损失将动态遗憾分解为对随机序列的期望切换遗憾和受控方差，从而可以直接调用现成的切换遗憾算法作为黑盒。在强凸、指数凹和一般凸损失下分别得到与极小极大最优界匹配的动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态遗憾最小化在强凸和指数凹损失下获得最优界的现有方法依赖于对离线最优比较器序列的KKT条件分析（Baby and Wang, 2021; 2022），技术复杂；而 Zhang et al. (2025) 基于混合性的方法虽避免了KKT分析，但需要对高斯混合分布族进行KL投影，计算昂贵且难以实现。此外，Pasteris et al. (2024) 的切换遗憾方法虽能给出一般凸损失的最优动态遗憾界，但无法推广到强凸和指数凹损失。因此需要一个简单、统一的框架来统一处理这三类损失的动态遗憾最小化。

Method: （1）随机序列构造：给定比较器序列 u_{1:T}，为每个容差 ε>0 构造辅助随机序列 V_{1:T}，满足 E[V_t]=u_t（逐轮无偏），E[||V_t-u_t||²]≤ε²（受控方差），E[J(V_{1:T})]≤P_T/ε（受控的期望切换次数）。当移动距离 Δ_t<ε 时，以概率 p_t=Δ_t/ε 更新到 u_{t-1}+δ_t/p_t 以保持无偏性；当 Δ_t≥ε 时直接设为 u_t。
（2）归约框架：通过满足不等式 f_t(x_t)-f_t(u)≤ℓ_t(y_t)-ℓ_t(u)（性质6）和 ℓ_t(v)≤ℓ_t(u)+⟨∇ℓ_t, v-u⟩+(H_t/2)||v-u||²（性质8）的代理损失，将动态遗憾分解为：期望切换遗憾 + (ε²/2)∑H_t。
（3）统一算法：算法1以任意切换遗憾算法 L 为黑盒，在增广域 Y=B(0,R) 上运行，预测 y_t 投影回 X 得到实际决策 x_t，并使用 Cutkosky (2020) 的投影修正梯度 h_t。对三类损失分别配置：强凸损失用 IRESET+OGD，指数凹损失用 IRESET+ONS，一般凸损失用 RESET+OGD。

Result: 理论结果匹配三类损失的极小极大最优界（对数因子除外）：
• 强凸损失：Õ(T^{1/3} P_T^{2/3})（定理3）
• 指数凹损失：Õ(d^{2/3} T^{1/3} P_T^{2/3})（定理4），将 Zhang et al. (2025) 的维度依赖从 O(d) 改善到 O(ζζ)
• 一般凸损失：O(√(T(1+P_T)))（定理5）
所有结果通过简单统一的归约得到，无需 KKT 分析或 KL 投影。辅助随机序列仅用于理论分析，实现时无需构造。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个简单统一的框架，将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化，绕过了现有方法中复杂的KKT条件分析和KL投影。通过构造无偏随机辅助序列和合适的代理损失，可以将任何具有切换遗憾保证的现成算法作为黑盒使用，在强凸、指数凹和一般凸损失下分别获得极小极大最优的动态遗憾界。该框架突出了简洁性和通用性，随机序列仅用于分析而不需在执行中生成，因此易于理解和实现。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [105] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"生成式反演"工作流，将相互竞争的地质文字描述直接转化为可被水头观测检验的空间先验，并据此对候选地质解释进行早期定量排序。核心链路为：文本到图像基础模型生成图像集合 → 解释专属 VAE 学习潜在表示 → 监督反演网络将水头观测映射至潜在空间 → 解码为对数渗透率场 → 稳态流动模拟得到预测水头 → 基于高斯型残差计算相对兼容性权重。合成基准（Johansen Formation，三类解释，925个测试用例）与真实场地（WIPP Culebra 白云岩，两版概念模型）均得到与独立证据一致的正确排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险地下决策常在数据严重匮乏阶段做出，多位专家可能对同一地下系统提出相互竞争的地质解释，但项目早期缺乏实用方法判断哪种更接近真实。现有方法要么依赖主观判断，要么依赖早期项目不具备的密集数据，这种不确定性通常要持续到若干百万美元级的钻井之后。该研究旨在填补"文字地质解释"与"动态观测评价"之间的鸿沟，使已有水头记录能够在钻井之前对候选概念模型进行可量化的区分。

Method: 构建四阶段工作流：(1) 用文本条件扩散模型为每个候选解释 C_k 生成 N_k=1600 张 256×256 灰度地质图像；(2) 为每个解释单独训练卷积 VAE（编码器 4 层卷积 + 2 个线性层输出均值与对数方差，d_z=128；解码器对称，含边缘差分损失 λ_e=0.1 与 β-VAE 退火策略 β(t):0→0.01，训练 3000 周期）；(3) 监督反演网络 f_k（3 层 256 单元全连接 + 线性层）将含噪水头向量映射到潜在空间，损失包含重建 MSE、潜变量 MSE 与 L2 范数正则，DPFEHM（Julia/Flux）作为冻结的正向算子生成训练目标；(4) 稳态单相流动方程在 128×128 网格上求解，图像灰度线性映射至 [−30,−25] 的 ln K，左右边界为定水头头驱动梯度；250 个固定监测节点；观测噪声 σ_obs=1%，并引入 σ_mod=2% 模型误差底；最终用高斯型兼容性分 s_ik=−N_obs/2·(r_ik/σ_i)^2 通过 softmax 转化为相对兼容性权重 W_k，并对 925 个测试用例采用均值后再 softmax 的聚合方式，避免将相关样本当成独立复制。

Result: 在基于 Johansen Formation 的合成基准上，三类解释（Precise & Accurate / Accurate / Mismatched）的平均水头 RMSE 分别为 0.197、0.227、0.280，分布整体按预期次序平移但存在重叠；个体案例中 Accurate 与 Mismatched 偶有不可区分（案例 1 的 RMSE 分别为 0.154 与 0.161），但聚合权重仍给出正确排序。导出场重建的相对 L2 误差（仅作为诊断）显示 Precise & Accurate 在两个代表性用例上分别为 0.331 与 0.345，显著优于 Mismatched 的 0.486 与 0.873。在 WIPP Culebra Dolomite 真实应用中，向扩散模型额外提供边界轮廓作为约束条件，得到修订版与原版概念模型的相对兼容性权重分别为 0.991 与 0.009，水头残差分布几乎无重叠，与基于独立现场证据做出的概念模型修订方向一致。

Conclusion: 将竞争性的地质文字解释作为可检验假设是可行的：基于文本到图像基础模型的生成式先验能将描述直接转化为可被流动观测检验的空间结构；已有水头观测确实包含区分竞争性概念模型的信息，因此该测试可在新增表征数据之前开展；但在结构复杂的场地，仅靠文本不足以稳定生成图像，需要配合边界轮廓辅助条件。最关键的遗留问题是排序结果对解释措辞（prompt）的敏感性，其次是图像灰度与绝对渗透率间的标定缺位及稳态单相二维物理过程的局限。该方法是有限候选集上的相对评分而非绝对后验概率，不能替代专家判断与独立同行评审，但可在采集新数据之前为决策提供可量化的额外证据。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [106] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASGARD是一个两阶段的教师-学生RL框架，通过在RL控制器与执行器之间插入监控器来实时检测并纠正被篡改的动作命令，使无人机控制对动作空间攻击具有韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的无人机控制器易受动作空间攻击威胁——攻击者在策略生成动作命令后、执行器执行前对其进行篡改。现有防御（如ARMOR、RARL）仅在训练时针对传感器侧的扰动进行鲁棒化，无法在运行时拦截已被破坏的动作。

Method: 采用两阶段教师-学生训练管线。教师阶段：基于VAE的教师编码器融合无人机物理状态与动作攻击特权信息（含目标动作、偏移量、持续时间），生成"动作攻击感知"潜变量，供PPO控制策略和教师监控器MLP使用；学生阶段：通过监督学习将教师编码器和教师监控器蒸馏为基于LSTM的TVAE学生编码器和学生监控器，在部署时仅依靠物理状态历史运行。监控器作为轻量MLP置于策略与执行器之间，每步主动纠正或放行动作，无需单独检测步骤。

Result: 在四种单通道攻击（Pitch/Roll/Thrust/Gain）下，ASGARD平均95%任务成功率、零坠毁率、约0.30m状态漂移；在四通道同时攻击下，67%成功率、10%坠毁率。相比之下，Baseline-RL和ARMOR在单通道下仅约40-50%成功率，在All攻击下完全失败（0%成功率、100%坠毁率）。训练速度比ARMOR快5倍。对未见过的攻击通道和渐进式隐蔽攻击也表现出强泛化能力（如Roll隐蔽攻击下100%成功率）。

Conclusion: ASGARD通过"动作攻击感知"潜变量、教师-学生蒸馏、运行时监控器纠正三大创新，弥补了现有RL防御在动作空间攻击后恢复方面的空白。但其在其他机器人平台和sim-to-real迁移的适用性仍需未来工作验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [107] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于可穿戴传感器生理信号的情感识别任务进行了跨架构、跨数据集的深度学习对比研究。在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态数据集上，统一评估了双向LSTM、Transformer和TCN三种时序深度学习架构在腕带、胸部及多模态融合配置下的性能，并结合软投票集成、经典机器学习基线、传感器消融、采样频率敏感性及梯度显著性分析进行系统比较。结果表明，多模态融合在所有架构和数据集上一致优于单模态配置；Transformer在WESAD上表现最佳（99.02%），而LSTM在EmoWear的唤醒度和效价分类中均最优（91.80%/89.96%）；集成学习主要提升了跨被试稳定性；4 Hz是兼顾性能与计算效率的实用采样频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理情感识别在心理健康监测、情感计算和人机交互等领域具有重要价值。然而现有研究通常仅评估单一模型、单一传感器配置或单一数据集，无法系统揭示架构设计、传感模态和实验设置对识别性能的影响。此外，采样频率对性能与计算开销的权衡、集成学习的稳定性收益、不同架构在不同情感任务中的相对优势等关键问题均缺乏统一框架下的深入研究。

Method: 采用统一的预处理管道（全部信号重采样至4 Hz，2.5秒窗口），在LOSO-CV协议下对比双向LSTM（2层）、Transformer编码器（3层多头自注意力）和TCN（128滤波器，扩张卷积）三种架构。在WESAD（15人，3类：基线/压力/愉悦）和EmoWear（48人，唤醒度/效价二分类）上分别测试腕带、胸部、多模态三种配置。提出软投票集成策略（对LSTM/TCN/Transformer的softmax输出取平均），与逻辑回归和随机森林基线对比。进行传感器消融实验和梯度显著性分析，并在EmoWear上系统评估1–16 Hz五种采样频率的影响。所有指标报告均值±标准差。

Result: 在WESAD上：Transformer多模态准确率最高（99.02%±0.51%），集成模型标准差最低（±0.13%）；腕带优于胸部（TCN腕带98.20% vs 胸部86.35%）。在EmoWear上：LSTM多模态唤醒度（91.80%±1.06%）和效价（89.96%±0.36%）均最优，Transformer表现最差（79.08%/77.04%）。多模态融合在所有架构上一致优于单模态，效价维度的融合增益更大（+9.35个百分点）。采样频率从1 Hz升至4 Hz带来显著性能提升（唤醒度+7.91pp，效价+10.30pp），4 Hz以上收益递减但训练时间倍增。深度学习模型在多模态配置下显著优于随机森林基线（WESAD差距31.52pp），但在胸部单模态设置下差距缩小。显著性分析显示ECG/HR、EDA、BVP、RES为主要贡献通道，ACC通道重要性较低。

Conclusion: 架构性能排名具有数据集依赖性，不存在普遍最优的架构：Transformer在长时连续、控制性强的WESAD记录中表现最佳，而LSTM在短时片段、自然视频诱发的EmoWear数据中更具鲁棒性。多模态融合始终优于单一传感器配置，腕带信号单独使用即可获得较强性能，但效价识别尤其受益于胸部信号的补充。软投票集成主要价值在于降低跨被试方差而非提升均值准确率。4 Hz是性能与效率的理想平衡点，1 Hz在资源受限场景下仍优于传统基线。架构、传感器和采样频率的选择应基于具体应用场景和记录条件，而非单一基准。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [108] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SR 提出了一种混合符号回归框架，利用预训练 Transformer 生成多个表达式草图，通过条件 MCTS 和并行局部树编辑（结合尺度感知常数拟合与符号修复）来恢复闭合方程。在 SRSD-Feynman（含/不含虚拟变量）和六个标准基准上均取得最高的符号求解率，同时保持前二的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索式符号回归方法灵活但每次从随机初始化搜索代价高；纯神经生成方法虽快却容易产生符号错误。论文旨在结合两者优势：用学习到的结构先验快速聚焦搜索空间，再通过显式搜索与修复达到精确的符号等价恢复（而非仅数值拟合）。

Method: 三阶段框架：（1）两阶段预训练——先用对比学习（双向 InfoNCE）对齐数值观测与符号表达式的嵌入空间（数值端用 Set Transformer，符号端用 Transformer 编码器），再训练自回归解码器生成保留算子结构但将变量与常数替换为占位符的"草图"（sketch）；预训练样本使用 SymPy 规范化并在不同输入分布/域下采样；（2）推理时用随机解码获得多个草图，丢弃非法/重复者，通过条件 MCTS 将解码出的变量标记映射到真实输入并用对数间隔的多起点优化拟合常数，取 Top-24 初始化并行搜索；（3）并行局部恢复阶段：每条路径执行尺度感知常数拟合（Levenberg-Marquardt，对数间隔初始化）与结构修复（子树替换算子/变量/子树，每若干步重新拟合常数，共 300 次变异尝试），所有路径共享 Hall of Fame，最终按验证集 NMSE 与复杂度挑选并做常数简化与物理常数吸附。

Result: 在 SRSD-Feynman 三个难度上均取得最高符号求解率（Easy/Medium/Hard = 84.7%/55.0%/34.8%），且在含虚拟变量设置下仍保持领先（74.0%/54.5%/31.2%）；在 Nguyen、Korns、Keijzer、Vladislavleva、Strogatz、Livermore 六个基准上同样取得最高符号求解率，改进幅度 12.5–33.3 个百分点；速度比 SR4MDL 快 6.8–24.3 倍，比 PySR 快 1.3–2.2 倍；消融显示仅解码器+ MCTS 符号恢复约 30–43%，加入完整并行局部搜索后提升至 83.3–90.0%。

Conclusion: 学习到的结构先验可有效缩小符号回归搜索空间，而搜索阶段的常数再拟合和符号修正确保了精确的代数等价恢复；MOSAIC-SR 在多项基准上同时实现了最高的符号求解率与有竞争力的预测精度，并对无关输入具有鲁棒性。未来工作包括扩展预训练分布以处理高维/复杂结构方程，以及面向真实物理系统的稀疏观测恢复。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [109] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了最大化编码率缩减（MCR²）框架在分布外（OOD）泛化方面的理论局限性。论文证明，仅靠MCR²目标追求良好的编码几何结构，并不能保证在新环境中的可靠预测：基于环境（不稳定）特征的编码器可以达到全局编码最优，但在相关性反转后预测几乎完全失败，即使存在完美稳定的预测特征可供利用。此外，即使直接引入不变性原理（如IRM和REx所体现的共享最优编码器共享最优性约束），这种失效仍无法消除。这意味着MCR²需要额外的假设或学习原则来建立跨环境稳定的预测关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MCR²在可解释表征学习和白盒架构设计方面取得了成功，且在理论上能产生类间正交、子流形结构清晰的表示，但其在分布偏移下的可靠性尚未被系统研究。作者在Waterbirds数据集上的实验观察到MCR²编码器在背景-标签相关性反转的测试组上表现严重退化（总体准确率46.84%，远低于随机猜测；水鸟-陆地背景组准确率仅10.90%），这引发了对MCR²目标本身能否保证OOD泛化可靠性的理论质疑。

Method: 1. **理论分析框架**：定义总体MCR²目标、预测风险和二元分类模型（含稳定特征C=Y和环境特征S=Y⊕B_e）。2. **精确计算**：推导出全局编码上界J*(a)（Lemma 3.1），环境编码器的精确目标值和预测风险（Proposition 3.2），并证明二者之间的定量间隙界。3. **构造反例**：设计包含稳定特征和环境特征的二元模型，证明仅用环境特征的编码器可达任意接近编码最优但目标误差接近100%（Theorem 3.3）。4. **不变性扩展**：提出不变编码率不变ICR²，要求共享编码算子在所有训练环境中同时达到最优（Theorem 4.1），证明此约束不足以消除OOD失效。5. **数值验证**：在合成数据和Waterbirds上验证理论预测。6. **补充分析**：包括二阶矩等价性、奇编码器软判定的局限性（Proposition C.2）、全支持下精确优化保证零目标风险的结论（Proposition B.1）。

Result: **主要发现**：1. MCR²全局最优不保证OOD预测可靠性——基于环境特征的编码器可达到J*(a)（精确最优）或接近J*(a)（任意小间隙），但在相关性反转后测试误差达100%或任意接近100%，尽管存在完美稳定特征。2. ICR²（共享最优编码算子约束）无法消除此失效——恒等算子在所有训练环境中对f_S均最优，约束满足但预测仍完全失败。3. 在公共输入支持、有限精度和有限训练环境下，此间隙-失效鸿沟持续存在。4. 仅全变差类条件分布稳定性（公式19-20）能为固定分类头提供OOD预测风险的有限界；MCR²相等的编码值不蕴含此稳定性。5. Waterbirds上ResNet-18+MCR²在背景不匹配测试组上仅获46.84%总体准确率，显著低于预期。6. 数值实验（200,000样本/环境，50随机种子）确认理论与实证一致。

Conclusion: MCR²目标本身无法保证OOD泛化的可靠性。结构化的判别性表征几何（类间正交、类内紧凑）是表示学习的理想特性，但并不确保跨环境稳定的预测关系。直接引入IRM/REx式不变性原理（通过共享最优编码算子）仍不足以解决此问题，因为同一编码算子可在特征-标签相关性反转后仍保持最优。因此，要使MCR²获得可靠的OOD保证，需要引入新的额外假设或学习原则（如限制密度比、已知标签保持变换或配对干预等），以建立编码目标与跨环境稳定预测之间的联系。这项工作首次系统分析了MCR²在OOD泛化方面的局限性，明确区分了"组织类表示的能力"与"跨环境可靠预测的能力"。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [110] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stiefel-AdamW，一种用于线性分解块 W=BA 的几何感知优化器，将其中一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上，同时保留 AdamW 的逐坐标自适应预条件，可作为 AdamW 的几乎即插即用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中的 LoRA 适配器、低秩压缩层、自注意力的 Q-K 乘积等线性分解块 W=BA 存在规范对称性 GL(r)，标准欧几里得优化方法忽略该几何结构，会导致因子发散和训练不稳定，限制可用学习率。

Method: 将因子 A 约束在 Stiefel 流形 St(n,r) 上（AAⁿ=I），B 保持欧几里得；将全 GL(r) 规范对称性松弛为紧致正交对称 O(r)；在环境欧氏空间执行一阶、二阶矩估计，仅在更新前做切空间向投影和流形回缩；支持 Cayley 变换、QR、极分解、Newton-Schulz 等多种可插拔回缩方式。

Result: 在 GPT2/ViT/Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 微调以及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的完整预训练中，Stiefel-AdamW 在几乎不增加开销的前提下，一致优于 AdamW、Scaled AdamW、GeoLoRA、LoRA-RITE、LoRA-Pro、Cayley Adam 等基线，并在大学习率下表现出更高的稳定性。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 通过将因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上保留了 AdamW 的逐坐标自适应预条件优势，同时获得了黎曼优化的稳定性（梯度有界、学习率更稳定）和标准收敛保证；该方法可广泛应用于所有 W=BA 形式的分解块；局限在于仍残留正交不变性，未来工作需在固定秩矩阵流形上实现完全不变的自适应方法。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [111] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的表示级预编码器门控机制（pre-encoder gate），作为插件模块插入到基于Transformer的时间序列预测模型中，用于在编码器处理前显式调节协变量表示的准入强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协变量丰富的长期时间序列预测中，现有基于Transformer的预测器（如TimeXer、iTransformer、PatchTST）通常构建协变量表示后直接传入编码器，缺乏显式的准入控制阶段。作者将预编码器协变量准入定位为一个接口问题，研究是否需要在编码器边界处显式调节协变量信息的进入。

Method: 方法核心是一个两层的轻量级MLP门控模块：接收协变量表示 $E_g \in \mathbb{R}^{B \times N_c \times D}$，通过GELU激活和Sigmoid函数为每个表示单元计算准入分数 $S$，然后通过逐元素乘法对表示进行重加权。门控公式为：$S = \sigma(\phi(E_g W_1 + b_1) W_2 + b_2)$，$\widetilde{E}_g = S \odot E_g$。该门以零额外调优协议（zero-extra-tuning protocol）作为插件附加到TimeXer、iTransformer和PatchTST中，编码器和预测头保持不变。此外，提出了使用正则化变体（usage-regularized variant），通过添加 $\lambda$ 控制的惩罚项来降低平均准入分数，并采用软启动初始化（soft-start initialization）以保证训练初期的梯度流通。

Result: 在ETTm1、ETTm2、Traffic、Energy和ILI五个数据集上的实验表明：(1) 门控变体在配对比较中与基线竞争力相当，在Traffic上TimeXer-Gate和PatchTST-Gate增益最明显；(2) 预编码器放置优于编码器后放置（PostEncoderGate），且冻结门参数（FrozenGate）无法复现相同效果，说明位置和可学习性均重要；(3) 软启动初始化优于更开放的初始化设置；(4) UsageGate可通过增大 $\lambda$ 有效降低平均准入分数，同时保持与无惩罚设置接近的预测误差（ETTm2中甚至取得最低MSE/MAE）；(5) 在ETTm2的VIF-PFI诊断案例中，高VIF协变量组（HULL+MULL）的学习门权重低于其余协变量。参数增加仅为 $D^2/2 + D + 1$，开销极小。

Conclusion: 本文将预编码器协变量准入定位为协变量丰富长期预测中的接口问题。实验证明，所提出的轻量级门控可在不修改编码器和预测头的前提下，有效控制协变量表示的准入强度，且使用正则化惩罚能在保持预测精度的同时降低平均准入。该门是一个紧凑的插件接口，适用于基于Transformer的预测骨干网络。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage.
