This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [112] [Toward individual-level calibration in affect recognition with perceptual adjustment queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21073)
*Xuanzhou Chen,Sankaraleengam Alagapan,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"感知调整查询"（PAQ）的个性化感知校准框架，通过直接估计每位被试在面部情感谱上的最小可觉差（JND），解决面部情感感知任务中因个体感知敏感性差异导致的"感知失校准"问题，并在两选一强制选择（2AFC）任务中验证其效果优于非校准基线和群体级Weibull校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部情感感知任务默认相同刺激对所有被试施加同等难度，但个体在感知灵敏度和情感中性基线上存在系统性差异（个体差异、年龄/性别/临床状态等），导致固定物理强度差在不同被试间产生不同的认知负荷，使得跨被试比较的情绪效应与感知难度混淆，损害行为与临床推断的有效性。群体级校准无法捕捉个体偏离。

Method: 1) 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM，含 Ray3、Ray3 HDR、Veo2、Veo3）通过"视觉身份锚+语言轨迹"提示生成身份一致、单调、连续的100帧面部表情渐变路径（中性→高兴/悲伤），克服AU方法（伪影/鬼影）、GAN方法（身份漂移/非线性）、扩散方法（中间帧模糊）、ChatGPT提示（身份丢失/不单调）的局限；2) 从Chicago Face Database选取8个种族-性别身份，构建跨被试共享的参考刺激集；3) PAQ任务：被试拖动滑块定位其1-JND点，得到个体δᵢ(x)函数；4) 将2AFC任务的固定Δx=11帧距离替换为个体1-JND距离（x与x+δᵢ(x)）；5) 通过元认知难度判断（二项）和反应时方差分解（被试间+被试内）进行验证；6) 对照基线：非校准（Δx=11）和群体级Weibull校准（取p̂=0.5处的Δx*=λ̂√ln2）；7) 两阶段众包研究：先导研究N=52（非校准vs PAQ），主研究N=72（群体级vs PAQ）。

Result: 元认知判断：McNemar精确单侧检验显示，相较基线PAQ改善率47/66=71.2%（p=0.0004），相较群体级校准52/77=67.5%（p=0.0014），均拒绝零假设；按被试汇总后仍显著（p=0.0002和p=0.0004）。反应时方差：基线→PAQ，被试间方差从~11.3s²降至~2.4s²（-78.9%），被试内方差从~4.7s²降至~2.0s²（-59.4%）；群体级→PAQ，被试间方差从~24.7s²降至~3.8s²（-84.6%），被试内方差降至~5.3s²（-78.6%）。平均反应时从~5.00s降至~2.65s（-47%，p=3.98×10⁻⁷），被试内SD从~2.38s降至~1.23s（p=1.32×10⁻⁵），偏度保持不变（~1.1）。Wilcoxon符号秩检验经Bonferroni校正后，情感谱上16个位置全部显著，情感极端端（happy₈₈减少3.76s、sad₆₆减少2.928s）获益最大，中性附近位置获益最小但仍显著。

Conclusion: PAQ作为一种认知轻量的查询机制，能够有效恢复被试在面部情感谱上的个体感知标度，在2AFC任务中显著优于非校准基线和群体级Weibull校准，实现了个体水平上的任务难度均衡，并大幅降低反应时水平及被试间/被试内方差。VLM提示管线为PAQ提供了身份保留、单调、连续的刺激路径。局限：实验阶段顺序固定（PAQ在前）可能引入熟悉度混淆；样本限于8个种族-性别组、124名Prolific被试；未测试临床群体（抑郁、自闭症）；单次会话内未评估PAQ JND的重测信度；感知函数单调性假设可能因疲劳被违反。

Abstract: Behavioral tasks measuring facial affect perception assume that identical stimuli impose equivalent perceptual difficulty across participants. However, this assumption is systematically violated by individual differences in perceptual sensitivity. Using an affective perception task as our testbed, we propose a framework to normalize for perceptual difficulty that directly estimates each participant's Just Noticeable Difference (JND) along the facial affect spectrum via cognitively lightweight perceptual adjustment queries (PAQs). We use these PAQ-inferred JNDs to re-express stimulus distances, constructing difficulty-equated tasks in perceptual space. We validate the framework in a Two-Alternative Forced-Choice (2AFC) task using two complementary behavioral measures: binary metacognitive difficulty judgments and response time variance decomposition. We find that PAQ calibration significantly equalizes perceived task difficulty at an individual level when compared to both the non-calibrated baseline and population-level Weibull calibration, while also reducing mean response time and between-subject variance in response time. These results establish PAQ as a principled and practical instrument for individualized perceptual calibration in facial affect recognition.

</details>


### [113] [REFINEPPO: Learning Continuous Control Policies by Iterative Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21108)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Sagar Kamarthi,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFINEPPO 提出了一种称为迭代动作精化（Iterative Action Refinement, IAR）的连续控制策略构造方法，将传统的一次性从状态预测动作的方式改为通过共享残差网络对初始动作建议进行多步细化，并将其与 PPO 集成。在 14 个 Gymnasium 连续控制任务上，该方法在多数任务上匹配或超过标准 PPO，并在若干任务上表现出更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续控制策略（如 PPO 的 Gaussian actor）要求神经网络在单次前向传播中直接由状态映射到动作分布，无法在形成初始预测后对动作进行渐进式修正。当动作维度变高、动作分量之间存在相互依赖时，单次计算隐式学习这种跨维度协调往往需要更多样本。论文旨在探索一种计算偏差：让策略学习"如何改进一个动作"而非"如何直接预测最优动作"，并验证迭代精化是否能在不改变 PPO 优化目标的前提下带来性能与样本效率优势。

Method: 方法核心是 Iterative Action Refinement (IAR)：从零初始化动作建议 m₀=0，共享的残差网络 fθ(s, mₖ) 以当前状态 s 与当前建议 mₖ 为输入预测残差修正，按 m_{k+1}=mₖ + c_{k+1}·fθ(s,mₖ) 进行 K 步细化，参数 θ 在所有精化步之间共享。经 K 步后，末端建议 m_K 作为对角高斯策略的均值，方差 log σθ 保持原有的可学习形式。作者将此机制嵌入标准 PPO（其余裁剪代理目标、critic、优势估计、熵项均不变），算法名为 REFINEPPO。设计选择上提出三种修正幅度调度：Uniform (cₖ=1)、Inverse (cₖ=1/k)、Average (cₖ=1/K)，并讨论精化深度 K∈{1,2,4,8} 的影响，其中 K=1 退化为无迭代的一次性映射。

Result: 在 14 个 Gymnasium 连续控制基准（涵盖 Classic Control、Box2D 与 MuJoCo）上、每环境 5 个随机种子、训练 6M 步的设定下，REFINEPPO 在多数环境上获得与 PPO 相当或更优的最终回报，其中 HalfCheetah-v5（+87.4%）、Swimmer-v5（+60.2%）、BipedalWalkerHardcore-v3（+85.7%）等提升明显；学习曲线显示在 6 个代表性环境中样本效率均优于 PPO。在 HumanoidStandup-v5 上进行的机制分析显示：(1) 不同动作分量的精化进度不均匀，某些分量在早期已接近终值；(2) 岭回归分析表明已先建立的中间动作分量对后续精化步骤具有显著预测信息（ΔR²>0）。消融实验显示 K=4 表现最佳，K=8 出现轻微下降，K=1 显著弱于 K>1；调度方面，Average 调度在所有 MuJoCo 任务上一致优于 Uniform 与 Inverse。

Conclusion: 论文得出三点结论：(1) IAR 是一种简单、模块化的迭代动作构造机制，仅修改策略均值的计算而不改变 PPO 目标；(2) 将其集成得到的 REFINEPPO 在 14 个连续控制任务上匹配或超越标准 PPO 并在部分任务上加速收敛；(3) 实证分析支持"渐进式、跨维度协调动作构造"是迭代精化有效的核心机制。论文亦指出收益随动作维度增大更明显，且对精化深度和更新调度敏感。未来工作包括将 IAR 扩展到 SAC、TD3 等其他 actor-critic 框架、自适应（按状态决定）精化步数，以及在真实机器人操控等高维任务上验证。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved strong performance across a wide range of continuous-control problems. These continuous-control policies, however, are often defined as direct mappings from an observed state to an action or action distribution, requiring a single feed-forward network to construct an optimal control decision in one pass. While effective, this formulation leaves little opportunity for the policy to reconsider or progressively improve an action once an initial prediction has been formed. In this work, we explore an alternative approach: rather than learning only to directly predict an action, can a policy learn to iteratively improve one, and can this iterative process provide advantages during policy learning? We introduce Iterative Action Refinement (IAR), an iterative action-construction method that constructs control actions through a sequence of learned residual corrections. Starting from an initial proposal, a shared refinement network repeatedly conditions on the observed state and the current action proposal, allowing each refinement step to revise the action constructed by preceding steps. The final refined proposal is then used to determine the action executed by the agent. We integrate this iterative action-construction mechanism with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), yielding REFINEPPO. We evaluate REFINEPPO across 14 benchmark control tasks, complemented by controlled ablations of refinement depth and update schedules and analyses aimed at understanding why iterative refinement is effective. Across these environments, REFINEPPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard PPO while demonstrating faster convergence on several tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Method: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Result: AI enhancement unavailable; see the original abstract.

Conclusion: AI enhancement unavailable (incomplete_output). Original arXiv metadata is preserved.

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [115] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对传统Sonar数据需转为图像格式处理的再分析难题，提出基于CSV格式的替代数据处理方法，在Hammerhead AUV测试平台上实现了91.18%的总处理时间降低及更优的ML目标检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Sonar数据处理依赖图像表示，需在自主系统（如AUV）上完成计算密集型的帧预处理，导致延迟高、信息丢失，且传统去噪方法（如均值滤波、Wiener滤波）难以有效保留边缘细节，亟需轻量化、高保真的替代方案。

Method: 1) 直接使用Ping360声呐输出的CSV原始数据（含UNIX时间戳、角度、扫描强度序列），跳过图像化步骤；2) 采用NumPy向量化操作与并行计算优化数据结构；3) 在CSV数据上应用SciPy中值滤波与背景减除进行降噪；4) 使用K-Means聚类结合凸包计算实现目标检测；5) 基于Hammerhead AUV（搭载LattePanda Sigma与ROS2 Humble）进行实验验证，对比CV方法。

Result: 1) 总处理时间降低91.18%（从0.0374s降至0.0031s），仅滤波步骤提升13.11%；2) 在Gate（大目标）与Broom（小目标）的隔离测试中，CSV方法更好地保留了目标特征，CV方法在Gate场景中误将目标滤除；3) 差分图显示CSV方法去除的几乎全为非目标信息；4) K-Means聚类在CSV数据上的目标检测聚类效果显著优于CV数据；5) SNR、PSNR等评价指标均有所提升。

Conclusion: 基于CSV的Sonar数据处理方法在计算效率、信息保真度及检测清晰度上均显著优于传统CV图像处理，为AUV等自主系统的实时水下目标检测提供了更优方案；未来工作将扩展数据集与声呐类型、引入HDBSCAN等聚类方法、并融合相机、Sonar、DVL构建多传感器融合跟踪分类框架。

Abstract: The dominant method of processing sonar data is using image-based representations, requiring the preprocessing of image data on autonomous systems. We propose an alternative data processing method for remote sensing applications via the use of data in Comma-Seperated Value format. Experimentation on our alternative approach shows a reduction of processing time by 91.18%, an improvement in accurate object detection by Machine Learning, and an increase in SNR (Signal-to-noise ratio), PSNR (Peak signal-to-noise ratio), and other evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [116] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的逐层结构化稀疏化方法，通过对浅层两端子网络进行内权重归一化并仅对外权重施加组惩罚来剪枝神经元。理论证明该解耦形式在任意正齐次激活下与特定联合惩罚在最优解处等价。实验表明该方法相比联合惩罚基线具有更宽的正则强度可用范围和更低的灾难性过剪枝率，并在分类、稀疏恢复、高维PINN及OPT-1.3B等场景中保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度网络（尤其是Transformer）的全连接层占据绝大部分参数量，部署到资源受限硬件面临挑战。传统的后训练剪枝需多轮重训练，稀疏感知训练则因优化困难常损精度。联合惩罚所有层容易导致"雪崩式"过剪枝，即一旦某层开始被剪枝，惩罚会连锁放大地移除更多神经元，亟需一种更稳定的结构化稀疏化方法。

Method: 方法核心是"神经元级联归约"（Neuron Cascade Reduction, NCR）：（1）将网络拆分为浅层两端子网络；（2）对每个块的内权重行施加单位球面约束并进行归一化（式11），消除尺度不确定性；（3）仅对外权重矩阵施加组ℓ_{2,1}惩罚（Group Lasso），通过投影-近端梯度下降求解；（4）按层序贯处理，每层处理完后再对缩减后的下游块重复。理论证明：对于任意正齐次激活函数（任意阶p），受限解耦目标在最优解处与特定联合内/外权重惩罚等价（定理1、3及推论4），全局解通过神经元级重缩放相互映射。该等价性使近端算子（组软阈值）能在有限步内精确移除神经元，而联合形式的L2权重衰减仅在极限产生零值。算法提供固定和自适应两种形式，自适应形式通过精度门控、乘性调度和再生机制动态调整惩罚强度α。

Result: 五个数值实验验证了方法的鲁棒性：（1）双螺旋分类（30个种子）：解耦方法前沿稀疏度93.0%，位列结构化组惩罚前四，与DropNeuron（95.6%）和入向行Group Lasso（94.9%）在同一梯队；（2）稀疏函数恢复（L=2,3,5,8层）：解耦方法的可控遍历宽度（1.05–1.10个数量级）显著大于入向行Group Lasso（0.62–0.67），约为1.7倍；崩塌率（0.23–0.28）低于基线（0.29–0.38），且差距随深度计算扩大；匹配稀疏度下测试误差略优；（3）消融实验：逐层解耦贡献了遍历宽度，归一化贡献了尺度不变性（已归一化解耦方法的稀疏度在c∈[10^{-1.5},10^{1.5}]范围内保持在49.3-49.9%），每块一次归一化优于每步归一化（崩塌率0.05 vs 0.82）；（4）10维Poisson PINN（ReLU³激活）：90%稀疏度下解耦变体的中位误差2.40%、最大7.84%、崩塌9/30，优于入向行Group Lasso的9.40%、51.85%、21/30；（5）OPT-1.3B FFN块：在20%和40%神经元预算下取得最低WikiText-2困惑度（19.5和29.1），可控遍历0.466个数量级，约为OICSR的2.5倍。计算预算控制表明崩塌率优势不源于额外计算量；更新规则控制（Adam与普通梯度互换）表明可控性模式对优化器选择具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的解耦逐层结构化稀疏化方法在理论上具有最优解等价性保证，在实践上相比联合惩罚方法展现出更宽的正则强度可用范围、更低的灾难性过剪枝率，以及对同质重缩放的尺度不变性。这些特性在从简单合成实验到PINN乃至OPT-1.3B规模的多个实验中均得到验证。研究表明解耦的主要贡献在于逐层结构，归一化则消除了权重尺度的不确定性。方法实例化为凸Group Lasso，非凸惩罚（如MCP）以及在更多模型架构（特别是非齐次激活如GELU的门控Transformer）上的扩展是未来工作的自然方向。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [117] [HMB-GAN: Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN for Vector Shape Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21158)
*Elian Hugh Thiele-Evans,Binh Duong Pham,Hani Omar M Alharbi,Liibaan Aaden,Syed Umer Hasnain Zaidi,Prem Prakash Jayaraman,Muhammad Saeed,Boris Eisenbart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HMB-GAN，一种基于多段贝塞尔曲线拼接与几何连续性约束的端到端可微分生成框架，用于合成CAD可用的矢量几何形状；对比了量子增强生成器与经典生成器，在点云指标上量子版本略优且收敛更快、参数更少，但受模拟器开销制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的几何生成工作多局限于栅格化图像或单段贝塞尔曲线表示，难以直接生成CAD可用的、保持几何连续性的闭合矢量形状；同时，探索混合量子-经典生成模型在结构化几何建模中的可行性与边界。

Method: 构建HMB-GAN端到端可微框架，通过多段贝塞尔曲线的拼接（stitched multi-segment Bézier）构造闭合形状，由构造本身保证几何连续性（geometric continuity enforced by construction）；生成器分别采用量子线路（量子增强）与经典神经网络实现，并在统一对抗训练框架下进行对比，使用点云分布指标与几何形状统计量评估。

Result: 量子增强生成器在收敛速度、参数数量上优于经典生成器，且在点云分布指标上略有提升；但由于量子模拟器开销过大，受当前硬件/仿真约束限制，量子模型的经典仿真评估代价高昂，整体几何形状统计性能仍有不足。

Conclusion: 验证了通过混合量子架构建模结构化几何（多段贝塞尔矢量表示）的可行性，证明量子生成器在参数效率和收敛性方面具备潜力；但当前量子模拟器的硬件限制使实际优势难以充分发挥，需待量子硬件成熟。

Abstract: We explore the use of hybrid quantum-classical generative adversarial networks for synthesising CAD-ready vector geometries. Unlike prior work that operates in rasterised or single-Bézier domains, we introduce HMB-GAN (Hybrid Multi-Bézier GAN), an end-to-end differentiable generative framework that constructs closed shapes through stitched multi-segment Bézier representations with geometric continuity enforced by construction. We compare a quantum-enhanced generator with a classical generator within this architecture and evaluate them across point cloud distribution metrics and geometric shape statistics. Results show that despite faster convergence, a reduction in model parameter count, and slightly improved performance on point cloud metrics, the quantum generator suffers from excessive simulator overhead and thus classically-simulated evaluation suffers from hardware constraints. These results demonstrate the feasibility of modelling structured geometries through hybrid quantum architectures whilst highlighting contemporary hardware limitations.

</details>


### [118] [M2G-LLM: Enhancing Clinical Prediction via Multimodal Graph Reasoning and LLM Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21164)
*Inyoung Choi,Sukwon Yun,Jiayi Xin,Jie Peng,Tianlong Chen,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M2G-LLM 是一个将图神经网络（GNN）与大语言模型（LLM）相结合的临床多模态预测框架，通过构建患者级图、对比对齐多模态嵌入，并将融合后的上下文向量注入 LLM 中间层残差流，实现对文本与结构化/影像数据的联合推理，在 MIMIC-IV 和 MIMIC-CXR 数据集的一年死亡率和30天再入院预测任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗多模态 LLM 存在三个主要局限：(1) 多数方法通过伪 token 注入将非文本模态投射到 LLM 输入流，导致上下文窗口被长患者历史消耗殆尽；(2) 通常仅处理单次就诊，缺乏纵向时间建模能力；(3) 无法利用临床相似患者之间的群体级信息。因此需要一种能够融合多模态、纵向与跨患者上下文，同时保持 LLM 预训练语言能力不被破坏的集成框架。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于 GNN 的信息传播——为每种模态（文本、实验室、诊断码、影像）分别构图，结合时间边和跨患者相似性边（top-k + 余弦阈值策略），使用三层 GCN 进行 3 跳消息传递；(2) 多模态对齐——以临床文本为中心模态，采用类似 ImageBind 的对比学习（InfoNCE 损失）将实验室、诊断码、影像映射到 d=4096 的共享潜在空间，再通过学习门控机制融合为患者级上下文向量；(3) 残差流注入——将上下文向量通过前向钩子注入 Llama 3-8B 最后四层 Transformer 的注意力和 MLP 子模块输出处（λ_mha=0.10, λ_mlp=1.0），不修改 LLM 架构与预训练权重。缺失模态通过 GNN 消息传递从邻居节点重建。

Result: 在一年死亡率预测任务中，M2G-LLM 达到 78.98% 准确率、64.55 F1 和 70.22 AUC；在30天再入院预测任务中达到 85.75% 准确率、52.35 F1 和 54.80 AUC，均优于 HAIM、M3Care、MUSE、mmFormer、HeLM、LLMM、Llama 3-8b 和 LLaVA 等基线。消融实验表明：移除实验室或诊断码模态性能显著下降（F1 从 64.55 降至 58.34），图传播冻结使 F1 降至 59.72，多模态对齐冻结使 F1 降至 47.21。t-SNE 可视化证实对齐后各模态嵌入趋向统一空间。在缺失模态场景下性能衰减更优雅（AUC 从 0.702 降至 0.669 vs mmFormer 从 0.693 降至 0.587），并能生成临床可解释的自然语言解释。仅训练 0.66% 参数（53.11M/8.03B），每患者推理 154ms。

Conclusion: M2G-LLM 通过将 GNN 的关系推理能力与 LLM 的语言理解能力相结合，为多模态 EHR 数据提供了一个有效、可解释且对缺失模态鲁棒的临床预测框架。其核心贡献在于：(1) 首个从跨患者纵向图中推导上下文向量并对 LLM 残差流进行深度条件化的框架；(2) 输入依赖而非参数化的条件向量设计；(3) 通过图传播隐式处理模态缺失。局限性包括：计算上受限于 N×N 相似性矩阵的二次方内存增长、缺乏外部队列验证、解释质量缺乏系统评估、时间边与相似性边的独立贡献尚未分离。未来工作将聚焦于跨机构泛化、临床医生在环验证和主动模态选择。

Abstract: Integrating diverse data modalities --- such as clinical notes, laboratory results, and medical imaging --- is essential for advancing clinical decision-making. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable performance in processing unstructured clinical text, their limited capacity to incorporate non-text modalities hinders their broader utility in healthcare applications. Here, we introduce M2G-LLM (Multimodal MedGraph-LLM), a novel framework that enhances LLMs with multimodal integration and alignment via Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). Our approach models temporal relationships between patient visits, propagates information across clinically similar patients, and aligns heterogeneous data sources to construct enriched multimodal context vectors. These vectors are injected into the intermediate layers of the LLM, enabling joint reasoning over textual and non-textual modalities. We evaluate M2G-LLM on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets, demonstrating improvements in clinical prediction tasks over strong baseline models. Our results highlight the promise of combining the language understanding of LLMs with the relational reasoning capabilities of GNNs for comprehensive, multimodal healthcare analysis.

</details>


### [119] [TierKV: Long-Context On-Device LLMs via Predictive Multi-Tier KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21172)
*Zhihao Shu,Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim,Jie Hu,Kun Yuan,Minghai Qin,Gagan Agrawal,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TierKV 是一个面向移动端 LLM 长上下文推理的框架，通过预测式多级（精确 / 低秩 SVD / 闪存卸载）KV 缓存管理，在保持精度的同时显著降低内存占用并提升预填充吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端部署 LLM 时，KV 缓存随序列长度线性增长，成为内存瓶颈；现有低秩压缩存在重建开销、token 驱逐造成不可逆信息丢失、闪存卸载存在 IO 阻塞等问题，难以在受限内存预算下同时兼顾精度与速度。

Method: 提出 PMCO（Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization）：在解码前利用 prefill 隐藏状态预测生成长度与缓存需求，将 token 分到 Tier-0（精确）、Tier-1（联合 SVD 压缩）和 Tier-2（闪存卸载）三层，联合优化每层 SVD 秩与层间边界，并以闭式求解器在线选择 (n, m, R)；运行时采用融合 Split-Path Attention 核（on-chip K 重建 + 潜在空间 V 累积）以及 I/O–计算重叠调度，将 SVD 重建嵌入闪存预取气泡中。

Result: 在三种移动 SoC（Adreno 750/740、Mali-G715）和八个文本/视觉/音频模型上，相比 llama.cpp、MNN-LLM、MLC-LLM，预填充吞吐最高提升 17.6×，RAM 常驻 KV 缓存减少 12.5–34%（平均 25%），相同内存预算下最大上下文长度扩展约 2.6×；精度损失较小（Llama-3.2-3B MMLU -1.7、GSM8K -1.5）；多轮缓存复用显著优于基于驱逐的方法。

Conclusion: 将移动端 KV 缓存视为预测式资源分配问题而非被动压缩，可在严格内存预算下同时保持完整上下文、提升预填充吞吐并控制精度损失；预测式多级缓存设计是面向长上下文端侧 LLM 推理的有效路径，未来可扩展至混合 SSM-注意力模型与前缀缓存场景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are moving onto mobile devices for increasingly diverse workloads over text, images, video, and audio. These applications often require long contexts, making the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck because it grows linearly with sequence length and is accessed at every decoding step. Prior work reduces KV-cache footprint through low-rank compression, token eviction, or flash offloading, but the resulting reconstruction overhead, irreversible token loss, or I/O stalls can offset the benefit of saving memory. We present TierKV, a mobile LLM inference framework built on Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization (PMCO). Before decoding starts, PMCO predicts future cache demand from prefill hidden states and jointly assigns tokens to exact, low-rank, and flash-offloaded tiers under the device memory and accuracy budgets. This formulation retains access to the full context, removes the circular dependency of reactive eviction, and admits a closed-form solver that selects tier boundaries and per-layer ranks at runtime. Across eight text, vision, and audio models on three mobile SoCs, TierKV improves prefill throughput by up to 17.6x over existing mobile LLM frameworks, reduces RAM-resident KV cache by 12.5-34%, thereby enabling substantially longer contexts under the same memory budget, while incurring only minor accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [120] [Reliability-Centered Evaluation of Sparse Longitudinal CT Lesion-Size Forecasting with Conformal Interval Calibration and Gompertz-Inspired Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21197)
*Lingfei Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对稀疏纵向CT随访中病灶增长预测面临的少样本挑战，构建了基于DeepLesion与Deep Lesion Tracker的五次访视基准轨迹数据（205条轨迹/129例患者），系统比较了不同不确定性量化方法（MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble、残差尺度、共形校准等）在固定预测地平线（T3→T4的对数变化量）下引入更早历史观测的预测精度、不确定性可靠性及轨迹一致性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向CT随访中病灶大小预测常受限于稀疏观测（仅少数先验访视可用），传统稀疏到终点的分析方法难以控制预测地平线，导致"额外历史信息是否真正提升预测"无法厘清；且预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性常被分开评估，缺乏联合考量。

Method: 1) 数据：基于DeepLesion与DLT构建五次访视（Visit 1–5）的同一病灶纵向轨迹基准（205条/129例）；2) 设计：采用固定访视索引地平线（预测T3→T4的log变化量），逐步加入更早观测m=1,2,3,4；3) 方法：MC Dropout、Deep Ensemble、Cohort-Level Feature GP、残差尺度不确定性、共形后校准；4) 正则：Gompertz启发的轨迹一致性正则，λ*由患者分组CV选择；5) 评估：RMSE、覆盖度、区间宽度、解剖学亚组性能、全局群体参考对比。

Result: 1) 十个种子的平均RMSE：m=1/2/3/4分别为0.4726/0.4305/0.4499/0.4513，m=2时最低，增加历史未带来RMSE增益；2) 覆盖度：m=4时Cohort-Level Feature GP接近95%标称水平，Deep Ensemble、MC Dropout、残差尺度偏保守；3) 患者级共形校准接近或优于标称覆盖但区间更宽；4) Gompertz正则最优λ*=0（未被选中）；5) 全局群体参考常与个体病灶变化方向相反；6) 各解剖学亚组预测难度差异显著。

Conclusion: 在固定预测地平线下，额外的历史观测对稀疏纵向CT病灶预测带来的增益有限；预测精度、不确定性可靠性与轨迹一致性三者并不必然同步提升，应在稀疏纵向影像任务中进行联合评估。

Abstract: Sparse longitudinal CT follow-up limits lesion-size forecasting when only a few prior observations are available. We constructed a five-visit DLT-derived same-lesion trajectory benchmark from DeepLesion and Deep Lesion Tracker (DLT), yielding 205 trajectories from 129 patients. We compared an exploratory conventional sparse-to-final analysis with a primary fixed visit-index horizon design predicting the common log change from T3 to T4 while progressively adding earlier observations, evaluating predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, post-hoc conformal interval calibration, subgroup performance, and Gompertz-inspired trajectory regularization. The evaluated methods showed partially overlapping point-prediction accuracy but distinct uncertainty behavior. Mean held-out RMSE across ten training seeds was 0.4726, 0.4305, 0.4499, and 0.4513 for m = 1, 2, 3, 4, indicating the lowest mean RMSE at m = 2; additional history did not improve RMSE. At m = 4, raw Cohort-Level Feature GP coverage was near the 95% nominal level, whereas MC Dropout, Deep Ensemble, and residual-scale intervals were conservative. Patient-level conformal calibration generally produced near-nominal or conservative coverage at the cost of wider intervals. Patient-grouped development cross-validation selected lambda* = 0 for the Gompertz-inspired term. A global population reference frequently opposed lesion-level change directions, and prediction difficulty varied across anatomical subgroups. Overall, additional historical observations provided limited predictive benefit once the prediction horizon was controlled, while predictive accuracy, uncertainty reliability, and trajectory consistency did not necessarily improve together, and should be evaluated jointly in sparse longitudinal imaging.

</details>


### [121] [MIRCID: Inferred Hub-miRNAs Drive Cross-Task Improvements in Drug Mechanistic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21280)
*Xin Cao,Yigang Chen,Jiatong Xu,Ziyue Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Shenyu Wang,Yangyi Zhang,Xiaoxuan Cai,Shidong Cui,Zihao Zhu,Xiang Ji,Hsi-Yuan Huang,Yang-Chi-Dung Lin,Hsien-Da Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIRCID提出一个比较框架，评估从L1000转录组数据推断的转录因子活性和miRNA表达在药物机制建模中的价值。核心贡献HubmiRNet（残差网络）从977个标志基因推断414个核心miRNA（PCC 87.72%），并在通路分类和MoA检索两项任务中证明miRNA增强比TF活性更一致地提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物扰动转录组（如L1000）通常缺乏匹配的miRNA测量数据。已有方法如SiCmiR可推断miRNA表达，但其推断特征在药物扰动建模中的下游价值尚不明确，特别是与转录因子活性（TFA）相比孰优孰劣。本研究旨在系统比较不同推断调控特征层在药物机制建模任务中的迁移效用。

Method: 1) HubmiRNet：残差神经网络（977输入→4096→3个残差块→414/1298输出），SGD训练（lr=0.4，cosine退火，2500 epoch），从L1000标志基因推断HubmiR表达。2) TFA推断基准测试：比较VIPER、TIGER、Priori三种算法×CollecTRI、DoRothEA、DoRothEA(Pancancer)三种网络，选定Priori+DoRothEA。3) 下游任务评估：PROGENy通路分类（11类，4种模型：RF/MLP/KAN/ResNet）和L1000药物MoA检索（4种评分算法×6细胞系）。4) 表征分析：CCA、CKA、多输出岭回归线性可恢复性评估，以及PCA/AE/RP作为通用嵌入对照。

Result: 1) HubmiRNet在414-HubmiR任务上PCC达87.72%（RMSE=0.491），优于MLP和Transformer；1298-miRNA任务PCC 71.21% vs SiCmiR的67.30%。2) Gene+HubmiR在所有4种分类模型中均获最高平均准确率和macro-F1，准确率提升2.52–6.39百分点，F1提升2.13–6.94百分点；TFA增益较小且不稳定。3) CCA/CKA分析显示HubmiR保留基因来源结构但具有部分独立、非线性的表征几何。4) MoA检索中LM+Mi一致优于LM单独；混合效应模型证实miRNA系数为正，TF系数接近零或为负。5) 50/595样本-运行被HubmiR"挽救"（Gene-only错→Gene+HubmiR对），12被"损害"，挽救/损害比4.17倍。

Conclusion: 推断的HubmiR特征作为转录组数据的"生物学信息重编码"，在通路分类和MoA检索两类药物扰动建模任务中提供比TF活性更一致、更可迁移的增益。其效用源于从配对mRNA-miRNA监督中学到的非线性结构，而非额外独立观测。控制分析表明该增益超越通用嵌入压缩，但仍需在独立测量的扰动miRNA上进一步验证其恢复真实调控响应的能力。

Abstract: Drug mechanism-of-action (MoA) modeling commonly relies on perturbational transcriptomes, but matched microRNA (miRNA) measurements are often unavailable. Inferred regulatory features offer a scalable way to reuse these data. Here, we present MIRCID, a framework comparing gene expression with inferred transcription factor (TF) activity and miRNA expression across pathway classification and similarity-based MoA retrieval. HubmiRNet infers 414 pan-cancer hub miRNAs (HubmiRs) from 977 L1000 landmark genes, achieving a Pearson correlation coefficient of 87.72\%; its 1,298-output variant also outperformed SiCmiR on the full-miRNA task (71.21\% versus 67.30\%). In the evaluated comparisons, miRNA augmentation provided more consistent gains than TF activity. Generic embedding controls showed model-dependent utility, while complementarity analyses identified a distinct, partially linearly recoverable representation that retained gene-derived structure. Illustrative rescue cases linked improved classification to biologically plausible miRNA patterns in samples with weak transcriptional signatures. These findings support inferred HubmiRs as a biologically informed recoding of transcriptomic data for perturbational drug modeling, while leaving recovery of measured perturbational miRNA responses to further validation.

</details>


### [122] [Multi-Subject Pretraining Enables Short-Calibration Personalization for Closed-Corpus Surface EMG Speech Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21288)
*Chenqian Le,Beatrice Fumagalli,Yasamin Esmaeili,Xupeng Chen,Tianyu He,Nikasadat Emami,Adeen Flinker,Yao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于表面肌电(sEMG)的静默语音接口在有限数据条件下的跨被试个性化方法。通过27名被试的留一被试交叉验证，证明了多被试预训练结合少量目标被试校准(约3分钟)即可在50句封闭语料上实现21.7%字符错误率(CER)和31.9%词错误率(WER)，显著优于零样本迁移和直接微调单被试检查点。预训练被试数量越多，个性化效果越好(从1人增至26人，CER从74.4%降至21.7%)。但个性化效果高度依赖于预训练中出现的句子，在未见句子上CER飙升至78.6%，表明该方法尚不具备开放内容解码能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于sEMG的静默语音接口面临两大核心障碍：(1)跨用户差异性大导致模型泛化困难；(2)新用户需要大量校准数据，实际部署不便。以往研究要么依赖大量单被试数据，要么只解决预训练或校准一个方面的问题。本文旨在系统研究在每位参与者数据极少(<0.5小时)的受限场景下，同时量化预训练被试规模和目标被试校准时长两个数据缩放因素对个性化的影响。

Method: 采用留一被试交叉验证(LOSO)，27名语音正常参与者使用标准化的8通道面部/颈部sEMG装置录制50句TIMIT语料的Aloud和Mimed发音。模型架构为基于Gaddy & Klein的卷积-Transformer编解码器，CTC目标函数预测字符序列，配合KenLM语言模型解码。流程为：(1)从已发布的单被试检查点初始化；(2)在26名非留出被试上进行多被试预训练；(3)在留出被试的约13分钟校准数据上微调。对比条件包括：零样本迁移、随机初始化预训练+微调、直接微调检查点、是否使用被试专属MLP适配器等。校准时长扫描测试了1/3/5/10/13分钟。还进行了未见句子测试，即将5句评估句从所有sEMG训练数据中移除。

Result: 主要结果：(1)多被试预训练+目标被试微调(MLP)达到最优21.7% CER/31.9% WER，远优于零样本迁移(49.3% CER)和直接检查点微调(67.6-68.0% CER)；(2)随机初始化预训练效果较差(44.9% CER)，且5/27折不收敛，说明检查点初始化贡献显著；(3)预训练被试从1增至26人，宏平均CER从74.4%降至21.7%，所有27人都有改善；(4)3分钟校准(20.5% CER)与完整13分钟池(21.7% CER)无统计学显著差异；(5)被试专属MLP适配器无显著收益；(6)未见句子测试中CER飙升至78.6%、WER达99.9%，表明仅在封闭语料上有效；(7)Mimed始终比Aloud更难(27.9% vs 15.4% CER)。

Conclusion: 在封闭语料和有限数据场景下，多被试预训练结合短时长(约3分钟)目标被试校准可有效实现sEMG解码器的跨被试个性化，且需要相当数量的预训练被试(>15)才能获得显著收益。检查点初始化在优化稳定性和精度两方面都贡献重要。然而，该方法严重依赖于预训练中出现的句子内容，对完全未见句子的泛化能力极差，因此不应被解读为开放词汇的语音解码能力。未来需验证其在纵向使用、电极偏移和临床用户群体中的表现。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG)-based silent speech interfaces are limited by cross-user variability and calibration burden. We study a limited-data setting in which each of 27 speech-typical participants contributed less than 0.5 h of data (21.3 min on average) across Aloud and Mimed speech. Within a closed 50-sentence corpus, we used leave-one-subject-out evaluation, initializing from a released single-subject checkpoint, pretraining on non-held-out participants, and fine-tuning on the target participant. This pipeline achieved 21.7% character error rate (CER) and 31.9% word error rate (WER), compared with 49.3% CER without target-subject calibration and 68.0% CER for direct checkpoint fine-tuning. Multi-subject pretraining from random initialization followed by fine-tuning reached 44.9% CER and did not converge under the fixed schedule in 5 of 27 folds, indicating substantial optimization and accuracy benefits from checkpoint initialization. Macro-averaged CER declined from 74.4% with one pretraining participant to 21.7% with 26. Three minutes of target-subject calibration achieved 20.5% CER and 31.7% WER, with no statistically significant difference from the full approximately 13-min pool (21.7% CER and 31.9% WER). A subject-specific adapter provided no detectable benefit. Excluding the five evaluation sentences from all sEMG model-training data increased CER and WER to 78.6% and 99.9%. These results support short-calibration personalization in a standardized-montage, closed-corpus setting.

</details>


### [123] [FairLMs: A Turnkey Library for Fairness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21296)
*Jiale Zhang,Michael Larionov,Zichong Wang,Zhipeng Yin,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairLMs 是一个 Python 库，通过显式声明模型能力和输入需求，将语言模型公平性研究的三个核心活动——偏见度量、缓解方法和数据集/评分工具诊断——整合在统一接口下，包含33个度量指标、14个缓解组件和14个诊断组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在高风险领域应用日益广泛，其偏见行为可能对弱势群体造成伤害。然而现有的公平性工具各自只覆盖工作流的一部分（如通用公平工具包、评估框架、偏见基准库、嵌入级库等），研究者需要为每个度量单独寻找实现并适配不同的模型接口、证据格式、访问约束和结果类型，导致工作流碎片化且难以比较不同方法的结果。

Method: FairLMs 采用基于声明（declaration-based）的设计架构，包含四层结构：(1) 证据容器和模型适配器提供输入；(2) 度量、缓解和诊断组件在执行前通过声明检查兼容性；(3) 每个组件声明其支持的架构、所需能力、接受的证据容器和返回类型；(4) 结果携带完整配置信息（provenance）。支持三种 Transformer 架构（仅编码器、仅解码器、编解码器）和托管完成 API，包含16个内置基准加载器。通过 `apply` 统一接口和 `compare_before_after` 方法实现缓解前后的统一评估。

Result: FairLMs 提供33个内在和外在偏见度量指标（如 WEAT、SEAT、CrowS-Pairs、equal-opportunity gap）、14个缓解组件（涵盖四种干预类别）、14个数据集和评分工具诊断（分为代表性、刻板印象泄露、构建偏差和评分工具行为四组）、16个基准加载器，支持三种 Transformer 架构和托管完成 API。通过参考实现验证正确性，对有公开参考值的度量家族在相同检查点和证据上复现了已发表的值；使用 golden fixtures 固定诊断值；契约测试套件检查每个组件的声明和适用性。

Conclusion: FairLMs 通过让度量、缓解和诊断组件声明其对模型和证据的要求，使得兼容组件可以组合使用、缓解后的系统可在相同配置下重新度量、结果背后的数据集和评分工具可被审计。遵循相同声明规范的新组件可在不修改库的情况下扩展其功能，为语言模型公平性研究提供了一个可复用、可比较、可扩展的整合平台。

Abstract: Fairness research on language models involves measuring bias, applying mitigation methods, and examining the evidence on which an evaluation rests. Existing tools offer complementary functionality through different interfaces, so combining them requires reconciling model interfaces, evidence formats, access constraints, and result types before applicability can be checked or methods compared. We introduce \textbf{FairLMs}, a Python library that connects these activities through explicit declarations of model capabilities and input requirements. It provides 33 intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, 14 mitigation components spanning four intervention categories, 14 dataset and scoring-instrument diagnostics, adapters for the three Transformer architectures and supported hosted completion APIs, and benchmark loaders. Declarations are checked before execution and results carry the configuration under which they were obtained, so that compatible components can be combined, methods compared under a common protocol, and workflows extended to new models and datasets. The source code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [124] [Fast And Accurate Text Content File Type Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21306)
*Manu Nandan,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 Labeler——一种用于文本内容文件类型识别的轻量级双输入 CNN 模型，配合自定义分词器，在准确率（Macro F1=0.98 vs 0.91）、速度（3.7× 更快）和体积（28% 更小）上均优于 Magika。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全等场景需要快速准确的文件类型识别工具；签名方法易规避且对文本类型准确率低，Magika 等模型方法计算开销大、占用多 CPU，影响用户体验。

Method: 自定义分词器保留符号并按大小写边界分割子词；双输入 CNN 处理文件首尾各 4KB，共享 Embedding 与 1D Conv 层后拼接；模型约 715KB，单 CPU 推理。

Result: Macro F1=0.9804 vs Magika 0.9057；推理 1.05ms vs 3.85ms（3.7× 更快）；模型小 28%；所有 54 种文件类型 F1 均高于 Magika，稀有类型仅 2000 训练样本即超越 Magika。

Conclusion: Labeler 在文本文件类型识别任务上实现 Pareto 改进，证明针对领域定制（分词器+架构）比通用大规模模型在有限算力预算下更有效，样本效率高，适合网络安全等运行时受限场景。

Abstract: A common requirement across organizations is to have a tool that can identify file types based on their contents, particularly in the cybersecurity domain where magic numbers and file extensions can not be trusted. While existing tools work well in practice, there is plenty of room for improvement either in terms of computational load and time for detection in the case of model based tools like Magika or in terms of accuracy of detection in the case of file parsing tools that use programming language constructs. In this study, we propose a neural network model for identification of types of text content files, especially source code, that is more accurate and faster than other available tools. Our experiments on open-source files indicate that it is not only more accurate on average for text-content file-type identification, but also approximately four times faster than Magika, while being 28% smaller in size.

</details>


### [125] [An Introduction to Compression-Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21309)
*John Hurwitz,Edward Raff,Charles K. Nicholas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述并形式化了基于压缩的机器学习方法，提出由压缩算法、上下文、压缩度量、预测技术四个设计选择构成的统一框架。核心思想是无损压缩算法（如gzip）可通过归一化压缩距离（NCD）或最小描述长度（MDL）原理转化为机器学习方法；任何自回归模型可通过熵编码转化为无损压缩方法。作者在文本、恶意软件、图像三个领域进行了实证验证，发现压缩方法与传统基线方法具有竞争力，且在恶意软件分类上显著优于基线，设计选择的不同配置可使准确率提升高达0.62。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mahoney早在1999年就指出预测下一个字符应作为AI的试金石，并注意到该任务等价于压缩。尽管此后AI和压缩都取得了巨大进展，但二者之间的交叉研究仍属小众领域且潜力未被充分发掘。压缩与预测在信息论中本质等价（MacKay称之为"一枚硬币的两面"），这一联系为利用压缩解决机器学习问题提供了理论基础。作者旨在系统性地综述和形式化这一领域，并回答一个核心问题：更好的压缩比是否能转化为更好的预测性能？

Method: 1. 理论基础：建立从Kolmogorov复杂度→信息距离→归一化信息距离→归一化压缩距离（NCD）的理论链条；通过Shannon信源编码定理和交叉熵建立压缩长度与概率/似然的对应关系，证明MDL与MAP推断的等价性。2. 统一框架：将现有压缩式ML方法形式化为四个设计选择——压缩算法C、上下文S（含上下文源和上下文注入机制）、压缩度量M、预测技术P。3. 两大范式：压缩即相似性（NCD，通过k-NN或聚类预测）与压缩即似然（MDL，选择最佳压缩模型的类别）。4. 实验设计：在AG News（文本）、Drebin（恶意软件）、MNIST（图像）三个数据集上，系统地扫描压缩程序（gzip、bzip2、LZMA、zstandard）、压缩度量（NCD、CDM、CLM、CosS、条件码长）、预测技术（k-NN、MDL）等设计选择，包含少样本和全数据集两种设置。

Result: 1. 压缩式ML方法与传统的逻辑回归、线性SVM、TF-IDF n-grams基线方法在文本和图像领域具有竞争力，在恶意软件分类上则显著优于基线（少样本提升高达+0.266）。2. 设计选择对性能影响显著，跨配置扫描可使准确率提升高达0.62（MNIST数据集上从0.27提升至约0.88）。3. MDL相比NCD计算复杂度更低（O(n|Y|) vs O(n²)），且在文本和恶意软件领域常获得更高准确率。4. 聚合操作（aggregation operation）是关键设计选择：MNIST上字节串拼接导致性能退化（0.27），而像素级加法聚合可使性能提升至0.88。5. zstandard从压缩级别3提升至15在110个配置中有82个获得改善，平均提升0.016。6. 证明了一个统一命题：在上下文压缩长度均匀的条件下，NCD和MDL产生相同的候选排序，从而揭示二者本质上的统一性。

Conclusion: 作者通过将压缩式机器学习形式化为四个核心设计选择的统一框架，揭示了该领域文献的统一结构，并证明该框架具有实用价值。实验表明，简单的gzip+k-NN参考配置远非最优，通过系统性地调整设计选择可获得显著性能提升。该领域在恶意软件分类上具有独特优势（深层学习难以处理可执行文件的表示学习）。未来研究方向包括：利用神经压缩器（如LLM、VAE）改进压缩式ML（初步结果显示神经压缩器的压缩比优势未必转化为NCD分类性能优势，可能由于C(xy)项的机制差异）、改进或发明新的压缩表征（如LZJD、BWMD）、以及替代的聚合方法（特别是在神经压缩背景下）。总体而言，压缩与机器学习的交叉领域仍存在大量待探索的开放问题，呼应了Mahoney关于"更好压缩促进AI进步"的历史预言。

Abstract: Any lossless compression algorithm (like gzip) may be converted into a machine learning method, via either Normalized Compression Distance or the Minimum Description Length principle. Any auto-regressive model may be converted into a lossless compression method via entropy coding. This seemingly circular dependence has unrealized potential in modern artificial intelligence and machine learning, and we survey and formalize the various strategies that have been used to leverage compression for machine learning. We introduce and empirically validate a design framework for compression-based ML, finding compression-based methods competitive with conventional baselines and decisively stronger on malware. We find that varying these design choices yields accuracy gains of up to 0.62.

</details>


### [126] [Routine Blood Tests Outperform CRP for Distinguishing Bacterial From Viral Infection in Children](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21332)
*Mihaela Demireva,Zhecho Mitev,Djuna Chinareva-Klimentova,Svetoslav Ivanov,Georgi Nalbantov,Dimitar Mitev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对906名2-14岁儿科患者，比较了基于血常规（CBC）+CRP的机器学习模型与仅用CRP的传统决策规则在区分细菌与病毒感染上的表现。最佳XGBoost模型AUC达81.7%，显著优于CRP基线模型（57.4%），且WBC、LYM、MON%比CRP更具诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科急性感染中病毒与细菌的鉴别是临床难题，CRP单独使用存在局限（10-40 mg/L灰区、早期敏感度低、某些病毒感染也可升高），导致儿科医生倾向于过度处方抗生素，加剧抗菌素耐药性问题。在成人中已有研究表明多标志物联合可提升鉴别准确率，而儿童群体中常规血检组合的诊断潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 回顾性研究，纳入906名2-14岁确诊病毒或细菌感染的儿科患者（病毒482例/细菌424例）。使用18个CBC参数+CRP+年龄作为输入特征，比较逻辑回归（LR）与XGBoost（带/不带CRP共4个模型），以5折分层交叉验证调参和评估，以AUC、敏感度、特异度为指标，并通过SHAP进行特征重要性解释。基线模型为仅以CRP=22 mg/L为切点的决策规则。另设50例保持集（16病毒/34细菌）作为外部验证。

Result: 最佳模型为含CRP的XGBoost，AUC=81.7%（95% CI 78.8%-84.3%），敏感度70.8%，特异度79.2%。不含CRP的XGBoost AUC为80.8%（仅低1个百分点），说明CRP贡献边际。LR模型AUC约79.7-79.8%，特异度更高（82-84%）但敏感度较低（66-68%）。CRP基线模型AUC仅57.4%，敏感度30.9%，特异度85.6%。保持集验证趋势相同（XGBoost w/CRP AUC=74.1%）。SHAP分析显示WBC、LYM、MON%是最有影响力的特征，PDW、NEU%、RDW、MPV、HGB、MCV影响最小。

Conclusion: 含CBC的机器学习模型（特别是XGBoost）显著优于CRP单独决策规则，能更好区分儿童病毒与细菌感染，有望辅助儿科医生减少抗生素过度处方。研究提示WBC、LYM、MON%比CRP更具信息量。局限性：单中心数据、缺乏症状发作至检验的时间间隔、各年龄组样本不足。后续需多中心研究验证，并比较模型与儿科医生实际决策的优劣。

Abstract: Acute infectious diseases are among the leading causes of medical consultations and hospitalizations in children worldwide. These infections are predominantly caused by viruses or bacteria, yet differentiating between the two remains a common clinical challenge. As a result, pediatricians often default to the safer option of prescribing antibiotics contributing to the growing problem of antimicrobial resistance. The objective is to assess the additional predictive value of CBC towards determining the current infection. This retrospective study used data from 906 pediatric patients aged between 2 and 14 years who were tested positive either for viral or bacterial infection between 2022 and 2026. Inclusion criteria further required availability of CBC results and CRP level measurements. These laboratory parameters as well as age were used as input features for several supervised classification models. Model performance was evaluated using AUC, sensitivity and specificity. The best performing model is XGBoost, which included all features, achieving out of-sample performance of AUC of 81.7% and sensitivity of 70.8%, specificity of 79.2%. All trained models outperform a CRP-based only decision-rule model in terms of AUC. We suggest that the decision to prescribe antibiotics should be based on a number of factors, including but not limited to CBC, some of which are not currently incorporated into routine practice.

</details>


### [127] [IntBMoE: Integrating Block-Level Conditioning into Expert Composition for Full-Participation Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21346)
*Ran Cheng,Longfei Xu,Zheng Liu,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IntBMoE，一种块级条件化MoE架构，通过解耦专家参与度、执行成本和参数物化成本三个维度，实现全池参与、稀疏执行和有限物化。在ImageNet-1K、MiniPile语言建模和IntTravel序列推荐任务上均优于现有MoE基线，并已部署于高德地图生成式推荐系统，在60ms延迟预算下服务亿级用户，带来2.4%的相对UVCTR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE设计无法独立控制三个关键量：(1) 参与度（每个token有多少专家贡献知识）、(2) 执行度（实际计算多少专家，决定计算成本）、(3) 物化度（需构建存储多少份专家参数集，决定内存成本）。稀疏路由限制参与度；稠密输出混合增加执行成本；参数合并增加物化成本。三者被耦合在一起，存在根本性的权衡困境。核心问题是：能否让每个token受益于全部专家池，同时不需稠密执行或无限参数物化？

Method: IntBMoE的核心设计包含：(1) 块级参数合成——通过学习码本（K个条目）经轻量超网络（线性-LayerNorm-ReLU主干加两个输出头）为每层专家池生成value和gate两组合成系数，以1/√E方差保持因子进行无约束线性组合（允许负系数，可超出凸包），合成K个可复用的多层块；(2) Token级块路由——每个token通过两层ReLU MLP路由器独立选Top-k块；(3) 块条件化特征过滤——用码本嵌入与token拼接经sigmoid生成软掩码，为不同块提供不同token视图；(4) 双路径残差门控（DPRG）——将专家池分别合成value路径和gate路径，通过v⊙(1+λ·SiLU(g))实现非线性耦合，两路径间加RMSNorm与LayerNorm；(5) 输出聚合——选中的k个块输出按路由概率加权求和，加上一个始终激活的共享SwiGLU专家；(6) 推理缓存——块参数仅依赖学习到的码本/超网络/专家基，模型固定后可一次性预计算缓存，使请求时计算量与专家池大小E解耦。

Result: 主要实验结果：(1) ImageNet-1K上使用8层DeiT-Tiny骨干，IntBMoE达到Top-1 73.76%、Top-5 91.48%，相比稠密骨干提升7.36/3.79个百分点，超过最强基线SMEAR 1.98/1.15个百分点；(3) 消融实验表明gate路径贡献最大，去除后Top-1降至68.04%；(2) 消融研究显示1层块、固定λ、去gate路径、softmax系数化均显著降低性能；(4) 在MiniPile语言建模上PPL降至14.5878（比稠密降低12.4%，比最强基线降低2.9%），IntTravel序列推荐上HR@1达0.6852、HR@5达0.8692、NDCG@5达0.7850，均为最优；(5) 缓存机制使推理内存与FLOPs随专家数E增长保持恒定（104.33MB/3.457 GFLOPs）；(6) 全池参与度验证：移除任一专家基均导致Top-1下降；(7) 块路由呈现类别条件化与深度依赖性，不同块学到不同合成配方（深层余弦相似度降至0.01）；(8) 已在高德地图POI推荐系统全面部署，在阿里巴巴T-Head PPU上平均延迟19ms、P99延迟38ms，处理约5000 QPS，带来2.4%相对UVCTR提升。

Conclusion: IntBMoE成功解耦了MoE中参与度、执行度和物化度三个相互耦合的量，通过"先构建后路由"的两阶段设计，使每个token能从完整专家池受益的同时保持稀疏执行和固定参数开销。DPRG在专家池线性组合基础上引入非线性耦合，增强了块表达能力。该方法在视觉、语言建模和序列推荐任务上均表现优异，并在大规模工业推荐系统中得到验证。其块预计算缓存机制使推理复杂度与专家池规模解耦，是其在60ms严格延迟预算下能服务亿级用户的关键。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) scales capacity, but existing designs cannot set three quantities independently. For a single token, participation is how many experts contribute knowledge to its output, execution is how many are actually computed (compute cost), and materialization is how many expert-sized parameter sets must be built and stored (memory cost). Sparse routing keeps execution and materialization low, but shrinks participation: for each token, only a few experts contribute. Dense output-mixing restores full participation, but its execution grows with the number of experts. Parameter-merging keeps execution at one expert, but its materialization grows with the number of routing decisions. We propose IntBMoE, a block-conditioned MoE that decouples all three by pairing dense expert composition with sparse block execution. Its blocks come from a small learned codebook, one per entry. At each internal layer, a lightweight hypernetwork merges all expert bases in that layer's pool into one composed expert. Participation is full, because every composed expert draws on the entire pool. Execution stays sparse, because a router sends each token to only a few blocks. Materialization is bounded, because the codebook, not the input, fixes how many blocks exist. Dual-Path Residual Gating (DPRG) further couples two independently composed paths through multiplicative gating. Experiments on image classification show consistent gains over representative sparse and dense MoE baselines. Additional experiments on language modeling and sequential recommendation validate its generalization beyond vision. IntBMoE is fully deployed in AMap's generative recommendation system, serving hundreds of millions of users under a 60ms latency budget, with a 2.4% relative UVCTR gain in online A/B testing. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [128] [Probabilistic Forecasting of Business Process Executions with Neural Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21382)
*Jiaxin Yuan,Daniela Grigori,Han van der Aa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoTPP，一种基于Transformer Hawkes Process (THP) 的生成式神经时间点过程模型，用于业务过程执行的多任务预测性监控。核心创新在于通过零膨胀LogNormal混合密度显式处理真实事件日志中普遍存在的相等时间戳问题，并在单一前向传播中同时输出下一个活动、下一事件时间和剩余时间的完整预测分布。在10个公开日志上的实验表明，MoTPP在点准确率上与判别式基线持平，但在剩余时间分布的校准和锐度上占优，且推理速度最快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性过程监控（PPM）主流深度学习方法（LSTM、Transformer、GNN）均为判别式且确定性的，仅输出单一预测点而不提供置信度分布，无法满足运行时QoS和SLA管理对不确定性量化的需求。时间点过程（TPP）作为连续时间事件序列的概率生成模型，天然具备不确定性感知能力，但现有神经TPP假设"简单点过程"（无同时刻事件），而真实事件日志中相当比例（本文研究高达46%）的连续事件具有相同时间戳，导致标准TPP的预测分布在零处赋零概率，违背数据本身的生成机制。

Method: MoTPP保留了THP的Transformer编码器（多头因果自注意力+可选LSTM），替换其输出层为三个线性预测头：(1) Tie head产生零膨胀logit π（瞬时转移概率）；(2) Mixture head参数化K个LogNormal分量的混合密度，捕捉多模态事件间隔；(3) Activity head产生下一活动分类logit。三个头联合参数化下一活动与下一时间的条件联合密度p*(a,τ) = p*(a)·p*(τ)，通过精确对数似然进行训练。推理时：下一活动取argmax；下一时间取闭式期望；剩余时间通过S=50次蒙特卡洛自回归采样获得经验分布。其关键贡献是采用零膨胀LogNormal混合密度——以概率π将τ=0建模为点质量，以概率1-π从K分量LogNormal混合中生成τ>0，从而显式建模相等时间戳。

Result: 在10个公开日志上对比6类基线（THP-B、SuTraN、ED-LSTM、Hawkes变体、LA-CR、UQ方法）：(1) 点准确率：MoTPP在Group A（高频小词汇量日志）上最优（如Sepsis上剩余时间MAE 9.46d vs SuTraN 14.83d），但SuTraN/ED-LSTM在Group B（低频大词汇量）下一活动准确率更优；(2) 不确定性：MoTPP在6/10数据集上校准误差MA最低，AURG在4/10上最高，但MPIW锐度不如重校准回归方法；(3) 效率：MoTPP训练208分钟（远低于SuTraN 706分钟、LA-CR 1541分钟），推理0.03ms/prefix（比SuTraN快52倍）。作者承认剩余时间评估存在"horizon asymmetry"——MoTPP使用了真实剩余长度J和真实中间事件（teacher forcing），而其他模型使用专用head不含此信息。

Conclusion: MoTPP是首个面向多任务PPM的生成式神经TPP，能原生处理真实事件日志中的相等时间戳。其核心价值不在点准确率的全面超越，而在于提供运行时服务监控所需的两个关键特性：(1) 校准的剩余时间分布支持基于风险的SLA决策；(2) 单次前向传播的低延迟推理适应在线服务时延预算。生成式范式将预测监控从点估计提升为可信且可操作的风险量化工具。未来方向包括：更丰富的TPP模型、后缀预测等扩展任务，以及将预测分布反馈到服务组合的运行时自适应（如SLA保护的重路由或准入控制）。论文也指出数据层面的限制——粗粒度时间戳和高质量事件日志的稀缺制约了模型能力上限。

Abstract: Operators of service-based systems act on forecasts of how a running execution will continue, and such a forecast is actionable only if its reliability is known. Mainstream deep-learning models for this task are discriminative and deterministic: they emit a single next activity and a single remaining-time estimate, without a distribution to reason over. We instead cast the problem as generative sequence modelling with marked temporal point processes, which define a joint density over the next mark and its inter-event time and therefore deliver predictive distributions by construction. Real event logs violate the simple-point-process assumption these models rest on, since consecutive events frequently carry identical timestamps; we handle such ties explicitly and combine a transformer encoder with a mixture decoder over inter-event times, trained by exact log-likelihood. On ten public logs, the resulting model matches discriminative baselines on point accuracy, dominates them on the calibration and sharpness of remaining-time distributions, and is the cheapest at inference, since a full predictive distribution is obtained in a single forward pass without sampling.

</details>


### [129] [Tracing the Evidence Behind Zero-Shot Time-Series Forecasting: A Source-First Taxonomy and Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21425)
*Delun Kong,Wanyun Ling,Chenxi Liu,Ziyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对零样本时间序列预测（zero-shot TSF）领域"零样本"定义模糊的问题，提出应将其视为"证据访问声明"而非单纯的"无参数更新"训练状态。作者构建了一个以证据来源为先的分类法，区分三类主要证据来源（冻结LLM先验复用、参数化时间序列预训练、检索增强外部记忆），并提出四项跨切审计维度（任务接口、预测对象与评分、预测时上下文、资源预算），最终主张在基准排行榜中披露证据边界与接口假设，使基准进展真正反映可迁移的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前零样本时间序列预测的"无目标特定参数更新"条件，未明确规定系统可使用何种证据。冻结LLM经序列化提示、基于广泛预测语料预训练的时序模型、以及检索增强预测器，均可满足"无更新"条件，却依赖截然不同的可迁移证据。现有以架构为中心的综述无法区分这些系统在证据访问层面的根本差异，导致排行榜分数不可比、基准进展无法真实反映可迁移能力。

Method: 1. 提出"证据边界"概念：区分评估前（预训练参数）与评估中（提示、协变量、检索）可用的信息源。
2. 构建源优先分类法（三层）：顶层分三类证据来源——冻结LLM先验复用（含数值序列化LLMTime、预测感知提示LSTPrompt）、参数化预训练（含数值时序预训练Lag-Llama/TimesFM/Chronos/MOIRAI/Time-MoE、归纳偏置导向TTM/Mamba4Cast/TiRex/Reverso、跨模态对齐ChatTime）、检索增强记忆（含可学习检索TimeRAF、检索模式融合TS-RAG、查询引导检索过滤Cross-RAG）。
3. 提出四项跨切审计条件：任务接口（编码/提示方式）、预测对象与评分（点预测vs概率预测及其评分规则）、预测时上下文（可用变量、协变量、检索序列）、资源预算（模型规模、采样预算、检索库规模）。
4. 给出最低披露清单与基准治理议程：建议将排行榜行视为"证据契约"，按证据访问分设封闭证据、检索声明、开放证据三条赛道，并进行证据通道消融诊断。

Result: 1. 提供了可操作的源优先分类法，将代表性方法按其报告配置中的主要证据来源归类（如图1、图2所示），并区分了主次证据通道。
2. 形成了一套含五项最低披露项（证据访问、任务接口、预测对象与评分、预测时上下文、资源预算）的清单（表3）。
3. 通过LLMTime、Chronos、TimeRAF三个案例（表2）展示了同一"无更新"标签下证据访问与评估条件的根本差异。
4. 提出三层基准治理赛道划分（封闭证据、检索声明、开放证据）与证据通道消融诊断方法。

Conclusion: 零样本时序预测应同时通过证据访问与评估条件来解读。源优先分类法区分了冻结LLM复用、参数化时序预训练与检索增强记忆三类证据来源；四项审计维度记录每个分数的产生方式；同时报告两者方能将排行榜条目转化为可审计的证据声明，而非孤立排名。该论文为使零样本TSF基准真正反映可迁移预测能力、推动领域透明化提供了系统性的治理框架与未来研究议程。

Abstract: Zero-shot time-series forecasting (TSF) is often described as forecasting without target-specific parameter updates, but that training-status condition does not specify what evidence the system may use. A frozen language model prompted with serialized values, a time-series model pretrained on broad forecasting corpora, and a retrieval-augmented forecaster may all satisfy the no-update condition while drawing on different transferable evidence. This paper argues that zero-shot TSF should therefore be governed as an evidence-access claim. We propose a source-first taxonomy that separates three primary evidence sources---frozen LLM prior reuse, parametric time-series pretraining, and retrieval-augmented external memory---from the architectures that implement them. After the source is identified, four additional audit questions remain: task interface, forecast object and scoring, prediction-time context, and resource budget. The resulting agenda is to make zero-shot leaderboards auditable by reporting evidence boundaries and interface assumptions alongside scores, so that benchmark progress reflects transferable forecasting capability rather than undisclosed changes in context, memory, or budget.

</details>


### [130] [Decision-Focused Learning for Mean-Variance Portfolio Optimization via KKT-Based Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21427)
*Kensei Nosaka,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对均值-方差投资组合优化（MVO）提出了一种基于KKT最优性条件的决策聚焦学习（DFL）单层优化方法，避免了现有DFL方法因使用代理损失或约束松弛而导致预测训练与评估时约束MVO之间的结构性失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两阶段框架先预测期望收益再求解MVO，预测误差最小化与下游组合决策质量不对齐。现有DFL方法（如SPO+、IPO）为可处理性而依赖代理损失或松弛约束，造成训练与评估的结构性失配。

Method: 将MVO-based DFL建模为双层优化问题，下层为带预算和卖空约束的MVO；利用下层MVO的KKT最优性条件（含驻点条件、原始可行、对偶可行和互补松弛条件）将双层问题转化为单层非线性优化问题，可由标准非线性优化求解器（KNITRO）直接求解。此外引入ℓ₂正则化项，将预测模型参数锚定至参考解（如IPO-CF或PFL解），以缓解非凸互补条件带来的数值不稳定性。

Result: 在2003–2025年真实ETF数据上，采用滚动窗口回测，对比8只国际ETF和9只S&P 500行业ETF两种资产池。在国际池中，DFL-KKT取得最高Sharpe Ratio（1.101）和最终财富（4.590）以及最低累计决策损失（1.644）；在行业池中，同样取得最高SR（1.136）和FW（6.490）以及最低CDL（2.969）。正则化在两种参考解设置下均持续提升累积财富表现。

Conclusion: 所提KKT-based单层DFL方法在不松弛MVO约束的前提下实现决策聚焦学习，并在多项投资指标上优于SPO+、IPO-CF、IPO-GRAD、PFL及被动策略。正则化方案有效提升稳定性。未来方向包括：降低高维资产下的计算成本、处理非凸互补条件引起的局部最优、以及引入基数约束、一致性风险度量与鲁棒优化扩展。

Abstract: Mean-variance portfolio optimization (MVO) is a central framework in data-driven asset management. A widely adopted approach is a two-stage framework that first predicts expected returns and then solves the optimization problem based on these predictions, with the predictive models trained by minimizing prediction errors. However, this objective of prediction is not aligned with the quality of the downstream portfolio decision. Decision-focused learning (DFL), which directly minimizes the downstream decision loss within the learning process, has thus emerged as a promising direction. However, existing DFL approaches to MVO rely on surrogate losses or constraint relaxations for tractability, creating a structural mismatch between predictive model training and the constrained MVO solved at evaluation. We propose a single-level optimization formulation that incorporates the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions of the lower-level MVO into the upper-level learning problem. This formulation explicitly preserves the budget and short-sale constraints while remaining tractable for standard nonlinear optimization solvers. Rolling-window experiments on real-world ETF (Exchange Traded Funds) data across two asset universes with different correlation structures show that our method achieved the best performance on multiple investment metrics and also demonstrated performance improvement due to the proposed regularization.

</details>


### [131] [Optimal Randomized Proper Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21445)
*Zachary Chase,Idan Mehalel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了随机化恰当在线学习（randomized proper online learning）在可实现设置下的最优期望错误界为 O(L(H) log T)，其中 L(H) 是 Littlestone 维度，T 是时间跨度。该结果将 Daskalakis 和 Golowich (STOC 2022) 的已知最优上界 O(L(H) log⁶T) 改进至 O(L(H) log T)，并在最坏情况下达到了最优（至多差一个通用常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线学习中，虽然 Littlestone 维度已经对非恰当学习（improper learning）的最优错误界给出了精确刻画（即 M(H,T) = min{L(H), T}），但恰当学习（proper learning）的最优错误界 M_prop(H,T) 此前缺乏紧致界。恰当学习在许多实际场景中至关重要——例如当假设类编码了预定义的模型族、可行的决策集合，或需要保持语义/可解释性约束时，非恰当分类器无法有意义地实现。确定恰当学习中 polylog(T) 因子是否必要，是此前的公开问题。

Method: 上界证明（第4节）：对任意可学习类 H 和时间跨度 T ≥ 2，构造一个随机化恰当学习算法，使其期望错误数不超过 O(L(H) log T)。下界证明（第5节）：对每个正整数 d，构造一个 Littlestone 维度为 O(d) 的类 H，使得当 T ≥ d² 时，任意随机化恰当学习器的期望错误数至少为 Ω(L(H) log T)，证明 log T 因子在最坏情况下是必要的。

Result: 定理 1.1 证明了 M_prop(H, T) = O(L(H) log T)。上界对所有类 H 成立；下界表明存在类使得 M_prop(H, T) = Ω(L(H) log T)（当 T 足够大时）。这将先前 Daskalakis-Golowich (2022) 的 O(L(H) logT) 上界大幅改进，并回答了他们提出的公开问题——log T 因子是必要且充分的。

Conclusion: 本文为随机化恰当在线学习提供了最优（至多常数倍）的期望错误界 O(L(H) log T)，确定了恰当学习与非恰当学习之间的计算复杂性差距——恰当学习在最坏情况下确实需要额外的 log T 因子代价。这一结果在理论和实践上都有重要意义，揭示了在要求预测器必须属于给定假设类时不可避免的额外学习开销。

Abstract: We prove that the optimal expected mistake bound of online learning a function class $\mathcal{H}$ by a randomized proper learning algorithm is $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log T)$, where $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ is the Littlestone dimension of $\mathcal{H}$ and $T$ is the time horizon. Our result improves upon the previously best known bound of $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log^6 T)$ given by Daskalakis and Golowich (STOC 2022), and is optimal up to a universal constant for worst-case classes.

</details>


### [132] [Understanding LLM Quantization through Activation-Guided Compensation and Orthogonal Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21450)
*Yamato Narita,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型W4A4后训练量化，提出了一种精确的局部误差分解框架，将量化误差分解为激活引导的权重补偿项（AGWC）和正交残差两部分。通过对残差进行界分析，给出了随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择和通道缩放的实践指导，并提出了无需反向传播的L2-SmoothRot方法，在8个Llama和Mistral模型上取得了与基于梯度训练的SpinQuant相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W4A4激进量化中激活异常值会降低有效量化分辨率。虽然现有方法（权重优化、通道缩放、正交旋转）能缓解该问题，但它们各自针对的误差分量及相互关系不清晰，缺乏统一的理论框架来指导这些变换的设计与组合。

Method: 1) 将局部权重-激活量化误差精确分解为激活引导的权重补偿（AGWC）项和正交残差；2) 利用持久性通道异常值（CO）和常规激活量对残差进行界估计；3) 分析Hadamard旋转下的固定、随机和采样三种情形对CO量的影响；4) 从正则项的二阶矩界推导L2缩放规则，进一步松弛可恢复SmoothQuant风格的L∞缩放；5) 基于理论提出L2-SmoothRot方法，包含符号化在线旋转（SOR）、校准驱动的符号采样（SS）和L2通道缩放（L2S）。

Result: 在8个Llama和Mistral模型（1B-13B）上的W4A4+KV4量化实验中，L2-SmoothRot在所有模型上均取得最低WikiText-2困惑度（Llama3-8B: 7.20 vs SpinQuant 7.33）和最低C4困惑度，零样本平均准确率在5个模型上最优，其余3个与SpinQuant差距在1.09个百分点以内。该方法无需反向传播，通过RTN预筛+GPTAQ精筛的符号选择策略即可实现竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 精确的局部误差分解框架清晰区分了权重补偿可处理的误差部分与需要变换设计的正交残差部分，为旋转、符号选择和通道缩放提供了统一的理论指导。随机符号可抑制持久异常通道间的建设性干涉，二阶矩平衡自然导出L2缩放规则，L∞是其进一步松弛。该框架将现有量化方法置于共同理论体系下，并证明无需梯度训练即可达到先进性能。

Abstract: Post-training weight-activation quantization reduces the memory and inference costs of large language models, but aggressive W4A4 quantization remains difficult because activation outliers degrade effective quantization resolution. Although weight optimization, channel-wise scaling, and orthogonal rotation mitigate this problem, the error components they address and their relationship remain unclear. Using an exact decomposition of local weight-activation quantization error into an activation-guided weight compensation term and an orthogonal residual, we bound the residual using persistent channel-wise outlier and regular activation quantities. This decomposition clarifies which error components can be addressed by weight compensation and which require transformation design. We then use the residual bounds to derive practical guidelines for applying randomized Hadamard rotation, sign selection, and channel scaling. In particular, the analysis explains how random signs suppress constructive interference among persistent outlier channels, how sampling multiple sign patterns can improve transformation selection, and how second-moment balancing leads to an $L_2$ scaling rule while a further relaxation recovers SmoothQuant-style $L_\infty$ scaling. We evaluate these guidelines through backpropagation-free configurations across eight Llama and Mistral models, obtaining performance competitive with gradient-trained SpinQuant.

</details>


### [133] [Efficient Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21457)
*Heinke Hihn,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PainNAS方法，一种在留一被试（LOSO）评估框架下实现高效、无泄漏神经架构搜索（NAS）的块级方法，通过将受试者分块并在块间共享NAS运行，将搜索次数从N次减少到B次（B≪N），在BioVid热痛数据集上以显著更少的参数量和FLOPs实现了与现有方法相当的被试级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化疼痛评估等情感计算领域，留一被试交叉验证（LOSO）是常用的被试无关评估协议，但将NAS嵌入LOSO评估中计算开销极大，完全嵌套实现需要N次独立架构搜索，整体复杂度为𝒪(N²)，且现有NAS方法要么存在数据泄漏问题，要么无法有效处理被试级外部评估所需的重复搜索需求。

Method: PainNAS是一种交叉拟合块级NAS方法：1）将N个受试者划分为B个不相交的外块（outer blocks），每个外块对应一次NAS搜索，搜索时排除该块内受试者以保证无泄漏；2）在搜索中使用剩余受试者进行K折内部交叉验证（subject-disjoint inner folds）评估候选架构；3）使用均值-标准差惩罚目标函数 J_b(a) = Ā_b(a) - β·σ̂_b(a)/√N_b（β=1.0），在奖励平均被试级准确率的同时惩罚跨被试高方差的估计；4）搜索后对选中架构使用86个非目标被试数据进行OPEN轮额外训练（OPEN取NAS中早停前epoch数的中位数），最终在被试j上评估一次。早期融合使用Optuna+TPE采样器搜索卷积块数（3-5）、宽度乘数、核大小（7/11/15）、归一化和池化类型等。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上：1）二分类任务中，PainNAS早期融合达到83.71%准确率（±14.5），比手工设计基线高1个百分点，MC任务比先前方法高2.8个百分点；2）计算效率方面，B=20,K=3配置以8.80 MFLOPs实现83.71%准确率，相比基线134.42 MFLOPs降低15.3倍；二分类FLOPs降低5.4-18.7倍；3）运行时间从完全嵌套NAS的72.81h（二分类）和157h（MC）分别降至8.69h和19.3h（约8倍加速）；4）方差惩罚目标函数使早期融合架构FLOPs降低81.0%（二分类）和51.8%（MC），但晚期融合反而增加45.5%和128.7%；5）架构选择倾向：可分离卷积（9/10）、组归一化（7/10）、平均池化（7/10）、池化大小4（9/10）、避免批量归一化、MC任务偏好更宽网络和全局平均池化头。

Conclusion: PainNAS提供了一种资源感知的方法在LOSO评估下实现无泄漏的神经架构搜索，在保持与完全嵌套NAS和先前手工设计方法相当的被试级泛化性能的同时，显著降低了架构复杂度和计算成本（参数和FLOPs大幅减少，运行时间约8倍加速），且方法对超参数B和K稳定；早期融合场景下方差惩罚目标函数对发现高效架构特别有效；未来工作可将PainNAS与元学习结合以快速适应未见受试者，并设计专用于LOSO设置的NAS算法。

Abstract: Deep neural architectures are widely used for signal processing in automated pain assessment systems. However, architecture design has remained largely a manual task despite the potential efficiency benefits of Neural Architecture Search (NAS). Embedding NAS in a Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation is computationally demanding because a fully nested implementation requires $N$ independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$. We propose a block-based, leakage-controlled approach that shares NAS runs between subjects, reducing the number of searches from $N$ to $B$, where $B \ll N$, dubbed PainNAS. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, PainNAS yields comparable subject-level accuracy with substantially fewer parameters and FLOPs.

</details>


### [134] [What Must Survive? Exact Task-Information--State Frontiers for Resource-Sufficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21523)
*Ronald Katende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在下游任务未知但可获得有限任务提示信息时，系统状态压缩所需保留的最小坐标数。作者给出了精确的任务信息-状态边界（task-information–state frontier），证明其等价于一个划分优化问题，并分析了计算复杂度和应用实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，系统状态需要在未来任务完全明确之前进行压缩，但可能预先获得有限的任务提示信息。核心问题是：在保留状态资源有限的情况下，多少任务信息能减少多少必须保留的状态坐标？这对于长上下文注意力、域分解数字孪生、多任务表示等场景具有重要理论意义。

Method: 方法基于线性代数和欧几里得宽度理论。对于线性任务族 $T_u$，在任务提示 $a(u) \in [K]$ 下，通过将任务空间划分为最多 $K$ 个单元，使每个单元内堆叠算子 $T_C$ 的秩最小化。具体包括：(1) 利用 DeVore-Howard-Micchelli 非线性宽度和 Borsuk-Ulam 定理建立精确边界；(2) 给出公共核下界与直和律；(3) 通过 3-Partition 归约证明强 NP 难性；(4) 推广到近似恢复情形。

Result: 核心结果为精确边界公式 $p^*(K) = \min_{|\mathcal{P}|\leq K} \max_{C\in\mathcal{P}} \mathrm{rank}(T_C)$，以及其逆形式 $b^*(p) = \lceil \log_2 \kappa(p) \rceil$。在直和条件下获得封闭形式 $p^*(K) = c + g\lceil m/K \rceil$。三个应用实例：(1) 长上下文注意力：9 位任务信息将 524,288 坐标压缩至 1,024；(2) 域分解数字孪生：得到接口+局部状态的界面律；(3) 层次多任务模型：3 位将 3,136 坐标压缩至 448，渐近接近 328 单任务下限。此外证明了在任意固定正近似容差下寻找最优划分仍是强 NP 难的。

Conclusion: 本文建立了任务信息-状态权衡的精确边界，将问题归结为堆叠秩的划分优化。理论表明任务信息通过联合任务算子低秩的划分来减少状态需求。该边界是变分性的而非算法性的——寻找最优提示划分在一般情况下是强 NP 难的。研究限于连续坐标维度和线性任务算子，非线性情形需要更一般的宽度理论。

Abstract: A system may be compressed before its downstream task is fully known. We ask how much retained state is then necessary and how much can be saved by limited advance task information. For a finite family of linear tasks, a task message is revealed before state formation and the exact task only afterwards. For an advice alphabet of size $K$, the exact frontier is \[ p^*(K)= \min_{\substack{\Pcal\text{ partition of }\U\\|\Pcal|\le K}} \max_{C\in\Pcal}\rank(T_C), \] with the $b$-bit frontier obtained by setting $K=\min(2^b,|\U|)$. Thus advance task information reduces state through partitions whose joint task operators have low rank. We also give an approximate singular-value frontier, a common-core lower bound and exact direct-sum law, and strong NP-hardness of finding an optimal advice partition. The hardness persists at every fixed positive approximation tolerance. Three examples illustrate the result. A well-conditioned softmax attention construction gives an exact $524{,}288\to1{,}024$ coordinate frontier when nine bits resolve one of $512$ continuations. A domain-decomposed digital twin yields an interface-plus-local-state law and a weighted partition problem for heterogeneous regions. A hierarchical multi-task model gives a two-stage frontier in which three bits reduce the required state from $3136$ to $448$ coordinates, with further task information approaching the irreducible $328$-coordinate single-task floor.

</details>


### [135] [IncentRL: The Trade-Off Between Preference Guidance and Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21525)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IncentRL 框架，通过在外部奖励中加入结果分布与偏好分布之间的 KL 惩罚项来引入偏好引导，并在有限折扣 MDP 设定下对其外部值扰动、最优策略保持条件和大幅权重极限进行了理论分析，同时在 MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8 环境中进行了描述性实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中加入偏好信号可能无意间改变被优化的任务本身，因此需要在引入偏好引导的同时明确刻画其对外部任务性能的影响。

Method: 框架定义塑造奖励 $r_\beta(s,a) = r(s,a) - \beta D_{\mathrm{KL}}(p(\cdot|s,a) \| q(\cdot|s))$；在有限折扣 MDP 中假设有界塑造成本，证明了三个命题：（1）外部值扰动上界为 $\beta C_{\max}/(1-\gamma)$；（2）在严格动作间隔 $\beta C_{\max}/(1-\gamma) < \Delta$ 条件下原始最优策略保持不变；（3）大幅权重下策略最小化折扣累积偏好成本。实际实现采用手设计的距离代理、固定偏好分布与基于分数加权的 Beta 分布系数搜索。

Result: 在 MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8 环境中，$\beta=0.01$ 在 200 万步训练后达到 98% 的成功率（3 种子均值），而 $\beta=0$ 基线为 90.5%；均值回合长度分别为 29 和 75。系数搜索四轮的均值从 0.1057 降至 0.0173。作者明确指出实验仅为描述性，未将 KL 塑形与更简单的距离惩罚等控制条件隔离开。

Conclusion: IncentRL 提供了偏好引导与外部任务性能之间权衡的原则性视角，包括有界性条件、策略保持的充分条件和大幅权重下累积成本的解释。但现有实验尚不能建立 KL 特有的优势或贝叶斯适应；作者设计了一个六组前瞻机制研究（含直接距离、势能塑形等控制条件）以隔离这些因素。

Abstract: Preference-based reward shaping can guide reinforcement learning, but adding preference signals to the reward may unintentionally change the task being optimized. We address this problem with IncentRL, a framework that introduces preference guidance while explicitly characterizing its effect on external-task performance. IncentRL adds a Kullback--Leibler (KL) penalty between a specified outcome distribution and a preferred distribution. For finite discounted Markov decision processes with bounded shaping costs, we derive an external-value perturbation bound, establish a sufficient strict-action-gap condition for preserving the original optimal policy, and characterize the large-weight regime through discounted cumulative preference cost. Exact examples clarify the limits of these guarantees, including tied optima and support mismatch. We study a practical implementation using a hand-designed, distance-based outcome proxy, a fixed preference distribution, and score-weighted coefficient search. On MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8, the reported three-seed mean success rate after two million training steps reaches 98\% with coefficient 0.01, compared with 90.5\% for the reported zero-coefficient baseline, while the search progressively shifts toward smaller coefficients. Together, these results provide a principled view of the central trade-off in preference-based RL: using additional guidance to improve learning without excessively distorting the original task objective. The current experiments remain descriptive and do not yet isolate KL shaping from simpler alternatives.

</details>


### [136] [OpenMAS-GCom. A Diagnostic Benchmark for Graph-enhanced Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21527)
*Kairui Yang,Xunkai Li,Kaixiang Zhang,Minghao An,Zekai Chen,Yuxuan Ba,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenMAS-GCom 是一个面向图增强多智能体系统（G-MAS）的诊断基准。它通过受控干预（结构重连、节点移除、错误信息注入、worker失效），在固定任务、模型、提示与预算的条件下，将各系统的性能差异归因到具体的通信结构、角色分配与信息流上，并在29个数据集（6个领域）上评估17种配置，并新增400个多文档复杂任务 G-MAS-Complex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有G-MAS评估通常以最终得分比较系统，但模型、计算成本、通信模式与角色同时变化，导致性能差异难以归因——即"评估归因问题"。本文旨在通过诊断式基准解决该问题。

Method: 提出 OpenMAS-GCom 框架：(1) 将系统表示为四元组 O=(V,E,S,π)，统一封装协作单元、通信链路、共享中间信息与执行规则；(2) 设计两阶段诊断流程——参考执行后对单一组件施加受控干预；(3) 提出四类干预协议：结构重连（保度序列）、节点移除（去评论家/去专家）、信息注入（替换为格式有效但错误的中间项）、执行失效（worker不可用下的恢复）；(4) 引入400个四层难度 G-MAS-Complex 多文档任务，结合严格精确匹配（含来源标识、校验和约束）。

Result: 17个配置在29个数据集+400 G-MAS-Complex任务上的关键发现：(1) 中心得分优势随任务/配置组变化；(2) 在 G-MAS-Complex 上，仅 GoAgent (50.25%) 与 VeriMap (29.50%) 超过单智能体 DeepSeek 基准；(3) 保度重连70%使平均分下降6.89个百分点；(4) 专家节点移除比评论家移除造成更大损失（3.58 vs 0.58个百分点，"专家仅存"配置损失6.29）；(5) MAD与VeriMap基线得分相近，但在80%错误率下损失差距扩大至10.18个百分点；(6) GoAgent在worker失效下比AutoGen保留更多准确率；(7) 最高准确率（GoAgent）与最高每token准确率（AutoGen/VeriMap）出现在不同操作点。

Conclusion: OpenMAS-GCom 证明在受控条件下，不同组织组件对 G-MAS 性能的影响可以分离和量化。相似基准得分可能掩盖对错误信息、worker失效的不同鲁棒性反应，且最高准确率与最高效率通常不可兼得。基准代码已开源，支持可复现的组织诊断与配置选择。未来可扩展至更长视野、交互式和多模态任务。

Abstract: Graph-enhanced multi-agent systems (G-MAS) coordinate large language model agents through communication graphs and role assignments, which determine how agents exchange information and divide responsibilities. However, final-score comparisons across systems combine differences in models, communication patterns, roles, and computation costs, making performance differences difficult to attribute to specific communication structures, role assignments, and information flows. To address this evaluation attribution problem, we introduce OpenMAS-GCom, a benchmark for diagnosing how these components affect G-MAS performance through controlled interventions. We represent systems through collaboration units, communication links, shared intermediate information, and execution rules. OpenMAS-GCom compares original systems with versions modified by changing one component while keeping tasks, models, prompts, and budget limits fixed. We rewire communication edges, remove specialist or critic agents, replace intermediate messages with incorrect content, and disable workers during execution. The benchmark evaluates 17 single-agent, ordinary multi-agent, and graph-enhanced configurations on 29 datasets across six domains. We add 400 G-MAS-Complex tasks requiring agents to combine information from multiple documents, resolve conflicting records, and return specified values with source identifiers. Experiments show larger mean losses after specialist removal than after critic removal, different performance degradation under incorrect messages and worker failures despite similar original scores, and different configurations achieving the highest accuracy and accuracy per token on G-MAS-Complex.

</details>


### [137] [MACE: Memory-Agent Co-Evolution with Adaptive Memory Graphs for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21533)
*Kairui Yang,Minghao An,Xunkai Li,Ziheng Yi,Zekai Chen,Guangyuan He,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MACE框架，一种基于MemGoG图结构的记忆-智能体协同进化方法，用于LLM多智能体系统。该框架通过功能记忆单元保留协作依赖，结合多种关系连接单元，并在执行反馈的指导下自适应调整记忆组织和智能体记忆使用方式。在8个基准测试中平均得分81.11%，超越了10个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在检索多智能体协作经验时，难以同时保留功能依赖（动作的前置条件和后续输出）并适应不同呈现格式。实验显示：(1)按功能分组能提高内部依赖保留率(从68%升至83%)；(2)通过外部关系连接单元可将跨经验需求覆盖率从52%提升至69%；(3)内容相同的单元组合在"指南"与"清单"两种格式下表现优劣相反；(4)联合更新组合与格式的配对反馈优于分别更新。这些发现推动了记忆组织与智能体使用协同进化的需求。

Method: MACE包含两大核心组件：(1) **MemGoG（图之图记忆结构）**：将条件、动作、输出等节点组织为功能记忆单元（子图），并通过支持、抽象、操作化、角色投影、冲突、修复、反馈等7种类型化关系连接单元。(2) **MACE Loop**：包含Need-aware Memory Composer（基于相关性、效用、支持/冲突、代价、风险评分在预算约束下选择工作图）、Adaptive Agent-Memory Coupler（通过相关性矩阵匹配记忆与智能体，并生成包含内容、模式、格式、时机、强度、位置、源记录的配置化补丁）、Feedback Co-Evolver（记录局部、下游、团队三级反馈并更新单元效用与关系）。该框架通过执行反馈同时优化记忆组织与智能体使用方式。

Result: 在8个基准测试（MMLU、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、HumanEval、LiveCodeBench v6、AQuA-RAT、TabFact、TAT-QA）上，MACE平均得分81.11%，超过最强基线SAGE（78.97%）及其他9种方法（直接LLM 71.53%、MAGMA 69.71%、MAD 77.78%等）。消融实验表明Agent-Memory Coupler贡献最大（-5.88个百分点），其次为MemGoG（-5.20）。MACE使用4次LLM调用、6.87K token/查询，平均延迟1.36秒/查询，低于SAGE的2.41秒。在主干替换、网络波动、记忆预算缩减（25%仍达81.76%）、噪声检索（30%噪声仍达70.30%，优于SAGE的66.95%）等鲁棒性测试中均表现稳定。

Conclusion: MACE通过MemGoG结构有效保留多智能体协作中的功能依赖与单元间关系，并通过记忆-智能体协同进化机制同时优化记忆组织和智能体使用方式。实验验证了其在8个基准上的一致性最优表现、各组件的互补贡献、可接受的计算开销以及对部署扰动的鲁棒性。该框架为多智能体系统的经验复用提供了一种将记忆结构与使用方式联合优化的新范式。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems generate collaboration traces that record how agents plan tasks, verify intermediate results, and repair failures. Reusing these procedures requires preserving an action's prerequisites and the outputs needed by subsequent agents. Our empirical studies show that grouping these dependencies into functional memory units improves their retention, while connecting units increases retrieval of the units and links jointly required by a task. The preferred combination of units also changes between instructions and checklists, even when each combination's content is fixed across formats. Updating choices from the outcomes of each combination and format pairing outperforms scoring combinations and formats separately. These findings motivate MACE, a memory-agent co-evolution framework that adapts memory organization and agent memory use through execution feedback. Its MemGoG structure represents functional units as subgraphs of related conditions, actions, and outputs, connecting them through support, conflict, and repair relations. MACE Loop selects task-relevant units and relations within a memory budget and provides each agent with instructions or checklists for its current operation. It records the selected units, presentation formats, agent outputs, and task outcomes to update unit scores and relations for retrieval and inform subsequent presentation choices. Across eight benchmarks, MACE outperforms ten baselines with an average score of 81.11%, compared with 78.97% for the strongest baseline, SAGE.

</details>


### [138] [Trading Depth for Time in Recurrent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21605)
*Zeyi Huang,Xuehai He,Yong Jae Lee,Yelong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环 Transformer 中计算深度（层数）与时间步数之间的权衡。通过在 Latent Recurrent Transformer (LRT) 中插入潜在"思维 token"（latent thought token），让模型沿时间维度而非深度维度增加计算。在 16 层和 20 层 NanoChat MoE 骨干上，单个思维 token 分别恢复了 67% 和 81% 的加倍深度带来的 BPB 改进，同时参数量减少约 48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 扩展计算深度有两条路径：(1) 增加物理层数（每层独立参数化）；(2) 通过时间循环（如 LRT 中跨 token 的隐藏状态反馈）沿时间轴扩展计算。论文探讨：在这两种方式间应如何分配额外计算？额外的计算究竟是花在更多时间步上，还是花在更深的物理层上更好？

Method: 核心方法是在 LRT 的每个词汇 token 之间插入"思维 token"。思维 token 是连续潜在表示，无词表对应，不做采样预测，仅通过共享的 L 层骨干进行额外的隐藏状态精炼。计算流程为：词表 token → 思维 token → 下一个词表 token → 思维 token → ……，其中 LRT 的跨 token 反馈链将思维状态的最终隐藏状态传递到下一个词表 token 的计算中。具体配置：(a) L 层 LRT + 1 个思维 token vs 2L 层 LRT（无思维 token），两者每词表 token 解码块数相同；(b) 思维与词表位置共享注意力、MoE 和反馈投影参数，仅反馈 scale/gate 独立；(c) 训练采用多轮并行精炼（parallel multi refinement），每次处理扩展后的词汇-思维序列。

Result: 主要结果（BPB，越低越好，三种 seed 平均）：(1) 16 层基线：LRT 1×为 0.798，LRT 2×（加倍深度）为 0.780，LRT + 1 thought token 为 0.786——恢复 67% 改进，参数量减少约 48%（2.00× → 1.04×）；(2) 20 层基线：LRT 1×为 0.762，LRT 2×为 0.741，LRT + 1 thought token 为 0.745——恢复 81% 改进；(3) 增加至 2 个思维 token 进一步改善（16 层 0.779；20 层 0.739），但三倍深度 LRT 仍更优（0.771/0.731）；(4) LRT + 思维 token 优于同等块数下的 Loop Transformer（PonderLM-2、2-loop/3-loop/4-loop）；(5) 消融实验：移除跨 token 反馈（→0.795）大部分抵消对 PonderLM-2 的优势；移除 KV 反馈（→0.790）进一步损害性能。

Conclusion: 在循环 Transformer 中，"时间思维"是用参数高效方式替代物理深度扩展的有效手段。单个思维 token 以约一半的参数恢复了深度加倍带来的大部分质量提升；跨 token 反馈链和 KV 反馈是性能增益的关键来源。但同等解码块数下，三倍深度 LRT 仍有优势，表明独立参数层提供了共享重复变换无法完全替代的容量。未来工作包括精确的 FLOP 匹配、注意力开销的延迟/吞吐测量、推理时自适应思维 token 数量等。

Abstract: Recurrent Transformers increase computational depth through temporal recurrence, feeding each token's high-level hidden state into the computation of the next. This raises a natural question: is additional computation better spent on more temporal steps or greater physical depth? We investigate this question using Latent Recurrent Transformers (LRTs), which retain one backbone forward pass per vocabulary token during decoding and provide a controlled setting for comparing these two ways of adding computation. Specifically, we insert a latent thought token between consecutive vocabulary tokens. Each thought token passes through the same $L$ layers as a vocabulary token, sharing the backbone parameters and providing an additional stage of hidden-state refinement before predicting the next token. We compare this $L$-layer LRT against a $2L$-layer LRT without thought tokens. Both execute $2L$ Transformer blocks per vocabulary token during decoding, but the thought-token model uses fewer parameters. On 16- and 20-layer mixture-of-experts NanoChat backbones, one thought token brings the shallower model within 0.006 and 0.004 bits per byte of its double-depth counterpart, recovering 67% and 81% of the improvement with approximately 48% fewer total parameters. These results suggest that temporal thinking offers a parameter-efficient alternative to increasing physical depth in recurrent Transformers.

</details>


### [139] [Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows: Geodesic Symplectic Transport and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21647)
*Vincent Souveton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼神经哈密顿流（RNHF），将神经哈密顿流从欧几里得空间扩展到一般黎曼流形。其核心是通过黎曼度量固定的动能、学习的标量势能和显式的测地线跳蛙积分器实现内在、辛、保持相空间体积的生成映射。作者建立了系统的理论框架，解释所学习的哈密顿势为何具有可解释性：引入了"匹配势能"概念（其隐式分布即为目标分布），证明高斯情形下四分之一周期可精确传输，并通过反向分析证明当位置基分布足够宽时，模型被迫将基分布完全转移到动量，从而使学习到的势能逼近匹配势能。实验在欧几里得、双曲和球面三种几何上验证了模型的样本质量、计算效率和势能可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的哈密顿归一化流大多在欧几里得空间构建，难以处理球面方向、双曲层次结构等流形数据。三大未解决问题：（1）哈密顿生成模型能否内在地推广到黎曼流形？（2）所得到的模型是否能保留欧几里得版本中观察到的实用特性（竞争性样本质量、低计算成本、可解释势能）？（3）若有效，可解释性背后的机制是什么？

Method: RNHF 由三部分构成：（1）固定的黎曼动能 K(q,p)=½p^T G(q)^{-1}p；（2）参数化为 V_θ=-σ_p² log U_θ 的学习标量势能；（3）显式测地线 kick-drift-kick（KDK）辛积分器，包含半步势能 kick、指数映射的测地线漂移和沿测地线的平行传输。该离散映射对任意步长 h 都精确保持李ouville 体积。基分布采用内在的同向动量分布 N_g(0, σ_p²I)；编码器为位置条件下的内在高斯 q_φ(p|q)=N_g(μ_φ(q), Σ_φ(q))。训练通过变分 ELBO 进行，无需雅可比行列式或黎曼散度项。理论方面：推导相空间密度矩方程（连续性方程与动量方程），发现初始加速度沿"相对分数"τ grad_g log(π_V,τ/ρ_0) 方向；定义"匹配势能"V_⋆=-τ log ρ_1；证明高斯情形下归一化相空间旋转（定理2）；通过黎曼正常坐标做局部谐波分析（定理3）；通过反向流推导 V-V_⋆ = τ log R̄_T - τ log(ρ_T/ρ_1)（定理4），分解为"基分布残余记忆"与"模型偏差"。

Result: 在 R²（双模高斯混合）、H²（双模包裹高斯）和 S²（von Mises-Fisher 混合）三种二维流形上与 RCNF 比较：R² 上 RNHF 在 6 项指标中 4 项领先；H² 上 RCNF 占多数优势（指数体积增长使远端离群点影响能量距离）；S² 上 RNHF 在 5/6 项领先。训练速度约为 RCNF 的 2.2 倍（无散度计算），采样略慢（1.3×）但每点绝对成本低于 1 毫秒。可解释性方面：在高斯情形学习到的 σ_p=1.10 与理论预测 σ_p⋆=πa/2=1.10 精确吻合；势能的对数与目标 log ρ_1 的 Pearson 相关性达 0.96（R²）、0.91（H²）、0.96（S²），斜率分别为 0.89、0.83、0.98；学习到的势能井的位置和深度与目标模态一致，且较深井对应权重较大的模态。实验同时验证"宽位置基分布会迫使基分布信息转移到动量"这一理论预测。

Conclusion: RNHF 成功将神经哈密顿流内在化到黎曼流形，结构性地消除了雅可比行列式与散度计算。所学习的标量势能具有清晰的可解释性：在位置基分布明显宽于目标时，模型被迫将基分布信息转移到动量，从而学习到的势能自动逼近 V_⋆=-σ_p² log ρ_1 的"匹配势能"景观。理论框架（势能力与动量压力竞争、四分之一周期传输、匹配势能分解）为有限时哈密顿传输提供了完整的解释机制。开放问题包括：理论上刻画能被自治势能精确传输的 (ρ_0, ρ_1) 对；量化学习势能与匹配势能差距如何随基-目标宽度比缩放；推广到无闭式测地线的流形时需要隐式或近似辛积分器。

Abstract: Hamiltonian normalizing flows are attractive generative models because their phase-space maps are invertible and volume preserving, but most neural constructions are formulated in Euclidean space. We introduce Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows, which combine the fixed kinetic energy of a Riemannian manifold, a learned scalar potential, and an explicit geodesic leapfrog integrator. Our analysis explains how the learned Hamiltonian can be made interpretable. Every normalizable potential defines an implicit profile, and the position marginal initially accelerates along the relative score between that profile and the base. The matched potential is the interpretable specialization for which the implicit profile is the target. In the isotropic Gaussian case, the mechanism corresponds to a phase-space rotation. A local harmonic analysis extends this result around each mode of a general target on a manifold. The gap between the learned and the matched potential is the sum of a residual memory of the base and a bias of the model, and the two potentials agree when the position base has been transferred to the momentum. This can be achieved when the former is broader than the target. Numerical experiments on Euclidean, hyperbolic, and spherical spaces show competitive sample quality and numerical cost against a Riemannian continuous normalizing flow, and confirm the interpretability of the learned potential.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Gaussian Worlds: Latent Geometry Matters for JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21656)
*Léo Nicollier,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Klindt等人(2026)关于联合嵌入预测架构(JEPAs)表示崩溃防护的理论分析，将其从欧几里得高斯隐变量推广到嵌入式黎曼流形（如球面、环面）上的隐变量，证明高斯并非唯一可线性恢复的分布：在球面潜在空间中，匹配球面分布的最优表示能正交恢复潜变量，且近似恢复界严格优于高斯情形。实验验证了几何匹配的目标分布能带来更好的线性恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的JEPA理论分析（Klindt et al., 2026）局限于欧几里得空间中的高斯潜在变量，并声称高斯是唯一能在分布匹配下保证线性恢复的分布。该结论是否依赖于欧几里得假设？非欧几何（如球面、环面）下的潜变量是否也能实现线性恢复？本文旨在打破"高斯唯一性"，探索几何兼容的分布匹配机制。

Method: 1. 将JEPA的对齐（alignment）与分布匹配（distribution matching）框架推广到嵌入式黎曼流形上的潜变量空间。<br>2. 理论推导线性恢复的充要条件，分析流形几何与正样本对动力学（positive-pair dynamics）的相互作用。<br>3. 证明在潜变量均匀分布于球面、表示也匹配球面分布时，每个最优表示可正交恢复潜变量。<br>4. 推导球面情形下的近似恢复界，并与高斯情形进行严格比较。<br>5. 在高斯、球面、环面以及高维Clifford环面（Clifford torus）潜变量空间中进行对比实验。

Result: 1. 理论结果：球面均匀分布在分布匹配JEPA下可实现精确的线性（正交）恢复，证明高斯不是唯一具有此性质的分布——这一性质依赖于欧几里得假设。<br>2. 界严格更紧：球面世界的近似恢复界严格优于高斯世界的相应界。<br>3. 实验结果：<br>   - 几何匹配的目标分布（潜变量与目标同属一类流形）能产生更优的线性恢复；<br>   - 不匹配的目标分布会扭曲潜变量结构；<br>   - 上述优势在高维Clifford环面设置中依然成立。<br>4. 结论意义：JEPA的表示崩溃防护机制应考虑潜变量的几何先验，分布选择需与潜变量几何兼容。

Conclusion: 本文打破了Klindt等人关于"高斯是JEPA分布匹配下唯一可线性恢复分布"的结论，证明了在嵌入式黎曼流形（如球面、环面）上，分布匹配JEPA同样可实现线性恢复，且球面情形的近似恢复界严格更优。这表明JEPA中目标分布的选择应与潜变量几何相匹配，该结论在高维Clifford环面等复杂非欧空间中仍然稳健。

Abstract: Recent Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) prevent representation collapse by constraining learned representations to follow a prescribed target distribution, such as an isotropic Gaussian or the uniform distribution on a hypersphere. Klindt et al. (2026) showed that, under their Euclidean assumptions, matching a Gaussian target can recover Gaussian latent variables up to a linear transformation, and that the Gaussian is the unique distribution with this guarantee.
