Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [168] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力感知路由（AAR），通过在MoE路由器中融入注意力权重的时域与频域滑动窗口特征，在冻结基础Transformer、仅训练路由参数的前提下，提升数学推理能力（OLMoE GSM8K +3.37 pp），并揭示了路由-注意力的耦合机制（路由变化通过残差流放大下一层的注意力汇聚）以及深度敏感的"检索-推理"权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MoE路由器仅依赖token的隐藏状态进行专家选择，忽略了模型自身的上下文关系信息（即注意力权重所编码的序列内结构）。作者希望探索：通过解耦内容与上下文关系信息作为路由归纳偏置，能否改善模型行为与性能。

Method: Attention-Aware Routing (AAR)：在选定的MoE层并行于标准路由器引入辅助路由模块A_l，输入为token对前M个token注意力权重的滑动窗口（跨头平均）。该窗口经固定非学习特征映射φ处理，包含时域原始权重与DFT频域幅度对数拼接的增强表示。A_l以分块对角线性层将增强窗口映射为专家logits，再通过由隐藏状态调制的门控α与标准路由器logits进行逐专家插值。训练时冻结整个Transformer，仅优化路由参数W_r以及新增的A_l和G_l。

Result: 1) AAR在OLMoE上GSM8K提升+3.37 pp（55.36 vs 51.99），MATH-500提升+2.64 pp；在Qwen1.5-MoE和Qwen3.6-MoE（35B）上数学推理增益亦可复现（MATH-500 +1.13 pp）。2) 内部效应：第l层路由变化导致第l+1层注意力汇聚（attention sink）显著放大，证实路由-注意力形成耦合电路。3) 外部效应：错误答案长度平均-11.8%、P99 -14.9%；正确答案长度几乎不变；ramble rate下降23.9%。4) 深度敏感性：全层应用AAR_T使MMLU平均下降-2.1 pp，检索类学科（Biology/History/Philosophy）受损明显，推理类学科（Math/CS）保持或提升；最佳生效区间为中后层（约7-15层）。5) 注意力信号内在相关长度约20 token，与AAR_T最优窗口M=20一致。

Conclusion: AAR证明路由并非仅是分派机制，而是模型信息处理的关键自由度：仅通过路由层面的修改即可重塑下游注意力模式、缩短错误推理链，并在不损害检索任务的前提下提升数学推理能力。层选择性AAR同时作为可控探针，揭示了不同深度下路由-注意力电路承载信息的质性差异（中后层有利于推理，早层对检索至关重要）。主要局限在于AAR层无法使用FlashAttention且实验聚焦于SFT后适配阶段。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [169] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaLR提出了一种结合因果拓扑矩阵(CTM)与隐式微分的框架，将扩散语言模型(DLM)的推理过程重构为带约束的潜在空间优化，通过梯度引导的"思维修正"实现并行生成中的因果一致性，在GSM8K和Sudoku等复杂推理任务上达到DLM的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归(AR)模型存在局部贪婪性和单向信息流的缺陷，容易在长链推理中累积错误；而扩散语言模型(DLM)虽具备全局建模能力，但其双向注意力缺乏因果结构约束，会产生"目的论谬误"(Teleological Fallacies)——即模型在生成原因时错误地依赖于结果。两种范式都无法模拟人类推理中"因果修正"的迭代过程。

Method: CaLR采用两阶段机制：(1) 通过专家模型提取因果拓扑矩阵(CTM)，用{1, 0, -1}编码有效的因果支持、中性区域和反向依赖惩罚，定义离散{-1,0,1}^{L×L}的约束掩码；(2) 构建因果引导的潜在修正损失L_align = (1/N)Σ||δ* ⊙ P(M_i)||_1，其中最优修正向量δ*通过求解隐式优化问题获得，利用隐函数定理计算梯度，并通过共轭梯度法避免Hessian求逆。总损失为L_total = L_diff + λL_align。理论上证明该机制能以O(1)解码轮数模拟深度为d的逻辑电路，空间复杂度为O(w)。

Result: 在GSM8K上达到58.36%准确率(部分报告76.70%)，超越LLaDA-8B(44.17%)约14个百分点，也超过Llama-3.1-8B(57.32%)；在4×4 Sudoku任务上仅用200个训练样本即达到87.89%准确率，相比LLaDA-8B(77.05%)提升10.84%，而Llama-3.1-8B仅8.60%；并行解码稳定性显著优于基线，如HumanEval在激进并行设置(~8 tokens/step)下优势从+21.8%扩大到+33.6%；消融实验显示去除因果对齐模块(w/o P.II)平均准确率从65.93%降至61.33%，下降最大。

Conclusion: CaLR通过将因果元知识注入扩散模型的连续潜在动力学，使DLM能够严格遵守离散逻辑约束。理论上证明了该框架以最优计算复杂度模拟深度d逻辑电路(常数解码轮数和O(w)空间复杂度)；实验上验证了连续扩散流形可以掌握传统上被认为仅限AR系统的复杂符号推理任务，为扩散语言模型在逻辑推理领域建立了新的性能标杆，同时也为安全生成系统提供了可控的潜在空间操作基础。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [170] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe III,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段参数高效框架，将 DINOv3 Vision Transformer 通过 LoRA 适配到水下合成孔径声纳（SAS）自动目标识别（ATR）任务。核心结论是：唯一的领域适配阶段（LoRA）将 AUPRC 从 0.300 提升至 0.679（仅训练 0.26% 参数），而后续的难负样本挖掘和监督对比学习阶段相对于各自的匹配对照均无显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下 SAS 自动目标识别面临目标图像稀缺、背景杂波复杂（声学拟态物）、以及人工标注成本高等瓶颈。标准监督深度学习在极端类别不平衡（~119:1）下表现受限，且大型视觉基础模型（预训练于自然图像）与水下声学传播物理之间存在显著领域鸿沟。需探索参数高效适配方案以弥合该差距。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) Stage 1 在冻结的 DINOv3 ViT-L 主干上注入 LoRA 模块（仅注入注意力层的 Q/K/V/O 线性投影），并使用可训练的 2-to-3 通道卷积 stem 处理双波段（HF+BB）SAS 输入，以非对称损失进行二分类训练；(2) Stage 2 采用难负样本挖掘（从 Stage 1 编码器选取得分最高的 5000 个杂波片段）进行微调，并以等量的随机选择杂波作为匹配对照；(3) Stage 3 在 Stage 2 基础上叠加监督对比学习（SupCon）损失，采用双视图批次和 128 维投影头。关键方法论特征是每个阶段均与匹配对照（matched control）进行比较，并在三个随机种子下重复，以隔离各机制的独立贡献。

Result: 主要结果（盲测集，85% 召回率匹配操作点）：(1) LoRA 域适配带来 +0.38 AUPRC 的决定性提升（0.300→0.679±0.027），秩 4 即可达到相当效果；(2) 适配器容量在低秩处饱和，r=4 与 r=64 不可区分，LoRA 与全参数微调在 ViT-S 和 ViT-L 上均无显著差异；(3) 难负样本挖掘 vs 随机选择：-0.0045±0.0119 AUPRC（差异不显著）；(4) SupCon vs Stage 2：+0.0002±0.0096 AUPRC（无显著效果）；(5) 唯一可分离的轴是校准误差（ECE 从 0.0116 降至 0.0030）。

Conclusion: 在水下 SAS ATR 这一数据规模下，单次参数高效适配阶段（LoRA）足以完成大部分工作；堆叠在适配主干之上的难负样本挖掘和监督对比学习课程均未能偿还其设计与计算成本。空结果的机制在于：挖掘操作作用于已被编码器充分拟合的数据划分上，且有监督阶段在对比损失施加之前已完成了大部分目标-杂波几何结构的塑造。工程实践应将精力集中于核心适配步骤的优化和操作点的选择，而非设计复杂的多阶段训练课程。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [171] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注意力图拓扑特征（Forman-Ricci曲率、外向性分数、注意力熵）的单次前向传播方法，用于检测大语言模型中的幻觉生成。该方法通过线性探针在四个LLM架构和两个基准测试上均优于Laplacian特征值和EigenScore等基线，平均AUC提升约3.4个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法主要依赖多次采样自一致性方法，计算成本高且无法揭示幻觉产生的内在机制；而基于注意力权重的方法（如Laplacian特征值）缺乏对注意力图中信息瓶颈的深入理论分析。作者旨在通过图论和信息几何的角度，捕捉LLM生成过程中的拓扑特征，从而实现高效且可解释的单次检测。

Method: 将注意力权重视为有向加权图的邻接矩阵，提取三类拓扑特征：(1) 互向外性（Mutual Outgoingness）——基于加权出度之和，捕捉半局部信息流；(2) 注意力联合熵——作为FRC惩罚项的线性时间复杂度代理，衡量全局信息分布均匀度；(3) 每头自注意力平均值。对每头分布取10/50/90百分位数，构成与响应长度无关的固定大小特征向量，训练线性探针进行二分类。

Result: 在Mistral 7B、LLaMA 3.1 8B、Phi-4和Qwen3-32B四个模型上，针对TruthfulQA和NQ-Open两个基准，在两种采样温度下进行评估：方法在大部分配置下优于Laplacian特征值和EigenScore基线，平均AUC提升约3.4个点。分析发现：LLaMA和Mistral的幻觉响应表现为更高的外向性（扩散的上下文检索）和更低的注意力熵；Phi-4和Qwen3-32B则表现为过度自注意力。最后一个Transformer层的区分度最强。

Conclusion: 信息流瓶颈（特别是上下文共享受损）是跨架构一致的幻觉拓扑签名，但具体表现形式因架构而异：LLaMA/Mistral表现为注意力扩散，Phi-4/Qwen3表现为过度自注意力。所提出的拓扑特征提供了一种高效、可解释且与响应长度无关的幻觉检测方案。未来需进一步研究模型规模、知识缺失与上下文错位对幻觉的不同影响。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [172] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型微调过程中内部表征变化与因果重要性之间的解耦关系，发现表征变化最大的层与EAP识别的因果重要组件所在的层并不重合，且任务间EAP组件重叠度高反而会导致跨任务性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调虽被广泛用于适配LLM到下游任务，但其如何重塑模型内部机制尚不清楚，尤其是模型级表征变化与对任务性能负责的关键因果组件之间的关系不明，这阻碍了高效且有原则的微调策略设计。

Method: 采用两种量化方法：(1) 通过KL散度衡量注意力模式变化，通过logit lens探针和CKA衡量表征级变化；(2) 使用Edge Attribution Patching (EAP) 识别因果相关组件。以top-400边对应的组件作为关键组件。在GPT-2 Small、Llama-3.2-1B、Qwen2-0.5B、Llama2-7B四种模型上，针对情感分类、问答、机器翻译三类任务（各两个数据集，共6个数据集）进行全参数微调实验，并分析EAP组件重叠度与跨任务性能迁移的关系。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 微调引起的注意力和表征变化是非均匀的，较大模型变化幅度更小；(2) EAP识别的因果组件高度集中于特定层（归一化熵0.40–0.74），但这些层与表征变化最大的层相关性很弱；(3) 跨任务EAP组件重叠度与性能退化呈正相关——组件重叠越多，在不同性质任务间迁移时性能退化越严重；(4) 冻结共享组件可恢复部分性能（提升2.05%–84.34%），证实了干扰机制；(5) 关键EAP组件主要由MLP神经元和注意力头构成，logit节点在归一化后被放大。

Conclusion: 有效的微调由少量局部化的EAP识别组件驱动，而非广泛的表征变化；模型级表征变化的幅度仅与因果重要性弱相关。任务间共享的因果组件并不能良性迁移，组件重叠反而意味着更强的干扰效应，这为设计更高效和有针对性的微调策略提供了启示。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [173] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TinyCeNN-LM提出了一个质量门控的后训练注意力转换框架，将预训练语言模型中的注意力层替换为受CeNN（细胞神经网络）启发的细胞-循环层。核心贡献在于将候选架构与严格的提交/回滚策略分离，并在SmolLM2-135M和Qwen3.5-0.8B上验证了选择性转换的可行性，同时揭示了NLL指标与表征保真度之间的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的KV cache随上下文长度线性增长，是长序列推理的主要瓶颈。虽然新型高效序列模型（Mamba、RWKV、RetNet、Gated DeltaNet等）已在从头训练中取得进展，但如何将已训练好的Transformer模型中的注意力层转换为更高效的替代层，仍是一个兼容性难题——不当替换会破坏下游层所依赖的表征分布。现有方法（如RADLADS的跨架构蒸馏、KL引导的层选择）专注于训练新学生或选择哪些层保留softmax注意力，但对"提交决策"（commit decision）本身的严格化缺乏关注。

Method: 1. **统一抽象层定义**：定义五元组𝒞ℓ=(Φℓ, Ψℓ, ℛℓ, 𝒢ℓ, 𝒬ℓ)，分别对应有界局部算子、循环状态更新、可选路由、门控融合和质量验证策略，保留CeNN的局部邻域、共享动力学、状态演化三大原则。
2. **三种实例化**：Integrated Memory（保守的局部-循环融合）、MemoryFusion（并行局部分支与全局循环分支加门控）、PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32（W=32精确因果局部路径 + GDN2循环记忆 + 一跳CLVR路由 + 头级别门控融合）。
3. **质量门控契约**：联合使用四个指标——NMSE≤0.15、Cos≥0.94、ΔNLLinc≤0.015、ΔNLLcum≤0.05，任何一项失败触发回滚；同一套阈值在SmolLM2和Qwen3.5间跨模型迁移使用，无逐层重调。
4. **顺序提交/回滚流程**：每轮训练单一候选层，通过后冻结并接受，否则回滚至上一接受点；训练损失为λfℒalign + λKLℒKL + λCEℒCE + λgℒgate的组合。
5. **状态复杂度分析**：将循环记忆的持久状态与KV cache的T线性增长对比，量化Cache缩减潜力。

Result: **SmolLM2-135M（PDelta3严格模式）**：层0–2被接受（累积ΔNLL=+0.01209），层3被拒绝（NMSE=0.2265, Cos=0.8949虽NLL指标通过，但表征门失败）。

**Qwen3.5-0.8B（PDelta3严格模式，选择性转换）**：在混合主干中替换三个全注意力锚点（层3、7、11），全部接受，最终累积ΔNLL=+0.02073。

**Integrated Memory V3（SmolLM2）**：在256/512/1024/2048上下文上困惑度保持在原模型−0.07%至+0.93%范围内，Cache缩减随上下文增长，在2048处达到6.01%。

**Qwen3.5-0.8B FastEval抽样（200项，每基准50项）**：原生30.0%；PDelta3-CLVR 28.5%、MemoryFusion 30.5%、Integrated Memory 32.0%。仅作功能性sanity check，不具统计稳健性。

**当前参考实现的吞吐**：PDelta3 12.20 tok/s、MemoryFusion 8.89 tok/s、Integrated Memory 5.80 tok/s，均低于原生13.37–13.78 tok/s（未优化kernel）。

Conclusion: 1. 严格PDelta3转换可在SmolLM2和Qwen3.5-0.8B上接受多处替换，跨模型阈值迁移有效。
2. SmolLM2层3揭示了"表征失配但NLL可接受"的度量分歧，这是该方法的核心诊断发现，表明短视的语言模型损失不足以保证局部功能保真。
3. 保守的Integrated Memory可在保持困惑度的同时实现上下文相关的Cache缩减。
4. 转换为功能上可用的独立模型（200项sanity check未出现功能崩溃），但不足以建立基准优越性。
5. 当前证据支持"保守的结构性转换"，而非"通用注意力替换"或"速度优势"——后者需要优化流式kernel。
6. 仍需：强制接受对照实验（layer-3的rollback vs NLL-only对比）、完整Qwen锚点覆盖、阈值敏感性分析、多种子统计、组件消融以巩固因果性主张。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $\Delta\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $\Delta\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [174] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于临床医生视角的AI辅助精神科初诊质量保证评估平台InterviewPlayground，通过记忆增强的模拟患者实现跨访谈风格对比，并在6名临床医生的试点研究中对比了GPT-4o LLM访谈者的表现，揭示了单纯信息恢复率无法捕捉的临床安全相关失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助精神科初诊系统的快速发展，医疗系统在患者使用前需要可重复、可比较的临床质量保证评估方法。然而现有评估存在三大挑战：(1)不同临床医生访谈风格差异大；(2)临床医生时间有限，负担重；(3)需要评估临床相关性能而非单一分数。直接使用真实患者测试未经验证的AI系统存在伦理和临床风险。

Method: 方法包括三大组件：(1)模拟患者构建——基于InterviewPlayground记忆增强检索架构，将12个精神科初诊病例(本研究使用3个：重度抑郁伴自杀意念、广泛性焦虑、双相II型障碍)转化为含约200个记忆槽的人格描述，每个病例包含10个二元评估字段作为评分真值；(2)模拟初诊平台——20分钟单次访谈界面，配合配套教程和响应延迟；(3)初诊评估——包括提取信息回忆(精度与准确率)、转录文本响应性(极性问句开头、邀请详述)、推断信息回忆(无依据临床推断)、安全顾虑识别与特征化等四个维度。通过GPT-4o填写与临床医生相同的回忆表实现对比。

Result: 在16次访谈(临床医生6次+LLM 10次)的对比中：模拟患者在18/20字段上保持一致；临床医生完成时间中位数25.3分钟；LLM恢复正性真值字段更多(88.0% vs 38.9%)且精度相近(95.97% vs 100%)，但无依据推断更多(56.8% vs 27.8%)，安全顾虑识别率更高(90% vs 50%)但特征化率更低(33.3% vs 66.7%)；9/10次LLM访谈中患者披露被动自杀意念后LLM转换话题；临床医生产生无问题消息占9%，LLM仅占2%。

Conclusion: 该平台为AI辅助精神科初诊系统提供了实用、可重复的临床医生基础质量保证框架。核心贡献在于：(1)证明了信息恢复率单一指标会误导质量保证；(3)揭示了LLM在检测安全顾虑方面表现更好但跟进特征化方面严重不足的临床关键失败模式；(3)验证了记忆增强模拟患者在跨访谈风格对比中的可行性。论文为部署前的大规模临床试验奠定了机制基础，未来工作需在真实病例上验证模拟器、跨模型家族测试，并在临床监督环境中验证整个质量保证方法。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [175] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地比较了LLM智能体在芯片设计中采用不同抽象层次（RTL、HLS、编译器辅助）的设计流程，提出并验证了AHRR（Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement）流程，在11项基准任务上实现相对直接RTL设计2.6倍的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM智能体的芯片设计方法大多直接在RTL层面工作，而高层次综合（HLS）将调度、流水线和RTL生成委托给编译器，但智能体能否通过利用更高层次抽象来设计出更好的芯片尚不清楚。本文旨在探究抽象层次本身是否有助于智能体生成更高质量的设计。

Method: 提出并比较四种设计流程：(i) 直接RTL设计，(ii) 基于智能体的HLS设计，(iii) HLS后RTL细化，(iv) 编译器后HLS细化。将(ii)+(iii)组合为AHRR流程。使用pi编码智能体框架，Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.6-sol两种模型，在11项基准任务（含LLM推理、机器人、内存、GEMM等）上，以AMD Alveo U55C FPGA为目标平台进行端到端评估。评估采用隐藏测试集，覆盖率强化至100%，并使用隐藏测试台验证正确性。

Result: 1) 基于智能体的HLS设计相对直接RTL设计获得2.31倍几何平均加速；2) 加入RTL细化后，AHRR提升至2.62倍加速；3) HLS后RTL细化在16个有效案例上相对其起点获得1.17倍加速；4) 编译器后HLS细化可在已优化的编译器输出上进一步提升（如StreamHLS上SHL2提升3.3倍，AutoSA上GEM1提升1.3倍）；5) 案例研究显示HLS将设计知识提炼为抽象供智能体利用，而RTL细化能恢复编译器遗漏的低层级优化机会（如用查找表替换常量乘法器、重新排序运算以削减DSP使用）。

Conclusion: AHRR流程结合了HLS的架构杠杆和RTL的周期精确控制，通过机器智能（前沿AI模型）与人类智能（HLS及领域专用编译器中的设计知识）的结合，是智能体芯片设计的有前途的工作流程。该方法有望克服HLS采纳的历史障碍，加速HLS及高层次设计方法论的工业应用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [176] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了"特异性优化"(SpecOpt)这一新的分子设计任务，旨在对已有的良好表征药物进行最小化结构修饰，以增强其对目标蛋白的选择性（降低脱靶结合）。为此构建了基于ChEMBL的基准（1848个化合物），并开发了一个智能体框架：将化合物对接至靶标与脱靶蛋白，通过残基感知的原子-蛋白接触差异提供信号给GPT-5.4大语言模型，由LLM提出定向结构修改。在915个化合物上将平均特异性差距从−0.72提升至+0.47 kcal/mol，84.8%的化合物得到改善，平均Tanimoto相似度保持在0.72。消融实验证明，残基身份信息是核心信号——去除后所有29个化合物均无法改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子药物常因脱靶蛋白结合产生副作用。现有计算方法多关注从头生成选择性化合物，而非优化已存在药物的选择性。这在药物开发中很重要，因为许多候选药物虽具有良好效力、药代动力学和合成可达性，却保留了不需要的脱靶活性，需要在保留其优势的前提下进行最小化结构修改。

Method: 1) 任务定义：给定化合物m、靶标T和脱靶集合O，优化目标是最小化结构改变以最大化特异性差距g(m)=min_O s(m,O)-s(m,T)；2) ChEMBL基准构建：基于药物机制注释确定靶标，基于测量活性确定脱靶，校正变异面板/蛋白复合体/通用生物体重复，最终含1848个化合物；3) 差异接触分析：将化合物对接至靶标和每个脱靶，按7Å阈值识别每原子的接触残基，分为target-only/offtarget-only/shared-same-residues/shared-different-residues四类，并标记"生长机会"；4) BRICS片段聚合，生成片段级编辑建议（remove/keep/redesign/grow/neutral）；5) GPT-5.4作为LLM智能体，在Tanimoto≥0.4、ADMET容差、重新对接选择性三重标准下迭代修改（每轮8候选、4轮）。

Result: 在915个化合物上：特异性差距从−0.72改善至+0.47 kcal/mol，84.8%（776/915）化合物得到改善，平均Tanimoto相似度0.72。575个基线负差距化合物中33%转为正差距。乙多沙班（edoxaban）案例从−5.6改善至+1.5 kcal/mol（Tanimoto 0.76）。关键消融：将残基感知分类替换为二元接触指示器使29/29化合物改善归零，证明残基身份信息是核心优化信号，主要信号来源为"共享-不同-残基"（5668个片段分类）和"生长机会"（2321个）。跨化合物记忆、姿势一致性警告和组合机制无显著改善。在FLT3设定下23个药物中20个获得改善，差距从−2.03变为−0.91 kcal/mol。

Conclusion: SpecOpt确立了"特异性优化"作为独立的分子设计任务，与从头选择性生成互补。残基感知的差异接触是约束分子优化的关键信号，而非简单的接触存在与否。LLM能将这些信号转化为具体化学编辑。现有药物可在保留结构相似性的同时被系统性改善预测特异性。多重局限性仍存在：仅优化至多3个选定脱靶、所有改善基于Vina对接评分（而非实验验证）、对接评分在区分靶标/脱靶方面区分能力有限（中位Spearman ρ=−0.075）。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>


### [177] [Implicit Rule Induction with Test-Time Task Embeddings in ARC-like Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21181)
*Adrien Deliège,Claas Beger,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Melanie Mitchell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的测试时训练协议 Embed-TTT，通过先冻结预训练 Vision ARC (VARC) 模型的主干网络、仅优化任务嵌入向量，再冻结嵌入并微调主干的方式，在四个 ARC 类基准上生成与底层任务规则更对齐的语义化任务嵌入，并支持跨数据集的概念检索与组合规则插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC 类基准仅评估最终输出正确性，无法区分模型是真正推断出了底层规则还是依赖捷径或记忆；现有 Full-TTT 协议联合微调 18M 参数与任务嵌入，导致测试嵌入与训练嵌入存在显著分布偏移，丧失语义结构。

Method: 两阶段测试时训练协议：(1) Embed-TTT——冻结 VARC 主干，仅优化 512 维任务嵌入；(2) Embed-Full-TTT——冻结已学习的嵌入，再微调主干。在 ARC-AGI-1、ConceptARC 及两个自定义可控数据集（LUCD 非参数、Moves 参数）上，通过嵌入检索 Top-k 匹配、线性分类/回归探针评估规则归纳质量，并分析嵌入空间的 UMAP/PCA 几何结构与组合插值/外推能力。

Result: Embed-TTT 在 ARC-1 训练任务检索 Top-5 匹配率达 86.25%（Full-TTT 仅 6.5%），ConceptARC 概念线性分类准确率达 60.6%（Full-TTT 21.3%，随机 6.2%），自定义 LUCD 线性分类准确率达 100%（Full-TTT 11.3%）；仅优化嵌入即可在 ARC-1 pass@2 解决 17% 任务；嵌入恢复 Moves 数据集的网格几何结构，支持规则插值但不支持外推；近邻检索可实现 36% 的 ARC-1 概念正确标注。

Conclusion: Embed-TTT 实现了对 ARC 类任务中规则归纳与规则执行的清晰分离，产生的任务嵌入在嵌入空间中保留语义结构与底层规则几何，支持跨数据集分析与组合插值；然而其在未见规则上的执行能力受限，凸显了 ARC 类基准需要更明确地区分分布内与分布外规则并独立评估规则归纳能力的必要性。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>


### [178] [AI-GRACE: A Use-Case Operationalization Framework for Agentic AI: From Organizational Objectives and Obligations to Deployment Capabilities and Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21192)
*John Cuneo,David Chun,Gaurav Khanna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI-GRACE是一个面向代理型AI用例的操作化框架，通过治理-风险-保证-控制-证据（GRACE）五环节，将组织目标与义务转化为技术能力、逻辑架构及部署决策，并辅以Agent操作边界和风险对齐独立级别（RAIL）机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负责任AI研究多停留在原则层面，缺乏从治理目标到工程实施的端到端衔接；组织部署代理型AI时面临模型可信度之外的实施问题（如能力资格、架构落地、运行时约束、证据留存），亟需可追溯、可重用的方法论。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，以情境方法工程（SME）指导裁剪与复用；提出七个风险域（使命与价值、AI行为与权限、安全与人类影响、网络安全、隐私、运营韧性、法律/监管/政策）；推导保证（A）、控制（C）、证据（E）需求；定义Agent操作边界与RAIL五级独立度（0-4）；通过虚构的零售银行助手用例进行端到端示例化。

Result: 给出从用例意图到合格部署设计的推理路径（治理→风险→A/C/E→能力资格→逻辑架构→部署决策），展示银行助手用例中如何通过MCP集成、运行时检查、独立审批绑定、证据关联等机制满足EU AI Act、GDPR、DORA、GLBA、Regulation E等法规要求；当前决策授权工程与评估，但未授权部署。

Conclusion: AI-GRACE为组织提供了连接治理承诺与工程实现的结构化方法，支持跨用例的复用与重评估；但尚未经过实证验证，未来需通过对照实验和现场研究评估其对部署决策、返工、运行结果与复用的实际影响。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [179] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoVer是一个统一的GRPO框架，通过信息增益奖励和多样性感知测试选择，解决自博弈代码生成RL中的两个核心缺陷（许可性崩溃和集中偏差），在五个基准测试中显著提升了pass@1指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈代码RL方法存在两个耦合缺陷：许可性崩溃（pass-rate奖励被无区分度的平凡测试最大化）和集中偏差（i.i.d.采样的测试聚集在模态输入上，导致估计器方差膨胀）。需要一种方法能同时解决这两个问题，并提供比固定测试套件更强的训练信号。

Method: 提出CoVer框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 信息增益（IG）奖励——使用互信息度量每个自生成测试的pass/fail向量与分级GT锚定正确性信号之间的关联，通过协方差符号门控只奖励正向区分性测试；(2) 三阶段多样性感知选择——通过无效性过滤、输入字符串去重、执行配置文件去重，将候选池裁剪为行为非冗余的测试套件，提高IG估计器的有效样本大小。两者通过单策略GRPO联合优化，coder和verifier角色共享同一策略。

Result: 在LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces五个基准测试中，7B规模macro平均pass@1达到35.94%（+5.76分），14B规模达到42.90%（+7.08分），均为同规模最佳。作为CodeT流水线的即插即用骨干，CoVer-7B额外带来+3.50分提升。在Llama-3.1-8B上也验证了跨家族泛化能力（+8.94分）。消融实验证实了每个设计选择的必要性：GRPO、Diversity-aware选择、graded GT等冗余的executor。

Conclusion: 将验证器学习构建为信息论、方差感知的估计问题，比最大化pass-rate提供了更有原则且经验上更强的方法路径。该框架的通用原则——奖励验证器提供关于固定正确性锚点的信息增益，并在低冗余探测集上测量——可能扩展到单元测试以外的领域，如数学推理和结构化预测，前提是可定义分级正确性信号。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>


### [180] [Ability-Residual Decoupled Modeling for Affective Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21214)
*Boyuan Zhao,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARACD/ARCACD框架，通过能力残差解耦解决情感认知诊断中的"情感污染"问题：在认知诊断骨干网络之后、情感模块之前，显式建模学生-项目残差层（全局偏差+低秩交互）和概念残差层（共享偏差+学生-概念个性化偏差，通过Q矩阵约束的注意力聚合），将稳定的认知偏差从情感表征中分离出来，使情感模块专注于非认知状态的猜测/失误调制。在四个公开数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感认知诊断方法隐含假设认知诊断骨干网络已充分解释认知结构，将剩余响应变化归因于情感状态。但实际数据中存在项目校准偏差、概念系统偏差、学生-概念个性化偏差和潜在学生-项目匹配偏差等稳定认知残差。当这些残差未被显式建模时，情感模块被迫吸收它们，导致"情感污染"——情感表征中混入认知残差信息，削弱可解释性和诊断可靠性。

Method: 提出能力残差解耦框架，包含三个组件：(1) 可替换的认知诊断骨干网络（DINA/IRT/MIRT/NCDM/RCD/SCD）；(2) 能力残差模块，在logit尺度上修正基础预测，包含两层——学生-项目残差层（学生全局偏差bs + 项目全局偏差bi + 低秩学生-项目交互⟨us,vi⟩/√d）和概念残差层（共享概念偏差bk + 学生-概念个性化偏差b_{s,k}，通过Q矩阵约束的注意力机制加权聚合）；(3) 情感模块，基于学生情感表征和概念难度生成情感向量，通过猜测/失误调制响应概率。支持有真实情感标签的监督训练（MSE损失）和无标签的对比训练（基于响应标签构建正负样本的InfoNCE损失）。

Result: 在ASSIST2017、ASSIST2012、ASSIST2009和Junyi四个数据集、六种骨干网络上验证：ARACD在监督设置下12个比较中均提升ACC/AUC并降低RMSE/MAE；ARCACD在无监督设置下也获得一致增益。情感对齐指标在ASSIST2012上全面改善，ASSIST2017上多数情况改善。消融实验表明学生-项目残差层贡献主要个性化修正，概念残差层提供互补修正。污染诊断显示：加入能力残差后，情感表征与合成认知偏差的相关性从0.5878降至0.0665，R²从0.4716降至0.0568，偏差主要被吸收到能力残差通道（相关性0.6890）。长尾学生诊断和个体案例研究进一步支持框架有效性。

Conclusion: 情感认知诊断应不仅关注响应预测改进，还需审视情感表征是否包含不属于情感状态的认知残差。能力残差解耦通过结构化分配建模角色——先用学生-项目残差层和概念残差层吸收稳定认知残差，再用情感模块处理非认知调制——提高了情感诊断的可靠性、可解释性和预测稳定性。未来工作可结合多模态情感观测和跨平台教育数据评估能力残差解耦的泛化性。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis infers students' concept mastery from response logs. However, students' responses are not determined by mastery alone: non-cognitive factors such as emotion, engagement, and fatigue can also affect performance. Affective cognitive diagnosis therefore extends conventional cognitive diagnosis by incorporating affective states. Existing methods often assume that the cognitive diagnosis backbone has already explained ability, item, and concept effects, so the remaining errors can be attributed mainly to affect. We argue that this assumption can be insufficient in real educational data: item calibration bias, systematic concept bias, personalized student-concept deviations, and latent student-item matching can form stable cognitive residuals. Without an explicit modeling pathway, these residuals may leak into affective representations, producing affect contamination. To address this problem, we propose an ability-residual decoupled framework for affective cognitive diagnosis. The model first captures unmodeled cognitive residuals through student, item, concept, student-concept, and low-rank student-item components, and then uses an affective module to modulate guess/slip effects. A Q-matrix-constrained concept residual attention mechanism adaptively aggregates only item-relevant concept residuals. Experiments on ASSIST2017, ASSIST2012, ASSIST2009, and Junyi with six cognitive diagnosis backbones show response-prediction gains across the reported comparisons and generally improved affect alignment when affect labels are available. Ablation studies, leakage probes, principal component analysis visualization, long-tail analysis, and case studies further indicate that ability residuals absorb stable cognitive bias, reduce cognitive contamination in the affective branch, and enhance the robustness and predictive accuracy of cognitive diagnosis models.

</details>


### [181] [A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Framework for Perceptual Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21221)
*Hongyan Wei,Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的、完全可微的神经-软符号(neuro-soft-symbolic)框架,将视觉感知与任务规划统一在单一计算图中。框架通过维护连续软符号状态、引入可微的soft-T_P转移算子,以及在规划窗口内优化动作logits,使得任务级梯度能够回流至感知参数。在Blocksworld上,该方法解决了40/40 LatPlan任务和596/600 PlanBench任务,显著优于LatPlan和o1-preview等基线,同时计算成本和时间大幅降低。在感知不确定性消融实验中,将冻结感知下的59%成功率提升至83%。此外,该框架在Logistics和Sokoban等跨域任务上也展现了良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的感知-规划方法存在两类局限:(1)硬符号神经-符号方法在感知与规划之间引入硬符号承诺,丢弃了感知不确定性,且无法将任务级梯度反馈给感知模块;(2)基于语言模型(LLM/LRM)的方法可能产生幻觉或语义不一致的预测,无法保证满足逻辑约束。因此,需要一种能在保留感知不确定性的同时强制执行逻辑规则、并支持端到端梯度传播的统一框架。

Method: 1. **软符号感知**:CNN骨干网络+RoI池化,双向线性bilinear head输出实体间的连续软支撑关系矩阵F∈[0,1]^{N×(N+K)};2. **可微soft-T_P转移算子**:通过product-t-norm提升逻辑规则(如clear(X)、legal move、目标排他性、STAY语义等),以连续运算模拟逻辑约束;3. **短时域规划与重规划**:在窗口长度T内优化动作logits a_t,损失函数包含目标项、移动惩罚、循环惩罚和不动点一致性项;4. **感知–规划联合优化**:在感知连接变体中,通过KL锚点正则化允许任务级梯度更新感知参数θ;5. **动作解码与执行**:将动作分布解码为合法动作序列,在PyBullet+Franka Panda臂仿真中验证任务–运动一致性。

Result: **Blocksworld**: LatPlan-40上40/40 (100%) vs LatPlan 33/40; PlanBench-600上596/600 (99.33%) vs o1-preview 587/600,平均求解时间9.96s vs 40.43s,成本$0.89 vs $42.12/100实例;零不可执行计划。**感知不确定性消融**: 高斯模糊子集上,冻结感知成功率59% → 全可微感知83% (+24%)。**跨域**: Logistics 196/200 (98.00%) vs o1-preview 188/200; Sokoban 33/55 (60.00%) vs 7/55。**短时域窗口消融**: T=3最佳,有效率100%、最优率76.7%。**任务–运动仿真**: 解码动作序列成功在PyBullet中通过pick-and-place原语执行。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个任务无关、领域通用的完全可微神经-软符号规划框架,验证了连续软符号表示与可微规则提升在感知任务规划中的有效性,证明了端到端梯度传播能显著提升感知不确定性下的规划鲁棒性,并在效率上大幅优于基于大推理模型的基线。未来工作将扩展至更多交互实体、更长依赖链、更丰富动作模式、部分可观测性和几何约束等更具挑战性的具身AI与机器人规划场景。

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>


### [182] [CogGym: Towards Large-Scale Comparative Evaluation of Human and Machine Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21259)
*Lance Ying,Jinzhou Wu,Yingshan Susan Wang,Shivam Aarya,Luca M. Schulze Buschoff,Harry Chen,Katherine M. Collins,Andrea de Varda,Shuhao Fu,Sean Dae Houlihan,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang,Samuel Kiegeland,Tetsu Kurumisawa,Rongzhi Liu,Ryan Liu,Ningshan Ma,Kathryn McGregor,Younes Strittmatter,Polina Tsvilodub,Jacob Hoover Vigly,Sarah Wu,Enjie Xu,Yiling Yun,Kelsey Allen,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Brian Christian,Evelina Fedorenko,Michael C. Frank,Michael Franke,Tao Gao,Samuel J. Gershman,Robert D. Hawkins,Jennifer Hu,Julian Jara-Ettinger,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Sydney Levine,Tal Linzen,Hongjing Lu,Timothy O'Donnell,Desmond C. Ong,Steven T. Piantadosi,Rebecca Saxe,Eric Schulz,Tianmin Shu,Felix A. Sosa,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tan Zhi-Xuan,Tomer Ullman,Fei Xu,Ilker Yildirim,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Thomas L. Griffiths,Tobias Gerstenberg,Kevin Smith,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CogGym框架，旨在系统比较AI模型与人类在认知科学实验中的行为对齐程度。作者将258项认知实验标准化为统一的"实验标记语言"（EML），并评估了50个大语言模型。研究发现更大的模型与人类判断的对齐程度更高，但在常识推理任务上的提升速度远慢于在数学和编程等正式推理基准上的提升，且模型与人类的拟合度仍远低于人类自身的一致性水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统虽然能力日益增强，但其与人类思维和行为的相似程度仍缺乏标准化、可扩展的衡量框架。现有的AI基准测试侧重于客观答案的准确性，而认知科学实验关注的是人类在面对带有不确定性、系统性偏见和个体差异时的反应模式。当前评估AI与人类认知对齐的研究较为分散，缺乏系统性和可复现性。

Method: 1）从30多个研究实验室收集258项已发表的认知实验（涵盖物理推理、因果学习、概念学习、道德判断等常识推理任务）；2）开发"实验标记语言"（EML），将异构实验标准化为统一的JSON格式；3）构建半自动化的AI代理+人工审核管道，将原始实验转换为EML，平均每个实验约需1小时；4）使用统一提示在50个LLM上运行实验，每个试验重复10次；5）使用R²（人类与模型均值响应之间的Pearson相关系数平方）和归一化分布散度（连续项使用Earth Mover's Distance，分类项使用Jensen-Shannon Divergence）作为对齐指标；6）对15个子集实验招募521名人类参与者进行复现以验证实验保真度。

Result: 1）模型与人类对齐随模型规模和发布时间增加而提升（R²在文本上r=+0.81，图像r=+0.75，视频r=+0.81），但在常识推理上的提升远慢于在GPQA Diamond、MATH和HumanEval等正式推理基准上的提升；2）即使最佳模型（Gemini 3.1 Pro）也仅达到R²=0.59（文本）、0.58（图像）、0.43（视频），远低于人类自身信度（文本0.93，图像0.95，视频0.92）；3）视频实验对齐程度显著低于文本和图像；4）物理推理是对齐程度最低的主题；5）在控制模型规模后，发布时间不再显著预测对齐程度；6）人类复现数据与原始人类数据的R²=0.84，显著高于Gemini 3.1 Pro与原始人类的R²=0.62，表明低对齐源于真实的模型差异而非数据质量问题；7）模型识别出源论文但识别能力与拟合度不相关，说明污染不是主要问题。

Conclusion: CogGym提供了一个可复现、可扩展的科学基础设施，用于比较AI模型与人类在认知任务上的行为对齐。研究表明，尽管更大的模型在模拟人类判断方面有所改善，但模型与人类常识推理的对齐程度仍远低于人类自身的一致性水平，且在视频和物理推理任务上差距尤为显著。这种对齐差距可能源于当前训练流程更擅长具有可验证答案的领域（如数学和编程），而主观判断和复杂视觉场景推理能力不足。CogGym将作为"活"评估框架持续纳入新实验，帮助研究者诊断模型在哪些方面像人、哪些方面不像人。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling human intelligence are parallel goals shared by artificial intelligence (AI) and cognitive science. As AI systems grow increasingly capable, in what ways do model responses resemble human responses, and where do they systematically diverge? The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. We introduce CogGym, a scalable, unified framework grounded in cognitive science for systematically comparing model and human behavior on matched experimental trials. CogGym uses a semi-automated, human-in-the-loop pipeline to standardize diverse experimental paradigms into a task-agnostic Experiment Markup Language (EML), enabling reproducible and faithful comparison at scale. For initial release, we curate and standardize 258 cognitive experiments from 100 papers that focuses on human commonsense reasoning, and evaluate 50 large language models against human responses. We find a clear scaling trend where larger and more recent AI models better reproduce human judgments. Yet AI models' improvement on such common reasoning tasks is considerably slower than the gains observed on formal-reasoning benchmarks like math and coding, and model--human fit remains well below human splithalf reliability ($R^2 = 0.93$ on text, $0.95$ on image, and $0.92$ on video) with the best models achieving $R^2 = 0.59$ on text, $0.58$ on image, and $0.43$ on video experiments. We intend for CogGym to provide a living evaluation framework that continually incorporates new cognitive science experiments to characterize where model behavior resembles human behavior, where it systematically diverges, and how those patterns change as models and experiments evolve.

</details>


### [183] [PlaceReasoner-Beta: Reasoning-Driven Macro Placement and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21263)
*Qiufeng Li,Chengxuan Wang,Rongqian Chen,Quan Cheng,Yihui Ren,Chia-Tung Ho,David Z. Pan,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PlaceReasoner-Beta，一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的多智能体框架，将VLSI宏块布局(macro placement)重新定义为一个视觉推理与验证驱动的闭环优化问题，而非传统的黑盒数值优化。同时发布开源基准PlaceReasoner-Bench，包含16个任务（8个设计×2种宽高比），用真实布线后PPA和DRC进行评估而非代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏块布局方法存在三大局限：(1) 主要优化线长等代理目标而非端到端PPA质量；(2) 缺乏多模态推理与领域知识整合；(3) 设计空间探索效率低且对floorplan几何适应性差。本文旨在通过agentic推理范式弥补这些差距。

Method: 提出四智能体闭环框架：(1) Planner（VLM）基于floorplan图像、宏块规格、连接性生成N个候选布局；(2) Geometric Checker执行几何合法性检查与修复（含力导向合法化）；(3) Physical Checker基于早期物理实现反馈进行空间诊断与优化；(4) Post-route Optimizer利用最终布线PPA和DRC结果做迭代精炼。所有智能体共享一个封装EDA工具调用与布局专家知识的skill pack，将高层推理与底层执行解耦。基准采用Yosys+OpenROAD+Nangate45+FakeRAM 2.0全开源工具链。

Result: 在PlaceReasoner-Bench的16个任务上，PlaceReasoner-Beta在所有8个方形(1:1)任务上取得DRC-clean方法中最佳时序，post-route TNS相对经典基线场平均降低61.2%（1:1）和53.0%（2:1）。它是唯一能在全部8个细长(2:1)设计上以≤2个DRV完成布线的方法，且多数设计上布线长度更短（尽管从未显式优化线长）。消融实验证实视觉接地与skill pack对精炼过程互补且关键。运行时约12分钟/设计，token消耗约290k/设计。

Conclusion: 该工作将宏块布局从单目标坐标优化转变为可检查、可验证、可迭代的多模态推理过程，并提供可复现的开源端到端基准。未来的工作方向包括：微调本地VLM骨干、多样性感知候选采样、以及直接从post-route反馈中学习布局策略。

Abstract: Automated macro placement remains a fundamental challenge in VLSI physical design. Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [184] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生产环境中LLM Agent的高效周期性评估问题，对比了四种部分评估方法，并基于实际部署经验给出推荐方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产LLM Agent随着模型、提示、工具等迭代需要反复评测，但完整评测（519题约3小时）成本高昂。已有高效评估方法主要在公开基准上对不同模型做校准，对于同一个生产Agent随时间演化的周期性评测场景了解甚少。

Method: 利用某生产分析Agent（数万月活用户）在52天内的574次历史评测运行，按时间划分为287次校准运行与287次留出测试运行。对比四种方法：随机采样、历史缓存、难度分层固定子集（含加权估计与gp-IRT估计）、IRT自适应测试（Rasch与多维2PL + D-optimal选择）。指标：得分保真度（MAE）与排序保真度（Spearman、Kendall）。并开展跨Agent迁移实验（5个Agent家族299次运行）与校准窗口长度敏感性分析。

Result: 1）多维2PL自适应测试得分保真度最佳：执行200题（38.5%）MAE仅1.03pp，k=400时降至0.29pp；2）小预算下难度分层固定子集最优（k=100时gp-IRT为2.65pp）；3）历史缓存排序保持好但得分MAE高；4）固定子集无需重新校准即可迁移至5个Agent家族，11/12预算下gp-IRT优于随机采样；5）校准窗口从4周缩短至1天（14次运行）几乎不损失精度（k=200时MAE由1.40pp变为1.41pp）。最终选择难度分层固定子集部署，因其操作简单、支持并行执行、便于逐题对比诊断。

Conclusion: 对于周期性演化的生产Agent评估，难度分层固定子集是统计精度与运维简便性的最佳平衡点；自适应测试在统计上略优但需要串行编排与逐次能力更新，运维成本更高；历史缓存虽简单但得分估计误差偏大。建议在Agent发生重大变化时重新校准，并定期跑全量基准以监测固定子集估计偏差。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience. Using 574 historical runs of the production benchmark, split chronologically into calibration and held-out periods, we compare random sampling, historical caching, fixed representative subsets, and IRT-based adaptive testing. The results show that multidimensional 2PL adaptive testing achieves the best overall score fidelity: executing 200 questions, 38.5% of a full run, yields 1.03 pp of MAE. We nevertheless deployed difficulty-stratified fixed subsets because of their operational simplicity, and show they transfer without recalibration to five other agent families and remain stable across calibration windows as short as one day. Drawing on this deployment experience, we report practical recommendations for recurring production-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [185] [GameASG-Bench: Benchmarking Autonomous Software Generation for Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21293)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Yi Chen,Shusheng Xu,Fan Li,Huan Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GameASG-Bench 是一个面向自主软件生成（ASG）的浏览器游戏生成基准，包含 47 个任务（12 个主要游戏类型，2D/3D 兼有）。其核心创新在于将行为可测试性作为生成任务的一部分——在生成前预先声明评估接口规范（合法起始场景、玩家级操作、稳定快照、拒绝行为、不变量），通过 L1 源码检查和 L2 浏览器执行检查结合语义观察、真实输入和运行时证据进行评估。实验覆盖 9 个智能体栈，关键发现：高平均检查通过率（如 GPT-6-Astra 的 L2 通过率 93.2%）掩盖了任务级合规差距（最高严格任务成功率仅 55.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的代码生成基准（如函数级、仓库级）无法验证生成的完整应用程序是否满足行为需求。一个程序可能通过编译、包含合理的机制，但因其控件、共享状态、渲染和用户交互逻辑无法协同工作而不可用。组件级正确性无法完整评估自主软件生成（ASG），需要一个能够测试生成应用运行时行为是否满足规范的基准。游戏开发因其行为密集、交互复杂且边界明确，是 ASG 的理想测试领域。

Method: 1. **预生成评估接口规范**：任务作者在生成前定义 window.__gameTest 接口（reset、loadScenario、input、getSnapshot 四个方法），固定合法起始场景、玩家级操作、稳定快照、拒绝行为和不变量，允许私有实现开放。2. **分层评估**：L1 检查源代码合规性（结构性检查、正则表达式、反模式），L2 检查在无头 Chromium 中执行的行为，结合语义观察、真实浏览器输入和运行时证据。3. **需求优先级**：P0（先决条件）、P1（核心需求）、P2（扩展能力）。4. **严格任务成功**：要求有效交付、评估完成、且所有 L1 检查和所有适用的 L2 P0/P1 检查通过。5. **质量控制**：独立验证参考实现 + 自动验收测试。6. **实验设计**：通过四个研究问题（端到端性能、工具访问与轮次预算、推理努力、框架选择）系统评估 9 个智能体栈。

Result: **RQ1（端到端性能）**：9 个智能体栈中，GPT-6-Astra + Codex CLI 获得最高严格任务成功率 55.3%（26/47），Claude-Opus-5 为 51.1%（24/47）。L1 平均通过率 97.7%-99.6%，但严格任务成功率仅 14.9%-55.3%，暴露了高平均通过率与完整合规之间的差距。**RQ2（工具访问与轮次预算）**：DeepSeek-V4-Flash 全工具访问获 18 项成功，限制工具下仅 6-9 项；轮次预算 30→120 时，严格任务成功率从 14.9% 升至 38.3%，完成任务数从 10/47 增至 47/47。**RQ3（推理努力）**：高推理努力获 19 项成功（最高），最大推理努力仅 18 项但 P1 检查通过率最高（86.7%）。高努力比最大努力节省 26.9% 推理 token。**RQ4（框架选择）**：Claude Code 与 Codex CLI 各获 18 项成功，但仅 10 项在两者下均成功，暴露了不同成功集合。**诊断分析**：真实输入检查和场景-时间组合检查可发现组件分别测试时遗漏的交互失败。

Conclusion: GameASG-Bench 表明，在自主游戏生成中评估行为合规性具有重要价值，但其揭示的核心问题远超游戏领域：高的平均检查通过率会掩盖单个必需检查的失败，导致任务级合规差距。即使 L2 检查通过率达 93.2%，严格任务成功率仅 55.3%。该基准提供了一个通用协议和任务级诊断工具，可用于研究需求合规性，实验证明工具访问、轮次预算、推理努力和框架选择均影响结果，且效果并非单调。预生成评估接口规范使固定检查能够一致地访问不同的私有实现，结合真实输入与运行时证据，有效识别违反任务需求的具体行为。

Abstract: Autonomous software generation (ASG) aims to turn human requirements into executable applications, but delivering these applications does not necessarily establish that their interacting components satisfy the specified behavioral requirements. We introduce GameASG-Bench, a benchmark that makes behavioral testability part of the generation task for game development. Our design declares an evaluation interface specification before generation, fixing legal starting scenarios, player-level actions, stable snapshots, rejection behavior, and invariants while leaving private implementations open. Concretely, we include: (i) static L1 checks that assess source-level compliance; and (ii) browser-executed L2 checks that combine semantic observations with real input and runtime evidence. We implement this protocol as 47 browser-native game-generation tasks spanning 12 primary genres and both 2D and 3D interaction, each with executable checks and an independently verified reference implementation. Our experiments answer four key questions about end-to-end agent performance, tool access and nominal turn budget, reasoning effort, and harness choice. Across nine agent stacks, the highest observed mean L2 check pass rate is 93.2%, yet the highest observed strict task success rate, requiring all L1 and applicable L2 prerequisite and core requirement checks, is only 55.3% (26/47 tasks). For DeepSeek-V4-Flash, full tool access and larger nominal turn budgets yield more strict task successes, while the strict task success rate is not monotonic in reasoning effort. Both tested harnesses achieve 18 strict task successes, but only ten tasks succeed under both. These results expose task-level compliance gaps that high average check pass rates actually obscure.

</details>


### [186] [LEGIT: Credentialing Protocol for Trustworthy AI Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21325)
*Steve Drew,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEGIT是一个面向AI代理市场（agentic marketplaces）的凭证协议，通过三层架构——认证层（Certification）、声誉层（Reputation）和市场分配层（Marketplace Allocation）——将代理的能力测量、任务结果记录与拍卖机制连接起来。其核心是一份签名的结构化凭证，将领域质量（q）、单位解决任务成本（CoP）、代理配置（模型、harness、工具、参数）、评估预算（token budget）、证据和有效期绑定在一起，由认证机构用Ed25519签名。论文通过18种代理配置在GAIA（127题）和MATH-500（127题）上的对照实验，以及基于折扣Beta更新的Sybil攻击经济分析，证明凭证必须同时记录质量与成本、并绑定预算和工具配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理市场（如OpenAI GPT Store、Salesforce AgentExchange、AWS Marketplace、Agent Stack等）中，买家无法可靠地比较各代理在不同配置、工具、prompt、harness下的真实能力。已报告的benchmark分数难以跨任务、软件栈和预算验证，导致"劣币驱逐良币"的市场逆向选择问题。现有工作（HELM、τ-bench、Efficient Agents、AgentDiet、GAIA leaderboard等）已证明harness设计可使同一模型的工具调用能力相差29%、成本降低28.4%、输入token减少39.9–59.7%，但缺少将这些测量与可验证身份绑定的协议层。

Method: 协议采用三层架构：
1. **认证层（Layer 1）**：定义代理a=(M,S,T,P)，即模型依赖M、harness S、工具集T、参数配置P；凭证Γ_a=(ξ_a, q_a, e_a, π_a, t_a, σ_a)，其中q_a,D为领域D平均质量、e_a,D=CoP为单位解决任务成本（总成本除以总credited quality，超预算记零分）；作用域ξ_a记录配置、域名、评分规则、token预算B_D、币种、价格、评估日期和签发者；证据π_a指向记录输出；σ_a为签发者签名。
2. **声誉层（Layer 2）**：采用Jøsang折扣Beta更新p(θ_d|data)=Beta(α_d, β_d)，引入持久因子η∈(0,1]对历史观察做几何折扣，α_d^(t)=η·α_d^(t-1)+s_d，β_d^(t)=η·β_d^(t-1)+(1-s_d)；声誉分ρ_a,d^(t)=α_d^(t)/(α_d^(t)+β_d^(t))；新身份初始α=β=1。
3. **市场分配层（Layer 3）**：基于Che的多维评分框架，定义Score(b_a)=w_q·q_commit+w_e·(1−e_a,D/e_max)+w_ρ·ρ_a−w_p·p_a/p_max，权重非负且和为1，w_p>0；支付规则采用第二高分数作为支付门槛。
评估方法：在GAIA 127题共享任务池上比较3个模型（claude-haiku-4-5、gpt-5.6-luna、gpt-5.6-sol）× 6种harness（baseline、ReAct、plan-execute、reflective retry、system_heavy、notes_memory），含受限与不受限web检索，温度=0，固定model snapshot，报告质量、CoP和Pareto支配；统计用配对bootstrap CI（2000重采样）和回顾性精确配对检验（含Bonferroni校正）。Sybil分析在折扣Beta模型下推导闭式n_rep，对完美反馈和含噪Bernoulli反馈（p=0.9、0.8）分别量化每个身份的锁定存款和已花费费用。

Result: 在B=8000 token下，gpt-5.6-sol baseline解55题，gpt-5.6-luna baseline解43题，claude-haiku-4-5 baseline解19题；模型对比κ̂：sol/luna=1.28[1.09,1.55]，luna/haiku=2.26[1.64,3.55]，sol/haiku=2.90[2.04,4.69]；所有三个baseline模型配对检验经Bonferroni校正后均显著（最小p=1.69×10⁻⁸）。四个harness×三个模型的析因分析显示：模型F=72.96（η²=0.088），harness F=0.82（η²=0.002），交互F=0.56（η²=0.002）；六harness扩展分析F值同harness Harness=0.71；15个模型内harness配对检验经校正后均不显著（最大校正p=1.000）。受限于B=16000 token不受限检索：luna web搜索相对baseline解决38→48题（成功率提升26.3%），sol检索后反而下降54→52题；luna每次成功任务成本飙至baseline的17×；检索在luna的68题、sol的70题上超出token预算。受限检索（≤2次调用）：luna 37→57题（κ̂=1.54，95%CI[1.21,2.10]，p=0.007），sol 56→63题（κ̂=1.13，CI[0.92,1.41]）；luna每次成功任务成本为baseline的5.7×。预算敏感性：luna web搜索在B′=8000时仅达baseline的0.45×，在B′=16000时达1.26×，排名随预算翻转；sol无工具在B′=4000即达全预算质量的95%。同模型内harness的CoP比最高1.43×（sol）、1.63×（luna）、4.95×（haiku）。重复性：5次运行标准差haiku=3.4（约20%）、luna=3.1（约8%）、sol=1.1（约2%），即使在温度0下也存在显著波动。MATH-500（127题，B=8000）：haiku 118→120、luna 123→125、sol均127；retry成本比为2.29×、2.36×、2.41×。Pareto前沿含luna baseline、sol ReAct、sol reflective retry三点；纯质量排序会隐藏整个成本轴。Sybil经济性：在完美反馈、η=0.9下，达到ρ_target=0.70需2次交互、0.80需3次、0.90需6次、0.95需10次、0.99需23次；闭式公式与直接迭代在36种(η, ρ_target)组合下完全一致。存款s_min=$10、$100、$500，每次费用$1时，每个身份从ρ=0.5到0.90需$6+$10=$16，ρ=0.95需$10+$10=$20，ρ=0.99需$23+$10=$33（总投入$33–$533）。含噪反馈（p=0.9）下达到0.99需约166次交互；p=0.8下34.1%试验在5000次内无法达成0.99。

Conclusion: LEGIT通过签名结构化凭证将代理能力测量、声誉记录与市场分配三层耦合，凭证作用域显式包含模型、harness、工具、参数、域名、token预算和证据记录，使买家可独立验证而非依赖厂商自报质量。对照实验证明：模型间质量差异显著且经校正后稳健，harness间质量差异在该网格下无统计显著性，但同一模型内CoP可相差至4.95×，且检索工具的收益随模型和token预算翻转，因此质量与成本必须同列、配置与预算必须绑定。Sybil经济分析在折扣Beta模型下推导了闭式所需交互数n_rep，给出"锁定存款+已花费费用"作为操纵声誉的资金下限，为协议参数化提供定量依据。论文明确未评估端到端市场表现、战略性出价、长期凭证漂移等维度，提出未来工作需在保留任务上对比credential-based selection与仅模型/仅质量/仅价格基线。

Abstract: Agentic marketplaces are emerging where AI agents with varying capabilities autonomously complete specialized tasks for buyers. A major challenge of such marketplaces is that buyers cannot easily determine which agent will perform best on their tasks. Reported benchmark scores may be difficult to verify or compare across tasks, software, and budgets. We introduce LEGIT, a credentialing protocol connecting certification, reputation, and proposed marketplace allocation. Certification binds measured quality and cost per solved task to an agent configuration, task domain, evaluation budget, and evidence through a signed record. Reputation links records of past task outcomes to the same identity, subject to the reliability of the reported feedback. Buyers and agents can verify credential records and inspect optional visual profiles. Evaluations reveal cost differences between agent configurations with similar observed task success, and show that comparisons depend on the evaluation budget. These results support binding performance measurements to the tested configuration and resource limits. A complementary analysis quantifies the deposits and fees required for reputation manipulation under a stated Sybil attack model.

</details>


### [187] [Offline Multimodal Large Language Models for Decision Support in Air Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21390)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Jelton A. Cunha,Gabriel Dietzsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对空军作战中因隔离/安全限制而无法使用云端AI的痛点，提出了一套基于离线大语言模型（LLM）的多模态检索增强生成（RAG）决策支持架构，并联合巴西空军（FAB）4名图像分析员开展了先导性实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代空天作战中，分析员需在断网/气隙环境下，依据厚达数百页的技术条令对侦察图像中的电子目标（如雷达、天线）进行判读，认知负荷极高且易出错；现有高性能云端LLM因安全合规问题无法部署于涉密环境，且通用LLM存在幻觉问题，难以满足条令可追溯的要求。

Method: 1. 提出模块化离线多模态RAG架构：文档分段（1024 token，128 overlap）→ 去重 → OCR+视觉语言模型（MiniCPM-V 8B）生成图表描述 → BGE-M3多语言嵌入→ChromaDB存储。2. 两阶段检索：Bi-Encoder粗筛Top-20，BGE-Reranker-v2-m3 Cross-Encoder重排，取Top-5注入Qwen-2.5 14B等本地生成模型。3. 引入用户反馈机制（thumbs up/down）动态调整Prompt规则。4. 基于FAB的MCA 200-5电子目标条令开展N=4人先导研究，对比人机在10题知识测试中的表现，并用Raw NASA-TLX测量人工撰写REMIR报告的认知负荷。

Result: 1. 知识测试：3名分析员得10/10，1名得8/10（平均26.5分钟）；系统（Qwen2.5:14b优化模式）同样得8/10，仅用7.1分钟。2. 模型性能与Prompt优化：Qwen2.5:14b综合最优；强制要求简答（Optimized Regime）较要求详细解释（Standard Regime）带来最高15倍加速（如Llama3.1:8b从44s/题降至2.94s/题），且准确率反升（5→7分），揭示小模型存在"自洽性偏置"问题。3. 认知负荷（RTLX均值4.21/7）：心理需求最高（6.0/7），努力次之（5.0/7），性能自评良好（2.0/7）。

Conclusion: 研究证实离线多模态RAG架构在严苛安全环境下具备可行性与实用性，能在保证条令可追溯的前提下显著提升速度并匹配其准确率；人工REMIR撰写对资深分析员仍属高负荷工作，凸显AI辅助的必要性。未来工作包括：1）执行已设计好的AI辅助条件组内评估协议；2）引入CLIP/SigLIP实现图-图检索；3）模型量化与批处理以适配低功耗硬件；4）开发自动生成侦察报告模板；5）拓展至其他条令；6）建立输出忠实度的自动化验证指标。

Abstract: Air operations rely on complex rules, established procedures, and time-critical analysis under limited connectivity and strict security constraints. In such environments, analysts must combine written doctrine with images, often without access to external computing resources. This paper studies offline large language models as decision support tools, deployed in isolated and restricted environments to give analysts access to doctrinal knowledge that remains traceable to its original sources through natural language interaction. We describe a modular retrieval-augmented architecture suitable for operation without Internet connectivity, supporting both text and image input from technical manuals. As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we report a pilot study with four image analysts of the Brazilian Air Force, combining (i) a doctrinal knowledge assessment based on their electronic-target identification doctrine, comparing human and proposed system performance on the same test, and (ii) a measurement of the cognitive workload involved in manually producing a reconnaissance target report (Relatório de Missão de Reconhecimento - REMIR) without AI assistance. The results show a demanding manual task, especially in terms of mental demand (6.0/7) and effort (5.0/7), while the proposed system matches the human score (8/10) and completes the assessment in 7.1 minutes (compared to a human average of 26.5 minutes), establishing a baseline for future AI-assisted evaluation. Finally, we describe a future evaluation protocol to systematically compare manual and AI-assisted workflows.

</details>


### [188] [DENSE: Distilling Agent Trajectories into Evidence-Grounded Shortcut Trees for Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21423)
*Siyuan Liu,Fan Yu,Dongyu Ru,Yizhu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 DENSE 方法与 REFIT 评估协议，旨在不依赖后验结果标签的情况下，从智能体执行轨迹中提炼可复用的反馈。实验证明 DENSE 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 基准上显著提升了多个模型的严格通过率并降低了 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体部署产生大量执行轨迹，但任务级验证和专家标注难以扩展。后验结果标签不可得时，如何从轨迹本身提取可复用的反馈以支持智能体自我优化，是核心问题。

Method: 提出两阶段方法 DENSE：(1) 从有序动作-观察队列中识别语义边界，构建嵌套子任务树；(2) 自底向上压缩连续尝试为"捷径节点"，利用后续恢复证据跨层级调和遗留问题，并选择性渲染已完成/未完成分支。提出 REFIT 评估协议：共享同一初始轨迹（run0），各方法独立生成反馈，重置环境与上下文进行 run1，仅以严格通过率作为反馈效用衡量标准。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1（89 任务）上，DENSE 在 MiniMax-M2.7、DeepSeek V4 Pro、GPT-5.5、Kimi K2.6 四个接收模型上均取得非特权反馈方法中的最高严格通过率，较 run0 提升 7.12–15.64 个百分点；recipient token 减少 19.0%–43.6%。GPT-5.5 消融实验验证了嵌套子任务分析与捷径构造及问题调和的必要性。MiniMax 上 DENSE 甚至超过使用后验验证信号的 Verifier Feedback（+7.49 pp）。

Conclusion: 缺失后验标签的轨迹仍包含可复用的经验证据。DENSE 通过嵌套捷径树将可复用进展与未完成需求显式关联，使接收方能在新环境中更高效地重建成功路径并避免重复探索。这种基于证据的轨迹复用为减少对外部监督依赖的智能体自优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Online agent deployments produce abundant execution traces, while task-specific verification and expert annotation are costly to scale. We study how to distill these traces into reusable feedback without post-hoc outcome labels, drawing on their evidence of local progress, recovery, and unfinished requirements. We introduce DENSE (Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions), which organizes this evidence into evidence-grounded nested shortcut trees. DENSE compresses redundant attempts, reconciles issues across levels using recovery evidence, and summarizes completed branches while expanding unresolved ones, linking reusable progress to remaining obligations. We introduce REFIT, a source-paired protocol comparing feedback from shared initial trajectories under post-hoc outcome blindness, with environments and model contexts reset for fresh attempts at the same tasks. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, DENSE achieves the highest strict pass rate among tested non-privileged feedback methods across four recipient models. Relative to initial executions, strict pass rate improves by 7.12-15.64 pp, with 19.0-43.6% fewer observed recipient tokens in reruns. GPT-5.5 ablations support combining nested subtask analysis with shortcut construction and issue reconciliation. These findings point toward agent self-refinement through evidence-grounded trajectory reuse with less reliance on external supervision.

</details>


### [189] [GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21432)
*Kaichen Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dingqian Hong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GVPO++ 提出 Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO)，一种无需重要性采样的 LLM 后训练方法，通过将 KL 约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权，提供了唯一最优解保证，并自然扩展到跨词表的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）及扩展目标族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 等现有方法依赖重要性采样处理离策略样本，导致重要性比率无界、梯度估计高方差不稳定，限制了实际部署。同时 DPO 存在多解及对齐失败问题，缺乏唯一最优性保证。在线蒸馏则受限于词表兼容性和标准 reverse-KL 的长度偏差。

Method: 核心方法包含三个层面：(1) 通过组内权重"零和"条件消去配分函数 Z(x)，将 GVPO 梯度构造为隐式奖励与实际奖励中心距离之差的均方误差；(2) 给出三种等价解释：负对数似然、MSE 回归和方差-协方差正则化分解；(3) 将 GVPO 推广至 OPD，定义扩展 reverse-KL 目标族，使权重方案 f(x,y) 引入诱导的 Boltzmann 分布 P_θ、P_t。理论证明唯一最优解定理 (Theorem IV.1–IV.3) 及扩展目标一致性定理 (Theorem V.1)。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-Math-7B 数学推理基准（AIME2024、AMC、MATH500、Minerva、OlympiadBench）上 GVPO 平均 52.02%，优于 GRPO (46.69%)、Dr.GRPO、Remax、Reinforce++、GSPO、GMPO、GPG、Cispo 等基线。Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 及 Reasoning Gym 多领域实验均验证了泛化性。OPD 实验中，使用 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 和 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 作为教师，GVPO 平均分别达到 35.99% 和 35.35%，比 SFT/SeqKD/PG/GKD/MiniLLM 提升 10%+，且支持跨词表蒸馏。消融实验表明 Var 和 Cov 正则项均为训练稳定的必要条件，熵正则替代会导致训练崩溃。摘要任务上 GVPO 也全面优于 DPO（奖励 5.75 vs 4.83，GPT-4o 胜率 69% vs 31%）。

Conclusion: GVPO 通过零和权重消去配分函数，同时获得唯一最优解保证和灵活的离策略采样能力，无需重要性采样即可支持 off-policy 训练与经验回放。其推广到 OPD 后可在序列级运作，兼容不同词表族系，并支持通过权重函数 f(x,y) 设计扩展目标族以抑制长度偏差等不良模式。理论与实验共同确立了 GVPO 作为 LLM 后训练与在线蒸馏的新范式。

Abstract: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling. We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling. Beyond general post-training, we show that GVPO naturally extends to on-policy distillation (OPD). Furthermore, GVPO enables the optimization of a broad family of extended OPD objectives, providing a principled foundation for diverse objective design. By unifying theoretical guarantees with practical adaptability, GVPO establishes a new paradigm for reliable and versatile LLM post-training and on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [190] [Risk-Aware Occupancy for Safety-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21470)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,Yiren Zhao,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"风险感知占据"(Risk-Aware Occupancy)作为端到端自动驾驶的密集表征,将其统一为BEV风险场,并设计ROIDrive框架与RiskOcc4D-nuScenes数据集,在碰撞率上实现显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶采用稀疏表征(目标+车道线),难以显式处理非结构化障碍物、遮挡区域和复杂交互等安全风险,缺乏连续空间场形式的可查询规划风险。

Method: 1) 提出Risk-Aware Occupancy:在BEV平面统一编码全局静态占据、地图交通规则约束、未来动态占据为可比较的归一化风险分数;2) 搭建ROIDrive:含独立RiskOcc分支(Bi-Mamba2+SDMap+先验,滑动窗口采样注入planning queries)+时序查询一致性TQC;3) 构建RiskOcc4D-nuScenes数据集,采用4条自动化标注流水线。

Result: 将RiskOcc融入GenAD后,开环碰撞率在UniAD指标下降低52.9%(0.17%→0.08%),ST-P3指标下降低35.0%(0.40%→0.26%);10余种3D占据骨干在RiskOcc4D基准上完成迁移,FB-Occ综合最优,DHDOcc-M在分类mIoU和动态Mask-MAE领先;消融证实RiskOcc优于3D-Occ/二值图/高斯图等替代表征。

Conclusion: 风险感知占据是一种面向规划最小充分统计量的密集BEV风险表征,与现有E2E框架正交兼容,可作为安全监督信号与中间特征,显著降低碰撞率,为安全导向的端到端自动驾驶提供了可解释、可检查的感知-规划统一接口。

Abstract: Sparse representation formulates the environment perception for the end-to-end driving system as a set of discrete elements like objects and lane lines. This formulation meets safety risks in crowded, occluded scenes dealing with unstructured obstacles, uncertain regions, and intricate interactions. In this paper, we propose a dense representation, risk-aware occupancy, to characterize planning-relevant risks in an explicit and uniform manner. It jointly encodes global scene occupancy, map-derived traffic constraints, and future dynamic agent occupancy into a unified BEV map. The unified BEV map captures the risk evidence for trajectory planning in both spatial and temporal dimensions. We design an E2E network, ROIDrive, to realize risk-aware occupancy. It predicts risk-aware occupancy with an independent branch and injects it into planning queries for safety-oriented trajectory generation. In addition, to quantify the safety problem, we introduce RiskOcc4D-nuScenes built upon nuscenes and occ3d-nuscenes. Our risk-aware occupancy yields relative open-loop collision reductions of 52.9% under the UniAD metric and 35.0% under the ST-P3 metric on nuScenes.

</details>


### [191] [Driving on Registers, Reasoning on Risk: Risk-Aware Occupancy for Register-Based End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21486)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,YuKai Qin,Yiren Zhao,Lidong Yu,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RRDrive，一种基于寄存器（register-based）的端到端自动驾驶规划框架，核心创新是引入"风险感知占据栅格"（Risk-aware Occupancy, RiskOcc）作为密集、时序对齐、可按轨迹查询的风险表示，以同时改善多模态候选轨迹的生成质量与排序可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于寄存器的端到端规划器依赖稀疏场景表示（智能体与地图特征），缺少对非结构化障碍、道路边界、不确定区域以及未来交互的连续证据，导致两大耦合失效模式：(1) 候选生成预算浪费在不安全/偏离路线/无效的候选上；(2) 几何上高度相似的优质候选因缺乏局部未来风险信息而被误排。基线中坏候选与差候选占19.74%，oracle最优候选平均仅排在第33.9位。

Method: RRDrive在NAVSIM的DrivoR基线之上，从两条通路融入RiskOcc：(1) 风险条件化候选生成——风险分词器将BEV风险场（静态场景风险 R^s、时序动态风险 R^d、车道边界风险 R^l）编码为紧凑token，通过带LayerScale门控的双向交叉注意力与相机场景token融合，引导轨迹解码器将有限预算集中到可行行为；(2) 风险感知候选选择——对每条候选沿其自车足迹在时间对齐的风险场上进行双线性采样，取top-k均值得到局部风险剖面，汇总为均值/最大/最小/终点风险描述符，仅附加到风险敏感子指标（TTC、EP）的评分头。标注方面，构建了RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM（400×400网格，0.25m分辨率，11个序数风险类别，T=12帧@0.5s），采用三管线自动标注（地图派生车道风险、点云全局风险、动态代理风险）；并设计了独立的外部RiskOcc预测器（BEV编码器+解耦的静态/车道/动态头）以在非oracle条件下验证框架鲁棒性。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest上，RRDrive取得PDMS 0.951（相对基线0.937提升1.5%），平均候选PDMS从0.797升至0.858（+7.7%），好候选占比由80.3%升至88.7%，坏候选占比由13.6%降至7.6%；场景级Spearman由0.407升至0.584（+43.5%），oracle recall@1由5.1%升至15.4%（3.0×），oracle排序由33.9降至27.7。在生成困难场景候选PDMS提升30.2%（0.540→0.703），在混淆困难场景好候选内Spearman由0.265升至0.674（+0.41），oracle recall@1由3.6%升至24.9%。消融显示增益主要集中于EP（+0.0228）与TTC（+0.0083），安全子指标始终饱和。在传感器预测的有损RiskOcc下，PDMS仍达0.9400，超过无RiskOcc基线但低于oracle上界0.9506。

Conclusion: 本文将紧凑场景表示识别为多模态轨迹生成与排序的共享瓶颈，并通过密集、时序对齐、可按轨迹查询的风险表示加以解决。RRDrive在NAVSIM上达到端到端方法中的最高PDMS 0.951，显著改善候选质量与排序可靠性，尤其在最困难场景；外部RiskOcc预测器验证了增益不依赖oracle风险。主要局限在于多步时序动态RiskOcc的预测质量，是进一步提升的关键方向。

Abstract: Multimodal trajectory prediction improves behavioral coverage in end-to-end autonomous driving, but existing methods remain limited by sparse scene representations. Incomplete evidence leads to low-quality candidate generation and unreliable ranking among geometrically similar trajectories. On a register-based baseline, bad and poor candidates constitute 19.74% of the candidate set, while the oracle-best candidate ranks only 33.9th on average. We propose RRDrive, which introduces risk-aware occupancy as a dense, temporally aligned, and trajectory-queryable representation. Its global structure guides high-quality multimodal generation, while candidate-conditioned risk queries support fine-grained selection. We further construct RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM with automatic risk annotations. RRDrive achieves a selected-trajectory PDMS of 0.951, representing a 1.5% relative improvement over the baseline (0.937), and improves the average candidate PDMS by 7.7%. In challenging scenes, it improves candidate PDMS by 30.2% and increases the Spearman correlation among good candidates by 0.41, from 0.26 to 0.67. To move beyond this oracle setting, we further develop an external RiskOcc predictor, a perception module that estimates risk-aware occupancy directly from sensor inputs. The competitive performance validates the representation's feasibility.

</details>


### [192] [LogicTrack: Auditing Reasoning Trajectories of Large Language Models with Formal Logic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21492)
*Jingyu Hu,Shu Yang,Weiru Liu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LogicTrack是一个神经符号框架，通过自动形式化（auto-formalization）和自动定理证明器在推理生成时逐步审计大语言模型的思维链（CoT），并提出基于求解器的回溯奖励（SBR）来引导即时回溯再生，同时可扩展为带回溯轨迹的自监督微调数据，以同时提升推理过程的可验证性和最终答案正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的CoT优化方法主要依赖基于结果（outcome-based）的反馈，对中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性缺乏验证，导致模型可能通过逻辑上有瑕疵的路径到达正确答案，这在法律、科学等高风险领域不可信；同时现有过程监督方法依赖人类或LLM评判作为反馈信号，对逻辑正确性来说是非确定性的，难以可靠扩展。

Method: 1) 步骤分解：将每个NL推理步骤分解为上下文Ci、解释Ei、结论qi；
2) 自动形式化与验证：使用gpt-4o-mini作为autoformalizer将(C,Ei,qi)形式化为SMT-LIB规范Fi=(Φi,qi)，通过反证法由Z3求解器检验Φi⊧qi是否成立；
3) 求解器回溯奖励SBR：结合前提忠实度奖励R_fidelity（检查形式化断言是否基于原始前提且联合可满足）和求解器验证奖励R_verify（语法正确且逻辑有效/反驳/部分信用），公式为SBR=λ·R_fidelity+(1-λ)·R_verify；
4) 奖励引导的回溯搜索：当SBR低于阈值θ_step或运行平均低于θ_avg时触发回溯，附带失败反馈从失败位置重新生成；
5) 搜索策略：默认贪婪回溯，并与Beam Search和MCTS对比；
6) 回溯SFT扩展：利用求解器验证的真实回溯轨迹（错误步骤+回溯token<backtrack>+正确续步）构造约6000条SFT数据，添加<backtrack>作为新词表项，使用LoRA在Qwen2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-8B上微调2个epoch。

Result: 在8个推理基准（e-SNLI、FOLIO、LogiQA、ProntoQA、ProofWriter、QASC、SARA、SemEval）和7个LLM（Gemini-2.5、GPT-4o-mini、GPT-5-nano、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen2.5-14B）上，LogicTrack在133/168的基准-指标对比中超越BASE基线，最终答案准确率（OA）持平或改善，验证加权准确率（VWA）显著上升（如GPT-4o-mini从21.51%升至46.61%、Llama-3.1-8B从9.24%升至32.75%），未验证比率（UR）大幅下降（如Gemini-2.5在FOLIO从75.63%降至27.79%）；消融显示R_verify贡献最大、完整SBR最优，贪婪回溯优于Beam Search和MCTS；LogicTrack-SFT在OA上进一步超越推理时LogicTrack（如Llama-3.1-8B从56.81%升至68.08%），且在OA上优于LogicReward（0.6808 vs 0.4338），表明其构造了更高质量的SFT训练数据。

Conclusion: LogicTrack作为生成时神经符号审计框架，通过自动形式化与求解器逐步验证有效提升了CoT的可验证性和最终答案正确率；求解器反馈是VWA提升的主要驱动力；其真实回溯轨迹可用于SFT，使模型将分步审计内化为固有能力；未来工作可关注自动形式化优化、验证奖励变体、回溯策略及让LLM进一步内化验证能力的微调方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has been shown to improve the performance of large language models (LLMs), yet existing optimization methods largely rely on outcome-based feedback, leaving the logical validity of intermediate reasoning steps largely unverified. To address the gap whereby LLMs arrive at correct final answers through logically flawed intermediate reasoning chains, we propose LogicTrack, a neuro-symbolic framework that audits reasoning trajectories by auto-formalizing each reasoning step into symbolic representations and verifying it with automated theorem provers. LogicTrack introduces Solver-Based Backtracking Reward (SBR), a step-wise scoring mechanism that quantifies logical soundness and guides backtracking tree search at inference time. We further extend LogicTrack to construct supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data with backtracking traces from its trajectories, enabling fine-tuned models to internalize step-wise auditing as an intrinsic capability. Extensive experiments across 8 reasoning benchmarks and 7 LLMs demonstrate that LogicTrack effectively improves both the verifiability of reasoning chains and final answer pass rate, thereby enhancing overall CoT quality and trustworthiness in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [193] [PolyBridgeBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Physics-Grounded Bridge Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21493)
*Zicheng Zhao,Dongyin Chen,Rui Xu,Yinghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyBridgeBench 是一个可执行的多模态桥梁设计基准，包含 189 个关卡和 27 个结构族，通过确定性合法性检查和原生物理仿真来评估 MLLM 在有限交互预算下生成、修复承重结构的能力。实验涵盖 6 个代表性 MLLM，发现确定性与动态功能之间存在显著差距，模型对材料预算高度敏感，且在严格预算下故障后修复能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准分别评估空间推理、结构有效性和物理施工，但无法判断 MLLM 是否能合成完整的承重结构并在仿真失败后进行修复。该工作旨在填补这一联合评估的空白。

Method: 1. 构建包含 189 关卡的 PolyBridgeBench，分为核心结构套件（149）和组合挑战套件（40）。2. 设计分层诊断协议：确定性合法性检查 → 原生物理仿真 → 固定预算下的修复。3. 分别度量 Valid@k、Success@k 和 Recovery@k。4. 在严格和宽松材料预算下评估 6 个 MLLM。

Result: 实验揭示：（1）松预算下 Gemini-3-Flash-Preview 以 84.7% Success@3 领先；严格预算下 Claude-Sonnet-5 以 50.8% 排名第一；（2）确定性有效性与动态功能之间存在显著差距（如 Qwen3-VL-Plus Valid@3 达 92.1% 而 Success@3 仅 12.2%）；（3）组合约束下所有模型表现急剧下降（严格预算下最佳 Success@3 仅 15.0%）；（4）故障后修复能力有限（严格预算下 Recovery@3 最高仅 22.0%）。

Conclusion: PolyBridgeBench 区分了规则合规与物理功能之间的差距，表明高确定性有效率不能预测动态成功，材料预算合规是必要但不充分条件。该基准为评估 MLLM 生成可执行工程结构的能力提供了可复现、可审计的测试平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models, or MLLMs, perform well at visual understanding and structured generation, yet these capabilities do not establish whether an engineering design will work when executed. Existing benchmarks assess spatial reasoning, structural validity, or physics-grounded construction, but they do not determine whether MLLMs can synthesize complete load-bearing structures and repair them after simulator execution exposes a failure. We introduce PolyBridgeBench, an executable benchmark for multimodal bridge design. A model receives a visual scene and structured engineering constraints and generates a complete node--member--material topology. Deterministic legality checks gate execution in a native dynamic physics simulation. Following an execution failure, the benchmark returns temporal visual evidence from the failed rollout and evaluates repair under a fixed interaction budget. Separate measurements of deterministic validity, dynamic functional success, and post-failure recovery identify the stage at which design fails. Experiments with six representative MLLMs across 189 levels expose a substantial gap between deterministic validity and dynamic success, pronounced sensitivity to material budgets, and limited post-failure recovery under the primary strict-budget setting.

</details>


### [194] [The Communication Bottleneck: A Round-Trip Study of Tree-Structured Expression Serialization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21509)
*Xavier Suau,Alex Ferrando de las Morenas,Luca Zappella,Samy Bengio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"往返协议"来测量语言模型通过自然语言传输树形组合结构的保真度。16个模型两两配对生成N×N通信矩阵，发现自然语言通道是有损且非对称的：至少73.6%的失败源于生成端，难度由树形结构（算子数、深度、右分支）驱动而非模型家族；约3600个微调样本即可让所有开源模型的性能超越未经微调的最强前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思考、多智能体管道和工具调用中，模型常将层次化结构序列化进自然语言。若该编码有损，下游模型无论多强都无法恢复原始信息。现有基准测试均评估单模型，无法衡量模型间通信保真度。本文将Allamanis等人提出的"通信鸿沟"作为研究对象，定量回答"多少树形组合内容能幸存于自然语言瓶颈"。

Method: 1) 程序化生成2-8个算子、深度2-6的算术表达式骨架（2450个）；2) 往返协议：生成器G将表达式e转为不含数学符号的文字题w（规则泄漏守卫确保通过纯语言通道），提取器E从w恢复ê，SymPy符号等价性作为精确oracle；3) 16个模型（10个自回归+2个扩散+4个前沿）两两配对形成N×N通信矩阵Q，边际值分离生成质量与提取质量；4) 多提取器共识进行故障归因；5) QLoRA在算术（匹配语义）和装配（不交叉域）两种微调上测试通道可训练性。

Result: 最佳通信对为GPT-5生成+Gemini-3.1-Pro提取达92.9%，高于任何自通信；角色互换最大可导致60.4pp差距。生成端至少73.6%的失败，错误算子数是最主要错误类型（71.8%）。SHAP归因显示模型规模占57.0%，算子数、深度、线性化复杂度ℓ(T)主导结构预测，右分支树比左分支更难（控制深度后Spearman ρ̄=-0.48）。算术微调（~3600样本）将所有开源模型提升至54-60%生成质量，超过未经微调的Gemini-3.1-Pro；装配域微调也有普遍提升但仍存差距。命题逻辑复制保留主要排名（生成Spearman ρ=0.85）。

Conclusion: 树形组合结构通过自然语言传输存在有损且非对称的瓶颈，生成能力是主要限制因素而非提取能力。失败由结构属性（算子数、深度、分支方向）驱动，与模型家族几乎无关；该瓶颈可通过少量微调克服（匹配语义下）或部分克服（跨域）。实际建议：按角色而非总体强度匹配模型；优先使用结构化中间表示（在14B-32B上NL tax达18-20pp）。该协议为审计多模型管道中的静默结构错误提供了原则性工具。

Abstract: When language models reason in chain-of-thought or exchange free-text intermediates, they serialize structured information into natural language. How much tree-structured compositional content survives this bottleneck? We propose a round-trip protocol that answers this question empirically for tree-structured expressions. A generator converts a procedurally generated arithmetic expression into a word problem, a separate extractor recovers the expression from the word problem alone, and symbolic equivalence provides an exact oracle. Evaluating all pairwise combinations of sixteen models yields a communication matrix whose marginals separate generation quality from extraction quality. Three main findings emerge. First, the channel is lossy and asymmetric: swapping which model generates and which extracts shifts accuracy by up to 60.4 points, and the best pair reaches 92.9% by combining different models on each end rather than the same model on both. Second, at least 73.6% of round-trip failures originate at generation, and difficulty is driven by tree structure (operator count, depth, right-branching) rather than model family. Third, the channel is trainable: ~3600 fine-tuning examples that share the evaluation's operators and tree shapes lift every open-weight model above untrained Gemini-3.1-Pro, an upper bound under matched semantics. A disjoint-domain regime with new operators and vocabulary also raises every open-weight model, confirming the gain is not an artifact of matched semantics, though a gap to the frontier remains. Together these results identify tree-structured expression serialization as a primary limiting factor when models communicate hierarchical structure through natural language.

</details>
